# UP-T — Project Documentation

Detailed reference documentation for the **UP-T** web-based custom-product designer: a React/Redux single-page app that lets users design and order custom-printed products (t‑shirts, bags, stickers, laser-engraved/cut items, acrylic stands, embroidery, and 3D‑printed products), preview them in 2D and 3D, and submit orders.

This document was generated by reading the project's source files directly (not inferred from file names) and covers, for every hand-written file: its purpose, every exported function/component, every significant field/prop/state key and what it represents, and — for the two largest files (`src/containers/App/index.jsx` and `src/containers/Options/index.jsx`) and the core Redux/middleware layer — every method and every action type handled.

## Scope & how this doc is organized

The codebase is large (~130,000 lines including vendored code) and falls into two categories, treated differently below:

- **Hand-written application code** (containers, components, Redux actions/reducers/middleware, API clients, app-specific utilities) — documented in full function/field-level detail.
- **Vendored third-party code** — code copied into the repo from external libraries rather than authored here. This includes:
  - `src/draw-tool/drawtool.js` — a pre-built, webpack-bundled canvas/design-tool engine (`/*! Draw-Tool library version: "1.1.733" */`, exposing a global `DrawTool` class wrapping a Fabric.js-style canvas). At 56,500+ lines of bundled module code, its *internal* implementation is not hand-maintained source in this repo, so instead of cataloging thousands of internal bundle functions, this doc documents its **public API surface** — every method/event actually called or listened to from the rest of the app — which is what matters for understanding and maintaining the application.
  - Several files in `src/utils/` are vendored copies of well-known open-source libraries (Three.js example loaders/controls, the `csg.js` BSP boolean-geometry library, `imagetracerjs`, a skeleton-tracing algorithm). These are documented at the "purpose + public API actually used by the app" level rather than line-by-line, and are explicitly marked **Vendored: yes** with their source identified.

Every section below was produced by directly reading the corresponding source files. Where an agent noticed a likely bug, dead code path, or unusual pattern while reading, it is called out inline (search for "bug", "dead code", "vestigial", or "Notable" in each section) — these are observations about the *existing* code, not documentation of intended behavior.

## Tech Stack & Project Layout

- **Frontend**: React 15 (`React.PropTypes`, class components, no hooks) + `react-redux` 4 + Redux 3, with `redux-actions` for action creators/reducers.
- **Canvas/2D design engine**: a vendored `DrawTool` library (`src/draw-tool/drawtool.js`), wrapping a Fabric.js-style canvas, exposed globally and used throughout the app to manipulate the print/engrave canvas ("sides"), layers, brushes, text, shapes, and laser cut-paths.
- **3D preview**: Three.js (`three` npm package plus several vendored/patched loaders in `src/utils`: `GLTFLoader`, `DRACOLoader`, `SVGLoader`, `OBJExporter`, `OrbitControls`) used for 3D product preview, acrylic-laser pedestal preview, and CSG (boolean geometry) cutting via `csg-lib.js`/`three-csg.js`.
- **Image processing**: custom algorithms in `src/utils` (`ImageCuttingLaser`, `MethodImageProcessing`, `CropFabricImage`) plus vendored tracing libraries (`ImageTracer`, `TraceSkeleton`) for generating laser cut-paths, keyholes, and pedestal outlines from rendered canvas pixel data.
- **Build**: Webpack 1.x, Babel 6 (ES2015 + React + stage-1 presets), SCSS via `node-sass`.
- **State**: two Redux reducer slices — `drawTool` (canvas/editor UI state) and `product` (catalog, filters, templates, user/session, 3D upload state) — combined in `src/reducers/index.js`, with nearly all business logic living in `src/middleware/draw-tool.js` and `src/middleware/product.js` rather than in the reducers themselves.
- **Collaboration**: Socket.IO-based real-time design co-editing ("room" chat, `src/containers/Options/FooterChat.jsx`).

Top-level source layout:
```
src/
  actions/       Redux action creators (draw-tool, product)
  api/           REST API client functions (extras, options, products)
  components/    Presentational/feature React components (~45 files)
  constants/     App-wide constants, enums, translations
  containers/    Top-level connected React containers (App, Header, Toolbar, Options, ...)
  draw-tool/     Vendored DrawTool canvas engine bundle
  middleware/    Redux middleware — the app's core business logic
  reducers/      Redux reducers (draw-tool, product)
  store/         Redux store configuration
  utils/         App-specific + vendored utility/algorithm code
  styles/        SCSS stylesheets
```

# Part 1 — Redux State Management & REST API Layer

## UP-T Redux / API Layer Reference

This section documents the Redux action creators, reducers, middleware (business logic), constants, store configuration, and REST API client functions that make up the state-management and networking layer of the UP-T custom-product designer. The canvas itself is driven by a vendored `DrawTool` singleton (`src/draw-tool/drawtool.js`, exposed as `window.DrawTool`); nearly all middleware logic exists to translate Redux actions into calls on `DrawTool.sides.selected` (the active print/engrave side, itself wrapping a Fabric.js canvas) and to keep Redux state in sync with what the tool does.

---

## src/actions/draw-tool.js

**Purpose:** Defines every Redux action creator related to the drawing canvas/editor — tool selection, brushes, text, layers, shapes/stickers, colors, zoom/undo/redo, image upload, 3D preview toggles, and loading flags. All are built with `redux-actions`' `createAction(type)`, so each is a function `payload => ({ type, payload })`; none contain business logic themselves — the real behavior lives in `src/reducers/draw-tool.js` (state shape) and `src/middleware/draw-tool.js` (side effects against `DrawTool`).

### Exports (action creator — action type — purpose)
- `resetDefaultParas` — `RESET_DEFAULT_PARAS` — resets/sets the `is_embroidery` flag (payload is a boolean).
- `setPedestalLaser` — `SET_PEDESTALL_LASER` — sets `is_createPedestalLaser` (`{active, data}`) for the acrylic/pedestal-laser stand workflow.
- `setStepAcrylic` — `SET_STEP_ACRYLIC` — (dispatched but not handled by the draw-tool reducer's switch; used elsewhere as a flag, e.g. read via `DrawTool.load_step_acrylic`).
- `saveBorder` — `SAVE_BORDER` — resets `history` to a blank collection (used when a border/workspace is saved).
- `updateBrushes` — `UPDATE_BRUSHES` — loads the list of available brushes (from `DrawerBrush` API records) into state.
- `updateFonts` — `UPDATE_FONTS` — loads the general/default font list.
- `updateFontsAl` — `UPDATE_FONTS_AL` — (handled in `middleware/product.js`) registers `@font-face`/web-fonts for **all** fonts via `DrawTool.fontLoader` or manual `<style>` injection for IE.
- `updateFontsByName` — `UPDATE_FONTS_BY_NAME` — (handled in `middleware/product.js`) registers a single font by name/urls, used when a saved design references a font not yet loaded.
- `updateFontsJP` / `updateFontsEN` / `updateFontsWD` / `updateFontsDGT` / `updateFontsBC` / `updateFontsEUL` / `updateFontsGothic` / `updateFontsToMor` / `updateFontsJPEM` / `updateFontsENEM` — `UPDATE_FONTS_JP` / `UPDATE_FONTS_EN` / `UPDATE_FONTS_WD` / `UPDATE_FONTS_DGT` / `UPDATE_FONTS_BC` / `UPDATE_FONTS_EUL` / `UPDATE_FONTS_GOTHIC` / `UPDATE_FONTS_TO_MO` / `UPDATE_FONTS_JP_EMBROIDERY` / `UPDATE_FONTS_EN_EMBROIDERY` — load font sub-categories (Japanese, English, Kana/"WD", "Design"/DGT, Brush/BC, European/EUL, Gothic, Mincho "To-Mo", Japanese/English embroidery fonts) into their own state slices.
- `updateCategoriesFonts` — `UPDATE_CATEGORIES_FONTS` — loads font category titles.
- `updateShapesCategories` — `UPDATE_SHAPES_CATEGORIES` — loads shape category list.
- `updateShapes` — `UPDATE_SHAPES` — loads shape SVG URLs for a category.
- `updateShapesLaser` — `UPDATE_SHAPES_LASER` — loads laser-cut shape categories.
- `updateModelShapesLaser` — `UPDATE_MODEL_SHAPES_LASER` — loads pedestal/model laser-cutting categories.
- `updateStickersCategories` — `UPDATE_STICKERS_CATEGORIES` — loads sticker category list.
- `updateProcessBar` — `UPDATE_PROCESS` — sets the numeric loading-progress value (0–100/0.x scale).
- `upload` — `UPLOAD` — generic upload-in-progress marker action (payload usage is consumer-defined).
- `updateCapacityUploading` — `UPDATE_CAPACITY_UPLOADING` — increments/decrements the running total of image bytes uploaded for the session (also mirrors the value onto `DrawTool.capacity_uploadingAllImage`).
- `setLoading` — `LOADING` — toggles the global `loading` spinner flag.
- `setLoadingProcess` — `LOADING_BAR` — toggles the progress-bar visibility flag.
- `setLoadingCategories` — `LOADING_CATEGORIES` — toggles the categories-loading flag.
- `setLoadingColors` — `LOADING_COLORS` — toggles the colors-loading flag.
- `setImageUploader` — `IMAGE_UPLOADER` — sets `image_uploader` counter/state (image uploader UI state).
- `setFilter` — `FILTER` — toggles a generic filter-panel boolean.
- `insertImage` — `INSERT_IMAGE` — inserts an uploaded image onto the selected side (middleware calls `DrawTool.sides.selected.items.addImage`), also clears `loading`.
- `setActiveTool` — `SET_ACTIVE_TOOL` — the primary tool-switch action (pointer/brush/text/embroider/laser tools/panning/grid/etc.); drives the large `SET_ACTIVE_TOOL` case in `middleware/draw-tool.js`.
- `empty` — `EMPTY` — asks for confirmation (SweetAlert) then clears all items from the current side.
- `emptyNow` — `EMPTY_NOW` — clears all items from the current side without confirmation.
- `emptyLaser` — `EMPTY_LASER` — asks for confirmation then clears laser-cut data across all sides.
- `zoomIn` / `zoomOut` — `ZOOM_IN` / `ZOOM_OUT` — zoom the canvas of the selected side.
- `zoomZone` — `ZOOM_ZONE` — sets `zoomZone` state and `DrawTool.sides.selected.zoom_zone`.
- `undo` / `redo` — `UNDO` / `REDO` — trigger `DrawTool.history.undo/redo` for the selected side.
- `remove` — `REMOVE` — removes the currently selected canvas item (or, for laser die-cut items, opens the `DeleteLineCut` dialog).
- `selectBrush` — `SELECT_BRUSH` — activates a named brush type on the selected side's items.
- `selectBrushColor` / `selectBrushSize` / `selectBrushOpacity` — `SELECT_BRUSH_COLOR` / `SELECT_BRUSH_SIZE` / `SELECT_BRUSH_OPACITY` — update brush options (color/width/opacity) in state and live on the active brush.
- `updateHistory` — `UPDATE_HISTORY` — replaces the `history` object in state (also clears `file_upload`).
- `updateBorder` — `UPDATE_BORDER` — recomputes laser cut-path clip borders from all laser items on the side (large geometry routine, see middleware).
- `changeBorder` — `CHANGE_BORDER` — stores a `margin` value in state and shifts all items on the side left by that amount.
- `updateCurentLaserBorder` — `UPDATE_CURENT_LASER_BORDER` — recalculates/activates the current laser cutting border and switches tool to `laser` or `layers` depending on mode.
- `updateCuttingImageBorder` — `UPDATE_CUTTING_IMAGE` — applies a laser cut path as an image crop mask, removing the laser path item afterward.
- `setBorderSameCM` — `SET_BORDER_SAME_CM` — resets the side's border to a plain rectangle matching its current cm dimensions.
- `addText` — `ADD_TEXT` — adds a new text item to the canvas with current `textOptions`, also sets `text` in state.
- `selectTextColor` / `selectTextFont` / `selectTextCategoriesFonts` / `selectTextSize` / `selectTextAlign` / `selectTextBold` / `selectTextItalic` / `selectTextVertical` — `SELECT_TEXT_COLOR` / `SELECT_TEXT_FONT` / `SELECT_TEXT_CATEGORIES_FONTS` / `SELECT_TEXT_SIZE` / `SELECT_TEXT_ALIGN` / `SELECT_TEXT_BOLD` / `SELECT_TEXT_ITALIC` / `SELECT_TEXT_VERTICAL` — update `textOptions` in state and apply the same property to the selected text item live.
- `selectText` / `selectTextOff` — `SELECT_TEXT` / `SELECT_TEXT_OFF` — (not handled in the reducer switch or middleware switch shown; used by UI components to toggle text-tool UI state directly, or reserved).
- `changeText` / `changeTextVal` — `CHANGE_TEXT` / `CHANGE_TEXT_VAL` — update the text content of the selected item (`CHANGE_TEXT` triggers the item's `setText` in middleware).
- `updateLayers` — `UPDATE_LAYERS` — replaces the layer list for a given side (`{side, layers}`) and clears `layersSelected`; also fires `DrawTool.trigger('layers:update')`.
- `updateChangeDesign` — `UPDATE_CHANGE_DESIGN` — sets the `change_design` dirty flag.
- `focusLayer` / `blurLayer` — `FOCUS_LAYER` / `BLUR_LAYER` — add/remove a layer uuid from `layersSelected` (multi-select in the layers panel).
- `alignLayer` — `ALIGN_LAYER` — invokes a named alignment method (e.g. `moveToIndex`) on `DrawTool.sides.selected.layers` with the currently selected layers.
- `sortLayers` — `SORT_LAYERS` — reorders layers (drag-and-drop) by calling `layers.moveToIndex` per item.
- `alignItem` — `ALIGN_ITEM` — invokes a named alignment method (e.g. bring to front) on the selected item.
- `selectShapeColor` — `SELECT_SHAPE_COLOR` — sets `shapeColor` state and applies fill to the selected path item.
- `insertShape` — `INSERT_SHAPE` — adds an SVG shape to the canvas (with shape-color fill), guarded by `checkItemCanAddValue`.
- `insertLaser` — `INSERT_LASER` — adds a laser-cut SVG + path data to the canvas.
- `insertPedestalLaser` — `INSERT_PEDESTAL_LASER` — adds a pedestal/acrylic-laser image item (only in `ACRYLIC_LASER` mode).
- `selectShape` — `SELECT_SHAPE` — (no dedicated middleware case; used by UI to track which shape category/shape is highlighted).
- `updateStickers` — `UPDATE_STICKERS` — loads sticker image URLs for a category.
- `insertSticker` — `INSERT_STICKER` — adds a sticker SVG to the canvas, guarded by `checkItemCanAddValue`.
- `toggleColorPicker` — `TOGGLE_COLOR_PICKER` — shows/hides the color picker and syncs `DrawTool.sides.selected.colorPicker.active`.
- `updateColorPicker` — `UPDATE_COLOR_PICKER` — sets the picked color value in state.
- `removeColor` — `REMOVE_COLOR` — removes a color (chroma-key style, tolerance 25) from the selected item's image via `removeColor()`, wrapped in a loading flag.
- `tapOrRibbonProcessingColorData` — `TapOrRibbonProcessing` — recolors "tap/ribbon" special-product images to one of a fixed palette depending on `DrawTool.modeToolDraw`.
- `rimImageToSvg` — `RIM_IMAGE_TO_SVG` — converts/traces the selected item's image rim to SVG via `items.rimImageProcessing()`.
- `updateStampsImageCategories` / `updateStampsLoveCategories` / `updateStampsImageFavorite` / `updateStampsLovesFavorite` / `updateStampsImage` / `updateStampsLove` — `UPDATE_STAMPS_IMAGE_CATEGORIES` / `UPDATE_STAMPS_LOVE_CATEGORIES` / `UPDATE_STAMPS_IMAGE_FAVORITE` / `UPDATE_STAMPS_LOVES_FAVORITE` / `UPDATE_STAMPS_IMAGE` / `UPDATE_STAMPS_LOVE` — load "stamp" sticker categories/lists and their favorites (favorites list capped to 30/50 items respectively).
- `selectItem` — `SELECT_ITEM` — marks a canvas item as selected; reducer derives `text`/`textOptions`/`shapeColor`/`is_image`/`item_brush`/`image_url_re`/font-category title from the item.
- `unselectItem` — `UNSELECT_ITEM` — clears selection state and fires `DrawTool.trigger('item:unselect')`.
- `setFileUpload` — `SET_FILEUPLOAD` — stores the list of files currently queued/mid-upload.
- `updateDegree` — `UPDATE_DEGREE` — rotates the selected item to a given angle (with special-case guards for acrylic-laser "mode_design" items) and triggers carpet/acrylic move checks.
- `updateScale` — `UPDATE_SCALE` — scales the selected item uniformly (preserving X/Y aspect ratio) and triggers carpet/laser checks.
- `updateColorsFill` / `updateColorImagesFill` — `UPDATE_COLORS_FILL` / `UPDATE_COLORS_IMAGE_FILL` — store detected fill colors / image-derived colors for a color-swap UI.
- `fillColors` — `FILL_COLORS` — replaces one color with another on the selected item (`{old_color, new_color}`); for tap/ribbon products, recolors the whole side and (for chat/online mode) broadcasts a color-update command.
- `updateTransform` — `UPDATE_TRANSFORM` — stores a transform-mode string.
- `updateTextEffect` — `UPDATE_TEXT_EFFECT` — stores the current text-warp effect name (e.g. `straight`, `curved`, `bulge`).
- `updateTextSpacing` — `UPDATE_TEXT_SPACING` — sets char/line spacing in state and applies it (`setSpacing` for warped effects, `setCharSpacing` otherwise) to the selected text item.
- `updateGridSize` — `UPDATE_GRID_SIZE` — sets `grid_cols`/`grid_rows` in state and rebuilds the on-canvas alignment grid.
- `updateImageupload` — `UPDATE_IMAGE_UPLOAD` — appends an entry to the `image_upload` array (upload history/queue).
- `setFlagOption` — `SET_FLAG_OPTION` — stores a generic string flag (e.g. `"design"` mode) read by other middleware to change behavior.
- `setTextOption` — `SET_TEXT_OPTION` — resets `textOptions.font` to `'normal_font'`.
- `updateShapes3D` — `UPDATE_SHAPES_3D` — loads the 3D shape category list.
- `update3DViewData` — `UPDATE_3D_DATA_VIEW` — stores the currently-active 3D model selection (`{selected, url, info, position, type_mesh}`).
- `change3DPreviewStatus` / `change3DPreviewLaserStatus` — `CHANG_3D_PREVIEW_STATUS` / `CHANG_3D_PREVIEW_LASER_STATUS` — toggle the 3D preview panel (normal / laser variants).
- `setLoadingKeys` — `LOADING_KEY` — toggles a "loading keyword filters" flag.
- `setLoadingAllProducts` — `LOADING_ALL_PRODUCT` — toggles a "loading all products" flag.
- `setLoadingAPI` — `LOADING_API` — toggles a generic "API call complete" flag.
- `setIsText2IMG` — `SET_TEXT_2_IMG` — toggles the text-to-image AI feature flag (`isText2Img`).

## src/actions/product.js

**Purpose:** Action creators for product catalog browsing, product/color/side selection, templates, filters (the large faceted-search filter UI), user/session info, favorites, 3D-preview ("Tripo") state, and the multi-file (PNG+ZIP) 3D upload pairing feature. Like `draw-tool.js`, these are thin `createAction` wrappers; behavior lives in `src/reducers/product.js` and `src/middleware/product.js`.

### Exports (action creator — action type — purpose)
- `toggleLoadProductContainer` — `TOGGLE_LOAD_PRODUCT_CONTAINER` — shows/hides the product list loading overlay.
- `toggleLoadProductCategoryContainer` — `TOGGLE_LOAD_PRODUCT_CATEGORY_CONTAINER` — shows/hides the category list loading overlay.
- `toggleLoadProductTypeContainer` — `TOGGLE_LOAD_PRODUCT_TYPE_CONTAINER` — shows/hides the product-type container and sets `currentCategory`/`currentID` (payload `{state, id}`).
- `toggleMobileNavigation` — `TOGGLE_MOBILE_NAVIGATION` — toggles the mobile nav drawer.
- `toggleBoxFilter` — `TOGGLE_BOX_FILTER` — toggles the filter box panel.
- `loadCategoryStyles` — `LOAD_CATEGORY_STYLES` — loads top-level category "styles" (e.g. clothing/flags/mobile/stone/laser groupings).
- `loadCategories` — `LOAD_CATEGORIES` — loads the full categories list.
- `loadColors` — `LOAD_COLORS ` — (note trailing space in the action type string) loads global color list.
- `loadCategoriesFilter` — `LOAD_CATEGORIES_FILTER` — loads categories for the filter UI and resets all filter-related state fields to defaults.
- `loadProducts` — `LOAD_PRODUCTS` — loads a page/set of products keyed by category/id.
- `loadMakers` — `LOAD_MAKERS` — loads maker/brand names.
- `selectColor` — `SELECT_COLOR` — selects a product color; drives the large color-reload flow in `middleware/product.js` (re-imports JSON design data for the new color).
- `reloadColor` — `RELOAD_COLOR` — re-applies the currently selected color's design data (used after loading a saved design), driving an equally large flow.
- `selectSide` — `SELECT_SIDE` — selects a print/side (front/back/sleeve/etc.), switching the visible canvas.
- `updateSideLaser` — `UPDATE_SIDE_LASER` — selects a side specifically for laser-mode products (filters out mask-laser items, clones laser items from other sides for symmetric layout).
- `loadProduct` — `LOAD_PRODUCT` — loads full product+color+side data; reducer computes `colorSelected`/`sideSelected`/pricing, middleware imports the Fabric JSON into `DrawTool`.
- `saveTemplate` — `SAVE_TEMPLATE` — saves the current side design as a reusable template (uploads PNG+SVG, then calls the templates API).
- `updateTemplates` — `UPDATE_TEMPLATES` — stores the loaded template list.
- `applyTemplate` — `APPLY_TEMPLATE` — applies a saved template's SVG/JSON onto the current side, with embroidery/print conflict checks.
- `removeTemplate` — `REMOVE_TEMPLATE` — removes a template id from state (after API delete).
- `loadProductWithDesign` — `LOAD_PRODUCT_WITH_DESIGN` — loads a product together with a previously-saved per-side design (admin/order edit flow); middleware `fromJSON`s each side.
- `updatePrice` — `UPDATE_PRICE` — recomputes displayed `price` from `initial_price` + payload delta.
- `setLoadingData` — `LOADING_DATA` — toggles the `loading_data` flag.
- `checkProduct` — `CHECK_PRODUCT` — toggles a "product checked/verified" flag.
- `updateUser` — `UPDATE_USER` — stores the logged-in user object/flag.
- `updateUserAccess` — `UPDATE_USER_ACCESS` — stores user access/permission info.
- `selectEmbroiderArea` — `SELECT_EMBROIDER_AREA` — selects a named embroidery sub-area as the active side.
- `loadDesignFn` — `LOAD_DESIGN_FN` — marks that the "load design" function has run (`load_design_fn: true`).
- `loadUserTemplateCategories` — `LOAD_USER_TEMPLATE_CATEGORIES` — stores user-template categories/tag tree (from `getTemplatesUserForDesign`).
- `selectTemplateCategory` / `unSelectTemplateCategory` — `SELECT_TEMPLATE_CATEGORY` / `UNSELECT_TEMPLATE_CATEGORY` — select/clear the active user-template category filter.
- `loadUserTemplatesFilter` — `LOAD_USER_TEMPLATES_FILTER` — stores the filtered user-template list.
- `removeUserTemplatesFilter` — `REMOVE_USER_TEMPLATES_FILTER` — clears user-template category/list state.
- `applyTemplateUser` — `APPLY_TEMPLATE_USER` — applies a user-saved template's canvas JSON onto the current side, with extensive embroidery/print/laser compatibility checks and image-size (30MB) limits.
- `showDesignModal` — `SHOW_DESIGN_MODAL` — shows/hides the "select design" modal.
- `selectDesignToModal` — `SELECT_DESIGN_TO_MODAL` — stores which design is targeted by the modal.
- `toggleLoadFontsCategoryContainer` — `TOGGLE_LOAD_FONTS_CATEGORY_CONTAINER` — loading flag for the fonts-category container.
- `loadFontsFilter` — `LOAD_FONTS_FILTER` — stores the fonts filter list.
- `showModal` — `SHOW_MODAL` — generic modal visibility flag (`is_show_md`).
- `loadImagePreview` — `LOAD_IMAGE_PREVIEW` — stores the list of preview images (clears 3D-info list if empty).
- `addImage3DPreview` — `ADD_IMAGE3D_PREVIEW` — appends/sorts a 3D-preview image entry by `index` and flattens all `list_image` arrays into `list_image_preview`.
- `loadPreviewColor` — `LOAD_PREVIEW_COLOR` — stores available preview colors.
- `loadImagickModel` — `LOAD_IMAGICK_MODEL` — stores Imagick/3D-model item list.
- `loadKeyFilter` — `LOAD_KEY_FILTER` — stores raw keyword-filter list.
- `updateKeyFilter` / `updateSexFilter` / `updateSPCaseKeyFilter` / `updateModelPhone` / `updatePrintFilter` / `updateMaterialArrFilter` / `updateModelSPCaseArrFilter` / `updateModelSPCaseArrFilterSelected` / `updateSceneFilter` / `updatePriceFilter` / `updateSizeFilter` / `updateTypeOfProducFilter` / `updateTypeOfProducFilterBag` / `updateColorFilter` / `updateSideFilter` / `updateMaterialFilter` / `updateValueFilter` / `updateCategoryFilter` / `updateCategorySubFilter` / `updateArrStatusFilter` / `updateTypeFilter` / `updateNumberProductFilter` / `updateValueProductSort` / `updateToogleFilterBox` — corresponding `UPDATE_*_FILTER` types — each stores one facet of the product-search/filter UI (keyword, sex/gender, smartphone-case type, model-phone, print method, material, scene, price bracket, size, product type incl. bag variant, color, side, category/subcategory, status, sort order, filter-box toggle).
- `getAllProductByMasterCategory` — `GET_ALL_PRODUCT_BY_MASTER_CATEGORY` — stores products fetched for a master category (`action.payload.products`).
- `updateCurrentId` — `UPDATE_CURRENT_ID` — stores the current product/category id.
- `updateAllKeyFilterPrice` / `updateAllKeyFilterCategory` / `updateAllKeyFilterMaker` / `updateAllKeyFilterSex` / `updateAllKeyFilterPrint` / `updateAllKeyFilterMaterial` / `updateAllKeyFilterScene` / `updateAllKeyFilterSize` / `updateAllKeyFilterColors` / `updateAllKeyFilterConditions` / `updateAllKeyFilterTypeOfSmartPhone` / `updateAllKeyFilterTypeOfBag` — corresponding `UPDATE_ALL_KEY_FILTER_*` types — store the master list of available filter option-sets (as opposed to the currently *selected* filter values above).
- `updateCatProductIdItem` — `UPDATE_CAT_PRODUCT_ID_ITEM` — stores `categoryProductItem` flag/id.
- `updateTypeProducts` — `UPDATE_TYPE_PRODUCTS` — stores `typeOfProduct` (numeric mode switch).
- `updateCurrentCategoryID` — `UPDATE_CURRENT_CATEGORY_ID` — stores `current_Category_id`.
- `setInfoChatSocket` — `SET_INFO_CHAT_SOCKET` — stores chat/socket connection info for the collaborative design-room feature.
- `setInfoRoomDesign` — `SET_INFO_ROOM_DESIGN` — stores design-room metadata for collaborative sessions.
- `updateAIModel` — `UPDATE_AI_MODEL` — stores the selected AI model (for text-to-image, `AIModel`).
- `loadFavoriteProducts` — `LOAD_FAVORITE_PRODUCTS` — stores the user's favorited products (`action.payload.products`).
- `updateFavoriteProducts` — `UPDATE_FAVORITE_PRODUCTS` — replaces the favorites array wholesale.
- `increaseFavoriteProducts` / `decreaseFavoriteProducts` — `INCREASE_FAVORITE_PRODUCTS` / `DECREASE_FAVORITE_PRODUCTS` — (handled in `middleware/product.js`) push/splice an item into/out of `favoriteProducts` then re-dispatch `updateFavoriteProducts`.
- `updateStyleCategoryID` — `UPDATE_STYLE_CATEGORY_ID` — stores `category_style_id`.
- `set3DTripo` — `SET_3D_TRIPO` — toggles `tripo3D_mode` (AI 3D generation mode).
- `setPreviewImage3D` — `SET_PREVIEW_IMAGE_3D` — stores the 3D preview image.
- `setUrl3D` — `SET_URL_3D` — stores the generated/uploaded 3D model URL.
- `setImage3DCapture` — `SET_IMAGE_3D_CAPTURE` — stores a captured screenshot of the 3D view.
- `setZipObjFile3d` — `SET_ZIP_OBJ_FILE_3D` — stores a zipped 3D object file reference.
- `addFilePair` — `ADD_FILE_PAIR` — appends a new `{png, zip}` pair to `filePairs`.
- `updateFilePair` — `UPDATE_FILE_PAIR` — updates the `zip` (or `png`) member of an existing pair by index (`{index, filePair}`).

---

## src/reducers/draw-tool.js

**Purpose:** The `drawTool` slice reducer (via `redux-actions`' `handleActions`). Owns nearly all canvas-editor UI state: active tool, brush/text options, layers, colors, history, zoom, uploads, and 3D-preview flags. Imports the live `DrawTool` singleton directly so several handlers read/write `DrawTool.*` properties (e.g. `is_embroidery`, `capacity_uploadingAllImage`) as a side effect of reducing — an unusual pattern that couples "pure" reducer logic to the imperative canvas engine.

### State shape (`initialState`)
- `activeTool` (string) — currently selected tool; defaults to `'sides'` on narrow screens (≤1080px) else `'pointer'`.
- `is_embroidery` (bool) — whether embroidery mode is active.
- `is_createPedestalLaser` (`{active, data}`) — pedestal/acrylic-laser-stand creation state.
- `activeBrush` (string) — selected brush type key.
- `brushOptions` (`{color, width[, opacity]}`) — current brush settings.
- `textEl` (object|null) — the currently selected text Fabric object (mirrors selection).
- `text` (string) — current/edited text content.
- `textOptions` (`{color, font, size, align, bold, italic, vertical}`) — text tool settings.
- `categoriesFontsOptions` (`{title}`) — active font-category label (localized, e.g. "日本語").
- `layers` (object, keyed by side) — per-side layer lists.
- `layersSelected` (array) — uuids of multi-selected layers.
- `color` (string, rgba) — a generic "current color" value.
- `shapeColor` (string, rgba) — fill color for shapes/paths.
- `stickersCat` / `stickers` (arrays) — sticker categories / sticker URLs for the active category.
- `stampsImageCat` / `stampsLoveCat` / `stampsImage` / `stampsLoves` / `stampFavorite` / `stampLovesFavorite` (arrays) — "stamp" sticker categories/lists and favorites (paid stamps + "love"/sumoppi stamps).
- `colorPickerColor` (string) — currently picked color.
- `colorPicker` (bool) — color picker open/closed.
- `availableFontsCategories` (array) — font category titles.
- `shapesCat` / `shapes` (arrays) — shape categories / shape URLs.
- `shapesLaser` / `shapesModelLaser` (arrays) — laser shape categories / pedestal-model laser categories.
- `shapes3D` (array) — 3D shape/model category+item metadata (used to look up per-product 3D previews).
- `is_3d_preview` / `is_3d_preview_laser` (bool) — 3D preview panel visibility (normal / laser).
- `shapes3DSelect` (`{selected, url, info, position, type_mesh}`) — the currently active 3D model selection.
- `history` (`{currentIndex, collection}`) — undo/redo snapshot stack (reset to a single empty entry on save).
- `zoomZone` (bool) — zoom-to-selection toggle.
- `loading` (bool) — global loading spinner.
- `selected` (object|null) — currently selected Fabric item (raw object, mirrors `DrawTool` selection).
- `file_upload` (array) — files queued/uploading.
- `process` (number) — progress bar value.
- `loading_process` (bool) — progress bar visibility.
- `loading_category` (bool) — categories loading flag (defaults `true`).
- `loading_colors` (bool) — colors loading flag.
- `filter` (bool) — generic filter-panel toggle.
- `image_uploader` (number) — image uploader widget state/counter.
- `capacity_uploading` (number) — running total bytes uploaded this session.
- `degree_rotate` / `scale` (number) — last rotate/scale UI values.
- `colors_fill` / `colors_Image_fill` (arrays) — detected fill colors for color-swap tooling.
- `transform` (string) — transform-mode marker.
- `text_effect` (string, default `'straight'`) — text warp effect.
- `text_spacing` (number, default `20`) — text char/line spacing.
- `multipler` (number, default `1`) — unused/reserved multiplier.
- `grid_rows` / `grid_cols` (number, default `20`) — alignment grid dimensions.
- `fontsTS` (array) — reserved font list.
- `is_image` (bool) — whether selected item is an image.
- `image_upload` (array) — history of uploaded images.
- `image_uuid` (string) — uuid of the selected image item.
- `flag_option` (string) — generic mode flag (e.g. `"design"`).
- `image_url_re` (bool) — whether the selected image's src looks like a remote/re-hosted URL (S3 or base64 PNG).
- `item_brush` (bool) — whether the selected item is a brush stroke.
- `change_design` (bool) — dirty flag set whenever layers update.
- `allFontsList` / `allFonts` (arrays) — full raw font records / font detail objects.
- `loadingKey` / `loadingAllProructs` (bool) — filter-keyword and all-products loading flags.
- `loadingAPIComplate` (bool, default `true`) — generic API-completion flag.
- `isText2Img` (bool) — text-to-image AI mode toggle.

### Action Types Handled
- `RESET_DEFAULT_PARAS` — sets `is_embroidery` from payload.
- `SET_PEDESTALL_LASER` — sets `is_createPedestalLaser`.
- `CHANGE_BORDER` — sets `margin` (not in initial state, added dynamically).
- `SAVE_BORDER` — resets `history` to `{currentIndex:0, collection:[{}]}`.
- `UPDATE_BRUSHES` — sets `availableBrushes` (mapped values) and `activeBrush` (first brush's value).
- `UPDATE_FONTS` — sets `availableFonts`, `availableFontsTitle` (via helper `getFontsTitle`), `allFonts`, default `textOptions.font`, `allFontsList`.
- `UPDATE_FONTS_JP` / `UPDATE_FONTS_WD` / `UPDATE_FONTS_DGT` / `UPDATE_FONTS_BC` / `UPDATE_FONTS_EUL` / `UPDATE_FONTS_GOTHIC` / `UPDATE_FONTS_TO_MO` / `UPDATE_FONTS_EN` / `UPDATE_FONTS_JP_EMBROIDERY` / `UPDATE_FONTS_EN_EMBROIDERY` — each sets its own `availableFonts*` array and updates `textOptions.font` to the first entry.
- `UPDATE_CATEGORIES_FONTS` — sets `availableFontsCategories` (titles).
- `UPDATE_SHAPES_CATEGORIES` / `UPDATE_SHAPES` / `UPDATE_SHAPES_LASER` / `UPDATE_MODEL_SHAPES_LASER` — set `shapesCat` / `shapes` / `shapesLaser` / `shapesModelLaser` from mapped payload.
- `UPDATE_3D_DATA_VIEW` — sets `shapes3DSelect`.
- `UPDATE_SHAPES_3D` — sets `shapes3D`.
- `UPDATE_STICKERS_CATEGORIES` / `UPDATE_STICKERS` — set `stickersCat` / `stickers`.
- `UPDATE_STAMPS_IMAGE_CATEGORIES` / `UPDATE_STAMPS_LOVE_CATEGORIES` — set `stampsImageCat` / `stampsLoveCat`.
- `UPDATE_STAMPS_IMAGE_FAVORITE` — sets `stampFavorite`, capped to the first 30 entries.
- `UPDATE_STAMPS_LOVES_FAVORITE` — sets `stampLovesFavorite`, capped to the first 50 entries.
- `UPDATE_STAMPS_IMAGE` / `UPDATE_STAMPS_LOVE` — set `stampsImage` / `stampsLoves` (an unused local `stampFavorite` filter by `.enjoy` is computed but discarded).
- `SET_ACTIVE_TOOL` — special-cased for `embroider`/`embroider_extend`/`_embroider` (syncs `is_embroidery` from `DrawTool.is_embroidery`) and `saveBorder` (resets `history`, clears `file_upload`); otherwise sets `activeTool` and clears `file_upload`.
- `SELECT_BRUSH` — sets `activeBrush`.
- `SELECT_BRUSH_COLOR` / `SELECT_BRUSH_SIZE` / `SELECT_BRUSH_OPACITY` — merge into `brushOptions` (`color`/`width`/`opacity`).
- `SELECT_TEXT_CATEGORIES_FONTS` — merges into `categoriesFontsOptions.title`.
- `SELECT_TEXT_COLOR` / `SELECT_TEXT_FONT` / `SELECT_TEXT_SIZE` / `SELECT_TEXT_ALIGN` / `SELECT_TEXT_BOLD` / `SELECT_TEXT_ITALIC` / `SELECT_TEXT_VERTICAL` — merge the corresponding field into `textOptions`.
- `ADD_TEXT` / `CHANGE_TEXT` / `CHANGE_TEXT_VAL` — set `text`.
- `UPDATE_LAYERS` — sets `layers[side]`, clears `layersSelected`, sets `change_design: true`.
- `UPDATE_CHANGE_DESIGN` — sets `change_design`.
- `FOCUS_LAYER` / `BLUR_LAYER` — add/remove a uuid from `layersSelected`.
- `SELECT_SHAPE_COLOR` — sets `shapeColor`.
- `TOGGLE_COLOR_PICKER` — sets `colorPicker`.
- `UPDATE_COLOR_PICKER` — sets `colorPickerColor`.
- `SELECT_ITEM` — the most complex handler: reads `DrawTool.sides.selected.items.selected.item` to derive `image_uuid`/`is_image`/`item_brush`/`image_url_re`; if the item is a text type, matches its `fontFamily` against every `availableFonts*` array to set the localized `categoriesFontsOptions.title`, and populates `textEl`/`text`/`textOptions` from the item's Fabric properties; if a `path` type, sets `shapeColor` from its fill. Always sets `selected` to the payload.
- `UNSELECT_ITEM` — clears `textEl`/`text`, resyncs `is_embroidery` from `DrawTool`, clears `selected`.
- `UPDATE_HISTORY` / `UNDO` / `REDO` — set `history` (and `UPDATE_HISTORY` also clears `file_upload`).
- `ZOOM_ZONE` — sets `zoomZone`.
- `INSERT_IMAGE` — sets `loading: false`.
- `LOADING` / `LOADING_KEY` / `LOADING_ALL_PRODUCT` / `LOADING_API` — set the respective boolean flags.
- `SET_FILEUPLOAD` — sets `file_upload`.
- `UPDATE_PROCESS` / `LOADING_BAR` — set `process` / `loading_process`.
- `FILTER` — sets `filter`.
- `LOADING_CATEGORIES` / `LOADING_COLORS` — set the respective flags.
- `IMAGE_UPLOADER` — sets `image_uploader`.
- `UPDATE_CAPACITY_UPLOADING` — adds payload to `capacity_uploading` and mirrors the new total onto `DrawTool.capacity_uploadingAllImage`.
- `UPDATE_DEGREE` / `UPDATE_SCALE` — set `degree_rotate` / `scale`.
- `UPDATE_COLORS_FILL` / `UPDATE_COLORS_IMAGE_FILL` — set `colors_fill` / `colors_Image_fill`.
- `UPDATE_TRANSFORM` — sets `transform`.
- `UPDATE_TEXT_EFFECT` / `UPDATE_TEXT_SPACING` — set `text_effect` / `text_spacing`.
- `UPDATE_GRID_SIZE` — sets `grid_cols`/`grid_rows` from payload.
- `UPDATE_IMAGE_UPLOAD` — appends payload to `image_upload`.
- `SET_FLAG_OPTION` — sets `flag_option`.
- `SET_TEXT_OPTION` — sets `textOptions.font` to `'normal_font'`.
- `CHANG_3D_PREVIEW_STATUS` / `CHANG_3D_PREVIEW_LASER_STATUS` — set `is_3d_preview` / `is_3d_preview_laser`.
- `SET_TEXT_2_IMG` — sets `isText2Img`.

### Helper functions
- `getFontsTitle(fonts)` — builds a `{[font.name]: font.title}` lookup object from an array of `DrawerFont` records; used by `UPDATE_FONTS` to populate `availableFontsTitle`.

---

## src/reducers/product.js

**Purpose:** The `product` slice reducer. Owns product catalog data, the selected product/color/side and its pricing, templates (site + user), the very large faceted product-filter state, favorites, chat/design-room info, and the 3D ("Tripo") preview/upload-pairing state. Also imports `DrawTool` directly to read `is_data`/`sides` when merging special border data during `LOAD_PRODUCT`.

### State shape (`initialState`)
- `loadProductContainer` / `loadProductCatContainer` / `loadProductTypeContainer` (bool) — loading overlays for product list / category list / product-type container.
- `mobileNavigation` (bool) — mobile nav open state.
- `category_styles` (array) — top-level category style groupings.
- `categories` (array) — full category list.
- `categories_filter` (array) — categories for the filter UI.
- `products` (object, keyed by id) — loaded product pages.
- `embroider_able` (bool) — whether the current product supports embroidery.
- `product` (object|null) — the currently loaded product record.
- `categoryStyle` (object|null) — the product's category style.
- `colorSelected` / `sideSelected` (object|null) — selected `ProductColor` / `ProductColorSide`.
- `templates` (array) — site design templates.
- `currentCategory` (id|null) — active category id.
- `initial_price` / `price` (number) — base product price / currently computed price.
- `sidesPrice` (object, keyed by side name) — per-side print price.
- `loading_data` (bool, default `true`) — product data loading flag.
- `check_product` (bool) — product-verified flag.
- `makers` (array) — maker/brand names.
- `user` (bool|object) — logged-in user info/flag.
- `userAccess` (object) — user permission info.
- `load_design_fn` (bool) — whether the load-design function has run.
- `templateUsersCategory` / `templateCategorySelected` / `templateUsers` — user-template category tree, selected category, and filtered template list.
- `showModalDesign` / `designSelected` — design-selection modal visibility and target.
- `loadFontsCatContainer` (bool) — fonts-category loading flag.
- `font_filter` (array) — font filter list.
- `is_show_md` (bool) — generic modal visibility.
- `list_image_preview` / `list_image_info3D` (arrays) — preview images and their 3D metadata (sorted by `index`).
- `list_preview_color` (array) — preview colors.
- `item_imagick_model` (array) — Imagick/3D model items.
- `price_item_free` (number) — reserved free-price value.
- `list_key_filter` (array) — raw keyword filter list.
- `boxFilter` (bool) — filter box visibility.
- `objKeyFilter` / `objSexFilter` / `objSPCaseFilter` / `objModelPhone` / `objPrintFilter` / `objMaterialArrFilter` / `objModelSPCaseArrFilter` / `objModelSPCaseArrFilterSelected` / `objSceneFilter` / `objPriceFilter` (default `'全て'`="All") / `objSizeFilter` / `objTypeOfSP` / `objTypeOfBag` / `objSideFilter` / `objColorFilter` / `objMaterialFilter` / `objValueFilter` / `objCategoryFilter` / `objCategorySubFilter` / `arrStatusFilter` — the *currently selected* values for each facet of the product filter UI (keyword, gender, smartphone-case type, phone model, print method, material array, SP-case model array + selection, scene, price bracket, size, product type/bag type, side, color, material, generic value, category/subcategory, status list).
- `typeFilter` (string) — active filter "mode" (e.g. `'category'`).
- `numberProductFilter` (number, default `1`) — pagination page number.
- `valueProductSort` (string, default `'3'`) — sort order key.
- `toogleFilterProp` (bool, default `true`) — filter panel expand/collapse.
- `allProductByMasterCategory` (array) — products for a master category.
- `currentID` (id|null) — current product/category id.
- `infoChatSocket` / `infoRoomDesign` (objects) — chat socket + design-room info for collaborative sessions.
- `priceFilters` / `keyCategoryFilters` / `keyMakerFilters` / `keySexFilters` / `keyMaterialFilters` / `keySceneFilters` / `keyPrintFilters` / `keySizeFilters` / `keyColorFilters` / `keyConditionFilters` / `keyTypeOfSPFilters` / `keyTypeOfBagFilters` (arrays) — the *master lists* of available options for each filter facet (as opposed to the `obj*Filter` selected values above).
- `typeOfProduct` (number, default `1`) — product type mode switch.
- `current_Category_id` (id|null) — current category id (duplicate-ish of `currentCategory`).
- `categoryProductItem` (bool) — category/product item flag.
- `favoriteProducts` (array) — user's favorited products.
- `category_style_id` (number, default `1`) — active category style id.
- `tripo3D_mode` (bool) — AI 3D generation mode toggle.
- `previewImage3D` (any|null) — 3D preview image.
- `url3D` (string) — generated/uploaded 3D model URL.
- `image3DCapture` (any|null) — captured 3D screenshot.
- `zip3dfile` (any|null) — zipped 3D object file.
- `filePairs` (array) — list of `{png, zip}` pairs for the 3D upload pairing feature.

### Action Types Handled
- `TOGGLE_LOAD_PRODUCT_CONTAINER` / `TOGGLE_LOAD_PRODUCT_CATEGORY_CONTAINER` / `TOGGLE_LOAD_FONTS_CATEGORY_CONTAINER` — set the respective loading flags.
- `LOAD_FONTS_FILTER` — sets `font_filter`.
- `TOGGLE_LOAD_PRODUCT_TYPE_CONTAINER` — sets `loadProductTypeContainer`, `currentCategory`, `currentID` from `{state, id}`.
- `TOGGLE_MOBILE_NAVIGATION` / `TOGGLE_BOX_FILTER` — set `mobileNavigation` / `boxFilter`.
- `LOAD_CATEGORY_STYLES` — sets `category_styles`.
- `UPDATE_STYLE_CATEGORY_ID` — sets `category_style_id`.
- `LOAD_CATEGORIES` — sets `categories`.
- `LOAD_CATEGORIES_FILTER` — sets `categories_filter` and resets essentially every `obj*Filter`/`arrStatusFilter`/`typeFilter`/`numberProductFilter`/`toogleFilterProp` field to its default (a full filter-panel reset).
- `LOAD_PRODUCTS` — sets `products[action.payload.id] = action.payload.data`.
- `LOAD_MAKERS` — maps payload to a plain array of maker names into `makers`.
- `LOAD_PRODUCT` — finds the matching color (by `colorSelected.id` if provided, else the `is_main` color); merges `border_special` from `DrawTool.is_data` into `DrawTool.sides._collection` if present; if `action.payload.Product` exists, computes `embroider_able` (from `embroider_able`, or `special_draw` 1/9), sets `DrawTool.embroider_able`, and returns a full state update (`colorSelected`, `sideSelected`, `product`, `categoryStyle`, `colors`, `initial_price`, `sidesPrice`, `price`, clears loading/nav flags); otherwise (a colors-only refresh) preserves existing `product`/`categoryStyle`/`price` and just updates `colors`/`sidesPrice`/`loading_data`.
- `LOAD_PRODUCT_WITH_DESIGN` — resolves color by `selected_color_id`, builds `sidesPrice`, resolves `categoryStyle` from `categories`, merges `category_id` into a cloned `Product` object, and sets `colorSelected`/`sideSelected`/`product`/`categoryStyle`/`colors`/pricing/`embroider_able`.
- `SELECT_COLOR` — finds the color by id, rebuilds `sidesPrice`, sets `colorSelected`, closes mobile nav.
- `SELECT_SIDE` / `UPDATE_SIDE_LASER` — find the side by id or name within the selected color's sides, set `sideSelected`, close mobile nav.
- `UPDATE_TEMPLATES` — maps payload to `DesignTemplate` objects into `templates`.
- `LOAD_USER_TEMPLATE_CATEGORIES` — sets `templateUsersCategory`.
- `SELECT_TEMPLATE_CATEGORY` / `UNSELECT_TEMPLATE_CATEGORY` — set/clear `templateCategorySelected` (and clear `templateUsers` on unselect).
- `LOAD_USER_TEMPLATES_FILTER` — sets `templateUsers`.
- `REMOVE_USER_TEMPLATES_FILTER` — clears `templateUsersCategory` and `templateUsers`.
- `SHOW_DESIGN_MODAL` / `SELECT_DESIGN_TO_MODAL` — set `showModalDesign` / `designSelected`.
- `APPLY_TEMPLATE` / `SAVE_TEMPLATE` — close product container / mobile nav (no data change beyond that).
- `REMOVE_TEMPLATE` — filters `templates` to remove the matching id.
- `UPDATE_PRICE` — sets `price = initial_price + action.payload` (a large block of commented-out per-side embroidery surcharge logic is present but inactive).
- `LOADING_DATA` — buggy: reads `state.payload` (always `undefined`) instead of `action.payload`, so this effectively always sets `loading_data: undefined`.
- `CHECK_PRODUCT` — sets `check_product`.
- `UPDATE_USER` / `UPDATE_AI_MODEL` / `UPDATE_USER_ACCESS` — set `user` / `AIModel` / `userAccess`.
- `SHOW_MODAL` — sets `is_show_md`.
- `LOAD_IMAGE_PREVIEW` — sets `list_image_preview`; also clears `list_image_info3D` if the payload is empty.
- `ADD_IMAGE3D_PREVIEW` — deep-clones `list_image_info3D`, pushes the new entry, sorts by `index`, flattens each entry's `list_image` into `list_image_preview`, and stores both.
- `LOAD_PREVIEW_COLOR` / `LOAD_IMAGICK_MODEL` — set `list_preview_color` / `item_imagick_model`.
- `LOAD_DESIGN_FN` — sets `load_design_fn: true`.
- `SELECT_EMBROIDER_AREA` — finds a side by name within the selected color and sets it as `sideSelected`.
- `LOAD_KEY_FILTER` — sets `list_key_filter`.
- `UPDATE_KEY_FILTER` / `UPDATE_SEX_FILTER` / `UPDATE_SP_KEY_FILTER` / `UPDATE_MODEL_PHONE` / `UPDATE_PRINT_FILTER` / `UPDATE_MATERIALARR_FILTER` / `UPDATE_MODEL_SPCASE_FILTER` / `UPDATE_MODEL_SPCASE_FILTER_SELECTED` / `UPDATE_SCENE_FILTER` / `UPDATE_PRICE_FILTER` / `UPDATE_SIZE_FILTER` / `UPDATE_TYPE_OF_PRODUCT_FILTER` / `UPDATE_TYPE_OF_PRODUCT_FILTER_BAG` / `UPDATE_SIDE_FILTER` / `UPDATE_COLOR_FILTER` / `UPDATE_MATERIAL_FILTER` / `UPDATE_VALUE_FILTER` / `UPDATE_CATEGORY_FILTER` / `UPDATE_CATEGORY_SUB_FILTER` / `UPDATE_ARR_STATUS_FILTER` / `UPDATE_TYPE_FILTER` / `UPDATE_NUMBER_PRODUCT_FILTER` / `UPDATE_VALUE_PRODUCT_SORT` / `UPDATE_TOOGLE_FILTER_BOX` — each sets its corresponding single `obj*Filter`/`arrStatusFilter`/`typeFilter`/etc. field verbatim from the payload.
- `GET_ALL_PRODUCT_BY_MASTER_CATEGORY` — sets `allProductByMasterCategory` from `action.payload.products`.
- `UPDATE_CURRENT_ID` — sets `currentID`.
- `UPDATE_CAT_PRODUCT_ID_ITEM` — sets `categoryProductItem`.
- `SET_INFO_CHAT_SOCKET` / `SET_INFO_ROOM_DESIGN` — set `infoChatSocket` / `infoRoomDesign`.
- `UPDATE_ALL_KEY_FILTER_PRICE` / `UPDATE_ALL_KEY_FILTER_CATEGORY` / `UPDATE_ALL_KEY_FILTER_MAKER` / `UPDATE_ALL_KEY_FILTER_PRINT` / `UPDATE_ALL_KEY_FILTER_SEX` / `UPDATE_ALL_KEY_FILTER_MATERIAL` / `UPDATE_ALL_KEY_FILTER_SCENE` / `UPDATE_ALL_KEY_FILTER_SIZE` / `UPDATE_ALL_KEY_FILTER_COLOR` / `UPDATE_ALL_KEY_FILTER_CONDITION` — set the corresponding `key*Filters` master list.
- `UPDATE_TYPE_PRODUCTS` — sets `typeOfProduct`.
- `UPDATE_CURRENT_CATEGORY_ID` — sets `current_Category_id`.
- `LOAD_FAVORITE_PRODUCTS` — sets `favoriteProducts` from `action.payload.products`.
- `UPDATE_FAVORITE_PRODUCTS` — replaces `favoriteProducts` wholesale.
- `SET_3D_TRIPO` — sets `tripo3D_mode`.
- `SET_PREVIEW_IMAGE_3D` / `SET_URL_3D` / `SET_IMAGE_3D_CAPTURE` / `SET_ZIP_OBJ_FILE_3D` — set `previewImage3D` / `url3D` / `image3DCapture` / `zip3dfile`.
- `ADD_FILE_PAIR` — appends payload to `filePairs`.
- `UPDATE_FILE_PAIR` — updates `filePairs[index]`, preserving the existing `png` if the entry already existed and overwriting `zip` from `filePair.zip`.

---

## src/reducers/index.js

**Purpose:** Combines the two slice reducers into the root reducer used by the store.

### Exports
- `default` — `combineReducers({ product, drawTool })` (imported as `product` from `./product` and `drawTool` from `./draw-tool`). Root state shape is therefore `{ product: {...}, drawTool: {...} }` matching the two reducer sections above.

---

## src/middleware/draw-tool.js

**Purpose:** The largest and most central middleware — roughly 1855 lines — implementing nearly all canvas-editing business logic by intercepting `drawTool`-slice actions and driving the vendored `DrawTool` singleton (Fabric.js wrapper) directly: switching tools, adding/removing/transforming items, managing brushes and text, computing laser cut-path clip borders (extensive vector-geometry code), undo/redo, and delete confirmations (via `sweetalert`/`sweetalert2`). It reads `drawTool` and `product` slice state at the top of every dispatch, bails out early (`return next(action)`) if no side is currently selected (`isSelectedSide()`), and — for most action types other than a short allow-list (`UPDATE_LAYERS`, `UNSELECT_ITEM`, `EMPTY`, `UPDATE_PRICE`, `UPDATE_COLORS_FILL`, `UPDATE_COLORS_IMAGE_FILL`, `LOADING_CATEGORIES`) — first finalizes any in-progress brush stroke (`items.finalizeBrush()`, debounced via `enableFinalizeBrush`/`setTimeout`) and schedules a `DrawTool.trigger('layers:update')` roughly 1s later. It always calls `next(action)` at the end so the action still reaches the reducers.

### Module-level helpers
- `isSelectedSide()` — returns `DrawTool.sides && DrawTool.sides.selected`; used as the middleware's overall early-exit guard.
- `DeleteLineCut()` (exported) — for laser die-cut items: computes bounding-box overlap (`CheckPointInRegions`, `CheckPointOverwrite` inner helpers) between the selected item and every other item on side 1 to find candidate "die-cut" layers, then opens a SweetAlert2 (`sw2`/`withReactContent`) dialog rendering the `<DialogDieCut layersAll={...} />` React component so the user can choose which overlapping layer(s) to delete; also calls `DrawTool.sides.selected.checkBorderEmbroierExisting()`.

### Action Types Handled
- `RESET_DEFAULT_PARAS` — sets `DrawTool.is_embroidery = action.payload`.
- `UPDATE_LAYERS` — fires `DrawTool.trigger('layers:update')`.
- `UNSELECT_ITEM` — fires `DrawTool.trigger('item:unselect')`.
- `SET_ACTIVE_TOOL` — the largest single case. Calls `DrawTool.stopCurentAction()` (unless switching to `carpet`) and fires `update:activeTool`. Special per-tool branches:
  - `changeBorder` — disables drawing mode and sets `changeBorder = true` on the side.
  - `uploadFile` / `shapes` / `sticker` / `brush` while `is_embroidery` — no-ops (returns early), disallowing those tools in embroidery mode.
  - Non-`layers` tools while `DrawTool.modeToolDraw < 2` — strips any items with `pathIndex === 3` from both the items collection and the Fabric canvas `_objects`.
  - `panning` — enables panning mode, disables drawing mode.
  - `brush` — enables drawing mode and activates the current `activeBrush` with `brushOptions`.
  - `brushlaser` / `laserLineVectorBrush` / `laserCurveVectorBrush` — enable drawing mode with a fixed blue color/width-1 laser brush variant and call `DrawTool.setLayerSetup(DrawTool.getCurrentTypeProductLaser())`.
  - `changeToCropPathBrush` — enables a crop-path brush.
  - `grid` — adds an alignment grid (`addGrid(grid_rows, grid_cols)`) and sends it to the back of the stacking order, unless the product is a laser product.
  - `embroider` — zooms into the embroidery area (side 1, non-SP products), applies any special border for the side, moves all Fabric objects out and back in (a re-render trick), triggers a history update, then after a short delay zooms back in and checks whether embroidery/print items already coexist in the same area — if so shows a warning `swal` and reverts the tool to `pointer`.
  - `embroider_extend` — similar flow for the "extended embroidery" mode, additionally computing an `infoArea` decision tree keyed on `borderSpecial.type_acess` (`FREE_STYLE` / `ONLY_EMBROIDERY` / `ONLY_PRINT_OR_EMBROIDERY` / `ONLY_PRINT`) to decide whether the extended-embroidery layer setup is allowed, showing a warning dialog and reverting to `_embroider` if not.
  - `_embroider` (exit embroidery) — filters items/canvas objects down to only those with meaningful text (`textEmboirEdit`) or non-text items, restores the side's starting border, disables embroidery mode, re-adds items to trigger a redraw, and (if in `EMBROIDER`/`EMBROIDER_EXTEND` mode) re-checks border access/existing embroidery and optionally triggers an online/chat draw update.
- `SELECT_BRUSH` — activates the named brush (`action.payload`) with current `brushOptions`.
- `UPDATE_CURENT_LASER_BORDER` — discards the active Fabric selection; if in laser-checking mode, activates the `laser` tool and rebuilds the border shape as a rectangle path from the side's current cm border, re-adding all items to force a redraw and pushing a history update; otherwise activates `layers` tool and, if laser/mask items exist, dispatches `updateBorder()` and broadcasts an `online:update` chat command, else clears `enableHoleFuiger`/`enableFuiger`.
- `UPDATE_BORDER` — (skipped entirely if `modeToolDraw === ACRYLIC_LASER`) the largest geometry routine in the file: iterates every laser-mode item on the side, converts its `pathDLaser`/`pathDLaserEx` point arrays through rotation/flip/scale transforms into border-space coordinates, runs a centroid/winding-direction correction, feeds the points through `ImageCuttingLaser` (from `src/utils/ImageCuttingLaser`) to get a cleaned/offset vector outline, clamps points to the border bounds, and accumulates the results into `enableFuiger`/`enableHoleFuiger` (used later for laser-cut hole/relief modeling) and a combined SVG path string (`stringPathClip`). If any clip path was computed and the mode is `LASER_STICKER`, applies it via `setBorderLaser`/`setBorder`, re-adds items to force redraw, and pushes a history update.
- `CHANGE_BORDER` — shifts every item's `left` by `-action.payload`, re-renders, and pushes a history update.
- `UPDATE_CUTTING_IMAGE` — for the first item whose `pathIndex` matches a laser/mask mode, converts its laser path into a per-image crop-mask point list (accounting for rotation, flip, and each image's own scale), applies it via `changeToCropPathProcessing` to every image item on the side, then removes the laser cut item itself, re-renders, pushes a history update, and broadcasts an `online:update` chat command.
- `SET_BORDER_SAME_CM` — resets the border to a plain rectangle matching the side's current cm bounding box, re-renders, pushes a history update.
- `SELECT_BRUSH_SIZE` / `SELECT_BRUSH_COLOR` — re-activates the current brush with updated `width` / `color` merged into `brushOptions`.
- `ADD_TEXT` — guarded by `DrawTool.sides.selected.checkItemCanAddValue()` (shows a warning `swal` and aborts if the side is full/restricted); builds Fabric text options from `textOptions`, clears the font's char-width cache, optionally converts to vertical text, sanitizes via `DrawTool.checkTextValue`, adds the text item via `items.addText`, and (for tap/ribbon product modes) selects the newly-added item.
- `CHANGE_TEXT` — sanitizes the new text via `DrawTool.checkTextItemValue`, calls `setText`/sets `.vertical` on the selected item, and triggers an `update_item` draw update.
- `SELECT_TEXT_COLOR` — sets fill color on the selected item.
- `SELECT_TEXT_FONT` — clears the font's char-width cache and calls `setFontFamily` on the selected item (re-applying if the item has an `effect`), then re-renders.
- `SELECT_TEXT_SIZE` — calls `setFontSize` on the selected item.
- `SELECT_TEXT_ALIGN` — calls `setTextAlign` on the selected item.
- `SELECT_TEXT_BOLD` / `SELECT_TEXT_ITALIC` — calls `setFontWeight('bold'|'normal')` / `setFontStyle('italic'|'normal')`.
- `SELECT_TEXT_VERTICAL` — toggles `.vertical` on the selected item and re-sanitizes/re-sets its text content for vertical vs. horizontal layout via `checkTextValue`/`checkTextItemValue`.
- `UNDO` / `REDO` — calls `DrawTool.history.undo/redo(sideId)`; for tap/ribbon product modes, re-dispatches `fillColors` with the tool's remembered `newColorPicked` after a short delay instead of a generic draw update; otherwise triggers a `side` draw update; for laser UI modes also triggers an `option:update` event.
- `EMPTY` — shows a confirmation `swal`; on confirm, tallies and frees uploaded-image capacity (`updateCapacityUploading`, `DrawTool.upDateImageUpload`), disables panning/drawing, resyncs embroidery mode, dispatches `unselectItem`, empties the side's items, finalizes any brush, checks embroidery borders, broadcasts an `EMPTY_NOW` online command, and (for carpet or acrylic-laser products) resets pulling/pedestal state accordingly; finally calls `DrawTool.checkStandLaserDesign()`.
- `EMPTY_NOW` — the same clearing logic as `EMPTY` but without the confirmation dialog or the online broadcast.
- `EMPTY_LASER` — confirmation dialog; on confirm, clears laser data (`items.emptyLaser()`) across **all** sides, resets pedestal border state if in `ACRYLIC_LASER` mode, then checks stand-laser design regardless of confirmation.
- `ZOOM_IN` / `ZOOM_OUT` — stops the current action, zooms the side, recalculates coordinates for every non-border object, recalculates canvas offset, re-renders.
- `ZOOM_ZONE` — stops the current action and sets `DrawTool.sides.selected.zoom_zone`.
- `REMOVE` — if the active item is a laser die-cut image, calls `DeleteLineCut()`; otherwise, for acrylic-laser "mode_design" items, calls `DrawTool.sides.selected.emptyItemHoldLaser([...])` (removing the item and its linked parent/child design) and rebuilds the pedestal border, else simply splices the item from the collection and removes it from the Fabric canvas; either way schedules `DrawTool.checkActionDeleteItem` and fires `item:unselect`.
- `ALIGN_LAYER` — invokes `DrawTool.sides.selected.layers[action.payload](...layersSelected)`.
- `ALIGN_ITEM` — invokes `DrawTool.sides.selected.items.selected[action.payload]()`.
- `INSERT_IMAGE` — calls `items.addImage(action.payload)`.
- `INSERT_SHAPE` — guarded by `checkItemCanAddValue`; calls `items.addSVG(url, shapeColor)`.
- `INSERT_LASER` — disables drawing mode, calls `items.addLaserSvgAndPoint(action.payload)`, then after 1s calls `DrawTool.checkLaserColor()`.
- `INSERT_PEDESTAL_LASER` — disables drawing mode; only in `ACRYLIC_LASER` mode, calls `items.addImageLaser(action.payload)`.
- `INSERT_STICKER` — guarded by `checkItemCanAddValue`; calls `items.addSVG(url)` (no color override).
- `SELECT_SHAPE_COLOR` — sets fill on the selected item.
- `SORT_LAYERS` — temporarily disables Fabric eventing (`DrawTool._evented = false`), reorders layers via `layers.moveToIndex` for each dragged item (reversed index), then re-enables eventing, pushes a history update, and triggers a `layer` draw update with the affected uuids.
- `TOGGLE_COLOR_PICKER` — sets `DrawTool.sides.selected.colorPicker.active`.
- `REMOVE_COLOR` — wraps in a `setLoading(true)`/`setLoading(false)` pair (10ms delay); calls `items.selected.removeColor(colorPickerColor, 25)` (chroma-key tolerance 25), closes the color picker, triggers an `update_item` draw update.
- `TapOrRibbonProcessing` — wraps in `setLoading`; picks a fixed candidate color palette depending on `modeToolDraw` (`TAP_RIBBON` / `TAP_RIBBON_2EDIT` / `RIBON_EXTEND_COLOR`), and if `DrawTool.newColorPicked` isn't in that palette resets it to the palette's first color; calls `items.selected.tapOrRibbonImageProcessingColor(color, 25)`, closes the color picker, disables drawing mode.
- `RIM_IMAGE_TO_SVG` — calls `items.rimImageProcessing()`.
- `UPDATE_DEGREE` — sets the selected item's angle (guarded for acrylic-laser "mode_design" items whose `mode !== '1'`), re-renders, checks carpet-side/acrylic-move/acrylic-sticker-move constraints depending on `modeToolDraw`, triggers an `item` draw update, and (for laser products, rate-limited via `DrawTool.checkTimeOfAction`) schedules `checkLaserColor()`.
- `UPDATE_SCALE` — computes the current X/Y scale ratio, applies the new scale to X while preserving the ratio on Y, re-renders, checks carpet-side constraints, triggers an `item` draw update, and (rate-limited, for laser products) schedules `checkLaserColor()`.
- `UPDATE_TEXT_SPACING` — for warp-style text effects (`curved`/`smallToLarge`/`bulge`/`largeToSmallBottom`) calls `setSpacing(payload/10)`, otherwise `setCharSpacing(payload)`; re-renders, checks carpet-side constraints, triggers `update_item` draw update.
- `FILL_COLORS` — calls `items.selected.setFillColorsStamp(old_color, new_color)`; for tap/ribbon modes additionally recolors the whole side via `items.imagechangeColorAll` and broadcasts two chained `online:update` chat commands (`UPDATE_SIDE` then, 600ms later, `UPDATE_COLOR_RIBON`); for all other modes triggers an `update_item` draw update instead.
- `UPDATE_GRID_SIZE` — removes and re-adds the alignment grid at the new `grid_rows`/`grid_cols`, re-renders. *(Note: this case has no trailing `break;`, so execution falls through into the `default:` no-op — harmless here since `default` does nothing, but worth flagging as a latent bug if more cases are ever appended after it.)*
- `default` — no-op.

---

## src/middleware/product.js

**Purpose:** The second major business-logic middleware (~2438 lines), focused on product/color/side switching, loading saved designs into the canvas, and template application. It reads `product` and `drawTool` slice state at the top of every dispatch and, for the handled action types, orchestrates long chains of `DrawTool.importJSON(...)`/`fromJSON(...)` calls, `setTimeout`-staged sequencing (to let Fabric.js finish async image loads between steps), capacity/size bookkeeping for uploaded images, and embroidery/laser/carpet/acrylic mode-specific special-casing. Always calls `next(action)`.

### Action Types Handled
- `INCREASE_FAVORITE_PRODUCTS` — pushes `action.payload` onto the current `favoriteProducts` array (mutates it in place) and re-dispatches `updateFavoriteProducts` with the mutated array.
- `DECREASE_FAVORITE_PRODUCTS` — finds and splices out the favorite whose `Product.id` matches the payload, then re-dispatches `updateFavoriteProducts`.
- `UPDATE_FONTS_AL` — for every font in the payload, registers it for use: for IE (`Trident`/`rv:11`/jQuery `$.browser.msie`) injects a `<style>` `@font-face` block referencing `eot`/`woff2`/`woff`/`ttf`/`svg` URLs directly into `document.head`; otherwise delegates to `DrawTool.fontLoader(name, urls)`.
- `UPDATE_FONTS_BY_NAME` — the same IE-vs-`fontLoader` branching as `UPDATE_FONTS_AL` but for a single font (`action.payload.name`/`.urls`); used to lazily register one font referenced by a loaded design that isn't already registered.
- `SELECT_COLOR` — a very large handler that switches the active product color: sets tool to `pointer`; for carpet products, removes pulling rules first. Tallies the number of in-progress data loads across all sides (`DrawTool.countNumberDataUseInSide`). Looks up any matching 3D preview model in `shapes3D` (by `preview` matching the new color id, falling back to matching the product id) and dispatches `update3DViewData` + a `view3d:update` trigger if found. Branches on embroidery mode:
  - **Non-embroidery:** after a 100ms delay, snapshots existing `overlayImage`s per side, resolves the new color's per-side JSON content (double-`JSON.parse` after `escapeJSON`), sets `DrawTool.is_choosing = true`, calls `DrawTool.importJSON(...)`. Inside the resolved promise: for carpet products, re-saves each side's border/`border_special` and reloads carpet border layout; always calls `checkZoomBorderInfo`/`checkLocationBorderAcrylic`; after another 1s, prunes stray extra DOM children under `.draw-tool` beyond the side count. Re-selects the previously active side, restores each side's `overlayImage`/overlay border, resets `is_embroidery`, and recomputes each item's `useItemData` flag based on `checkItemEmptyInSide`. After further delays, if the product is a laser product (or in `MASK_LASER` mode) dispatches `updateBorder()`; if `ACRYLIC_LASER`, calls `loadNewBorderPedestal()`. Finally (2s later) triggers a `color` draw update.
  - **Embroidery mode:** saves each side's `currentBoderAcess`, restores the side's starting border, disables embroidery, re-adds items to force a redraw, pushes a history update, then (100ms later) repeats the same `importJSON`/overlay-restore/`useItemData` sequence as above but restores the saved `currentBoderAcess` afterward instead of recomputing it.
  - In both branches, a `setInterval` polls `countNumberDataUseInSide` every 500ms until all expected items have finished loading (or 600 ticks/~5 min elapse as a timeout), then calls `DrawTool.rebackLockProduct()`, sets layer setup to `-1`, (for carpet) calls `changeCarpetPullingBorder()`, re-renders, and dispatches `setLoading(false)`.
- `RELOAD_COLOR` — structurally a near-duplicate of `SELECT_COLOR`'s two branches (non-embroidery / embroidery), used when re-applying the *currently selected* color's data (e.g. after loading a saved design) rather than switching to a *new* color id. Adds extra `DrawTool.responsiveTime.push(Date.now())` timing instrumentation throughout, and — uniquely — after the import, checks whether the side contains `EMBROIDER_EXTEND` or (`side.id == '1'`) `pathIndex === 1` embroidery items and, if so, runs a `setInterval`-staged sequence that cycles `setActiveTool('embroider_extend'|'embroider')` through each `border_special` entry (renaming the side each time via `saveNameSide`) to force-render every embroidery sub-area, before settling back on `_embroider` and clearing `DrawTool.isProcessChecking`/`lock_swal_data`.
- `SELECT_SIDE` — discards the active Fabric selection, disables embroidery, recomputes `useItemData` for all items on the *current* side, dispatches `unselectItem`, resolves the target `ProductColorSide` by id or name, calls `DrawTool.sides.select(...)` to switch the visible side, restores embroidery border if in `EMBROIDER`/`EMBROIDER_EXTEND` mode, resyncs embroidery/layer setup, and if the new side has a saved `overlayImage`, re-applies it and strips `clipTo` from all items. If a `serverSocketData`/`_collection_online` chat session is active, shows a SweetAlert2 loading spinner. After a short delay, branches by mode: carpet products trigger `carpet:update`/`update:pointer`; laser products filter out `MASK_LASER` items, clone laser items from *other* sides mirrored across the canvas center (`addLaserFromOldString`) so both sides show symmetric cut data, then dispatch `updateBorder()`; `EMBROIDER`/`EMBROIDER_EXTEND` modes restore the starting border, force a redraw, and (after ~4.1s) recompute `currentBoderAcess` polygon corners for each `border_special` region so embroidery hit-testing works on the new side; all branches close the SweetAlert2 spinner if one was shown.
- `UPDATE_SIDE_LASER` — a variant of `SELECT_SIDE` specialized for laser products: recomputes `useItemData`, strips `MASK_LASER` items if none remain useful, switches side, and after 700ms re-filters `MASK_LASER` items and clones laser items from other sides (same mirroring technique as `SELECT_SIDE`) before calling `items.triggerCreated()`.
- `LOAD_PRODUCT` — fires when a full product record is loaded. Resets `DrawTool._embroideryPaths`; if `Product` and `colors` are present: triggers a 3D view removal/reload sequence (same 3D-model lookup as `SELECT_COLOR`), sets `DrawTool.is_nail` based on `category_id === 62`, computes `embroider_able` (from `embroider_able` flag or `special_draw` 1/9) and sets `DrawTool.embroider_able`; determines `DrawTool.modeToolDraw` from `Product.special_draw` (falling back to `EMBROIDER` or `-1`) — for `RIBON_EXTEND_COLOR` mode, parses `color_draw` (`"--"`-delimited) into `DrawTool.colorSetup` and picks the first as `newColorPicked`. Saves each side's `currentBoderAcess`, builds the per-side JSON content array, dispatches `updateProcessBar(0.7)`, computes `overlayImages` per side, and calls `DrawTool.importJSON(...)`. On resolution: selects the first side via `DrawTool.loadFistSide`, dispatches `selectSide`/`setLoadingData(false)`, restores special borders (embroidery vs. carpet vs. plain), calls `checkZoomBorderInfo`/`checkLocationBorderAcrylic`, applies overlay images if present, ramps `updateProcessBar` toward 100, prunes stray DOM nodes, resets embroidery, re-enables selectability, clears loading flags, restores `currentBoderAcess` (embroidery) or rebuilds carpet canvas image (carpet), calls `checkStandLaserDesign()`, and — if the URL query included `url_image` — kicks off `DrawTool.checkImageToUpload(query)` to auto-import an external image. Fires a final `product:load` trigger.
- `LOAD_PRODUCT_WITH_DESIGN` — loads a product together with previously-saved per-side design JSON (admin/order-edit or "continue editing" flow). Similar 3D-model lookup, `is_nail` detection, `embroider_able`/`modeToolDraw` resolution as `LOAD_PRODUCT`. Resolves the target color by `selected_color_id`, imports the color's base JSON, then for each side with saved design data in `action.payload.sides[side.id]`: if the value is an image URL string, adds it directly via `items.addImage`; otherwise parses the saved Fabric JSON, pre-registers any custom fonts referenced (`saveInfoText.fontFamily` → `updateFontsByName`), and — after `document.fonts.ready` resolves — calls `side.fromJSON(...)` to rehydrate the canvas, then dispatches `updateLayers`, sets the active tool (`carpet` or `pointer`, unless `flag_option === 'design'`), and clears loading flags. Also fetches each image item's `Content-Length` via a `HEAD` XHR to update `updateCapacityUploading` bookkeeping. Finishes with delayed calls to `updateBorder`/`addImageCanvasToDesign`/`loadNewBorderPedestal`/`checkStandLaserDesign` depending on product mode.
- `APPLY_TEMPLATE_USER` — applies a *user-saved* template's canvas JSON onto the current side, with heavy compatibility checking: blocks the operation (with a Japanese "cannot mix embroidery and normal print on the same side" warning) if the current side already has embroidery items, or if the incoming template mixes embroidery and non-embroidery objects incompatibly (`interruptRenderDesign` logic), or if per-item `pathIndex` conflicts are detected. Sums image sizes via `HEAD` XHR requests and rejects (with a "30MB total" warning) if the combined upload size would exceed `31457280` bytes (30MB). Registers any missing fonts before proceeding. Rescales incoming object coordinates by `valueScaleAction` (the smaller of the horizontal/vertical border-size ratios between the template's original border and the current side's border) across `objects`/`objects_print`/`objects_embroidery`/`objects_special_draw`. Merges the (embroidery-filtered, if applicable) objects into the current side's existing JSON (`jsonCompress`) and re-adds them via `addItemLayer`. Clears/updates image capacity bookkeeping and finishes by reactivating pointer tool and re-checking embroidery state.
- `SAVE_TEMPLATE` — for carpet products, finalizes pulling state first. Aborts (with a warning) if the side has no layers. Finalizes any brush stroke. Serializes the side to JSON (`toJSON()`), strips `saveInfoText` from all three object arrays (`objects`/`objects_print`/`objects_special_draw`), aborts (with a warning) if the serialized data still references a `blob:http` URL (meaning an image upload hasn't finished yet). Captures a PNG preview (`getImagePreview`) and renders an SVG (`toSVG`); if the SVG references any `http`-based `xlink:href` images, first inlines them as base64 data URLs (`toDataURL` helper) before proceeding. Uploads both the PNG and SVG via `uploadByString`, then calls the `saveTemplate` API with the JSON payload and `old_work_area` sizing metadata; on success, refreshes the user-templates list (`removeUserTemplatesFilter` + `getTemplatesUserForDesign` → `loadUserTemplateCategories`/`selectTemplateCategory`) and shows a SweetAlert2 success toast; on failure shows an error `swal`.
- `APPLY_TEMPLATE` — applies a *site* template (as opposed to `APPLY_TEMPLATE_USER`'s user templates). For carpet products, resets pulling state first. Computes `infoSideCurent` (counts of embroidery vs. print items already on the side, shifting misplaced embroidery items sideways by 500px if found outside their designated area) and, if the template has no recorded `old_work_area`/size factor (a plain image/SVG template with no structured JSON), either adds the SVG directly (`items.addSVG`, blocked if the side has embroidery) or downloads and adds it as a sized image (`addImageWithSize`, capped by remaining capacity) via a `HEAD`-XHR size check. If the template *does* have structured `content_json`, parses it, filters its four object arrays down to items compatible with the current `modeToolDraw`, and runs an elaborate truth table (`xCano` boolean logic, documented inline as a "cacno logic" truth table comment) to decide whether mixing the template's print/embroidery content with the side's existing print/embroidery content is allowed, aborting with a Japanese warning if not. Pre-registers referenced fonts, sums and validates total image size (30MB cap) via `HEAD` XHR, merges the template's objects into the side's current JSON (respecting `ACRYLIC_LASER`'s exclusion of other acrylic-laser items), clears the side and re-adds everything via `fromJSONLayer`/`addItemLayer` (mode-specific: `ACRYLIC_LASER` polls `countNumberDataUseInSide` until fully loaded before rebuilding the pedestal border; `LASER_NO_HOLE` removes/re-applies "light style" items around the add). Finishes by clearing loading state, reactivating the pointer tool, updating image-capacity bookkeeping per item, and — depending on `modeToolDraw` — replaying the same `embroider`/`embroider_extend` per-area activation sequence seen in `RELOAD_COLOR`, rebuilding the acrylic-laser pedestal, or triggering a generic `side` draw update.
- `default` — no-op.

---

## src/middleware/index.js

**Purpose:** Barrel file that re-exports the three middleware modules for use by `src/store/index.js`.

### Exports
- `logger` — from `./logger`.
- `drawTool` — from `./draw-tool` (default export of `middleware/draw-tool.js`).
- `product` — from `./product` (default export of `middleware/product.js`).

---

## src/middleware/logger.js

**Purpose:** A minimal Redux logging middleware for development.

### Behavior
- `default = store => next => action => { ... }` — if `NODE_ENV === 'development'` (a global replaced at build time, not `process.env.NODE_ENV`), logs the raw `action` object to the console via `console.log`. Always calls `next(action)` to pass the action along the chain. No state is read or modified.

---

## src/constants/index.js

**Purpose:** Environment-driven endpoint/API-key constants, sourced from build-time environment variables (webpack `DefinePlugin`-style substitution). Several alternate hardcoded values are left commented out for reference (production `up-t.jp`, a `decodeapps.io` staging host, and a `tweb-api.local` dev host).

### Exports
- `HOST` (string) — `process.env.API_ENDPOINT`; base URL for the main REST API (used throughout `src/api/*.js`).
- `WEBHOST` (string) — `process.env.WEB_ENDPOINT`; base URL of the main web app (used for e.g. cart-check redirects).
- `API_NODE` (string) — `process.env.API_NODE`; base URL of a secondary Node-based API (referenced, e.g., in a commented-out GIF-conversion endpoint).
- `KEY_YAHOO` (string) — `process.env.KEY_YAHOO`; API key for a Yahoo-related integration (e.g. Yahoo Maps/search, not used directly in the files read here).
- `URL_REMOVE_BG` (string) — `process.env.URL_REMOVE_BG`; background-removal service URL.
- `URL_SERVER_CHAT` (string) — `process.env.URL_SERVER_CHAT`; base URL of the collaborative design-room chat server (used throughout `src/api/extras.js`'s chat functions).
- `URL_ORDERMADE` (string) — `process.env.URL_ORDERMADE`; base URL for the "order-made"/custom-order flow.

---

## src/constants/query.js

**Purpose:** A mutable singleton object representing the current session/query-string context, used across the app (and notably in nearly every `src/api/*.js` call) to attach `session=` and other query parameters to API requests.

### Exports
- `default` (object) — `query`, with:
  - `session` (string) — a hardcoded default session UUID (`'57fd8be9-24e4-4b84-b15e-5e1bac1f0fcf'`), presumably overwritten at runtime from the actual URL query string elsewhere in the app (e.g. on bootstrap).
  - `setData(obj)` (method) — `Object.assign(this, obj)`; merges arbitrary parsed query-string parameters (e.g. `admin`, `type_campaign`, `lock_item`, `room_chat`, `url_image`, `login_type`) onto the singleton so any module importing `query` sees the current values.

---

## src/constants/setting.js

**Purpose:** Miscellaneous global settings: a canvas pixel-area cap and category/maker enums used to branch UI and business logic by product category.

### Exports
- `MAXCANVAS` (number) — `15000000`; maximum allowed canvas pixel area (width×height), used as a safety/perf cap.
- `CATEGORY_CLOTHER` (string) — `'CATEGORY_CLOTHER'`; category-style key for T-shirts/apparel (Tシャツ・衣類).
- `CATEGORY_FLAG` (string) — `'CATEGORY_FLAG'`; category-style key for flags/banners (のぼり旗).
- `CATEGORY_OTHER` (string) — `'CATEGORY_OTHER'`; category-style key for miscellaneous goods (雑貨類).
- `CATEGORY_MOBILE` (string) — `'CATEGORY_MOBILE'`; category-style key for smartphone cases (スマホケース).
- `CATEGORY_STONE` (string) — `'CATEGORY_STONE'`; category-style key for power-stone accessories (パワーストーンアクセサリー).
- `CATEGORY_LASER` (string) — `'CATEGORY_LASER'`; category-style key for laser-cut products (comment mislabels it as スマホケース, likely copy-paste).
- `MAKERS` (array of strings) — `['PrintStar', 'UnitedAthle', 'glimmer', 'DALUC', 'TRUSS', 'LIFEMAX', 'WUNDOU']`; the fixed list of apparel manufacturer/brand names offered as a maker filter.

## src/constants/translations.js

**Purpose:** A tiny hardcoded i18n dictionary (English/Japanese) for a couple of UI labels; appears to be a minimal/legacy translation table (most of the app's Japanese strings are inlined directly rather than routed through this file).

### Exports
- `default` (object) —
  - `load` — `{ en: 'Load', jp: '開く' }`.
  - `save` — `{ en: 'Save', jp: '保存' }`.

## src/constants/draw3Ddefine.js

**Purpose:** Enum of 3D product-preview type identifiers, used to select which Three.js model/behavior to use for the live 3D preview.

### Exports
- `DRAW_3D_DEFFUNE` (object enum) —
  - `T_SHIRT` = `0` — t-shirt/apparel 3D preview.
  - `CUP` = `1` — mug/cup 3D preview.
  - `PHONE` = `2` — smartphone case 3D preview.
  - `HANDBAG` = `3` — handbag 3D preview.
  - `TYPE_CARD` = `4` — card/flat-item 3D preview.
