## src/store/index.js

**Purpose:** Configures and returns the Redux store, wiring up middleware, dev tools, and hot-module-replacement for reducers.

### Behavior
- `configure(initialState)` (default export) —
  - Chooses `createStore` either wrapped by `window.devToolsExtension()` (if the Redux DevTools browser extension is present) or the plain `createStore` from `redux`.
  - Builds `createStoreWithMiddleware` via `applyMiddleware(logger, drawTool, product)(create)` — **middleware order is `logger` → `drawTool` → `product`**, meaning every action is logged first (dev only), then handled by the draw-tool side-effect middleware, then the product side-effect middleware, before finally reaching the reducers via `next(action)`.
  - Creates the store with `rootReducer` (from `../reducers`) and the given `initialState`.
  - If Webpack HMR is active (`module.hot`), subscribes to hot updates of `../reducers` and calls `store.replaceReducer(nextReducer)` when they change.
  - Returns the configured `store`.

---

## src/api/extras.js

**Purpose:** REST/XHR client functions for miscellaneous "extra" features: image/PDF/GLB uploads (with and without progress reporting), AI text-to-image and translation/registration endpoints, background-removal and colorization, shape/sticker/stamp catalog fetches, color list, the collaborative chat/design-room API, cart-supplement conflict checks, and async 3D-model generation ("zen-3d"). Every function returns a `Promise` wrapping a `fetch` (or `XMLHttpRequest` where upload-progress is needed) call, generally resolving with parsed JSON (or a specific field of it) and rejecting on network/parse error. Most requests append `session=${query.session}` for session identification; POST bodies are typically `JSON.stringify`'d plain objects (not `FormData`) except where noted.

### Exports
- `upload(image)` — `POST ${HOST}/designs/design/image/upload`, body: `FormData{image, session}` — uploads a raw image file; resolves `data.image_url`; rejects `data.message` on non-OK response.
- `uploadPdf(mime_type, content)` — `POST ${HOST}/image`, body: `FormData{image: content}` — uploads a PDF/image blob; resolves the full parsed JSON response.
- `textJPToEN(text, key_ramdom)` — `POST ${HOST}/designs/design/file/text-jp-to-en`, JSON body `{session, content, access_session, mac_browser}` — translates Japanese text to English (AI-assisted design flow); on success stores/refreshes a `mac_browser` cookie via `setCookie`; resolves full response JSON, rejects with error JSON on non-OK.
- `registerProcessAi(text, key_ramdom)` — `POST ${HOST}/designs/design/file/register-text-ai` — registers text for an AI processing job; same cookie/response handling as `textJPToEN`.
- `textToImage(text, url, key_ramdom, keyModel)` — `POST ${HOST}/designs/design/file/text-to-image`, JSON body `{session, content, url, mac_browser, access_session, model}` — requests AI text-to-image generation; resolves full response JSON.
- `processingToImage(url)` — `POST ${HOST}/designs/design/file/processing-to-image`, JSON body `{session, url}` — polls/triggers processing of a previously-submitted image job; resolves full response JSON.
- `uploadByString(mime_type, content, extension)` — `POST ${HOST}/designs/design/file/upload`, JSON body `{session, File:{mime_type, content, extension}}` — uploads a base64-encoded file (image/svg/etc.); resolves `data.file_url`.
- `exportZipFile(content)` — `POST ${HOST}/products/product/export-zip-3d`, body: raw `content` — exports a 3D product bundle as a zip; resolves `data.file_url`.
- `uploadByLinkBG(linkImage, unLimit, extension, mime_type)` — `POST ${HOST}/designs/design/file/remove-bg-limit` (or `.../upload-remove-bg` if `unLimit` is true), JSON body `{session, link, api_jp:true, File:{mime_type, extension}, mac_browser}` — removes the background from an image given its URL, with a rate-limited vs. unlimited endpoint variant; updates the `mac_browser` cookie; resolves `data.file_url`, rejects the whole `data` object if `data.error` is set.
- `changeColorImg(linkImage)` — `POST ${HOST}/designs/design/file/photo-colorization`, JSON body `{session, link}` — AI photo colorization; resolves `data.file_url` only on HTTP 200, otherwise rejects the response.
- `getShapesLaser()` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/shapesLaser/categories?session=...&device_type=webbrowser` — fetches laser-cut shape categories; resolves `data.categories`.
- `getModelCuttingLaser()` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/pedestal-laser/categories?session=...&device_type=webbrowser` — fetches pedestal-laser model categories; resolves `data.categories`.
- `getShapes3D()` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/shapes3D/categories?session=...&device_type=webbrowser` — fetches 3D shape categories; resolves `data.categories`.
- `getShapes(id)` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/shapes?session=...&category_id=${id}` — fetches shapes within a category; resolves `data.shapes`.
- `getStickersCategories()` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer[-campaign]/stickers/categories?session=...&device_type=webbrowser[&demo_type=shujo]` — fetches sticker categories; switches to a `-campaign` endpoint variant and adds `demo_type` when `query.lock_item` is set; resolves `data.categories`.
- `getStickers(id)` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer[-campaign]/stickers?session=...&category_id=${id}[&demo_type=shujo]` — fetches stickers in a category, same campaign-mode branching; resolves `data.stickers`.
- `getStampsImageCategories()` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/stickers/categories?session=...&isForStampPaid=1&device_type=webbrowser` — fetches paid-"stamp" categories; resolves `data.categories`.
- `getStampsLoveCategories()` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/stickers/sumoppi-categories?session=...` — fetches "love"/sumoppi stamp categories; resolves `data.categories`.
- `getStampsImage(id)` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/stamp-paid?session=...&device_type=webbrowser&category_id=${id}` — fetches paid stamps in a category; resolves `data.stickers`.
- `getStampsLoveImage(id)` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/sumoppi-stickers?session=...&device_type=webbrowser&category_id=${id}` — fetches "love" stamps in a category; resolves `data.stickers`.
- `getStampsImageFavorite()` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/stamp-enjoy?session=...&device_type=webbrowser` — fetches the user's favorited (paid) stamps; resolves `data.stamp_enjoy`.
- `getStampsLovesFavorite()` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/stamp-enjoy?session=...&device_type=webbrowser&is_sumoppi=1` — fetches favorited "love" stamps; resolves `data.stamp_enjoy`.
- `getColors()` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/colors?session=...` — fetches the global color list; resolves full response JSON.
- `uploadByStringWithProgress(content, onProgress)` — `POST ${HOST}/designs/design/file/upload` via `XMLHttpRequest` — same as `uploadByString` but derives `mime_type`/`extension` from the `content` data-URI via regex, and reports `upload.progress` events to the optional `onProgress` callback; resolves `response.file_url`.
- `uploadPdfWithProgress(mime_type, content, onProgress)` — `POST ${HOST}/image` via `XMLHttpRequest`, body `FormData{image: content}` — progress-reporting PDF/image upload; resolves the full parsed response.
- `check(mime_type, content, extension)` — `POST ${HOST}/designs/design/file/upload`, JSON body `{session, File:{mime_type, content, extension}}` — near-duplicate of `uploadByString`; resolves `data.file_url`.
- `checkHeaderInfo(data)` — not a network call; returns a `fetch` options object (`method:'POST', mode:'cors', cache:'no-cache', credentials:'same-origin', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, referrerPolicy:'no-referrer', body: JSON.stringify(data)}`) shared by all chat-server calls below.
- `checkUserInfo(info, password)` — `POST ${URL_SERVER_CHAT}/api/get_info`, JSON body `{group_chat: info, session, password}` — validates access to a collaborative design room; on HTTP ≥400 shows a Japanese alert (room not started / design finished / cannot enter) and navigates back after 3s; resolves the parsed JSON otherwise.
- `addItemDesign(infoGroup, side, item_id, contentJson)` — `POST ${URL_SERVER_CHAT}/api/add_item` — adds a single canvas item to a shared design room; resolves parsed JSON.
- `addMutiItemDesign(infoGroup, side, contentJson)` — `POST ${URL_SERVER_CHAT}/api/add_mutile_item` — adds multiple items at once; resolves parsed JSON.
- `deleteItemDesign(infoGroup, item_id)` — `POST ${URL_SERVER_CHAT}/api/delete_item` — deletes an item from the shared room; resolves parsed JSON.
- `deleteSideDesign(infoGroup, side)` — `POST ${URL_SERVER_CHAT}/api/delete_side` — clears an entire side in the shared room; resolves parsed JSON.
- `loadItemDesign(item_id)` — `POST ${URL_SERVER_CHAT}/api/load_item` — loads a single item's data; resolves parsed JSON.
- `loadAllItemDesign(infoGroup)` — `POST ${URL_SERVER_CHAT}/api/load_all` — loads all items in the room; resolves parsed JSON.
- `loadAllItemActionDesign(infoGroup, items)` — `POST ${URL_SERVER_CHAT}/api/load_all_action` — loads/replays a specific set of item actions; resolves parsed JSON.
- `loadSideItemDesign(infoGroup, side)` — `POST ${URL_SERVER_CHAT}/api/load_side` — loads all items for one side; resolves parsed JSON.
- `sendMessageChat(infoGroup, author, message)` — `POST ${URL_SERVER_CHAT}/chat/send-message` — sends a chat message in the design room; resolves parsed JSON.
- `getAllMessageChat(infoGroup)` — `POST ${URL_SERVER_CHAT}/chat/all-message` — fetches full chat history; resolves parsed JSON.
- `getBeforeMessageChat(infoGroup, locationTime)` — `POST ${URL_SERVER_CHAT}/chat/before-message` — fetches chat messages before a timestamp (pagination); resolves parsed JSON.
- `deleteMessageChat(message)` — `POST ${URL_SERVER_CHAT}/chat/delete-message` — deletes a chat message; resolves parsed JSON.
- `checkPublicRoomDesign(infoGroup)` — `POST ${HOST}/design/check-public-room`, JSON body `{group_chat, session}` — checks whether a design room is public; rejects `[]` on non-OK, else resolves parsed JSON.
- `uploadGLBWithProgress(content, onProgress)` — `POST ${HOST}/designs/design/file/upload` via `XMLHttpRequest`, JSON body `{session, File:{mime_type:'model/gltf-binary', content, extension:'glb'}}` — uploads a binary glTF (.glb) 3D model with progress reporting; resolves `response.file_url`.
- `checkProductSupplied()` — `GET ${WEBHOST}/check-cart-supp` (`credentials:'include'`) — checks whether the cart already contains "supplement" items; resolves `{check, data}`; on network error alerts the user and resolves `{check:false, data:false}` (never rejects).
- `checkProductSuppliedAlert()` — not a network call; shows a SweetAlert2 confirmation ("supplements will be removed from cart if you continue / go to cart") and resolves `true` if the user chose to go back, `false` if they chose to proceed to cart.

## src/api/options.js

**Purpose:** REST client functions for editor "option" catalogs — brushes and the many font sub-category endpoints (each font category is a distinct `category_id` query param against one shared `/drawer/fonts` endpoint), plus font categories list. All resolve via `fetch(...).json()` unwrapping a named field, and reject on fetch/parse error.

### Exports
- `getBrushes()` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/brushes?session=...` — fetches the brush catalog; resolves `data.brushes`.
- `getFonts()` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/fonts?session=...&is_web=1` — fetches the default/general font list; resolves `data.fonts`.
- `getFontsJP()` — `GET .../drawer/fonts?...&category_id=1&is_web=1` — Japanese fonts; resolves `data.fonts`.
- `getFontsEN()` — `GET .../drawer/fonts?...&category_id=2&is_web=1` — English fonts; resolves `data.fonts`.
- `getCategoriesFonts()` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/fonts/categories?session=...&device_type=webbrowser` — fetches font category list; resolves `data.categories`.
- `getFontsWD()` — `GET .../drawer/fonts?...&category_id=13&is_web=1` — "WD"/Kana fonts; resolves `data.fonts`.
- `getFontsDGT()` — `GET .../drawer/fonts?...&category_id=14&is_web=1` — "Design"/DGT fonts; resolves `data.fonts`.
- `getFontsBC()` — `GET .../drawer/fonts?...&category_id=15&is_web=1` — brush-style ("BC") fonts; resolves `data.fonts`.
- `getFontsEuL()` — `GET .../drawer/fonts?...&category_id=16&is_web=1` — European ("EUL") fonts; resolves `data.fonts`.
- `getGothic()` — `GET .../drawer/fonts?...&category_id=17&is_web=1` — Gothic-style fonts; resolves `data.fonts`.
- `getTomorrowMorning()` — `GET .../drawer/fonts?...&category_id=18&is_web=1` — Mincho/"Tomorrow Morning" fonts; resolves `data.fonts`.

## src/api/products.js

**Purpose:** The largest API module — product catalog browsing, product/color loading, template CRUD (site + user), user session/auth check, favorites, the entire faceted-filter option-list API, per-product-type filter endpoints, and the newer async "zen-3d" 3D-model-generation job API (submit + poll). Nearly every function follows the same `fetch(...).then(checkInfoStatusSession).then(json).resolve(...)` pattern. Imports `convertFullsizeToHaftsize` (full-width→half-width string conversion) for tag normalization in `getTemplatesUserForDesign`.

### Exports
- `checkInfoStatusSession(response)` — not itself an API call; a shared response-guard invoked by most functions here. If `response.status` is `400` or `405` (and no error has already been shown this session, tracked via module-level `erroSave`), parses the JSON body and shows a Japanese `window.alert` — "Product not found" → out-of-stock message, otherwise a generic "session expired, please log in again" message — then calls `window.history.back()` after 3 seconds.
- `getCategoryStyles()` — `GET ${HOST}/products/category-styles?session=...` — fetches top-level category style groupings; resolves `data.category_styles`.
- `getCategories()` — `GET ${HOST}/products/categories?session=...&order=true&device_type=webbrowser` — fetches the category list, then **client-side injects three synthetic categories** not returned by the API: a "パワーストーン" (power stone) category (id `-101`, appended) and two id `-103`/`-102` categories for "中綴じ冊子" (saddle-stitched booklet) and "チラシ" (flyers) (both unshifted to the front); resolves the augmented array.
- `getProductsByCategory(categoryId)` — `GET ${HOST}/v2/products/models?session=...&category_id=${categoryId}&admin=${is_admin}&device_type=webbrowser[&type_campaign=yuyami]&from_tool=1` — fetches products in a category, honoring `query.admin` and a `yuyami` campaign flag from `query.type_campaign`; resolves full response JSON.
- `filterProductByMaker(maker, category_id)` — `GET ${HOST}/v2/products/makers?session=...&maker=${maker}&category_id=${category_id}&device_type=webbrowser&from_tool=1` — filters products by maker within a category; resolves full response JSON.
- `getProduct(productId)` — `GET ${HOST}/v2/products/models/model?session=...&product_id=${productId}&admin=${is_admin}&device_type=webbrowser` — fetches a single product's full detail; resolves full response JSON.
- `getProductWithColor(productId, colorID)` — same endpoint as `getProduct`, optionally appending `&color_id=${colorID}` — fetches a product pre-scoped to one color; resolves full response JSON.
- `getDefaultProduct(color_id, model_id)` — `GET ${HOST}/v2/products/models/model/default?session=...[&color_id=...|&model_id=...]&device_type=webbrowser` — fetches the "default" product variant, keyed by color id, model id, or neither; resolves full response JSON.
- `saveTemplate(image, svg, content_json, old_work_area)` — `POST ${HOST}/designs/templates/template`, JSON body `{session, DesignTemplate:{image_url, content_url, content_json, old_work_area}}` — creates a site design template; resolves the raw `response` (not parsed JSON).
- `getTemplates()` — `GET ${HOST}/designs/templates?session=...` — fetches site templates; resolves `data.templates`.
- `getTemplatesUserForDesign()` — `POST ${HOST}/designs/templates/user?session=...`, JSON body `{session, user:[{name:'test'}]}` — fetches the current user's saved templates and reshapes them into a tag-grouped tree for the "load design" UI: separates records into "own" (`user === "5fabbc3f1ea32"`, a hardcoded system/demo user id) vs. "other" designers, builds a de-duplicated, half-width-normalized tag list (`tag1`–`tag5`) per group via `convertFullsizeToHaftsize`, buckets each item under its matching tag node(s) (or the `"undefined"`/first bucket if untagged), and picks the best available `item_previewN` as `item_preview_show`; resolves an array containing only the first (own-designs) group node — `resolve([arrayElementData[0]])` (the "other users" branch is fully computed but discarded).
- `removeTemplate(id)` — `DELETE ${HOST}/designs/templates/template`, JSON body `{session, DesignTemplate:{id}}` — deletes a site template; resolves the raw `response`.
- `getProductWithDesign(item_id)` — `GET ${HOST}/products/product/design?session=...&item_id=${item_id}` — fetches a product together with a previously saved per-item design; resolves full response JSON.
- `getDesign(design_id)` — `GET ${HOST}/designs/design?session=...&design_id=${design_id}` — fetches a saved design (with `checkInfoStatusSession` guard); resolves full response JSON.
- `getDesignUser(design_id)` — same endpoint as `getDesign` but **without** the `checkInfoStatusSession` guard (used in user-facing contexts where a session-expiry redirect would be undesirable); resolves full response JSON.
- `saveProduct(colorId, sides, parentProductId, design_id, is_admin, admin_item_id, admin_order_id, design_name, is_embroidery, mode_draw)` — `PUT ${HOST}/products/product`, JSON body `{session, Product:{color_id, design_sides, design_id, admin_item_id, admin_order_id, design_name, is_embroidery, special_draw: mode_draw[, parent_product_id]}, is_admin}` — saves/finalizes the designed product (e.g. add-to-cart); if `query.room_chat` is set, adds `is_chat_design:true` and `room_chat_id`; resolves full response JSON.
- `saveProduct2(...)` — near-identical duplicate of `saveProduct` (same params/payload/endpoint) but **without** the chat-room fields; appears to be an alternate/legacy save path.
- `getProductColors(product_id)` — `GET ${HOST}/products/models/model/colors?session=...&product_id=${product_id}` — fetches available colors for a product; resolves full response JSON.
- `getUser()` — `GET ${HOST}/user/session/check?session=...` — checks/fetches the current session's user; resolves full response JSON.
- `getRemakeImage3D(product, recItem, index=0)` — `POST ${HOST}/design/create-image3d`, JSON body = `product` object extended with `session`, `index`, `remake_rec: recItem` — requests a "remake" 3D preview image render; resolves full response JSON.
- `getAllPreviewColor()` — `GET ${HOST}/design/item-remake` (guarded) — fetches remake preview item types, mapping the response array to `i => i.item_type_sub`; resolves the mapped array.
- `getAllKeyFilters(id, sub_id)` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/keyword?master_category_style_id=${id}&category_id=${sub_id||''}` — fetches keyword filter options; resolves `data.keyword.Keyword`.
- `getProductBykeyFilter(key, category_id)` — `GET ${HOST}/products/keyword?id=${key}[&category_id=${category_id}]` — fetches products matching a keyword filter; resolves full response JSON.
- `getAllProductsByMasterCategory(parentCategoryId)` — `GET ${HOST}/v2/products/models?session=...&parent_category_id=${parentCategoryId}&admin=${is_admin}&device_type=webbrowser[yuyami]&from_tool=1` — fetches all products under a master category; resolves full response JSON.
- `getProductByImagickModel(item_type, item_type_sub)` — `GET ${HOST}/design/product-item3d?session=...&item_type=${item_type}&item_type_sub=${item_type_sub}` — fetches the product matching a 3D "Imagick" model type; resolves full response JSON.
- `getAllKeyFilterPrice()` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/list-price-filter?type=price` — fetches price-bracket filter options; resolves full response JSON.
- `getAllKeyFilterMaker(id)` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/list-price-filter?type=maker&master_item_style_id=${id}` — fetches maker filter options; resolves full response JSON.
- `getAllKeyFilterSize(id)` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/list-price-filter?type=size&master_item_style_id=${id}` — fetches size filter options; resolves full response JSON.
- `getAllKeyFilterColors()` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/list-price-filter?type=colors` — fetches color filter options; resolves full response JSON.
- `getAllKeyFilterConditions(id)` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/list-price-filter?type=conditions` — fetches condition filter options (the `id` param is accepted but not used in the query string); resolves full response JSON.
- `getAllKeyFilterTypeOfSmartPhone()` — `GET ${HOST}/drawer/list-price-filter?type=smart_phone_case` — fetches smartphone-case-type filter options; resolves full response JSON.
- `getFavoriteProductItem()` — `GET ${HOST}/get-item-favorite?session=...` — fetches the user's favorite product items; resolves full response JSON.
- `removeFavoriteProductItem(id)` — `POST ${HOST}/delete-item-favorite?session=...&item_type=${id}`, JSON body `{session, item_type: id}` — removes a favorite (note: the returned promise never calls `resolve`/`reject` on success, only implicitly resolves `undefined` when the executor returns).
- `addFavoriteProductItem(id)` — `POST ${HOST}/add-item-favorite?session=...&item_type=${id}`, JSON body `{session, item_type: id}` — adds a favorite; the `.then(() => true)` result is discarded since the promise executor doesn't call `resolve` with it (same pattern issue as above — callers awaiting this promise get `undefined` on success, not `true`).
- `getAllKeyFilterByKey(key)` — `GET ${HOST}/get-data-filter?type=${key}` — generic keyed filter-options fetch; resolves full response JSON.
- `countUpointByUser1()` — `POST ${HOST}/designs/design/count-use-item-3d`, JSON body `{session}` — increments a 3D-item usage counter (no response is awaited/resolved — same unresolved-promise pattern as `removeFavoriteProductItem`).
- `countUpointByUser()` — `GET ${HOST}/designs/design/count-use-item-3d?session=...` — fetches the current 3D-item usage count; resolves full response JSON.
- `postUpload3dImage(fileUrl)` — `POST ${HOST}/designs/design/file/zen-3d`, JSON body `{file: fileUrl, session}` — submits an image to the "zen-3d" AI 3D-generation job queue; resolves `data.job_id`, throws/rejects if the response isn't OK or lacks a `job_id`.
- `getResultModel(id)` — `GET ${HOST}/designs/design/file/zen-3d/result/${id}?session=...` — polls a zen-3d job's result; resolves `{status, url}`, throws/rejects if the response isn't OK or the payload lacks `status`/`url`.
