## src/utils/TraceSkeleton.js

**Purpose.** This module reduces a binary raster mask (thick black/white shapes) down to a 1-pixel-wide topological "skeleton" and then vectorizes that skeleton into a set of polyline fragments — essentially the centerlines of the input shapes. In UP-T it underpins laser-cutting path generation: `src/utils/ImageCuttingLaser.js` builds a raster grid representing "inflated" regions around vector paths (used for spacing/hole computation for acrylic laser stands and pedestals) and calls `TraceSkeleton.trace(...)` to collapse those inflated regions back down to single-pixel-wide centerlines, which are then walked and reconnected into the final cut/fold path data (`addGrayImageAreaNegative` in `ImageCuttingLaser.js`).

**Vendored: yes** — this is a JavaScript port of the "TraceSkeleton" topology-preserving image-skeletonization/vectorization algorithm (the same algorithm distributed as the `skeleton-tracing` project, which ships both a WASM build and a pure-JS reference implementation). Evidence in the file: the thinning routine is explicitly cited as "Zhang-Suen algorithm" with a link to `http://agcggs680.pbworks.com/f/Zhan-Suen_algorithm.pdf` (lines 4–9); the recursive divide-and-conquer chunk-splitting/merging tracer (`chunkToFrags`, `mergeFrags`, `traceSkeleton`) matches that project's documented algorithm; and the block of methods at the end is explicitly labeled `//compatibility with wasm api` (line 359), providing `onload`, `fromBoolArray`, `fromCharString`, `fromImageData`, `fromCanvas`, and `visualize` wrappers that mirror the upstream library's dual JS/WASM interface even though no WASM is actually used here. There is no explicit copyright/license header or version string in the file (unlike ImageTracer.js), so the exact upstream commit/version cannot be confirmed, but the algorithmic and API fingerprints make the vendoring unambiguous.

### Exports

The file exports a singleton object `TraceSkeleton` (default export) with the following public methods:

- `thinningZS(im, w, h)` — In-place Zhang-Suen binary thinning/skeletonization of a flat `Uint8Array`/array-like binary image (`im`, indexed `y*w+x`), reducing thick strokes to 1-pixel-wide lines.
- `traceSkeleton(im, W, H, x, y, w, h, csize, maxIter, rects)` — Lower-level recursive vectorizer: given an already-thinned image (or region), recursively splits chunks along low-density seams, converts small chunks to polyline fragments (`chunkToFrags`), and merges fragments across chunk boundaries. Returns an array of polylines (each an array of `[x, y]` points). `rects`, if passed an array, is populated with the chunk bounding boxes used during recursion (useful for debugging/visualization).
- `trace(im, W, H, csize)` — The main entry point: runs `thinningZS` then `traceSkeleton` over the whole image. **(Used by UP-T.)** Called as `TraceSkeleton.trace(arrayImageTraceSkeleton, this.widthNew, this.heightNew, 1)` in `ImageCuttingLaser.js`, where `arrayImageTraceSkeleton` is a flat 0/1 array and `csize=1` (minimum recursive-chunk size, i.e. maximum skeleton detail). Returns `{ rects, polylines, width, height }`; the caller consumes `polylines` (walking each polyline's points to mark/connect skeleton pixels) and ignores `rects`.
- `onload(f)` — Compatibility shim for parity with the WASM-backed version of the library; since this pure-JS port needs no async module loading, it just invokes `f()` synchronously.
- `fromBoolArray(im, w, h)` — Convenience wrapper: converts a boolean array to 0/1 and calls `trace(..., csize=10)`.
- `fromCharString(im, w, h)` — Convenience wrapper: converts a packed string of char codes to a numeric array and calls `trace(..., csize=10)`.
- `fromImageData(im)` — Convenience wrapper: takes a canvas `ImageData` object, thresholds each pixel's red channel to 0/1, and calls `trace(..., csize=10)`.
- `fromCanvas(im)` — Convenience wrapper: extracts `ImageData` from a `<canvas>` element via `getContext('2d').getImageData(...)` and delegates to `fromImageData`.
- `visualize(ret, args)` — Debug/demo helper that renders a `trace()` result (`rects` and `polylines`) to an SVG string, with optional `args.scale`, `args.strokeWidth`, `args.rects` (toggle drawing chunk boxes), and `args.keypoints` (toggle drawing per-vertex markers). Not used elsewhere in the app.

None of `fromBoolArray`, `fromCharString`, `fromImageData`, `fromCanvas`, `onload`, or `visualize` are referenced outside this file — the only consumer (`ImageCuttingLaser.js`) calls `trace()` directly with a pre-built flat array.

## src/utils/SVGLoader.js

**Purpose.** SVGLoader parses SVG document text into Three.js-consumable path geometry. It walks the SVG DOM tree produced by the browser's `DOMParser`, interpreting `<path>`, `<rect>`, `<circle>`, `<ellipse>`, `<polygon>`, `<polyline>`, `<line>` elements (and their `transform`, `style`, `fill`/`stroke` presentation attributes), and converts each into a `THREE.ShapePath` built from `Path`/`Shape` curve segments (lines, cubic/quadratic beziers, and elliptical arcs converted to beziers). The result is a flat list of `ShapePath` objects (one per SVG path/shape element) plus the parsed XML document, which calling code can turn into `THREE.Shape` polygons (via `toShapes()`/`createShapes()`) for extrusion, filling, or stroking.

In this codebase it is used exclusively for laser-engraving geometry: raw pixel/point paths produced by the 2D draw tool are serialized into synthetic SVG path data, fed through `SVGLoader.parse()`, and the resulting shapes are used to build holes/cut regions for the 3D laser preview (`shapes = path.toShapes(true); holes.push(shapes[0])`), i.e. converting freehand/traced 2D outlines into `THREE.Shape` objects that the Three.js laser-preview scene extrudes or subtracts.

**Vendored: yes** — Three.js `examples/jsm/loaders/SVGLoader.js`. No explicit `@revision`/version banner is present in the file's header comments; the only version-relevant markers found are inline (an external MIT-licensed reference for numeric SVG parsing at line ~1053, and DPI/unit-conversion tables). It uses the modern ES6-class `Loader` subclass style consistent with a relatively recent three.js release, importing `SRGBColorSpace` (a newer three.js color-management API) rather than the older `sRGBEncoding` constant.

### Exports

- `SVGLoader` (default/only export, `export { SVGLoader }`) — a class extending `THREE.Loader`.
  - `constructor(manager)` — standard `Loader` constructor; also sets `this.defaultDPI = 90` and `this.defaultUnit = 'px'` (accepted units: `'mm'`, `'cm'`, `'in'`, `'pt'`, `'pc'`, `'px'`).
  - `load(url, onLoad, onProgress, onError)` — fetches `url` via an internal `FileLoader` (honoring `path`, `requestHeader`, `withCredentials`) and calls `this.parse(text)` on the response, invoking `onLoad(data)` with the parse result or `onError(e)` on failure. Not used directly by the app (the app only calls `parse()`).
  - `parse(text)` — parses an SVG string with `DOMParser` (`image/svg+xml`) and walks the document recursively, applying inherited style/transform state. Returns `{ paths, xml }` where `paths` is an array of `THREE.ShapePath` objects (each carrying a `.userData.style` object with resolved fill/stroke properties) and `xml` is the parsed `documentElement`. This is the method the app calls directly: `new SVGLoader().parse(svgString)`, then reads `result.paths[0].toShapes(true)` to get an array of `THREE.Shape` for laser-hole/cut geometry.
  - `static createShapes(shapePath)` — given a `ShapePath` (as found in `parse()`'s `paths` array), resolves subpath winding/containment (using an internal Bentley–Ottmann-style edge-intersection classifier) to correctly nest holes inside outer contours, returning an array of `THREE.Shape` objects. (This is effectively what `ShapePath.prototype.toShapes()` calls under the hood in three.js; the app calls it indirectly via `path.toShapes(true)`.)
  - `static getStrokeStyle(width, color, lineJoin, lineCap, miterLimit)` — builds and returns a plain style object (`{ strokeColor, strokeWidth, strokeLineJoin, strokeLineCap, strokeMiterLimit }`) with defaults (`width=1`, `color='#000'`, `lineJoin='miter'`, `lineCap='butt'`, `miterLimit=4`) suitable for passing to `pointsToStroke`.
  - `static pointsToStroke(points, style, arcDivisions, minDistance)` — given an array of `Vector2` points describing an open or closed path and a style object (from `getStrokeStyle()` or a path's `userData.style`), generates a flat, triangulated stroke outline and returns a `THREE.BufferGeometry` (position/normal/uv attributes, all at z=0) representing the stroke; returns `null` if no geometry was generated.
  - `static pointsToStrokeWithBuffers(points, style, arcDivisions, minDistance, vertices, normals, uvs, vertexOffset)` — lower-level variant of `pointsToStroke` that writes triangle data into caller-supplied `vertices`/`normals`/`uvs` arrays (for updating existing buffers) starting at an optional `vertexOffset`, returning the number of vertices written.

---

## src/utils/GLTFLoader.js

**Purpose.** GLTFLoader parses the glTF 2.0 3D-model format (both text `.gltf` JSON with external/embedded resources and binary `.glb`) into a Three.js scene graph. It supports the full glTF 2.0 core spec plus a large set of `KHR_*`/vendor extensions (Draco mesh compression via a separately-supplied `DRACOLoader`, Basis Universal / KTX2 textures, PBR material extensions such as clearcoat, transmission, sheen, specular, IOR, unlit, texture transform, mesh quantization, lights, draco/meshopt, etc.), building materials (`MeshStandardMaterial`/`MeshPhysicalMaterial`/`MeshBasicMaterial`), meshes/skinned meshes, cameras, lights, and `AnimationClip`s from the glTF JSON. The output is a plain result object containing a Three.js `Group` scene graph (not a `THREE.Scene`), the list of parsed scenes, animation clips, cameras, the raw glTF `asset` block, the internal `parser`, and a `userData` bag.

In this codebase it is the workhorse for all 3D product/model loading: the base t-shirt/product `.glb` models shown in the 3D preview and 3D-draw-tool editors (`src/containers/App/index.jsx`, `src/components/3d-preview/index.jsx`, `src/components/3d-triple/index.jsx`) are all loaded through a shared `GLTFLoader` instance. `3d-triple/index.jsx` additionally wires up the vendored `src/utils/DRACOLoader.js` via `setDRACOLoader()` for Draco-compressed geometry. Consuming code reads `gltf.scene` (assigning it into the Three.js scene, walking it with `traverse()` to tag meshes for shadows and recolor materials) — animations/cameras/asset fields from the result are not used by the app.

**Vendored: yes** — Three.js `examples/js/loaders/GLTFLoader.js`. No explicit revision/version comment banner is present in the header, but the file uses the older ES5 "IIFE + prototype" module pattern (`var GLTFLoader = (function () { ... GLTFLoader.prototype = Object.assign(Object.create(Loader.prototype), {...}); return GLTFLoader; })()`) rather than an ES6 `class`, and imports the deprecated `sRGBEncoding` / `RGBFormat` constants alongside `MeshPhysicalMaterial`/clearcoat-transmission extension support — indicating an older (pre-ES6-class-rewrite) three.js examples release than SVGLoader.js. Notably, this vendored copy has been extended with two non-stock methods not found in upstream three.js GLTFLoader: `getBuffer(url, onLoad, onProgress, onError)` (fetches and returns the raw arraybuffer without parsing, used by `App/index.jsx` to cache a model's raw bytes) and `loadBuffer(url, buffer, onLoad, onProgress, onError)` (parses an already-fetched buffer directly, skipping the network fetch — used by `3d-preview/index.jsx` when `this.props.buffer3d` is already cached in Redux state), so this is a locally-customized vendored copy rather than a byte-for-byte upstream mirror.

### Exports

- `GLTFLoader` (default/only export, `export { GLTFLoader }`) — constructor function extending `THREE.Loader` via prototype chain.
  - `constructor(manager)` — standard `Loader` constructor; initializes `this.dracoLoader = null`, `this.ddsLoader = null`, `this.ktx2Loader = null`, `this.pluginCallbacks = []`, and pre-registers built-in extension plugin factories (`GLTFMaterialsClearcoatExtension`, `GLTFTextureBasisUExtension`, `GLTFMaterialsTransmissionExtension`). The app calls this with no arguments (e.g. `new GLTFLoader()`), relying on the default `DefaultLoadingManager`.
  - `load(url, onLoad, onProgress, onError)` — fetches `url` as an `arraybuffer` via an internal `FileLoader` and calls `this.parse(data, resourcePath, onLoad, onError)` on the response; `resourcePath` is derived from `this.resourcePath`/`this.path`/the URL itself, used to resolve relative buffer/image URIs. This is the primary method the app uses: `loader.load(shapes3DSelect.url, (gltf) => { this.show3DModel(gltf, dataInfoMTL); }, undefined, (error) => {...})`.
  - `getBuffer(url, onLoad, onProgress, onError)` — **project-added, non-stock method.** Same fetch machinery as `load()`, but calls `onLoad(data)` with the raw arraybuffer instead of parsing it. Used in `App/index.jsx` (`loader3D.getBuffer(shapes3DSelect.url, (data) => { this.setState({info3Dsave:{url:..., data}}) }, ...)`) to cache the downloaded model bytes (e.g. into Redux `buffer3d`) for reuse.
  - `loadBuffer(url, buffer, onLoad, onProgress, onError)` — **project-added, non-stock method.** Skips the network fetch and calls `this.parse(buffer, resourcePath, onLoad, onError)` directly on an already-obtained arraybuffer. Used in `3d-preview/index.jsx` (`loader.loadBuffer(shapes3DSelect.url, this.props.buffer3d, (gltf) => { this.show3DModel(gltf, dataInfoMTL); }, ...)`) to avoid re-downloading a model whose bytes were previously cached via `getBuffer`.
  - `setDRACOLoader(dracoLoader)` — stores a `DRACOLoader` instance (returns `this` for chaining); required for models using the `KHR_draco_mesh_compression` extension. Used in `3d-triple/index.jsx`: `const dracoLoader = new DRACOLoader(); this.loader.setDRACOLoader(dracoLoader);` before calling `load()`. Throws `'THREE.GLTFLoader: No DRACOLoader instance provided.'` if a Draco-compressed primitive is encountered without one configured.
  - `setDDSLoader(ddsLoader)` / `setKTX2Loader(ktx2Loader)` — analogous setters for DDS texture and KTX2/Basis-compressed texture support (not observed to be used by the app; present for extension completeness).
  - `register(callback)` / `unregister(callback)` — add/remove a plugin factory function `(parser) => pluginInstance` from `this.pluginCallbacks`, used internally to enable per-glTF-file extension parsers (not called directly by app code beyond the constructor's built-in registrations).
  - `parse(data, path, onLoad, onError)` — parses either a JSON string (`.gltf`) or an `ArrayBuffer` (auto-detecting binary `.glb` via the `glTF` magic header) into extensions/content, builds a `GLTFParser`, and asynchronously resolves `onLoad(gltf)` where `gltf = { scene, scenes, animations, cameras, asset, parser, userData }` (`scene` is `dependencies[0][json.scene || 0]`, a `THREE.Group`, per the code's own comment "Loader returns Group, not Scene"). Called internally by `load()`/`loadBuffer()`; app code never calls `parse()` directly, only through those two entry points.

---

---

# Part 3 — App Container (root component)

## src/containers/App/index.jsx

### Purpose

`App` is the root React container of the UP-T product designer. It is a single, very large class component (4566 lines) that is mounted at the top of the application tree and connected to Redux via `connect(mapStateToProps)(App)`. It owns almost none of the "real" design data itself (that lives in the `drawTool` and `product` Redux slices and in the imperative `DrawTool` singleton, imported from `../../draw-tool/drawtool`), but it is the piece of code that:

- Bootstraps the whole session on load: reads the query string (`../../constants/query`), figures out whether the user is opening a fresh product, an existing design (`design_id`/`item_id`), a shared "room" design (`room_chat`), a color/model/category deep link, or a 3D/laser/embroidery/carpet product, and dispatches the corresponding Redux actions and REST calls (`../../api/products`, `../../api/extras`, `../../api/options`) to populate the store.
- Registers all of the imperative event listeners on the `DrawTool` fabric.js-based canvas engine (selection, rotation, scaling, delete, color-fill, carpet dragging, 3D figure updates, mode changes) inside `componentWillMount`, translating raw canvas events into Redux dispatches.
- Renders the overall page chrome: loaders/progress bar, the intro.js walkthrough (`Steps`), the desktop `Header` / mobile `HeaderMobile`, the `Toolbar`, the canvas itself (`DrawToolComponent`), the `Options` side panel, the 3D/laser preview components, the various "pick a product/category/font" modal-like overlay panels (`ProductLoad`, `ProductLaserLoad`, `FontLoad`), the "carpet" (repeating pattern) length/scroll controls, the saved-design picker modal, and `MobileNavigation`.
- Implements a large amount of business logic for product loading/switching (including confirmation dialogs when switching away from embroidery/laser/carpet modes would destroy incompatible content), category/maker/key-filter navigation and product list filtering/sorting, font loading and per-font lazy application, pricing calculation, and "carpet" (seamless/repeating pattern) length and scroll-position editing.

In short, `App` is the orchestration hub: it wires Redux state and dispatched actions to the imperative `DrawTool` canvas engine and to all of the presentational child containers/components that make up the editor UI.

### Imports (grouped)

- **React/Redux**: `React, { Component }` (React 15, uses `React.PropTypes`), `connect` from `react-redux`.
- **Utilities**: `parse` from `querystring`; `sweetalert2` as `sww` and `sweetalert` as `swal` (two different alert/dialog libraries used side by side); `MediaQuery` from `react-responsive`; `Steps` from `intro.js-react` (first-time-user walkthrough).
- **App-local constants/query**: `query` from `../../constants/query` (parsed query-string singleton), `* as Setting` from `../../constants/setting`, constants from `../../constants` (`URL_REMOVE_BG, HOST, WEBHOST, URL_ORDERMADE`).
- **Child containers/components**: `Header`, `HeaderMobile`, `Toolbar`, `Options`, `MobileNavigation` (containers); `Loader`, `ProcessBar`, `ProductLoad`, `FontLoad`, `ProductCard`, `FontCard`, `ProductLaserLoad`, `ProductLazeCard`, `ProductCardFilter`, `Preview3DComponent`, `Preview3DLaser`, `Design3D`, `Text2Image`, `LaserPedestal`/`LaserPedestalMobile`/`LaserKeyHold`/`LaserNoHold`/`LaserSticker`, `DrawToolComponent`, `FooterChat` (from `../Options/FooterChat.jsx`).
- **Draw engine**: `DrawTool` (the singleton fabric.js wrapper, `../../draw-tool/drawtool`) and `DrawToolComponent` (its React wrapper).
- **Redux action creators**: `* as ProductActions` (`../../actions/product`), `* as DrawToolActions` and `* as actions` (both point at `../../actions/draw-tool` — imported twice under two names and used interchangeably throughout the file).
- **API modules**: a large set of named functions from `../../api/products` (categories, products, colors, favorites, key-filters, design save/load, AI model, upload, etc.) and `../../api/extras` (stickers/stamps/shapes categories, laser models, chat-room/design-sync endpoints, shapes3D, upload-by-string) and `../../api/options` (fonts and brushes by language/category).
- **Misc helpers/utils**: `disableOnBeforeUnload`, `persistSession`, `FontDetect` (`../../utils/fontdetect`), `DeleteLineCut` (`../../middleware/draw-tool`), `MethodImageProcessing`, `GLTFLoader`, `loadQueryString`, `show2DPreviewDetail`, `setCookie`/`getCookie`.

### Module-level constants / helpers (outside the class)

- `STORAGE_KEY = 'AllProducts'`, `STORAGE_TIME_ALL_PRODUCTS`, `MINUTES_SET` (20 min), `HOURS_24` — control localStorage caching/expiry of the "all products" product list and related lookup tables (fonts, key filters).
- `CATEGORIES_ACC_STANDARD4`, `CATEGORIES_ACC_POPULARITY3` — category IDs that force a specific default product-sort value when navigated to.
- `REMOVE_ID_PRODUCTS` — hardcoded product IDs to hide from listings (Zozo-related exclusion).
- `T_SHIRT, SP_CASE, BAG, PAPER, CUP_KITCHEN, OTHER, KEY_CHAIN` — numeric category-style IDs used repeatedly to branch sort behavior.
- `TYPE_CATEGORY_EXTENT` — array of `{id, link}` for "virtual" categories that are actually external links (drawer-pasral site, paper-product pages) rather than in-app categories; consulted by `goToCategory`/`goTolinkUrl` and in render to decide whether a category tile navigates internally or externally.
- `loader3D = new GLTFLoader()` — module-level singleton GLTF loader used by the `view3d:update` DrawTool event handler to fetch 3D model buffers.
- `normalizeAngle(angle)` (declared inline inside `componentWillMount`) — helper that normalizes a rotation angle into `[0, 360)`, used by the `object:rotating` handler.

### State

`this.state` (React state, updated via `setState`):

- `category` (string) — title of the currently selected top-level category, used as the modal title for the product-type picker.
- `loadData` (bool) — guard flag; once `true`, prevents `loadData()`/`loadFonts()` from being invoked again from `componentDidMount`/`componentWillReceiveProps`.
- `category_active` (number) — the currently active category-style id (drives which category tab/filter UI is highlighted).
- `maker_active` (string) — currently selected maker filter, default `'全て'` (All).
- `keyword_filter_id` (string|null) — currently selected key-filter id for product filtering.
- `category_type_id` (number|null) — the category-style type currently being browsed (set in `filterCategory`).
- `stepsEnabled` (bool) — controls whether the intro.js `Steps` walkthrough is shown.
- `initialStep` (number) — starting step index for intro.js.
- `isText2Img` (bool) — always initialized `true`; passed nowhere obviously beyond initial default (actual `isText2Img` used in render comes from Redux props, not this state field).
- `steps` (array) — the two intro.js step definitions (element selector + HTML tooltip content) shown to first-time users.
- `introOptions` (object) — intro.js configuration (labels, tooltip class, `exitOnOverlayClick`, etc., all in Japanese UI text).
- `mobile` (bool) — result of `window.matchMedia('(max-width: 1079px)').matches`, computed once at construction (not reactive to resize other than via the resize handler set up in `componentDidMount`, which reads `this.state.mobile` but does not appear to update it).
- `cate_font_active` (string) — active font category tab, default `'日本語'` (Japanese).
- `is_font_loader` (bool) — shows a `Loader` overlay while a font is being applied/loaded (`applyFont`).
- `info3Dsave` (object `{url, data}`) — cached GLTF buffer/url for the currently selected 3D shape, populated by the `view3d:update` DrawTool listener and consumed by `Preview3DComponent`.
- `scrollCarpet` (number) — current horizontal scroll/view offset (in cm) for "carpet" (seamless repeating pattern) products.
- `scrollCarpetMax` (number) — max scroll bound for the carpet view, derived from border width.
- `settingCarpetLength` (number) — user-configured total carpet length in cm.
- `preview_2d` (bool) — whether the "2D preview" button/feature is enabled for the current product, toggled by the `control:mode` DrawTool event.

Other **instance fields** (not part of React state, not triggering re-render):

- `this.stateSelect` (number, default `-1`) — index of the currently selected item within `DrawTool.sides.selected.items._collection`; used to track selection across `object:selected`/`selection:cleared` events to mark items' `useItemData` flag.
- `this.scrollCarpetUpdate` — `{value_size, value_move, mode_up_size, mode_up_move, inteverResponse}`, state for the press-and-hold repeat behavior of the carpet +/- buttons.
- `this.enableOpenOldDesign` (bool) — guard used when loading a saved design so the subsequent product-colors refresh doesn't clobber the loaded design's data.
- `this.checkSelectDesign` (id|null) — guards against re-entrant "apply design to side" processing in `selectSideAndSetDessign`.
- `this.methodImageProcessing` (instance of `MethodImageProcessing`, lazily created) — used for 2D-preview image conversion and for building the carpet "resulting image" dialog HTML.
- `this.time_calcPrice` — `{intervalResponsiveTime, time: []}` — debouncing state for `calcPriceUpdate`/`calcPrice`.
- `this.intervalCheck` — `{rotating: {time, value, action}}` — throttling state for the `object:rotating` DrawTool event so `updateDegree` dispatches aren't flooded.
- `this.lvcLaserAccess` (bool, set in `filterCategory`) — whether the laser-specific product browsing UI (`ProductLaserLoad`/`ProductLazeCard`) should be used instead of the normal `ProductLoad`/`ProductCard`.
- `this.fontDetectInitialised` (bool, set lazily in `componentWillReceiveProps`) — guards one-time registration of `FontDetect.onFontLoaded` callbacks for every available font.

### Props

Declared via `static propTypes` (partial — actual usage reads many more props not listed in propTypes) and consumed via `this.props` / destructured in `mapStateToProps` (see below for the full Redux-supplied prop list). Key ones used throughout the file:

- `dispatch` (func) — Redux dispatch, used everywhere.
- `product` (object) — the currently loaded product record.
- `colorSelected` (object) — the currently selected color variant.
- `list_preview_color` (array) — colors that have an "imagick model" preview available (checked before calling `getProductByImagickModel`).
- `categories`, `category_styles`, `categories_filter` — category taxonomy data used for building the browse UI and for `filterCategory`/`goToCategory`.
- `products`, `allProductByMasterCategory`, `favoriteProducts` — product lists keyed by category / master-category / favorites, filtered and sorted extensively in `render()`.
- `currentCategory`, `category_style_id`, `typeOfProduct` — current navigation position.
- `loading`, `loading_category`, `loading_process`, `loading_data`, `check_product` — various async/loading flags controlling `Loader`/`ProcessBar` display and gating re-fetch logic in `componentWillReceiveProps`.
- `activeTool` — current drawing tool, checked e.g. in `componentWillReceiveProps` (`'text'`) and to avoid `onGetImfomations` running mid-brush-stroke (`'brush'`).
- `allFonts`, `allFontsList`, `availableFonts`, `font_filter`, `is_embroidery` — font data/state used by `loadFonts`, `applyFont`, `fillterFontsByCategory`.
- `sidesPrice` — per-side pricing map consumed by `calcPrice`.
- `templateUsers`, `designSelected`, `showModalDesign` — the "apply a previously-saved design" modal's data.
- `shapes3DSelect`, `is_3d_preview`, `is_3d_preview_laser`, `tripo3D_mode` — 3D/laser preview toggles.
- `infoRoomDesign` — multi-user "room" (shared design session) info.
- `objKeyFilter, objSexFilter, objPrintFilter, objMaterialArrFilter, objSceneFilter, objPriceFilter, objSizeFilter, objSideFilter, objColorFilter, objMaterialFilter, objValueFilter, objCategoryFilter, objCategorySubFilter, objSPCaseFilter, objModelPhone, arrStatusFilter, valueProductSort` — the full set of product-list filter/sort criteria applied in `render()`.
- `userAccess` — feature-flag-like object (`is_show_event_product`, `is_show_zozo_product`) used to hide certain products.
- `makers`, `filter` — maker list and generic filter flag passed down to the product picker components.
- `loadProductContainer`, `loadProductCatContainer`, `loadProductTypeContainer`, `loadFontsCatContainer` — booleans controlling which overlay panel (saved designs / category picker / product-type picker / font picker) is currently shown.
- `embroider_able`, `categoryProductItem`, `loadingAPIComplate`, `current_Category_id` — assorted product/category loading-state props used in `goToCategory`/`filterCategory`/`onFilterByKey`.

### Methods

Constructor-bound handlers (`this.x = this.x.bind(this)`): `goToCategory`, `typeSelectorBack`, `mobileClose`, `mobileBack`, `loadProduct`, `removeTemplate`, `loadProductWithDesign`, `loadDefaultProduct`, `calcPrice`, `sortProduction`, `sortMaker`, `goTolinkUrl`, `onStepHintChange`, `onStepExit`, `onStepComplete`, `onFilterByKey`, `onProductDetail`. Many other methods are defined as class-property arrow functions (auto-bound) instead.

- `checkFirstLanding()` — reads the `mac_browser` cookie and `query.master_category_style_id`; if either is present, sets `stepsEnabled: false`, otherwise `true`. Called from `componentWillReceiveProps` on every props update (so effectively runs continuously until the cookie is set).
- `componentWillMount()` — see Lifecycle section; registers custom rotation-control icons on `DrawTool`, wires up 12 `DrawTool.on(...)` event listeners (see below), performs the query-string based initial routing/loading (session redirect, room-chat/shared-design join flow, `item_id`/`design_id`/`change_model_id`/`color_id`/`model_id`/`category_id` deep-link loading, or `loadDefaultProduct()` fallback), and kicks off several unconditional API calls (`getUser`, `getAllPreviewColor`, `getCategoryStyles`, `getCategories`, `getAllKeyFilterPrice`, `getShapes3D`).
- `updateStateOfItem(that)` — given `that` (an App instance, usually `this`), re-evaluates the `useItemData` flag of the item at `that.stateSelect` based on `DrawTool.sides.selected.checkItemEmptyInSide(item)`. Largely superseded/duplicated by inline logic in the `object:selected`/`selection:cleared` DrawTool handlers; some call sites are commented out.
- `updateHistoryOfSides()` — empty stub (no-op).
- `goToCategory(id, title)` — main category-navigation handler. Adjusts sort value for certain category groups, reads/writes the `AllProducts` localStorage cache, special-cases `id === 4` (loads key filters) and `id === 1021` (redirects into the 3D "D3D001" product flow via `set3DTripo`/`loadProduct` and returns early), then dispatches `updateCatProductIdItem`, `updateCurrentCategoryID`, `updateCurrentId`, resets the process bar, and calls `getProductsByCategory(id)` to load/merge products into Redux (`loadProducts`) and makers (`loadMakers`), updating the localStorage cache as it goes. Toggles the category/type container visibility flags at the end.
- `typeSelectorBack()` — restores product sort value based on `category_style_id`, clears `currentCategoryID`, hides the type container and re-shows the category container (used by the desktop "back" button in the product-type picker).
- `mobileClose()` — stops the current `DrawTool` action and hides all mobile overlay containers (product/category/type/fonts).
- `mobileBack()` — like `typeSelectorBack` but also stops the current `DrawTool` action; used by the mobile product-list "back" button.
- `loadProductClose()` — stops current `DrawTool` action and hides the saved-products (`loadProductContainer`) panel. Note: references a free `dispatch` variable that is not defined in this method's scope (likely a bug — relies on a module/closure-level `dispatch`, which does not exist here; would throw if invoked with a config where it isn't dead code).
- `loadItem3D=(id,colorSelectId,color)=>` — resolves a color id (from `colorSelectId` or `color.ProductColor.id`) and, if it's in `list_preview_color`, calls `getProductByImagickModel(id,colorSelect)` and dispatches `loadImagickModel(data)`; otherwise dispatches `loadImagickModel([])`.
- `loadProduct(id, valueColorIdChange=null)` — the main "switch to a different product" flow. Sets `DrawTool.is_choosing`, stops current action, hides the product picker, handles embroidery-mode side setup, resets/starts the process bar, and (for carpet-mode products) resets carpet border state. Calls `getProductWithColor(id, valueColorIdChange)`, then: sets the chosen color as `is_main` if `valueColorIdChange` given, calls `loadItem3D`, and then branches extensively depending on whether the *new* product is category 500 (stamp-lock check), the *current* mode is `EMBROIDER_EXTEND`, the current mode is a laser mode (`checkProductLaser`), or plain embroidery — in each destructive case it shows a `swal` confirmation dialog before stripping incompatible items from all sides and finally dispatching `ProductActions.loadProduct(data)` plus `DrawTool.checkAndReloadChatInfo()`.
- `sortProduction(category_id, data)` — filters `data` down to items whose `Product.category_id === category_id`; simple synchronous helper, no side effects.
- `sortMaker(makers)` — reorders a `makers` array to match the canonical order in `Setting.MAKERS`.
- `checkEmbroiderInProduct=()=>` — after a 2.5s delay, if the current product is a laser product, dispatches `actions.updateBorder()` and calls `DrawTool.setLayerSetup(-1)`.
- `checkLoadingTextFont=()=>` — used only in the shared "room" (`query.room_chat`) flow when there's a pending `DrawTool.lastDesign`; shows a `sww` loading spinner, polls (`setInterval`) `DrawTool.changeProductSelectAll` until sides are populated, then ensures any needed fonts are loaded (dispatching `actions.updateFontsByName`) before waiting for `document.fonts.ready` and re-applying the design; also polls until the expected number of items are loaded or a 2-minute timeout elapses, then closes the spinner.
- `loadDefaultProduct(color_id, model_id, category_id)` — reads a `foodprint` session-storage override for `model_id`, then dispatches to one of four branches (by `color_id` / `model_id` / `category_id` / none) that call `getDefaultProductExtend(...)`, `loadItem3D`, and `dispatch(ProductActions.loadProduct(...))`; the `color_id` branch also schedules `checkLoadingTextFont()` after 1s.
- `loadProductWithDesign(data)` — loads fonts (`getFonts()`), then dispatches `ProductActions.loadProductWithDesign(data)` and `DrawToolActions.updateFonts(dataFont)`, with iOS-specific handling (delays and `setLoading(true)`/`selectColor`) for the acrylic-laser mode.
- `calcPriceUpdate=()=>` — debounced trigger for `calcPrice()`: buffers timestamps in `this.time_calcPrice.time` and, if not already running, starts a 1s interval that calls `calcPrice()` repeatedly and stops once no recent calls remain (essentially trailing-debounce over a rolling 500ms window). Bound to the `layers:update` DrawTool event.
- `getDefaultProductExtend=(color_id, model_id)=>` — calls `this.loadFonts()`, then wraps `getDefaultProduct(color_id, model_id)` in a `Promise` and resolves/rejects with its result.
- `calcPrice()` — iterates `sidesPrice` from props, computing a running total `p` by inspecting each `DrawTool` side's object count / embroidery counts / print-draw counts (carpet products use `settingCarpetLength` × per-cm price instead of per-side flat price), adds sticker pricing via `DrawTool.countStickerImage()`, and after a 500ms delay dispatches `ProductActions.updatePrice(p)`. Also calls `DrawTool.checkLockRevertSide` when in carpet mode.
- `removeTemplate(id)` — shows a `swal` confirm dialog; on confirm calls the `removeTemplate` API and dispatches `ProductActions.removeTemplate(id)`.
- `checkLoadModel=()=>` — if currently in `ACRYLIC_LASER` mode, fetches `getModelCuttingLaser()` and dispatches `updateModelShapesLaser(data)`.
- `checkAndClearStorage(key_local)` — clears `localStorage[key_local]` and the `STORAGE_TIME_ALL_PRODUCTS` timestamp if more than `MINUTES_SET` (20 min) has elapsed since the stored timestamp (or on any parse error). Called once from `componentDidMount` for the `AllProducts` cache.
- `loadPanelCategory()` — calls `filterCategory(1)` and shows the category picker container; invoked when `query.action === 'category'`.
- `loadData()` — fires off a batch of one-shot API calls (favorites, stickers categories, stamps categories/favorites/loves, font categories) and dispatches their results.
- `loadModelControl=()=>` — fetches 3D shapes, brushes, laser shapes, and colors, dispatching each into Redux (colors are additionally cached onto `DrawTool.colors` as raw hex/values).
- `loadFonts()` — the font bootstrap routine: fetches the main font list (`getFonts`), Japanese-title-filtered subset (`loadFontsFilter`), English fonts (split into print vs. embroidery subsets), then for several other font families (`WD`, `DGT`, `BC`, `EuL`, Gothic, Tomorrow-Morning) it first checks a localStorage cache and only calls the corresponding API (`getFontsWD`, `getFontsDGT`, etc.) if the cache is empty, writing results back to localStorage; also fetches Japanese fonts (`getFontsJP`) split into print/embroidery subsets.
- `loadJSON()` — debug helper: reads the value of a `#json` textarea and calls `DrawTool.sides.selected.fromJSON(json)` (its trigger UI is commented out in render).
- `filterLaserUser=()=>` — if there's a `currentCategory`, searches `categories_filter` for a laser (`CategoryStyle.id == "6"`) entry matching it and navigates there via `goToCategory`; otherwise searches the full `categories` list for the first laser entry. Used after switching into the laser master-category (`type==6`) in `filterCategory`.
- `filterCategory(type, tamp)` — the master "select category-style" handler (e.g., switching between T-shirts / bags / cases / laser / paper / 3D, etc.). Special-cases `type==76` as an external redirect to `URL_ORDERMADE`. Sets the active drawing tool to `"sides"`, updates sort value, dispatches `updateTypeProducts`/`updateStyleCategoryID`, builds a filtered/sorted `cate_sorted` category list and dispatches `loadCategoriesFilter`. For laser (`type==6`) schedules `filterLaserUser()`; toggles `set3DTripo` for `type==12`. Manages several localStorage caches with 24h/20min expiry windows (`setupTime`, `AllProducts`, key filters, fonts) before fetching `getAllProductsByMasterCategory(type)` (or reusing the cached copy) and dispatching `getAllProductByMasterCategory`. Also fires a battery of `getAllKeyFilterMaker/ByKey('type'|'print'|'material'|'scene')/Size/Colors/Conditions` calls that populate the various `objXxxFilter` Redux slices used later by `render()`'s filtering pipeline.
- `fillterFontsByCategory(name)` — filters `allFontsList` by `DrawerFontCategory.title === name` (and, if `is_embroidery`, additionally requires non-zero `is_embroidery`), updates `cate_font_active` state, dispatches `selectTextCategoriesFonts(name)` and `loadFontsFilter(fontList)`.
- `applyFont(font)` — hides the font picker, looks up the font record by name, dispatches `updateFontsByName`, sets `is_font_loader: true`, waits on `document.fonts.ready` then `FontDetect.onFontLoaded(font, ...)` to dispatch `selectTextFont(font)`, clear `fabric.charWidthsCache[font]`, re-measure any canvas text objects using that font (`_initDimensions` + `renderAll` + `triggerDrawUpdate('update_item')`), and finally clears `is_font_loader`. Has a 60s timeout fallback that also clears the loader flag.
- `filterCategoryColer(type)` — near-duplicate of part of `filterCategory`: filters `categories` by style id, updates `category_active` state, shows the type container, hides the category container, dispatches `loadCategoriesFilter`. (Appears to be an older/simplified variant; not obviously called elsewhere in the file, but kept as a class method.)
- `filterMaker(maker)` — sets `setFilter(true)`, updates `maker_active` state, and either reloads all products for the current category (`maker === '全て'`) or calls `filterProductByMaker(maker, id)`, dispatching `loadProducts` and clearing the filter flag.
- `onFilterByKey(key)` — sets `keyword_filter_id` state; for `key === 'all-products'` reloads the full category product list; for `key === 'all'` (with a `current_Category_id`) reloads that category's products and shows the type container; otherwise calls `getProductBykeyFilter(key, current_Category_id)`, falling back to a "virtual" empty category (id `16`) if no products match.
- `goTolinkUrl(id)` — resolves `id` (one of the `TYPE_CATEGORY_EXTENT` ids) to an external URL, appends the current query string, calls `disableOnBeforeUnload()`, prevents the default event, and navigates via `window.location = linkAcess`.
- `changeComponent()` — toggles between the 2D editor and the 3D/laser preview. Finalizes the active brush, hides Text2Image, deselects the current item; if currently in embroidery mode, exits it and re-saves the side name; then either toggles `is_3d_preview_laser` (with process-bar animation) for laser products, or toggles `is_3d_preview` for normal products.
- `isDisabledNextDesign()` / `isDisabledPreDesign()` — pure helpers that check whether `designSelected` is at the last/first index of `templateUsers.itemsDetail`, used to hide the forward/back buttons in the saved-design modal.
- `onStepExit()` — empty stub, passed as intro.js's `onExit`.
- `onStepComplete()` — sets the `mac_browser` cookie to `"1"` so the intro walkthrough won't show again (checked by `checkFirstLanding`).
- `onStepHintChange(event)` — updates a manually-injected `<p class="strong-step">` element inside the intro.js tooltip buttons row to show `"{step+1}/2"`.
- `selectSideOnchange=(location)=>` — toggles the design modal off/on (forcing a re-render) and sets `designSelected.locationSelect = location`, then dispatches `selectDesignToModal(designSelected)`.
- `selectDesignToModalNext=(val)=>` — advances/retreats the currently previewed saved design by `val` within `templateUsers.itemsDetail`, auto-selecting the first side (`locationSelect`) that has preview art, and dispatches `selectDesignToModal`.
- `setEmbroidery=(name, setEmbroider)=>` — deselects/renders the canvas, calls `DrawTool.sides.selected.saveNameSide(name)`, and sets the active tool to `'embroider'` or `'_embroider'` depending on `setEmbroider`.
- `selectSideAndSetDessign=()=>` — polling-friendly helper (called from a `setInterval` in the "apply saved design" modal flow) that, if no side is already mid-apply, finds a side with a pending `wattingAddDesign`, switches to it (handling embroidery zoom-out/back if needed), sets the process bar, and after delays dispatches `applyTemplateUser` then hides the modal; returns `false` while any side still has a pending design, `true` (implicitly, when the initial branch is skipped) once all are applied — used as the interval's stop condition.
- `ifLayersEmpty()` — returns `true` only if every `DrawTool` side is empty (no layers) after calling `checkEmpty()` on each; used to gate the "remove background" flow.
- `onGetImfomations()` — the "remove background" (`URL_REMOVE_BG`) launcher: if the design is empty and the tool isn't `'brush'`, redirects immediately with just product/color query params; otherwise rasterizes text (`DrawTool.rasterizeText`), strips unused (`useItemData === false`) items from every side's Fabric objects and item collection, serializes each side to JSON, calls `saveProduct(...)` with admin/order/design metadata, and finally redirects to the remove-bg tool URL with up to 4 image paths appended.
- `onPopup()` — shows a `sww.fire` promotional dialog for the "free design" service; on confirm calls `onGetImfomations()`.
- `onProductDetail()` — shows a `sww.fire` dialog rendering `product.html_tool` (raw HTML from the product record) with a "create with this item" confirm button (the `.then` handler that would act on confirmation is commented out).
- `on2DPreviewDetail= async ()=>` — shows a sweetalert loading spinner, grabs a transparent preview image from the current side's `FabricBorder` region (`getImagePreviewTransparentAdujst`), converts it via `MethodImageProcessing.updateImageTo2DPreview`, and calls `show2DPreviewDetail(imageData, mobile)`; hides the spinner on error.
- `theSizeToChange=(renderAgain=false)=>` — core "apply carpet length" routine. Syncs `DrawTool.sides.selected.settingCarpetLength` from state; if `renderAgain`, returns early (used just to persist the setting without recomputing the preview). Otherwise resets the process bar, computes new virtual border offsets from `settingCarpetLength`, calls `DrawTool.changeBorderVirtualCarpet`, `coppyAllDataCompoment()`, `checkCarpetLockBackAll()`, `changeCarpetPullingBorderPreview()`, captures preview images of side 1 (and side 2 if present) via `getImagePreviewTransparentAdujst`, restores the originally-selected side, updates the process bar, calls `DrawTool.addImageCanvasToDesign()` and triggers a `history:update`, then shows a `sww.fire` dialog rendering the carpet-length result HTML (via `MethodImageProcessing.loadDialogueHTMLCarpet`) with the captured preview images drawn onto canvases in `onBeforeOpen`.
- `theSizeToChangeCarpet=()=>` — sets the active tool to `'pointer'`, deselects on iOS, then after 200ms re-selects the current side and calls `theSizeToChange()` (the "repeat design" button handler).
- `isNumeric=(value)=>` — regex test for a signed integer string.
- `handleChangeValue = (e, action)=>` — handles the carpet "display range" scroll input for three actions: `'input'` (typed value, clamped to max), `'add'`/`'minus'` (increment/decrement by `e`), each time recomputing `_carpet_pulling_action.move`, shifting every non-locked item's `left` by the delta, updating `scrollCarpet`/`scrollCarpetMax` state, re-rendering the Fabric canvas, and scheduling `calcPrice()`. Shows `sww.fire` warnings and calls `mouseUpCarpet()` when limits are exceeded.
- `handleChangeLengCarpet = (e, action)=>` — analogous handler for the carpet "length" input (`'input'`/`'minus'`/`'add'`), clamped against `border_special[0].d_width[_load]`, updating `settingCarpetLength` state and scheduling `theSizeToChange(true)` + `calcPrice()`.
- `checkCarpetScreen=()=>` — returns the JSX for the carpet length/scroll-range control panel (only when `DrawTool.modeToolDraw === CARPET_PRODUCT`; otherwise returns `""`). Wires the +/- buttons to `onClickCarpet`/`mouseDownCarpet`/`mouseUpCarpet` and the text inputs to `handleChangeLengCarpet`/`handleChangeValue`, plus the "リピート" (repeat) button to `theSizeToChangeCarpet`.
- `setValueCarpetLocation=(value)=>` — clamps `value` into `[0, maxSize]`, recomputes `_carpet_pulling_action.move`, shifts item positions, updates `scrollCarpet(Max)` state, re-renders, and returns the clamped value.
- `onClickCarpet=(value_size, value_move)=>` — currently a no-op (both branches' bodies are commented out); kept as the onClick handler wired in `checkCarpetScreen`.
- `onChangeCarpetOption=()=>` — reads `this.scrollCarpetUpdate.value_size`/`value_move` (capped at ±20) and calls `handleChangeLengCarpet`/`handleChangeValue` accordingly; invoked repeatedly by the press-and-hold interval started in `mouseDownCarpet`.
- `mouseDownCarpet=(value_size, value_move)=>` — on press of a carpet +/- button, records the direction, clears any existing repeat interval, fires one immediate `onChangeCarpetOption()`, then starts a 200ms interval that doubles the step size each tick (acceleration) and calls `onChangeCarpetOption()` again.
- `mouseUpCarpet=()=>` — resets all `scrollCarpetUpdate` fields to 0 and clears the repeat interval.
- `addUniqueObjects(targetArray, sourceArray, key)` — pushes objects from `sourceArray` into `targetArray` (dedup by `Product[key]`), mutating and returning `targetArray`. Pure utility used by the render-time "sub-category" (hoodie type) filtering.
- `filterOtherParkerProducts(products)` — filters out products whose title contains any of several Japanese hoodie/sweatshirt/pullover/zip keywords (パーカー, スウェット, トレーナー, プル, ジップ). Declared but not observed to be called elsewhere in the file (appears to be a leftover/utility for the sub-category filter).
- `getDifference(array1, array2, key)` — symmetric-difference helper keyed by `Product[key]`, used to compute the "other hoodie types" bucket (`productByCon5`) in the render-time sub-category filter.
- `render()` — see Render Structure below.

**DrawTool event listeners registered in `componentWillMount`** (all closures over `dispatch`/`this`):
`history:update`, `carpet:update`, `object:selected`, `selection:cleared`, `object:rotating`, `object:delete_item`, `object:scaling`, `colorpicker:update`, `layers:update` (delegates to `calcPriceUpdate`), `figure3d:update` (delegates to `loadItem3D`), `control:mode` (toggles `preview_2d` state or calls `changeComponent`), `view3d:update` (loads a 3D buffer via `loader3D.getBuffer` into `info3Dsave` state).

### Lifecycle

- `componentWillMount()` — registers the custom Fabric.js corner-control icons (base64 PNGs for `mtr`/`tl`/`tr`/`br`/`bl`), wires the 12 `DrawTool.on(...)` listeners described above, then performs first-load routing: checks for a hostname-based redirect (`drawer.up-t.jp` → `up-t.jp`, `drawer-beta` → `beta.up-t.jp`) or a bare query string outside `mode_develop`/`session` (redirects to `/drawer/`), sets `DrawTool.query_string`/`query.setData`, persists the session if `query.session` is present, then fires `getUser`, `getAllPreviewColor`, `getCategoryStyles`, and `getCategories().then(...)` — inside that `.then` it branches on `query.yaman`/`lock_item` (product lock), `query.room_chat` (join/create a shared design room, with password-protected `sww.fire` prompt flow), `query.item_id`, `query.change_model_id`, `query.design_id`, `query.color_id` (including a special `IT3D00` 3D-tripo redirect), `query.model_id` (including `D3D001`), `query.category_id`, or falls back to `loadDefaultProduct()`. Also handles `query.master_category_style_id` (calls `filterCategory`) and `query.action === 'category'` (calls `loadPanelCategory`). Finally fetches `getAllKeyFilterPrice` and `getShapes3D`.
- `componentWillReceiveProps(nextProps)` — calls `checkFirstLanding()` every time; lazily registers `FontDetect.onFontLoaded` for every font in `nextProps.availableFonts` (once, guarded by `this.fontDetectInitialised`), re-measuring text objects using a newly-loaded font while the active tool is `'text'`. Dispatches `setLoadingCategories(false)` once categories arrive. Detects a product-id change to dispatch `checkProduct(true)`/`setLoadingColors(false)`. When `loading` transitions true→false, calls `checkLoadModel()` and (unless a `master_category_style_id` query param is present) fetches `getProductColors(nextProps.product.id)` to refresh available colors, handling `query.goto_category`. Similar refresh logic runs when `loading_process` or `loading_data` transition true→false. On the first `loading_data` completion, also triggers the one-time `loadData()`/`loadFonts()` bootstrap (guarded by `this.state.loadData`, mutated directly rather than via `setState`).
- `componentDidMount()` — clears stale `AllProducts` localStorage cache (`checkAndClearStorage`), loads favorite products, attaches a `document.body` `keydown` listener that handles arrow-key nudging in acrylic-laser/no-hole laser modes and the Delete key (removing the selected Fabric object, with special handling for laser cutting-line items via `DeleteLineCut`, acrylic-laser "hold" bookkeeping via `emptyItemHoldLaser`/`loadNewBorderPedestal`, and image-upload capacity bookkeeping via `updateCapacityUploading`). Attaches a `window` `resize` listener (mobile-only) that, after the offset width actually changes, snapshots each side's canvas JSON/scale, switches to the `layers`/`pointer` tool, and — once resize events settle for 2s or 5s have elapsed since the last change — dispatches `reloadColor(colorSelected.id)` to force the canvas to re-render at the new size. Finally calls `loadModelControl()` and (guarded by `this.state.loadData`) the one-time `loadData()`/`loadFonts()` bootstrap.

No `componentDidUpdate`, `shouldComponentUpdate`, or `componentWillUnmount` are defined.

### Render Structure

`render()` first destructures a very large number of props and the relevant slice of `this.state`, then runs an extensive **client-side product filtering/sorting pipeline** before returning JSX:

1. Starts from `allProductByMasterCategory` (or `favoriteProducts` if `currentCategory === 'itemFavorite'`, or `products[currentCategory]` if present).
2. Strips products based on `userAccess.is_show_event_product`/`is_show_zozo_product` flags.
3. Sequentially narrows the array by every active filter prop: `objKeyFilter` (maker keywords), `objSexFilter` (gender/target), `objModelPhone`, `objSPCaseFilter`, `objPrintFilter`, `objSceneFilter`, `objMaterialArrFilter`, `objPriceFilter` (min/max), `objSizeFilter`, `objSideFilter`, `objColorFilter`, `objMaterialFilter`, `objValueFilter`, `objCategoryFilter`, and a more elaborate `objCategorySubFilter` block that special-cases hoodie/sweatshirt sub-types (パーカー/スウェット/プル/ジップ) using `addUniqueObjects`/`getDifference`.
4. Applies `arrStatusFilter` keyword filters (`recommend`, `sameday`, `cheap` — computed via average price across all products, `quality`, `stylish`, `stock10`, `embroider`).
5. Sorts by `valueProductSort` (`1`=price asc, `2`=price desc, `3`=monthly revenue desc, `4`=manual `order` asc).
6. De-duplicates by `Product.id`.

The JSX tree returned (`<div className="app">`) then lays out, in order:

- `Loader` (while `loading || loading_category`) and `ProcessBar` (while `loading_process`); a small `.options-cp` wrapper that shows another `Loader` while a font is loading.
- `Steps` (intro.js walkthrough), driven by `stepsEnabled`/`steps`/`initialStep`/`introOptions` state and the `onStepHintChange`/`onStepExit`/`onStepComplete` handlers.
- Two `MediaQuery`-gated headers: `Header` (desktop, ≥1080px) and `HeaderMobile` (mobile, ≤1079px), both passed `myDesign` (from `query.new`) and a `filterCategory` callback.
- `.app-container`: optionally `Design3D` (when `tripo3D_mode`); `Toolbar` (hidden during any 3D/laser preview); `.app-container-inner` containing:
  - Conditional laser-mode side panels: `LaserPedestal`/`LaserPedestalMobile` (acrylic laser, desktop/mobile), `LaserKeyHold` (laser), `LaserNoHold` (laser no-hole), `LaserSticker` (laser sticker) — selected by `DrawTool.modeToolDraw`.
  - A button cluster (`list-button-three-preview`) with an "アイテム情報" (item info) button (`onProductDetail`), a conditional "2Dプレビュー" button (`on2DPreviewDetail`, shown when `preview_2d` state is true), and a toggle button that either returns to the editor (`changeComponent`, during 3D/laser preview) or opens the 3D preview (shown when a 3D shape is selected or the product is a laser product).
  - A lamp icon (`onPopup`, the free-design promo) shown when not in `tripo3D_mode`.
  - `preview3D` — `Preview3DComponent` (fed `this.state.info3Dsave.data`) or `Preview3DLaser`, depending on `is_3d_preview`/`is_3d_preview_laser`.
  - `FooterChat` when a shared-room design session is active (`infoRoomDesign.room_info`).
  - The carpet control panel (`this.checkCarpetScreen()`).
  - `DrawToolComponent` (the actual canvas/editor surface), passed `colors`.
  - `Options` (the right-hand tool options panel), hidden while `loading`.
- Two parallel `MediaQuery` blocks (desktop / mobile) rendering the overlay pickers, each duplicated for the two breakpoints with different `close`/`back` wiring:
  - `ProductLoad` for `loadProductContainer` (saved designs / templates), populating `ProductCard`s from `templates`.
  - `ProductLoad` or `ProductLaserLoad` (chosen via `this.lvcLaserAccess`) for `loadProductCatContainer` (top-level category picker), populating `ProductCard`/`ProductLazeCard`s from `categories_filter`, with clicks routed through `goToCategory` or `goTolinkUrl` depending on `TYPE_CATEGORY_EXTENT` membership.
  - `ProductLoad` or `ProductLaserLoad` for `loadProductTypeContainer` (product list within a category), rendering the filtered/sorted `arr` (and, on mobile or laser mode, `ProductCardFilter`/extra `selectItemCore` list) with clicks calling `loadProduct`.
  - `FontLoad` for `loadFontsCatContainer`, populating `FontCard`s from `font_filter`, wired to `applyFont`/`fillterFontsByCategory`.
- `MobileNavigation` (bottom nav for mobile).
- A hidden `#load-all-font` div (font preloading anchor) and commented-out debug `loadJSON` UI.
- `Text2Image`, shown when `isText2Img` (prop) is true.
- The saved-design "apply to side" modal (`#modalDesigns`), shown when `showModalDesign && designSelected`: shows the currently selected design preview (one of 4 side previews per `designSelected.locationSelect`), back/forward buttons (`selectDesignToModalNext`, disabled via `isDisabledPreDesign`/`isDisabledNextDesign`), per-side selector chips (`selectSideOnchange`), and an "適用する" (Apply) button whose `onClick` fetches `getDesignUser(designSelected.image_id)`, matches returned side JSON onto `DrawTool.sides._collection` (with carpet-mode side-id remapping), sets `wattingAddDesign` on each matching side, then polls (`setInterval`) `selectSideAndSetDessign()` until all sides have consumed their pending design or a 2-minute timeout/mismatch triggers an error `sww.fire`.

### Notable behaviors / caveats worth flagging

- Two names (`DrawToolActions` and `actions`) refer to the exact same action-creator module and are used interchangeably, sometimes within the same method.
- Direct mutation of `this.state.loadData`, `this.state.loadedFonts`/`loadedFont` (in `componentWillReceiveProps`) bypasses `setState`, relying on a later `forceUpdate()` or unrelated re-render to reflect the change — not idiomatic React state usage.
- `loadProductClose()` references a bare `dispatch` identifier that is not imported or destructured in that method's scope; calling it as written would throw a `ReferenceError` unless a global/module `dispatch` exists elsewhere (none is visible in this file).
- Heavy use of `localStorage`/`sessionStorage` (`AllProducts`, `STORAGE_TIME_ALL_PRODUCTS`, `setupTime`, per-font-family caches, `foodprint`) as ad hoc client caches with manual expiry logic duplicated across `checkAndClearStorage` and `filterCategory`.

---

# Part 4 — Options Container (tool side-panel)

## src/containers/Options/index.jsx

### Purpose

`Options` is the large side-panel container component that renders and coordinates every configuration panel of the UP-T design tool: side/color selection, brushes, text, stickers, stamps (site stamps and "love" stamps), shapes, image-color removal, layers, file upload, grid/guides, laser/acrylic cutting tools, embroidery, crop-path drawing, and template/design browsing. It is driven by `activeTool` (redux state) via a large `switch` in `render()`, which decides which panel `content` to show, and it also renders a floating "edit" toolbar (`change_object`) with rotate/scale/flip/copy-paste/move/delete controls for the currently selected canvas item.

Beyond pure rendering, `Options` is the primary orchestrator of interaction between the React/Redux layer and the imperative `DrawTool`/Fabric.js canvas engine: it wires DOM/window event listeners for dragging, rotating and scaling items; performs file upload and image-processing workflows (upload, PDF/PSD conversion, background removal, image recoloring); implements the laser/acrylic "die-cut" pipeline (generating cut paths, pedestals/stands, keyholes from canvas pixel data via `ImageCuttingLaser`/`MethodImageProcessing`); manages embroidery area selection flows using `swal`/`sweetalert2` dialogs; and manages font loading/detection via `FontDetect`. It is a single very large class component (no hooks) connected to Redux via `connect(mapStateToProps)`.

### Imports

- **React/Redux**: `React, { Component }` from React 15 (uses `React.PropTypes`), `connect` from `react-redux`.
- **Scrolling**: `Scrollbars` from `react-custom-scrollbars`, `Scrollbar` from `react-smooth-scrollbar`.
- **Dialogs**: `swal` (SweetAlert), `sww`/`sw2` (SweetAlert2), `withReactContent` wrapping sw2 into `SwalDialog` for JSX-based modal content.
- **Child/UI components**: `Layers`, `BoderPaths` (border-paths), `Sticker`, `Button`, `Upload`, `DropFiles`, `DropDownM` (drop-down-material), `ColorPicker`, `Icon`, `AddTextForm`, `TextOptions`, `Loader`.
- **Canvas engine**: `DrawTool` (from `../../draw-tool/drawtool`) — the imperative Fabric.js-based drawing/side/layer manager used throughout for canvas manipulation, mode flags (`modeSetup`, `modeToolDraw`), event bus (`on`/`trigger`/`triggerDrawUpdate`), and history.
- **Drag**: `Draggable` from `react-draggable` (used for the floating edit toolbar).
- **API/extras**: `uploadByString`, `uploadByStringWithProgress`, `uploadPdfWithProgress`, `uploadByLinkBG`, `getStampsImage`, `getStampsLoveImage`, `getStickers`, `getShapes`, `changeColorImg` from `../../api/extras`.
- **Redux actions**: `* as actions` and `* as DrawToolActions` (both from `../../actions/draw-tool`, imported twice under different aliases), `* as ProductActions` from `../../actions/product`.
- **Misc utils**: `* as Setting` (constants/setting), `FontDetect` (font-loading detection utility), `WEBHOST` constant, `ImageCuttingLaser` and `MethodImageProcessing` (pixel-level image processing utilities used for laser cut-path/keyhole generation).

### State

- `availableFonts` (array) — the current list of font names/objects available for the active font category/language; recomputed in `componentWillReceiveProps`.
- `loadedFonts` (object) — map of font name → boolean, tracking which fonts have finished loading via `FontDetect`.
- `loadedFont` (string|null) — most recently loaded font while text tool is active (used to refresh `AddTextForm`).
- `fontsStyles` (object) — map of font name → `{bold_allowed, italic_allowed}` populated from `allFonts` prop.
- `mobile` (bool) — result of `matchMedia('(max-width: 1079px)')`, used throughout render to branch mobile vs desktop layout.
- `dropzoneActive` (bool) — whether a drag-and-drop zone is active (used by `onDragLeave`).
- `tool_display` (string) — which sub-panel of the floating edit toolbar is expanded (`'edit_tool'`, `'text_effect'`, `'text_spacing'`, or `''`).
- `moving` (bool) — true while the title-header of the floating toolbar is being pressed/dragged (rotation handle interaction).
- `is_tab_color` (bool) — toggles between "edit" and "color" tab in the floating edit toolbar (`changePanel`).
- `radius` (number, default 50) — unused/legacy value carried in state.
- `grid_enable` (bool) — whether the guide grid overlay is currently visible.
- `loopConditions` (bool) — flag used to stop the repeat/hold loop (`changeValue`) e.g. when acrylic/laser bounds are hit.
- `is_fontsTS` (bool) — whether the current font category is an embroidery/"TS" font set (affects bold/italic allowance checks).
- `is_font_loader` (bool) — true while a font is being fetched/applied; shows `<Loader/>` in render.
- `enableCoppy` (bool) — whether an item is currently "copied" (clipboard has data), toggles copy button highlight.
- `ifLayersEmpty` (bool) — legacy/duplicate name also used as an instance method; state flag about whether the laser layer preview image should be considered empty.
- `imageLayerSide` (string) — data-URL/base64 preview image representing the current side's cut-mask, used as an icon for "cut to design" buttons.
- `showListOptionDieCut` / `showListActionDieCut` (bool) — expand/collapse state for the two collapsible sections of the die-cut panel (`DieCut()`).
- `is_ios` (bool) — detected from `navigator.userAgent` in `componentDidMount`; used to special-case canvas deactivation behavior on iOS.
- `thicknessDieCut` (number, default 3) — laser cut thickness value bound to (currently commented-out) thickness input; also sets `DrawTool.mmLaser_Limmit`.
- `showStamsTab` (number, default 1) — active tab (1=new, 2=popular, 3=favorite) for stamp panels.
- `callStampAPI` (bool) — whether the stamp list has finished loading (controls "Loading Stamps..." message).
- `stampImagesState` / `stampImageLovesState` (array) — sorted/cached stamp lists for the "stamps_image"/"stamps_love" panels.
- `stampFavoriteActive` (bool) — whether the "favorite" stamp sub-view is showing.
- `stateIDStampCate` (string) — currently selected stamp category id (or `'fa'` for favorites).
- `itemIDStampSelect` (string) — id of the last stamp clicked/inserted (used to highlight selection).
- `showOptionsSp` (bool) — secondary "show options" flag used for special (locked) UI modes, set via `showOptionsLock`.

### Non-state instance fields (set in constructor, not part of `this.state`)

- `showOptions` (bool) — whether the top options panel is expanded; mutated directly + `forceUpdate()` rather than via `setState` for perf.
- `lastState` — last seen `activeTool`, used in `componentDidUpdate` to detect tool changes.
- `fontDetectInitialised` (bool) — guards one-time font detection setup.
- `idImageDetect` (number) — counter used to generate unique upload image ids.
- `enableEditFont` (bool) — guards re-entrant font selection in `checkFontInsall`.
- `loadingNomalFont` (bool) — guards one-time default font load for non-embroidery mode.
- `dataCoppy` (object|null) — the serialized (`toJSON`) copy of the currently "copied" canvas item.
- `listDataSrcTocheck` (array) — cache of `{idBack, src}` pairs used to patch pasted-item image URLs once background upload completes.
- `beingUploadImage` (bool) — guards concurrent crop/upload flows.
- `timeSaveLaser` (number/timestamp) — throttling timestamp for laser minimum-size warning.
- `infoDraggable` (object `{x,y,enable,time}`) — tracks drag state of the floating toolbar to suppress accidental rotate/scale clicks while dragging.
- `size_option` (number) — unused/legacy counter.
- `pedestalOption` (object|null) — currently selected laser pedestal/stand model, used by `laserPadSelect`.
- `showOptionsLock` (object `{enable, mode, extends}`) — lock configuration restricting which `activeTool` values keep the options panel visible (used for laser UI modes).
- `id_product` (string|null) — last-seen product id, used to detect product changes in `componentWillReceiveProps`.
- `checkEventDuplicate` (object) — unused/legacy dedupe map.
- `textNoteInbox` / `contentOptions` — DOM refs captured via `ref` callbacks in `render_textnode`/`render_content_with_textnode`, used by `update_textnode_position`.

### Props

Declared via `static propTypes` and/or consumed from `mapStateToProps` (redux `connect`). Key props actually used in the component:

- `dispatch` (func) — redux dispatch, used everywhere to fire `actions`/`DrawToolActions`/`ProductActions`.
- `activeTool` (string) — currently selected tool/panel key; drives the render `switch`.
- `activeBrush`, `availableBrushes` (string/array) — brush selection UI (brush panel).
- `brushOptions` (object) — `{color, width}` for the brush panel.
- `textOptions` (object) — current text style (`font`, `align`, `vertical`, `bold`, `italic`, size etc.).
- `availableFonts`, `availableFontsTitle`, `availableFontsJP`, `availableFontsEN`, `availableFontsJPEM`, `availableFontsENEM`, plus many more `availableFontsXXX` variants (`WD`, `DGT`, `BC`, `EUL`, `Gothic`, `ToMo`, `TSC1`–`TSC10`) — per-category/per-language font lists pulled from redux and mapped into `state.availableFonts` depending on `categoriesFontsOptions`/embroidery mode.
- `availableFontsCategories` (array) — list of font category names for the category dropdown.
- `allFonts` (array) — full font metadata list, used to resolve font objects by name and populate `fontsStyles`.
- `categoriesFontsOptions` (object) — currently selected font category (`{title}`).
- `layers` (object) — per-side layer lists, keyed by side title, passed to `Layers` component.
- `side` (object) — currently selected product side (`content` JSON string with `id`).
- `shapeColor` (string) — currently selected shape fill color.
- `text`, `textEl` (string/object) — current text value/selected text element, used by `AddTextForm`.
- `stickersCat`, `stickers` (array) — sticker categories/list.
- `stampsImageCat`, `stampsImage`, `stampFavorite` (array) — "stamp" category/list/favorites.
- `stampsLoveCat`, `stampsLoves`, `stampLovesFavorite` (array) — "love stamp" equivalents.
- `shapesCat`, `shapes`, `shapesLaser`, `shapesModelLaser`, `shapes3D` (array) — shape categories/lists, including laser-specific shapes and pedestal models.
- `colorPicker` (bool), `colorPickerColor` (string) — color-picker tool state for "removeColor" panel.
- `selected` (object|null) — currently selected canvas item (redux mirror of `DrawTool` selection); presence toggles many UI branches.
- `colors`, `colorSelected` (array/object) — product color list and current selection.
- `loading_colors` (bool) — whether colors are still loading.
- `file_upload` (array) — list of filenames already queued/uploaded (dedupe guard).
- `capacity_uploading` (number) — running total of uploaded bytes, checked against a 30MB cap.
- `product` (object), `categoryStyle` (object) — active product/category metadata (used for tool notes, category checks like `CATEGORY_MOBILE`/`CATEGORY_FLAG`).
- `degree_rotate`, `scale`, `text_spacing` (numbers) — current transform values for the selected item, reflected into DOM inputs.
- `colors_fill`, `colors_Image_fill` (arrays) — fill colors of the selected item/image, rendered as a color palette.
- `transform` (string) — current CSS transform of the floating toolbar (`block-options`), used for rotation-handle geometry.
- `grid_rows`, `grid_cols` — grid configuration (not heavily used directly in this file beyond being passed through).
- `embroider_able` (bool), `is_embroidery` (bool) — whether embroidery is available/active for the current product/item.
- `fontsTS` — embroidery font-set flag.
- `is_image` (bool), `image_upload` (array), `image_uuid` (string), `image_url_re` (string) — describe the selected image item and whether its background-processed URL/upload is complete (drives loading spinners).
- `templateUsersCategory`, `templateCategorySelected`, `templateUsers`, `designSelected` — "design" (template) browsing panel data.
- `is_createPedestalLaser` (object `{active, data}`) — laser pedestal creation state.
- `is_3d_preview`, `is_3d_preview_laser` (bool) — when true, suppress the options/edit panels (3D preview mode).
- `user` (object|null) — current logged-in user, gates favorite-stamp messaging.
- `tripo3D_mode` (bool) — when true, `render()` returns `null` entirely.
- `item_brush` (bool) — whether selected item is a brush stroke (hides some image-only controls).

### Methods

Instance methods defined on the `Options` class (constructor-bound, class-field arrow functions, and prototype methods are all listed together in file order):

- `constructor(props)` — initializes `state` and instance fields (see State section), binds several handler methods explicitly.
- `reloadActiveLayer(value)` — if the current side's items form a closed laser shape (`checkLaser()`), dispatches `setActiveTool('laser')`.
- `setShowStamTab(id)` — sorts `props.stampsImage` by `created_at` (id 1) or `number_of_uses` (id 2) and stores the result plus the active tab id in state.
- `setShowStamLoveTab(id)` — same as above but for `props.stampsLoves` / `stampImageLovesState`.
- `actionStamp()` — dispatches `DrawToolActions.setActiveTool('pointer')` (used to "close" the favorites stamp view).
- `rotateObject(degree)` — directly sets `transform: rotate(...)` CSS on the `#degree` DOM node and the numeric value into `#degree-value`.
- `checkEmbroiderAreaSetup()` — builds a `SwalDialog` (sweetalert2-react-content) whose HTML lists each side's `border_special` embroidery areas as buttons; clicking one dispatches `ProductActions.selectEmbroiderArea`, calls `DrawTool.sides.select`, and `this.setEmbroidery(...)`.
- `onSettupEmbroide()` — dispatches to either `checkEmbroiderAreaSetup()` or `onSetEmbroiderArea()` depending on `DrawTool.modeToolDraw`.
- `onSetEmbroiderArea()` — shows a `swal` with embroidery placement buttons (left/right chest, sleeves, neck); the `.then` handler dispatches `ProductActions.selectEmbroiderArea`, sets `DrawTool.modeEmbroider`, selects the side, and calls `setEmbroidery`/`DrawTool.setEmbroidery`.
- `scaleObject()` — reads the selected item's `scaleX` (branching by item type), computes a percentage relative to `item.real_scale`, dispatches `DrawToolActions.updateScale`, and writes the percent into `#scale-value` (adjusting text-align based on magnitude).
- `resetModeNomal` (class field) — if in `ACRYLIC_LASER` mode, triggers `DrawTool.trigger('update:mode', {mode: 'laser'})` to reset mode.
- `addEvent()` — wires up all core DOM listeners: touch/mouse down on `#point` and `#title-header` (rotation drag start/stop, sets `moving` state), `mousemove`/`touchmove` on `#root` (computes rotation angle from pointer position relative to the rotation handle center, calls `update_rotate`, checks acrylic/laser bounds and calls `breakLoop()` if exceeded, triggers `DrawTool.triggerDrawUpdate('item')`), plus `DrawTool.on('item:unselect', ...)` and `DrawTool.on('item:option', ...)` — the latter is a large dispatcher handling `finish_acrylic`, `uploadbg`, `upload_file`, `select_pedestal`, `check_pedestal`, `check_pedestal_set`, `check_pedestal_mobile`, `clear_laser_cuting`, `clear_laser_border`, `clear_laser_pedestal`, `update_print_pedestal`, `finish_diecut_info`, and `check_all_pedestal_save` modes, routing to the corresponding instance methods.
- `resetModePrint` (class field) — calls `DrawTool.setLayerSetup(-1)`, deselects/locks all non-print items on the current side, re-renders, and disables drawing mode.
- `onSelectOptionLaser(select)` — looks up a laser pedestal model by id from `shapesModelLaser`, computes default placement (from an existing pedestal or defaults), dispatches `actions.insertPedestalLaser`, sets `DrawTool.drawModeIntervention`, and dispatches `actions.setPedestalLaser` with the accumulated pedestal data array.
- `checkCreatePedestalLaser` (class field, `enable`) — toggles pedestal-creation mode on/off: on enable, sets `drawModeIntervention` and dispatches `setPedestalLaser({active:true,...})`; on disable, calls `laserPadSelect()`, `DrawTool.loadNewBorderPedestal()`, resets intervention, and dispatches `setPedestalLaser({active:false, data:null})`; always calls `resetModeNomal()`.
- `getLaserPathSelectOption` (class field, `sample_option`) — finds a child "mode 2" pedestal-path option whose `father_id` matches `sample_option.id`, computes its default placement from `DrawTool.sides.selected.pedestal.position_stand`, and returns it.
- `laserPadSelect` (class field) — on iOS, discards active object first; if `this.pedestalOption` is set, resolves its path option via `getLaserPathSelectOption` and dispatches `actions.insertPedestalLaser`.
- `addMultipleEvent(dom, events, handler)` — utility that attaches `handler` for each event name in `events` array to `dom`; throws if `events` isn't an array.
- `addEventKeyBoard(props)` — adds window-level `keydown` (arrow keys call `movingObject`), `keyup` (snaps item to canvas center if within 10px), `mouseup`/`touchend` (removes the canvas center-point guide), and `mousedown`/`touchstart` (adds a center-point guide unless the item is text or in `CARPET_PRODUCT` mode) listeners.
- `addGrid(type)` — switches on `'grid'|'hidden'|'border'` to show/hide the `FabricBorder` and/or `grid_object`, updates `state.grid_enable`, and re-renders the canvas.
- `saveBorder()` — calls `DrawTool.saveBorder()` then dispatches `actions.setActiveTool('layer')`.
- `breakLoop()` (async) — clears the `loop` timeout, resets `timer`, sets `state.loopConditions = true` (awaited), and triggers `DrawTool.triggerDrawUpdate('item')`; used to stop the press-and-hold repeat loop.
- `changeDiameter()` — reads `#change-diameter` input value, sets it as the selected item's `diameter` (unflips it), updates state, re-renders canvas.
- `channgeImageCurent` (class field, `url`) — swaps the selected item's image source: clears `idBack`, sets `realSrcImage`/`currentSrc`, clears then reapplies filters (recomputing crop-path filter distances proportionally to the new image size) once the new image loads via `fabric.util.loadImage`, then dispatches `updateProcessBar(0.99)`/`setLoadingProcess(false)`.
- `onChangeColorIMG()` — dispatches loading true, calls API `changeColorImg(srcImg)` and on success calls `channgeImageCurent(url)`; on error shows a `sww` error dialog and clears loading.
- `changeRotate(type)` — `'input'|'reduce'|'increase'` — reads/derives a new degree and calls `update_rotate`, then `DrawTool.triggerDrawUpdate('item')`.
- `changeScale(type)` — `'input'|'reduce'|'increase'` — similarly calls `update_scale`.
- `changeTextSpacing(type)` — `'reset'|'reduce'|'increase'` — calls `update_text_spacing` with a new value.
- `update_text_spacing(value)` — dispatches `DrawToolActions.updateTextSpacing(value)` and calls `checkItemChangeAction` on the selected item.
- `onMouseDownHandle(o, t)` (async) — guards against drag conflicts, resets `loopConditions` to false, awaits `changeValue(o, t)`, then triggers a draw update; entry point for press-and-hold rotate/scale/move/spacing buttons.
- `changeValue(option, type)` — recursive `setTimeout`-based repeat loop (accelerating via decreasing `timer`) that calls the matching change method (`changeRotate`/`changeScale`/`movingObject`/`changeTextSpacing`) repeatedly while held, and calls `breakLoop()` if an acrylic/laser boundary check fails.
- `componentDidMount()` — detects iOS, sets `showOptionsLock` based on whether `DrawTool.modeToolDraw` is a laser UI mode (restricting to `sides`, `colors`, `pointer`, `shapes`, etc.), calls `addEvent()` and `update_textnode_position()`.
- `componentDidUpdate()` — calls `update_textnode_position()`; if `activeTool` changed since last render, re-evaluates `showOptions`/`showOptionsSp` against `showOptionsLock`, then `forceUpdate()`.
- `componentWillReceiveProps(nextProps)` (async) — a large multi-purpose handler: (1) re-derives `showOptionsLock` when the product id changes; (2) populates `state.fontsStyles` from `allFonts` once; (3) on first receipt of `availableFonts`, registers `FontDetect.onFontLoaded` callbacks per font to mark them loaded, refresh dependent text objects, and force update; (4) selects the correct `availableFonts` state array from one of ~18 possible `availableFontsXXX` props based on `categoriesFontsOptions.title` and whether embroidery mode is active; (5) forces the text font to the first Japanese font option if the current font isn't in the new JP list; (6) disables bold/italic if the newly selected embroidery font doesn't support them; (7) on item selection, dispatches `updateDegree`, calls `addEventKeyBoard`, and syncs the rotate/scale DOM via `rotateObject`/`scaleObject`.
- `changePanel()` — toggles `state.is_tab_color` (edit vs. color tab in the floating toolbar).
- `changeColorSVG()` — legacy jQuery call setting sticker SVG fill color (references an undefined `shapeColor` variable — appears to be dead/broken code).
- `changeImageUrlCurrentSide` (class field, `Side,indexItem,url,idImageUploadID`) — swaps a specific side/item's image element `src` and `realSrcImage`, reapplies filters, dispatches `updateImageupload`, and later calls `DrawTool.upDateImageUpload`.
- `checkDataUploadAgain` (class field, `idImageUpload,url`) — scans all sides/items for items whose `idBack` matches the uploaded image id and patches `realSrcImage`, then triggers `DrawTool.triggerDrawUpdate('image_item', ...)` if any were updated.
- `changeImageUrlFindInSide` (class field, `idImageUpload,url`) — iterates all sides/items to replace matching background-uploaded image URLs (with filter save/restore), dispatches `updateImageupload`, and schedules three staggered `checkDataUploadAgain` retries (500ms/1500ms/3000ms) as an eventual-consistency safeguard, finally calling `DrawTool.upDateImageUpload`.
- `checkImageDataToChangeUrl` (class field, `that_1,idImageUpload,url`) — updates matching items' `realSrcImage`, records the mapping in `listDataSrcTocheck` and `dataCoppy` (for paste), and either preloads the image before calling `changeImageUrlFindInSide` or calls it immediately, dispatching `setLoading(false)`/`triggerDrawUpdate('image_item', ...)`.
