- `accessViewCheckImage` (class field, `idBack,url`) — rewrites matching `src`/`realSrcImage` fields inside `DrawTool.history.history` snapshots so undo/redo reflects the final uploaded URL.
- `fileUpload(file)` — the main upload pipeline: validates via `checkItemCanAddValue`, category restriction (500), dedupe by filename, embroidery-mode block, MIME/extension checks (jpeg/png/gif/pdf/postscript/psd), size checks (20MB single, 30MB cumulative via `capacity_uploading`), then branches: PDF/PSD/GIF/PS go through `uploadPdfWithProgress` → `addImageWithSize`; other images go through either `uploadByStringWithProgress` (IE) or a base64 preview + async S3 upload via `uploadByString` with `addImageWithSizeID`, wiring in progress bar updates (`updateProcessBar`), capacity tracking (`updateCapacityUploading`), TAP/RIBBON post-processing (`tapOrRibbonProcessingColorData`), and rollback (`DrawTool.history.undo`) on error.
- `fileUploadBG()` — background-removal flow: validates category, resolves the selected item's real image URL (S3-hosted only), calls `uploadByLinkBG`, and on success calls `channgeImageCurent`; on failure shows a rate-limit or generic error `sww` dialog with links to the paid service.
- `fileUploadImageConvertSvg(file)` — near-duplicate of `fileUpload` specialized for laser/SVG conversion mode: after `addImageWithSize` resolves, if `DrawTool.checkProductLaser()` it sets the new item active and dispatches `actions.rimImageToSvg()`.
- `getStickers(id)` — fetches sticker list for a category and dispatches `actions.updateStickers`.
- `getStampsImage(id, type)` — for `'normal'`, fetches stamp images and stores them in state + dispatches `updateStampsImage`; for `'favorite'`, just flips `stampFavoriteActive`.
- `getStampsLove(id, type)` — same pattern for "love" stamps (`getStampsLoveImage`/`updateStampsLove`).
- `getShapes(id)` — fetches shapes and dispatches `updateShapes`.
- `getShapesLaser(id)` — fetches shapes and dispatches `updateShapesLaser` (laser shape variant).
- `getSideTitle()` — parses `props.side.content` JSON and returns its `id` (used as a `layers` lookup key).
- `flipObject(type)` — `'flipX'|'flipY'` toggles the selected item's flip flags and triggers a draw update.
- `copyItem()` — calls `DrawTool.sides.selected.copyItem()` (native DrawTool copy, distinct from the React-level copy/paste flow below).
- `copyItemSelect` (class field) — serializes the selected item (`toJSON`) into `this.dataCoppy`, augmenting with `DrawTool.getJsonDessignPadam()` fields, offsetting position slightly, and sets `state.enableCoppy = true`; if nothing is selected, dispatches `setActiveTool('layers')` and clears the copy buffer.
- `pasteItemSelect` (class field) — reconstructs the copied item into a new object on the current side via `FabricCanvas.toObject`/`loadFromJSON`, generating a fresh uuid, restoring any pending upload URL from `listDataSrcTocheck`; polls via `setInterval` until the new item count/timeout is reached, then reselects the new item, re-applies acrylic/carpet/laser bound checks, re-adds the grid if enabled, and triggers layer/history updates.
- `clipPathOver()` — dispatches `setActiveTool('changeToCropPathBrush')`, calls `DrawTool.sides.selected.setPathBorderItem()`, and triggers a `'side'` draw update (enters crop-path drawing mode).
- `finishPathOver()` — finalizes crop-path drawing: sets `beingUploadImage`, calls the free-drawing brush's `finisDrawAction()`, dispatches loading true, then (after a timeout) switches tool to `'layers'`, dispatches `updateCuttingImageBorder()`, clears loading, switches to `'uploadFile'`, and resets the floating toolbar transform.
- `onDrop(files)` — iterates dropped files and calls `fileUpload` for each.
- `onDragLeave()` — sets `dropzoneActive: false`.
- `checkItemChangeAction` (class field, `item`) — if in embroidery mode and the item's current placement no longer fits its embroidery-safe area, shows a `swal` warning and re-checks/re-renders.
- `movingObject(type)` — `'up'|'down'|'left'|'right'` nudges the selected item by 1px, re-renders, checks carpet-mode bounds, and calls `checkItemChangeAction`.
- `toggleTool(text)` — toggles `state.tool_display` between `''` and the given panel name (used for the floating toolbar's collapsible sub-sections).
- `addImageProcess` (class field, `img`) — returns a Promise that resolves once `img.onload` fires (sets `crossOrigin`), used to await image loads before canvas pixel operations.
- `checkImageCuttingLaser` (class field) — returns whether the current side already has an item flagged `imageCuttingLaser`.
- `showPopupImageCuttingLaser` (class field) — shows a `sww` warning "この機能は使用済みです" (feature already used) dialog.
- `ifLayersEmpty()` — computes/caches (into `state.imageLayerSide`) a downscaled black-silhouette preview image of the current design (used as an icon on "cut to design" buttons) by rendering the side to an offscreen canvas, inverting alpha via `ImageCuttingLaser.getImageDataInfoToPreview`, and returns whether the side is empty.
- `getImagePreviewData()` (async) — for laser/hole items, temporarily moves laser/hole items off-canvas, captures two preview images (with/without those items), computes fill/hole masks via `MethodImageProcessing`, draws detected cover regions back onto the composite canvas, and returns a merged `dataURL`.
- `getImagePreviewKeyhole()` (async) — similar two-pass capture but additionally iterates items individually to build a masked composite via `checkImageMask`, computes hole/cover polygon overlays with `detectAreaHoleAndFuirge`/`findCoverAreaHoleAndFuirge`, and returns the composite `dataURL` (used for the "keyhole" cut mode).
- `getImagePreviewDataAcryLic(mmLaser)` (async) — acrylic-pedestal variant of the preview generator: builds a masked composite per non-pedestal item via `checkImageMaskRegion`, applies pixel-array outset (`addPixelArrayImage`) by `mmLaser`, computes the shape's outer boundary points (`getPointCalculator`/`getNearPointInShape`/`fillYellowPointCenter`) and draws the extended pedestal-connection polygon; returns `{data, extend, out_distance}`.
- `getImagePreviewDataAcryLicStiker(mmLaser)` (async) — sticker-specific variant of the above (uses `getPointCalculatorSticker` instead of the pedestal point calculator); returns `{data, extend, out_distance}`.
- `getImagePreviewToKeyhole(mm_distance, disableDiecut)` (async) — calls `getImagePreviewKeyhole()`, rasterizes it to a canvas sized to the border, computes `mmLaser` outset distance from `mmLaser`/`mmLaserEx` side properties, runs `ImageCuttingLaser.getInfoDataVectorLaser` to derive SVG cut paths, adds each as a laser SVG item (`addLaserSVGDataInfo`), then (after 1s) calls `DrawTool.checkLaserColor()` and optionally resets to the layers panel.
- `getImagePreviewToShow(mm_distance, disableDiecut)` (async) — same pipeline as `getImagePreviewToKeyhole` but sourced from `getImagePreviewData()` (non-keyhole "die cut to design" flow).
- `getImageDiecutAcryLicSticker()` (async) — computes `mmLaser`, calls `getImagePreviewDataAcryLicStiker`, rasterizes and runs `ImageCuttingLaser.getInfoDataVectorLaserPedestal` (using `getPointLaserPedestal`/`getPointLaserPedestalNear`) to produce pedestal cut paths, keeps only the largest result, adds it as a laser SVG item, then resets print mode/border/layer setup.
- `getImagePreviewToShowAcryLic()` (async) — equivalent full acrylic-pedestal pipeline using `getImagePreviewDataAcryLic`, adding all resulting SVG cut paths (sorted by point count) rather than just the largest.
- `toggleOptions()` — if the active tool hasn't changed since last check, toggles `this.showOptions` and force-updates (button to show/hide the top options panel).
- `normalizeAngle(angle)` — returns `(angle+360)%360`.
- `update_rotate(degree)` — normalizes the angle, dispatches `DrawToolActions.updateDegree`, calls `checkItemChangeAction`.
- `update_scale(scaleNumber, scaleType)` — for laser items with `mmLaser_scale`, polls (via `setInterval`) after a debounce and shows a "minimum size is 4mm" warning if the scaled size would go below the minimum, forcibly resetting scale; otherwise computes a new percent based on `scaleType` (`reduce`/`increase`/`input`) and dispatches `DrawToolActions.updateScale`; always calls `checkItemChangeAction`.
- `setEmbroidery(name, setEmbroider)` — discards/re-renders canvas selection, calls `DrawTool.sides.selected.saveNameSide(name)`, dispatches `setActiveTool('_embroider')`, and if `setEmbroider` is true dispatches `setActiveTool('embroider_extend'|'embroider')` depending on mode, else clears `DrawTool.is_embroidery` and re-dispatches `'_embroider'`.
- `checkItemLaser` (class field) — checks whether the laser shape is closed (`checkLaser()`); shows success/warning `swal` accordingly and dispatches `setActiveTool('laser')` on success.
- `render_item_info()` — returns product/category-specific info JSX (currently mostly commented-out placeholders) plus embroidery action buttons (turn off embroidery / re-embroider elsewhere), scoped by `categoryStyle` (`CATEGORY_MOBILE`/`CATEGORY_FLAG`/default).
- `render_design_select_areas()` — returns `<BoderPaths/>` when `is_embroidery` is true, else empty.
- `render_textnode(class_name)` — returns a `<div className={'note '+class_name}>` rendering `product.tool_note` HTML (via `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`) if present; captures a ref (`textNoteInbox`) when `class_name === 'inbox'`.
- `render_content_with_textnode(content)` — clones the given `content` element, appending the `render_textnode('inbox')` node to its children and attaching a `contentOptions` ref.
- `update_textnode_position()` — a `requestAnimationFrame`-scheduled DOM-measurement routine that positions the tool note and the `options-toggle-button`(s) beneath the visible content by measuring bounding rects of sibling elements (mobile vs desktop logic differs — mobile hides all but the first visible toggle, desktop positions the single outer toggle beneath measured content), also nudging the "three-preview" button row down if it overlaps the note.
- `selectColor` (class field, `id`) — if in embroidery mode, turns embroidery off first; dispatches `ProductActions.selectColor(id)` after a 200ms delay.
- `selectSide(id)` — handles embroidery-mode cleanup (zoom-out for side '1', disable embroidery) before discarding canvas selection, dispatching `setActiveTool('sides')`, and after 200ms dispatching `ProductActions.selectSide(id)`.
- `renderText` (class field, `textEl, changeTVl, val`) — pads the text value with leading/trailing spaces; if `changeTVl` dispatches `changeTextVal` + triggers an `'update_item'` draw update, else dispatches `changeText`/`addText` depending on whether editing an existing element.
- `selectSideOnchange` (class field, `item`) — determines a preview "location" (1-4) based on which `item_previewN` field is populated, dispatches `ProductActions.showDesignModal(true)` and `selectDesignToModal` with the chosen location (used by the "design"/template browsing panel).
- `showSwwUploadImage` (class field) — shows a large informational `sww` dialog (white-body printing caveats and background-removal guidance) with embedded images/links.
- `setFontsUp()` — waits on `document.fonts.ready` then clears `is_font_loader`.
- `setFonts(font)` — resolves the font object from `allFonts`, dispatches `updateFontsByName`, sets `is_font_loader: true`, then on `document.fonts.ready` registers a `FontDetect.onFontLoaded` callback that dispatches `selectTextFont`, clears the loader flag, invalidates `fabric.charWidthsCache`, and forces re-measurement (`_initDimensions`) plus a draw update for any canvas text objects using that font family.
- `checkFontInsall` (class field) — in embroidery mode, if the currently selected font isn't in the available list, auto-selects the first available font (dispatching `selectTextCategoriesFonts` then calling `setFonts`); in normal mode, loads the default font once via `setFonts(textOptions.font)`.
- `checkViewCoppy` (class field) — a debounced (100ms) check that clears the copy buffer (`dataCoppy`, `enableCoppy`) if the current drawing mode no longer matches the mode the copied item was captured in.
- `onClickDieCutOptionLeft` (class field, `action`) — if in laser mode, updates the laser border and unselects the item, then (after 500ms) switches `activeTool` to the given action (uploadFile/text/sticker/brush/etc.) and collapses the die-cut option list.
- `finishAcryLic` (class field) — for `ACRYLIC_LASER` mode, blocks with a "design the pedestal first" dialog if a pedestal is still active; otherwise collapses the action list and either shows the "already used" popup or calls `getImagePreviewToShowAcryLic()`.
- `finishAcryLicSticker` (class field) — same gating pattern but calls `getImageDiecutAcryLicSticker()`.
- `clearImageCuttingLaser` (class field) — removes all items flagged `imageCuttingLaser` from the current side (detaching Fabric event handlers around the mutation for safety), re-renders, and switches tool to `'layers'`.
- `clearPedestalCuttingLaser` (class field) — inverse filter: keeps only pedestal (`mode_design`, `pathIndex==ACRYLIC_LASER`) items, removing everything else.
- `updatePedestalPrintInfoLaser` (class field) — stamps `mode_design` metadata onto any print-mode item lacking `last_info_acrylic`.
- `clearAndUpdatePedestalAgain` (class field) — combined cleanup: removes cutting-laser and pedestal items, clears stale `lastBorder`/resets `mode_design` on remaining print items.
- `clearBorderCuttingLaser` (class field) — clears `lastBorder` flags, re-applies the side's border (`setBorder`) and calls `DrawTool.refeshSideAndLayer(side)`.
- `checkAllItemLayerPedestalLaser` (class field) — reorders the layer stack so pedestal/mode_design items sink beneath related print items, using a `moveItemToEnd` helper and `side.layers.moveToIndex`, then dispatches `DrawToolActions.updateLayers` after triggering history/layer update events (temporarily disabling `DrawTool._evented`).
- `checkAllInfoPedestalSave` (class field) — when `DrawTool.stand_save_acrylic` is set, locks (`DrawTool.lock_stand_acrylic`), shows a "product changed, redo pedestal design" warning dialog, strips all `ACRYLIC_LASER`-indexed items from every side, then sequentially calls `clearImageCuttingLaser`, `clearBorderCuttingLaser`, `updatePedestalPrintInfoLaser`, and `checkAllItemLayerPedestalLaser` before deleting the `stand_save_acrylic` flag.
- `onClickDieCutOptionRight` (class field, `action, mm_distance=3, disableDiecut=false`) — ensures laser mode is active; for `action==='hole'` inserts a circular hole shape via `actions.insertLaser`; for `action==='dieCut'`, on `LASER_STICKER` mode it first purges existing cutting-laser items off-canvas then calls `getImagePreviewToShow`, otherwise directly calls it (both wrapped in `sww.showLoading()` + 500ms delay); collapses the action list.
- `onClickDieCutKeyhole` (class field, same signature) — identical structure to the above but targets `getImagePreviewToKeyhole` (the "keyhole" cut variant).
- `handleChangeLaser` (class field, `event, min, max`) — clamps a thickness input's value to `[min,max]` (with warning dialogs when clamped), stores it in state, and sets `DrawTool.mmLaser_Limmit`.
- `DieCut` (class field, arrow function) — **returns JSX** (not just logic): builds the two-step "Design → Cut plate" collapsible die-cut panel used for laser/acrylic products, wiring each list item's `onClick` to the option-selection and cut-action methods above; returns `""` for pure `LASER`/`ACRYLIC_LASER` base modes elsewhere handled by `content`.
- `finishBrush` (class field) — dispatches `setActiveTool('pointer')`; on iOS, discards canvas selection and re-renders, then re-selects the current side after 100ms.
- `draggableHandlerOnStart` (class field, `e,data`) — records the drag origin into `infoDraggable`.
- `draggableHandlerOnDrag` (class field, `e,data`) — detects whether the pointer has moved (vs. a stationary press) to distinguish a genuine drag from a click, calling `breakLoop()` and marking `infoDraggable.enable = true` when real dragging is detected (this flag is checked throughout the file to suppress accidental rotate/scale/move actions mid-drag).
- `draggableHandlerOnStop` (class field, `e,data`) — after 500ms, clears `infoDraggable.enable`.
- `stateInfoUpdateMenu` (class field) — if in a laser UI mode, forces `showOptions`/`showOptionsSp` false (used after inserting an item from a locked-UI panel like stickers/stamps).
- `render()` — see Render Structure below.

### Lifecycle

- `componentDidMount()` — detects iOS via UA sniffing into `state.is_ios`; computes `showOptionsLock` (which tools remain visible) based on whether the product's `modeToolDraw` is in `DrawTool.MODE_LASER_UI`; calls `addEvent()` to wire all DOM/DrawTool event listeners; calls `update_textnode_position()`.
- `componentDidUpdate()` — re-runs `update_textnode_position()` every update; when `activeTool` differs from `this.lastState`, re-evaluates the `showOptions`/`showOptionsSp` visibility against `showOptionsLock.mode`/`extends`, then force-updates; always refreshes `this.lastState`.
- `componentWillReceiveProps(nextProps)` (async) — the component's main synchronization point for props → local derived state; see full description in Methods above (font-category resolution, font-load callback registration, embroidery font validation, degree/scale DOM sync, `showOptionsLock` recompute on product change).
- No `componentWillUnmount` is defined (window/DOM listeners added in `addEvent`/`addEventKeyBoard` are not explicitly torn down).

### Render Structure

`render()` short-circuits to `null` if `tripo3D_mode` is true. Otherwise it destructures a large set of props/state and builds a `switch (activeTool)` that assigns JSX to a local `content` variable — each case represents one side-panel "tool":

- `'sides'` — scrollable list of `ProductColorSide` thumbnails (filtered to sides 3/4 for `CARPET_PRODUCT`), each selecting a side via `selectSide`.
- `'colors'` — scrollable list of product colors (`ColorPicker`-style swatches), each calling `selectColor`.
- `'pointer'` — item alignment buttons (`Button` icons: align top/vcenter/bottom/left/hcenter/right) dispatching `actions.alignItem`.
- `'brush'` — brush type dropdown (`DropDownM`), color picker, brush-size dropdown, and a "complete" button (`finishBrush`).
- `'text'` — shows a loading state until fonts are ready, then renders category/font dropdowns (`DropDownM` × 2, driving `checkFontInsall`/`setFonts`), alignment/vertical-text buttons, and `AddTextForm` bound to `renderText`.
- `'sticker'` — category buttons (`Button`) via `getStickers`, then a grid of `Sticker` thumbnails inserted via `actions.insertSticker` (SVG) or `DrawTool...addImageWithSize` (raster), with `DropFiles` upload support.
- `'stamps_image'` / `'stamps_love'` — near-identical large panels: category buttons, a favorites toggle, sortable tabs (new/popular/favorite driving `setShowStamTab`/`setShowStamLoveTab`), and stamp grids that call `DrawTool.sides.selected.items.addImageWithSize(...)` on click, with TAP/RIBBON post-processing.
- `'shapes'` — color picker (when category count is small) plus category buttons and a shape grid inserting via `actions.insertShape`.
- `'removeColor'` — color picker + pipette + "make transparent" button (`actions.toggleColorPicker`/`actions.removeColor`), shown only once an item is `selected`.
- `'layers'` — alignment buttons (`actions.alignLayer`) plus the `Layers` child component wired to `sortLayers`/`blurLayer`/`focusLayer`.
- `'uploadFile'` — instructions text + `Upload` button (`fileUpload`) + a "詳細" link opening `showSwwUploadImage`.
- `'grid'` — three `Button`s toggling `addGrid('hidden'|'grid'|'border')` (grid option hidden for laser products).
- `'brushlaser'` / `'laserLineVectorBrush'` / `'laserCurveVectorBrush'` / `'laser'` — laser tool palette: curve/line/brush cut modes, "empty laser", optional hole insert, a "cut to design" shortcut icon (`getImagePreviewToShow`/`showPopupImageCuttingLaser`), and a shape grid (`shapesLaser`) inserted via `actions.insertLaser`.
- `'changeToCropPathBrush'` — shape grid for crop-path drawing.
- `'embroider'` — empty placeholder panel.
- `'changeBorder'` — a single "刺繍領域保存" (save embroidery area) button calling `saveBorder`.
- `'selectPath'` — renders `<BoderPaths/>`.
- `'design'` — template/category browser: category buttons (`ProductActions.selectTemplateCategory`/`unSelectTemplateCategory`) and a grid of user templates (`selectSideOnchange`).
- `default` — a bare `DropFiles` wrapper.

After the switch, `render()` also computes: `webActionEmbroider` (embroidery on/off + relocate buttons, desktop-only, styled differently by `typeSizePointer`); the tab-switch header (`tool_switch`, edit/color tabs) and `list_colors` (fill-color palette, from `colors_fill` or, for TAP/RIBBON modes, `colors_Image_fill`); and `display_text_effect`/`hidden_change_object` flags gating the floating toolbar for text items and locked pedestal items respectively.

It then builds two mutually-exclusive floating toolbars:
- `change_object` — the main draggable (`Draggable` from react-draggable) "編集"(edit) panel: tab switch, color palette, rotation dial (`#hand-rotation`/`#degree`) with `+/-` buttons bound to `onMouseDownHandle`/`changeRotate`/`breakLoop` (press-and-hold), scale `+/-` buttons similarly, flip X/Y buttons, (for images) background-removal and crop buttons with loading spinners keyed off `image_upload`/`image_url_re`, copy/paste buttons (`copyItemSelect`/`pasteItemSelect`), directional move buttons, a delete section (`actions.remove()` + `DrawTool.triggerDrawUpdateChat`), and — when a curved/i-text item is selected — a "スタイル" (`TextOptions`) block and a "文字幅" (letter-spacing) block with its own press-and-hold `+/-` controls.
- `change_objectImageCuttingLaser` — a simplified variant (desktop-only) shown instead when the selected item `imageCuttingLaser` is true: tab switch, rotation dial, and just a delete button.

If `enableCutting` (the active Fabric brush is a laser brush) is true, `change_object` is instead replaced by a "カット編集"/crop-edit panel exposing point-add/point-erase toggles, optional curve-pairing controls, and a "完了する" (finish) button that calls `checkItemLaser()` + `updateCurentLaserBorder()` or `finishPathOver()`.

Finally, 3D-preview flags null out `change_object`/`content`, and if `DrawTool.MODE_LASER_UI` includes the current mode it triggers `DrawTool.trigger('option:update', {size_option:10})`. The component returns a wrapping `<div className="options-cp">` containing (in order): a `<Loader/>` when fonts are loading, the appropriate floating toolbar, `render_item_info()`, `render_content_with_textnode(content)`, the `DieCut()` panel (laser products only), `webActionEmbroider`, and the outbox tool-note (`render_textnode('outbox')`).

### Module-Level Constants / Helpers

- `SwalDialog = withReactContent(sw2)` — a SweetAlert2 instance enhanced to accept React elements as dialog HTML (used by `checkEmbroiderAreaSetup`, `checkAllInfoPedestalSave`).
- `LIMIT_ACCESS_REMOVE_BG = 10` — default daily-usage limit shown in the background-removal rate-limit message (overridden by the API error payload's `limit_acess` when present).
- `let loop;` — module-level `setTimeout` handle for the press-and-hold repeat loop (`changeValue`), shared across all instances of the component (not stored on `this`).
- `let timer = 50;` — module-level, mutable "acceleration" delay counter for the same repeat loop, decremented each iteration to speed up repeated rotate/scale/move actions.
- `mapStateToProps(state)` — module-level function mapping a large portion of `state.drawTool` and `state.product` into the props listed in the Props section above.
- `export default connect(mapStateToProps)(Options);` — connects the component with only `mapStateToProps` (no `mapDispatchToProps`; `dispatch` is injected automatically by `connect` and used directly throughout).

---

# Part 5 — Other Containers (Header, HeaderMobile, MobileNavigation, Toolbar, Design3D, FooterChat, GridChange)

## UP-T Container Components — Header, Navigation, Toolbar & Options Reference

This document covers seven container components used by the UP-T custom product designer: the desktop and mobile headers, the mobile navigation drawer, the main drawing toolbar, the 3D-model "Options" panel, the collaborative design-room chat panel, and a small grid-configuration option panel.

Several of these files share large blocks of near-duplicated logic (login/signup dialogs, "go to cart" form submission, embroidery-area setup, etc.) because they were evidently copy-pasted between the desktop and mobile variants and then diverged slightly. Where a duplicate exists, differences are called out explicitly; where copy-pasted code references a method or import that does not exist in that particular file (a latent bug), this is flagged as observed rather than fixed.

---

## src/containers/Header/index.jsx

### Purpose
`Header` is the desktop top app-bar for the design tool. It shows the UP-T logo, "open design" / "save design" buttons, the product-name dropdown, the color and side (front/back/sleeve) dropdowns, a cart button, an admin "re-render" button, and a 3D-preview modal. It is also the primary owner of the "add to cart" / checkout submission pipeline (both the classic 2D flatten-and-POST flow and the newer 3D-model flow), the login/signup modal cascade (via SweetAlert2), and the Yahoo! JAPAN OAuth "save & continue" flow. Most of its non-render code exists to marshal `DrawTool` (the canvas engine singleton) state into a `sides` payload, POST it to the backend via a dynamically constructed hidden `<form>`, and gate that submission behind various product-mode checks (laser cutting-line presence, embroidery mode, carpet-product side flips, upload-in-progress, "supplied product" confirmation, etc.).

### Imports
- **React/Redux**: `React, Component`, `connect` (react-redux).
- **UI**: `Button`, `DropDown` (custom components), `Carousel` (react-responsive-carousel, used inside the 3D preview modal).
- **Alerts**: `swal` (sweetalert), `sww` (sweetalert2) — used throughout for notices, login/signup dialogs, and error messages.
- **Canvas engine**: `DrawTool` singleton (`draw-tool/drawtool`).
- **API**: `getTemplates, saveProduct, saveProduct2, getPricesByColor, getRemakeImage3D, getFavoriteProductItem` (api/products); `checkProductSupplied, checkProductSuppliedAlert` (api/extras).
- **Redux actions**: `* as ProductActions` (actions/product), `* as DrawToolActions` and `* as actions` (both aliases of actions/draw-tool).
- **Constants/utils**: `WEBHOST, HOST, KEY_YAHOO` (constants), `query` (constants/query — a mutable query-string singleton), `* as Setting` (constants/setting), `disableOnBeforeUnload`, `MethodImageProcessing`, `loadQueryString`, `persistSession` (utils/sessionHelper), `parse` (querystring).

### State
- `sessionToken` (string|null) — session token extracted from the URL hash or search string, set by `getSessionParse()`.

Non-`state` instance fields also tracked: `numberOfColors`, `maxColorCount` (dev/legacy color-count UI), `showLoadmingUpload` (handle to an open "uploading" SweetAlert2 dialog, used to avoid opening it twice), `clear_cart_supp` (bool flag set once the user has acknowledged a "supplied product" warning, so it isn't re-asked).

### Props
From `mapStateToProps` (all read from Redux `state.product.*` / `state.drawTool.*`): `product, colors, colorSelected, sideSelected, price, layers, activeTool, category_styles, categories, loading_colors, image_uploader, user, multipler, embroider_able, is_embroidery, is_show_md, list_image_preview, change_design, list_preview_color, item_imagick_model, price_item_free, is_3d_preview, is_3d_preview_laser, shapes3DSelect, tripo3D_mode, previewImage3D, url3D, image3DCapture, zip3dfile, filePair` (`state.product.filePairs`). Plus `dispatch` from `connect`.
Not present in `mapStateToProps` but used in `render()`: `filterCategory` — a function prop supplied by the parent (App) component, passed to `DropDown`'s `onClick`.
Declared `propTypes` (a subset only): `product, colors, colorSelected, sideSelected, dispatch, price, layers, activeTool`.

### Methods
- `changCountColor(evt)` — dev/legacy: sets `this.maxColorCount` from an input's value, recomputes `numberOfColors` via `DrawTool.sides.selected.getCountColors(...)`, `forceUpdate()`.
- `getPrice()` — calls `getPricesByColor(colorSelected.id)` and caches the result on `this.productPrice`.
- `calcPrice()` — recomputes `numberOfColors`, then walks `this.productPrice.sides` to find the price bracket matching the current color count for the selected side, and dispatches `ProductActions.updatePrice(countColorPrice)` after 500ms.
- `downloadColorLayers()` — calls `DrawTool.sides.selected.getPreviewForLayers()` and, for each returned layer, programmatically creates an `<a download>` link and clicks it to trigger a browser download of that layer's PNG.
- `openProductLoad()` — stops the current DrawTool action, fetches `getTemplates()` and dispatches `ProductActions.updateTemplates`; if no user is logged in shows a "please sign up" `swal`, otherwise dispatches `ProductActions.toggleLoadProductContainer(true)` after 500ms.
- `openCategorySelect()` — finds the "clothing" category style (`Setting.CATEGORY_CLOTHER`) and filters `categories` to that style, then dispatches `ProductActions.loadCategoriesFilter(filter)` and `ProductActions.toggleLoadProductCategoryContainer(true)`. Note: the `.find(...)` call at line 132 (`this.props.category_styles.find(style.name === Setting.CATEGORY_CLOTHER)`) is not a proper predicate function — it references a free variable `style` rather than an arrow function — an apparent pre-existing bug in this code path.
- `setEmbroidery(name, setEmbroider)` (arrow) — discards/re-renders the active Fabric canvas selection, calls `DrawTool.sides.selected.saveNameSide(name)`, and dispatches `actions.setActiveTool('embroider')` or `'_embroider'` depending on `setEmbroider`.
- `selectColor(id)` — if currently in `EMBROIDER` mode, turns embroidery off first (`setEmbroidery('handLeft', false)`); dispatches `ProductActions.selectColor(id)` after 200ms; if the selected side is in draw mode, dispatches `actions.setActiveTool('brush')` after 500ms.
- `selectSide(id)` — handles `EMBROIDER`/`EMBROIDER_EXTEND` mode teardown (zooms out of the embroidery area, resets embroidery), discards the active selection, dispatches `actions.setActiveTool("layers")`, then dispatches `ProductActions.selectSide(id)` after 200ms.
- `getSessionParse()` — parses a `session*` query key out of `window.location.hash` (falling back to `.search`) and stores it in `state.sessionToken`.
- `handleSaveTemplate()` — if an image is still uploading, re-invokes itself after 500ms (dispatching `setLoading(true)` first); else sets the active tool to `'pointer'`, calls `checkLogin(saveDesign)`, and (if the user is logged in) dispatches `_embroider` tool if needed and then `ProductActions.saveTemplate()` after 500ms.
- `checkImageCuttingLaser()` (arrow) — returns `true` if any item on the first side has `imageCuttingLaser` set.
- `showPopupImageCuttingLaser()` (arrow) — shows a SweetAlert2 dialog instructing the user to cut the image first; message/graphic varies by laser sub-mode (`ACRYLIC_LASER` vs `LASER`/`LASER_NO_HOLE` vs generic).
- `goToCartEvent(reback_capture=false)` (async arrow) — the primary "go to cart" entry point (bound to the cart button and to the `DrawTool` `'cart:item'` event). Runs the "supplied product" confirmation gate (`checkProductSupplied`/`checkProductSuppliedAlert`) unless already acknowledged; for `CARPET_PRODUCT` mode, calls `DrawTool.checkCarpetRevertSide()` and polls every second until `DrawTool.finish_carpet` is set; for laser products, verifies a cutting line exists (else shows the popup and aborts) and, in `LASER` mode, checks for holes-near-cutting via `MethodImageProcessing`; sets `DrawTool.responsive_lock = true`, selects side `'3'` or `'1'` depending on mode, dispatches `updateProcessBar(0)`/`setLoadingProcess(true)`, finalizes the active brush stroke; for laser products updates the laser border and (`dispatch(ProductActions.updateSideLaser(...))`) the "other side" laser reference before calling `checkBeforeGotoCart()`/`goToCart()`; for embroidery mode selects side `'1'` and the `_embroider` tool first; otherwise calls `checkBeforeGotoCart()`/`goToCart()` directly.
- `goToCartToEdit()` (arrow) — admin-only "re-render" flow (see admin cart button in render). Polls every second until the number of loaded design items matches `DrawTool.numberDataItemAdmin`; then temporarily moves special-print items off-canvas, calls `DrawTool.rasterizeImage(...)` twice (for `"print"` then `"print_special"` layers, restoring item positions between calls), and finally calls `goToCartEvent()`.
- `callLoadDingScreen()` (arrow) — no-op placeholder; the SweetAlert2 loading-spinner code is commented out.
- `checkBeforeGotoCart()` (arrow) — shows/keeps open an "uploading, please wait" `sww` dialog while `DrawTool.checkSpeedupData()` is false (retrying every second); temporarily offsets special-print items off-canvas, calls `DrawTool.rasterizeImage(...)` twice with a `"data_sp_print1"` tag (print, then print_special), restores positions, then calls `goToCart()`.
- `login(content, api)` — raw `XMLHttpRequest` POST wrapper (returns a `Promise`) posting `email`/`password` form data to a login endpoint.
- `signup(content, api)` — raw `XMLHttpRequest` POST wrapper posting `nickname`/`email`/`password`/`regist_from_site`.
- `goToCart()` — builds the `sides` payload and resolves query-string flags (`is_admin, admin, admin_item_id, admin_order_id, pay_id, design_name, pay_item_id, admin_pay_id`) via `query.setData(parse(DrawTool.query_string))`. Aborts with a "no design" `swal` if `ifLayersEmpty()` (and tool isn't `'brush'`) or if `isTextNoise()` (iOS text-rendering-limit guard). Otherwise: disables the `beforeunload` warning, sets tool to `'pointer'`, calls `DrawTool.checkProductToInfoDetail()`, waits (via recursive `setTimeout` retries) for `DrawTool.checkSpeedupData()` and for any in-flight image upload to finish, then rasterizes text (`DrawTool.rasterizeText`), builds the per-side JSON payload, calls `saveProduct(...)`, and on success **dynamically constructs and submits a hidden `<form>`** POSTing to `WEBHOST/proc.php?run=appli2web` (or `run=callbackAddItem`, or an admin redirect) with dozens of hidden inputs: the raw `saveProduct` response fields, per-side rasterized images (`side.getEndProduct()`), embroidery data, sticker/stamp metadata, `cart_id`, `url_image`, `theme_id`, `room_chat`, `card_thank`, the "new design" flag, carpet-size data (`DrawTool.getSizeCarpet()`), the `clear_cart_supp` acknowledgment flag, and `item_quantity`.
- `processCart3D()` (async) — the 3D-product analog of `goToCart()`: builds a single-side `sides` payload containing the 3D capture image, model, and GLB file; calls `saveProduct(...)`; on success submits a hidden form to the same `WEBHOST` endpoints with 3D-specific fields (`endProduct: 'd3d'`, `3d_model`, `image_pre1/2`, empty `objEmbroider`/`objStampsTool_image`) or redirects to the admin info page.
- `goToCart3D()` (async) — entry point for the 3D cart flow (bound to the 3D cart button). Runs the same supplied-product confirmation gate as `goToCartEvent`; checks whether a ready `.zip`/`zip3dfile` exists for the current 3D capture; if not, shows a "please wait" `swal` and polls every 3 seconds (up to 60 attempts / 3 minutes) until the zip becomes available (or shows an error and gives up), then calls `processCart3D()`.
- `messageErr(message)` — shows and returns a SweetAlert2 error dialog with the given text.
- `checkLogin(type)` — the login/signup gate used by `handleSaveTemplate`/`saveDesign`. If no user, shows a SweetAlert2 choice ("ログイン" / "会員登録して保存"); the login branch shows an email/password form, POSTs via `login()`, and on success dispatches `ProductActions.updateUser(true)` + `updateUserAccess`, calls `persistSession`, loads favorites (`getFavoriteProductItem`), and — if the design isn't empty — calls `handleSaveTemplate(true)`; dismissing that dialog with "cancel" instead calls `this.saveDesign()`, and with "close" opens a nickname/email/password registration form (client-side validated with regexes for required fields, email shape, and password strength) that POSTs via `signup()` and follows the same post-login success path. If a user is already logged in, returns `true` synchronously (no dialog).
- `makeid(length)` — generates a random alphanumeric string (used as an OAuth `state` nonce).
- `gup(name, url)` — regex-based "get URL parameter" utility.
- `saveDesign()` — builds the current page URL (stripping any existing `session`/`design_id`/`cart_id` back down to just `cart_id`), then either redirects straight to the Yahoo! JAPAN OAuth authorization URL (if the design is empty) or first rasterizes the design, builds per-side JSON, calls `saveProduct2(...)`, appends the returned `design_id` to the URL, and *then* redirects to the Yahoo OAuth URL (with a random `state` nonce from `makeid(32)`).
- `goToMainSite()` — `window.location.href = WEBHOST`.
- `ifLayersEmpty()` — refreshes/reverses layers for `ACRYLIC_LASER` mode, then returns `true` unless at least one side is non-empty and has layers.
- `isTextNoise()` — on iOS devices only, returns `true` if any side reports `isTextNoise(50)` (a heuristic for excessive/complex text that iOS Safari can't reliably rasterize).
- `getPreview()` — generates the 3D-model preview shown in the "Modal" gated by `is_show_md`. Unselects the current item, resolves `query` session state, validates via `ifLayersEmpty()`/`isTextNoise()` (aborting with warnings), then — after the same upload/speedup wait loop as `goToCart()` — either calls `this.getReview(false,false)` (note: **`getReview` is not defined anywhere in this class** — this branch, taken only when `image_uploader != 0`, would throw if reached) or rasterizes text, builds the side payload, calls `saveProduct(...)`, and for each entry in `item_imagick_model` calls `getRemakeImage3D(data, item, index)` on a staggered `setTimeout` (1s apart), dispatching `ProductActions.showModal(true)` and `addImage3DPreview(res)` on success, or resetting loading state and showing a connectivity-error `swal` on failure.
- `hideModal` (arrow) — dispatches `ProductActions.showModal(false)`.

### Lifecycle
- `componentDidMount()` — calls `getSessionParse()`; subscribes to the `DrawTool` `'cart:item'` event, calling `goToCartEvent()` when fired (this is how other parts of the app — e.g. keyboard shortcuts or the toolbar — trigger checkout).
- A `componentWillReceiveProps` and a `componentDidUpdate` are present in the source but fully commented out (dead code), so they have no runtime effect.

### Render Structure
Renders `.app-header` containing: the UP-T logo (`onClick` → `goToMainSite`); a button group with "デザインを開く" (open design, gated on `user && !lock_item`) and "デザイン保存" (save, hidden for 3D-preview items); the product-name `DropDown` (`headProduct`, hidden/locked for certain `query.item_id`/`yaman`/`lock_item` combinations); the color `DropDown` (hidden for 3D-preview or `tripo3D_mode` items); the side `DropDown` (`side_to_show`, with special filtering to sides `"3"`/`"4"` for `CARPET_PRODUCT` mode); an admin-only "再レンダリング" button (visible when `query.login_type === 'admin'`, calls `goToCartToEdit`); the cart button (`cartSelect` — routes to `goToCart3D` for `tripo3D_mode`, otherwise `goToCartEvent`, with the price shown unless `query.room_chat` is set); an inline `ModalContentImagick` function component (defined inside `render()`) showing a `Carousel` of `list_image_preview` images plus "購入する"/"戻る" buttons, controlled by the `is_show_md` prop and `hideModal`; and a "プレビュー" button shown only when `list_preview_color` includes the current `colorSelected.id`.

---

## src/containers/HeaderMobile/index.jsx

### Purpose
`HeaderMobile` is the mobile equivalent of `Header`, rendered as the top bar on small viewports. It exposes the hamburger menu (which opens `MobileNavigation`), zoom in/out, undo/redo, a delete/trash button, the cart button, the 3D-preview modal, and the preview button — but *not* the product/color/side dropdowns, which live in `MobileNavigation` and `Toolbar` on mobile. Its "go to cart" / 3D-cart / preview submission logic is a near-verbatim duplicate of `Header`'s, adapted slightly for the mobile layout.

### Imports
Same family as `Header`: `React/Component`, `connect`, `swal`, `sww`, `WEBHOST/HOST`, `parse`, `query`, `Carousel`, `DrawTool`, `ProductActions`, `DrawToolActions` (+ `actions` alias), `Button`, `saveProduct`/`getRemakeImage3D` (api/products — notably **no** `getTemplates`, `saveProduct2`, or `getPricesByColor`), `checkProductSupplied`/`checkProductSuppliedAlert`, `disableOnBeforeUnload`, `MethodImageProcessing`, `loadQueryString`.

### State
None — the constructor does not initialize `this.state`; the component relies entirely on props and instance fields.

Instance fields: `clear_cart_supp` (bool, same "supplied product" acknowledgment flag as `Header`), `showLoadmingUpload` (referenced in `checkBeforeGotoCart`/`goToCart` but never initialized in the constructor — implicitly `undefined` until first assigned).

### Props
From `mapStateToProps`: `activeTool, selected, history, colors, colorSelected, sideSelected, price, layers, image_uploader, price_item_free, product, is_show_md, list_image_preview, list_preview_color, categoryStyle, item_imagick_model, tripo3D_mode, previewImage3D, url3D, image3DCapture, zip3dfile, filePair`. Plus `dispatch`.
Declared `propTypes` (subset): `activeTool, dispatch, selected, history, price, layers`.
Used in `render()`/`componentWillReceiveProps` but not in `mapStateToProps`: `calcPrice` — a function prop expected from the parent (this component does not define its own `calcPrice` method, unlike `Header`/`MobileNavigation`/`Toolbar`).

### Methods
- `toggleActiveTool(tool)` — dispatches `DrawToolActions.setActiveTool(tool)`.
- `showMobileNav()` — dispatches `ProductActions.toggleMobileNavigation(true)` and `DrawToolActions.unselectItem()`, deactivates/renders the Fabric canvas, and adds a `'fixed'` class to `document.body` (locks background scroll while the drawer is open).
- `undo()` — dispatches `DrawToolActions.undo(...)` for the selected side's history, `forceUpdate()`s, and (if `DrawTool.lock_product`) resets the lock via `DrawTool.rebackLockProduct()`/`setLayerSetup(...)` after 300ms.
- `handleSaveTemplate()` — if uploading, retries after 500ms; else sets tool to `'pointer'` and, if no user, shows a "please sign up" `swal` directly (unlike `Header`, this component has **no** `checkLogin`/`login`/`signup` methods — it does not offer an inline login dialog on mobile, just a notice); if logged in, sets embroidery tool if needed and dispatches `ProductActions.saveTemplate()` after 500ms.
- `redo()` — mirror of `undo()` for the redo direction.
- `checkImageCuttingLaser()` (arrow) — identical logic to `Header`'s.
- `showPopupImageCuttingLaser()` (arrow) — identical to `Header`'s (mode-specific cutting-line warnings).
- `setEmbroidery(name, setEmbroider)` (arrow) — identical pattern to `Header`'s.
- `selectSide(id)` (arrow) — identical pattern to `Header`'s (embroidery mode teardown, discard selection, `'layers'` tool, then `ProductActions.selectSide(id)` after 200ms).
- `goToCartEvent(reback_capture=false)` (async arrow) — near-identical to `Header`'s: supplied-product gate, `CARPET_PRODUCT` revert-side polling, laser cutting-line/hole checks, side selection (using `DrawTool.sides.selected.id` rather than a literal `'1'` for the non-carpet case), laser border update + `updateSideLaser`, embroidery-mode handling, then `checkBeforeGotoCart()`/`goToCart()`.
- `callLoadDingScreen()` (arrow) — no-op placeholder, same as `Header`.
- `checkBeforeGotoCart()` (arrow) — identical structure to `Header`'s (rasterize print/print_special layers, restore positions, call `goToCart()`).
- `goToCart()` — mirrors `Header.goToCart()`'s validation, upload-wait loop, `saveProduct` call, and hidden-form POST construction, with one notable difference: it does **not** include the `url_image`/`theme_id` hidden inputs that `Header`'s version adds; otherwise the same set of fields (`cart_id`, `endProduct`, `objEmbroider`, `objStampsTool_image`, `card_thank`, `room_chat`, `new`, `data_carpet`, `clear_cart_supp`, `item_quantity`) are appended.
- `ifLayersEmpty()` — identical logic to `Header`'s.
- `isTextNoise()` — same iOS-only check as `Header`'s, but uses a lower threshold: `side.isTextNoise(10)` versus `Header`'s `side.isTextNoise(50)`.
- `getPreview()` — mirrors `Header.getPreview()`, including the same reference to an undefined `this.getReview(false,false)` method in the `image_uploader != 0` branch.
- `hideModal` (arrow) — dispatches `ProductActions.showModal(false)`.
- `processCart3D()` (async) — mirrors `Header`'s 3D form-submission logic exactly (same field set).
- `goToCart3D()` (async) — mirrors `Header`'s zip-file-readiness polling logic exactly.

### Lifecycle
- `componentDidMount()` — subscribes to `DrawTool`'s `'cart:item'` event, calling `goToCartEvent()`.
- `componentWillReceiveProps(nextProps)` — if `this.props.loading` transitions (becomes true/false), calls `this.props.calcPrice()` (the parent-supplied function, not a local method).

### Render Structure
`.wrap-header-show-modal > .app-header` containing: hamburger button (`showMobileNav`), zoom-in/zoom-out buttons (dispatch `DrawToolActions.zoomIn()`/`zoomOut()`), undo/redo buttons (disabled when `activeTool` is `'changeBorder'`/`'brush'` or history is at its bounds), a delete/trash button (triggers `DrawTool.triggerDrawUpdateChat('online:update', ...)` to notify collaborators, then dispatches `DrawToolActions.remove()`; disabled when nothing is `selected`), and the cart button (`cartSelect`, same `tripo3D_mode`/`room_chat` branching as `Header`). Also renders the same inline `ModalContentImagick` 3D-preview modal component, and a `.wrap-pre-btn-mobile` "プレビュー" button gated on `list_preview_color` including the current color.

## src/containers/MobileNavigation/index.jsx

### Purpose
`MobileNavigation` is the slide-out drawer opened by `HeaderMobile`'s hamburger button. It provides "デザインを開く" (open saved design), "デザイン保存" (save design — with its own full login/signup dialog cascade, functioning as the mobile analog of `Header.checkLogin`), and "全削除" (clear canvas) actions, plus the Yahoo! JAPAN OAuth "save & continue" flow (`saveDesign`). It also contains leftover/dev-only UI (a "number of colors" counter and a "DCL" layer-download button) that appear unconditionally in the drawer regardless of product type.

### Imports
`React/Component`, `connect`, `parse` (querystring), `classNames`, `swal`, `sww`, `DrawTool`, `getTemplates, getPricesByColor, saveProduct2, getFavoriteProductItem` (api/products), `ProductActions`, `DrawToolActions` (note: **no** `* as actions` alias is imported here, unlike Header/HeaderMobile/Toolbar), `Button`, `Icon`, `query`, `WEBHOST, KEY_YAHOO, HOST`, `persistSession`.
Module-level `var count = 0;` — a load-once guard used by `componentDidUpdate` to ensure the post-Yahoo-login auto-save fires only a single time.

### State
- `sessionToken` (string|null) — same session-token-from-URL logic as `Header`.

Instance fields referenced but never initialized in the constructor: `this.maxColorCount`, `this.numberOfColors`, `this.productPrice` (used by the dev "colors-number" UI / `calcPrice`/`changCountColor` — effectively dead/legacy code paths not wired to any visible feature flag).

### Props
From `mapStateToProps`: `mobileNavigation` (bool, drawer open/closed), `product, colors, colorSelected, sideSelected, activeTool, user, loadDesignFn` (`state.product.load_design_fn`). Plus `dispatch`.
Declared `propTypes`: `mobileNavigation, product, colors, colorSelected, sideSelected, activeTool, dispatch`.

### Methods
- `getPrice()` — fetches `getPricesByColor(colorSelected.id)`, caches on `this.productPrice` (dev/legacy, duplicate of `Header`'s).
- `calcPrice()` — recomputes color count/price and dispatches `ProductActions.updatePrice` (duplicate of `Header`'s).
- `downloadColorLayers()` — downloads each layer's PNG via a synthetic `<a download>` click (duplicate of `Header`'s).
- `changCountColor(evt)` — updates `maxColorCount`/`numberOfColors` from an input (dev tool, duplicate of `Header`'s).
- `getSessionParse()` — identical session-token parsing logic to `Header`'s.
- `hideMobileNav()` — dispatches `ProductActions.toggleMobileNavigation(false)` and removes the `'fixed'` class from `document.body`.
- `openProductLoad()` — stops the current action, fetches templates, dispatches `updateTemplates`; shows a sign-up warning if not logged in, else toggles the load-product container after 500ms.
- `openCategorySelect()` — simply dispatches `ProductActions.toggleLoadProductCategoryContainer(true)` (simpler than `Header`'s version, which additionally filters by clothing category style).
- `setEmbroidery(name, setEmbroider)` (arrow) — same pattern as `Header`'s, **but calls `actions.setActiveTool(...)`, and `actions` is never imported in this file** — this is a latent bug: if this code path executes, it will throw a `ReferenceError`.
- `selectColor(id)` — dispatches `ProductActions.selectColor(id)` after 200ms; its embroidery-mode-teardown branch has the same undefined-`actions` bug as `setEmbroidery`.
- `selectSide(id)` — same embroidery-mode teardown plus `actions.setActiveTool("layers")` call — same undefined-`actions` bug — before dispatching `ProductActions.selectSide(id)` after 200ms.
- `messageErr(message)` — SweetAlert2 error dialog helper (duplicate pattern used across several files in this batch).
- `handleSaveTemplate()` — the full inline login-or-register SweetAlert2 cascade (login form → success dispatches `updateUser`/`updateUserAccess`, `persistSession`, loads favorites, then recurses into `handleSaveTemplate(true)`; "cancel" → `saveDesign()`; "close" → registration form with the same nickname/email/password regex validation as `Header.checkLogin`, POSTing via `signup()`). If already logged in, sets tool to `'pointer'`, sets embroidery tool if needed, and dispatches `ProductActions.saveTemplate()` after 500ms. (Unlike `Header`, this logic lives directly in `handleSaveTemplate` rather than a separate `checkLogin(type)` method.)
- `makeid(length)` — random ID generator (duplicate of `Header`'s), used for the OAuth `state` nonce.
- `saveDesign()` — Yahoo! JAPAN OAuth redirect flow, structurally identical to `Header.saveDesign()` (builds the current URL from `gup('session'/'design_id'/'cart_id', ...)`, rasterizes text, saves via `saveProduct2`, redirects to the Yahoo authorization URL with a random `state`).
- `login(content, api)` / `signup(content, api)` — raw XHR POST wrappers, duplicates of `Header`'s.
- `gup(name, url)` — query-param getter, duplicate of `Header`'s.
- `ifLayersEmpty()` — same emptiness check as `Header`'s, but without the `ACRYLIC_LASER` special-case layer refresh.
- `goToMainSite()` — `window.location.href = WEBHOST`.

### Lifecycle
- `componentDidMount()` — calls `getSessionParse()`.
- `componentDidUpdate()` — after returning from the Yahoo OAuth redirect (detected via a `login` query parameter on the current URL) and once `loading` is false and `loadDesignFn` is truthy, auto-triggers `handleSaveTemplate()` exactly once, gated by the module-level `count` variable (incremented to 1 on first trigger, preventing repeat auto-saves on subsequent re-renders).

### Render Structure
`.mobile-navigation` (toggled `active` via `mobileNavigation` prop) containing: a header row with the logo (`onClick` → `goToMainSite`) and a close button (`hideMobileNav`); a content section with "デザインを開く" (gated on `user && !lock_item`, where `lock_item` is derived from `query.yaman`/`query.lock_item`), "デザイン保存" (`handleSaveTemplate`), and "全削除" (dispatches `DrawToolActions.empty()`) buttons; and — always rendered, with no gating — a dev-leftover "colors-number" block (`numberOfColors` display plus a "Max count" number input bound to `changCountColor`) and a "DCL" button (`downloadColorLayers`).

## src/containers/Toolbar/index.jsx

### Purpose
`Toolbar` is the main drawing-tool palette: it lets the user switch between pointer/panning, text, image upload, stickers/stamps, AI image generation, brush, layers panel, embroidery placement, laser cutting-line drawing, color-transparency removal, saved-template browsing, and "delete all", plus zoom in/out, undo/redo, and a grid/guide toggle. It renders two structurally different layouts (a horizontally-scrollable button strip for mobile, a `react-smooth-scrollbar`-wrapped strip for desktop) and encodes a large amount of product-mode-specific gating logic (laser products, `CARPET_PRODUCT`, embroidery, "locked" query-string items, nail products) directly in `render()` and in the central `setActiveAction` dispatcher.

### Imports
`React/Component`, `connect`, `Scrollbar` (react-smooth-scrollbar), `classNames`, `Icon`, `query`, `DrawTool`, `Button`, `ProductActions`, `DrawToolActions` (+ `* as actions` alias), `sw2` (sweetalert2), `swal` (sweetalert), `getTemplatesUserForDesign` (api/products), `withReactContent` (sweetalert2-react-content) → `SwalDialog`.

### State
- `mobile` (bool) — whether the viewport matches `(max-width: 1079px)`, from `window.matchMedia`.
- `embroidery_view` (bool) — flags that the embroidery-area setup flow is active/visible (used for desktop embroider-button highlighting).
- `height` (number) — `window.innerHeight`, tracked alongside `mobile` but only lightly consumed.
- `enable_carpet` (bool) — for `CARPET_PRODUCT` mode, whether the "アイテム位置移動" (carpet-pulling) action is currently allowed, gated by whether enough design assets have finished loading (`DrawTool.numberDataOldDesign` comparison).

Instance fields (non-`state`): `helpArrows {left, right}` — controls the mobile horizontal-scroll arrow visibility; `colorSelect {background, textColor, typeEdit}` — a per-tool-key style map used to highlight the active tool button and reset all others; `lastEmbroidery` — snapshot of `props.is_embroidery` used to detect changes and re-run `setupValueColorClick()`; `lastTime {mode, time}` — debounce state for the `DrawTool` `'update:mode'` event (suppresses duplicate `setActiveAction` calls within 200ms of the same mode unless the mode itself changed).

### Props
From `mapStateToProps`: `activeTool, is_embroidery, zoom_zone` (`state.drawTool.zoomZone`), `selected, history, layers, colorSelected, sideSelected, embroider_able, is_nail, colors, templateUsers, templateCategorySelected, product, stampsLoveCat`. Plus `dispatch`.
Declared `propTypes` (subset): `activeTool, dispatch, selected, history, layers, colorSelected, sideSelected`.
Used in code but not in `mapStateToProps` (parent-supplied function props): `filterCategory`, `fillterFontsByCategory` (sic — typo preserved from source; filters the loaded font list by category, called with `'日本語'`/Japanese in `setActiveAction`).

### Methods
- `componentDidMount()` — see Lifecycle.
- `componentWillUnmount()` — see Lifecycle.
- `helpClickArrows(arrow)` — scrolls the `.toolbar` DOM element left/right by 70px when a scroll-arrow icon is clicked (mobile only).
- `helpScrollArrows()` — measures `.toolbar`'s `scrollWidth` (capped at 759px) against `window.innerWidth` to decide whether the toolbar overflows, and toggles `this.helpArrows.left`/`.right` (plus `forceUpdate()`s) accordingly as the user scrolls to either edge.
- `getIsMobile()` — sets `state.mobile` via `matchMedia('(max-width: 1079px)')` and `state.height` via `window.innerHeight`.
- `undo()` — dispatches `actions.undo(...)` for the selected side's history; then dispatches `DrawToolActions.updateLayers({layers: ...reversed layer list..., side})`; `forceUpdate()`s; resets any active `lock_product` after 300ms.
- `redo()` — mirror of `undo()`.
- `setEmbroidery(name, setEmbroider)` — discards/re-renders the canvas selection, saves the side name, sets the tool to `'embroider'`, `'embroider_extend'` (if in `EMBROIDER_EXTEND` mode), or `'_embroider'` depending on `setEmbroider`; after 200ms, snapshots `lastEmbroidery` and resets the tool to `'pointer'`.
- `onSetEmbroiderArea()` — shows an SweetAlert (`swal`) offering embroidery-placement choices (right chest / left chest / left sleeve / right sleeve), with a product-specific recommended-character-count note (`numberEmbroiderTex`: 3 for product `IT2745`, else 6); on selection, dispatches `ProductActions.selectEmbroiderArea(sideId)`, sets `DrawTool.is_embroidery = true`, selects the target `DrawTool` side, calls `setEmbroidery(...)` with the area name, and (for the sleeve options) also deactivates the canvas, disables panning/drawing mode, and calls `DrawTool.setEmbroidery(true)`.
- `checkEmbroiderAreaSetup()` — builds a `SwalDialog` (React-content SweetAlert2) listing every side's `border_special` embroidery zones as clickable buttons; clicking one closes the dialog, dispatches `ProductActions.selectEmbroiderArea(...)`, selects that `DrawTool` side, and calls `setEmbroidery(...)` for that zone's name. Used for `EMBROIDER_EXTEND` products, which can have multiple embroiderable zones spread across sides.
- `setupValueColorClick()` — (re)initializes `this.colorSelect.background`/`.textColor`/`.typeEdit` arrays, mapping every toolbar button key to its default (unselected) white/black styling; snapshots `lastEmbroidery`; `forceUpdate()`s.
- `setActiveAction(select)` — the central per-button click handler, invoked by nearly every toolbar `Button`. Resets `panning`/`carpet_pulling` flags on the selected side, filters fonts to Japanese, and recolors the just-selected button (highlighted pink `#F6CAC9`) while resetting all others to white/black. Branches on `select`:
  - `'text'` → also dispatches `actions.setTextOption()`.
  - `'carpet_pulling'` → toggles carpet drag-to-move mode on/off, gated by `DrawTool.enable_mouse_move` (shows a "please wait for images to load" `sw2` alert if not ready); toggling off switches the tool back to `'layers'`.
  - `'carpet_pulling_1'` → dispatches `actions.setActiveTool('pointer')`.
  - `'delete'` → dispatches `actions.empty()`, `forceUpdate()`s, and for laser products clears finger/hole arrays and re-dispatches `setBorderSameCM()` after 500ms.
  - `'embroider1'` → shows an `sw2` confirmation dialog with embroidery caveats; on confirm calls `onSetEmbroiderArea()` (`EMBROIDER` mode) or `checkEmbroiderAreaSetup()` (`EMBROIDER_EXTEND` mode); on cancel/dismiss reverts via `setEmbroidery('handLeft', false)` and resets layer setup.
  - `'brushlaser'`, `'laserCurveVectorBrush'`, `'laserLineVectorBrush'` → all three dispatch `actions.setActiveTool('brushlaser')` and highlight both the `laser` and `brushlaser` buttons (functionally identical branches, likely vestiges of renamed/merged tools).
  - `'laser'` → dispatches `actions.updateCurentLaserBorder()` and `DrawToolActions.unselectItem()`; highlights `laser`/`brushlaser` based on `DrawTool.checkModeLaser()`.
  - `'redo'`/`'undo'` → calls `this.redo()`/`this.undo()`.
  - `'zoomout'`/`'zoomin'` → dispatches `actions.zoomOut()`/`zoomIn()`, `forceUpdate()`s.
  - `'zoom_zone'` → dispatches `actions.zoomZone(!DrawTool.sides.selected.zoom_zone)` (nail-product partial-zoom toggle).
  - `'brush'` → dispatches `actions.setActiveTool('brush')`; for tap-ribbon product modes, after 200ms dispatches `actions.selectBrushColor(DrawTool.newColorPicked)` (defaulting to the product's first color or black if none picked yet).
  - default → dispatches `actions.setActiveTool(select)` directly (covers `uploadFile, layers, pointer, panning, sticker, stamps_image, stamps_love, removeColor, imageAI, sides, colors, grid`, etc.).
  Always finishes by calling `this.checkSpecialView()`.
- `checkSpecialView()` — re-derives the `'embroider'` button's highlight (based on `DrawTool.embroider_able && DrawTool.is_embroidery`) and the `'laser'`/`'brushlaser'` buttons' highlight (based on `DrawTool.checkModeLaser()`).
- `loadUserTemplateCategoriesDesign()` (arrow) — fetches `getTemplatesUserForDesign()`, dispatches `ProductActions.loadUserTemplateCategories(data)`, optionally auto-selects the first category via `ProductActions.selectTemplateCategory(data[0])`, and calls `setActiveAction("design")` to switch into template-browsing view.

### Lifecycle
- `componentDidMount()` — calls `setupValueColorClick()`; adds a `window` `'resize'` listener that calls `getIsMobile()`/`helpScrollArrows()`; calls both once immediately; subscribes to `DrawTool` events: `'update:activeTool'` and `'item:unselect'` (both close the color picker if open, via `DrawToolActions.toggleColorPicker(false)`), `'update:mouse'` (drives `enable_carpet` for `CARPET_PRODUCT` mode based on loaded-asset counts), `'update:pointer'` (forces the visual active tool to `'pointer'`), and `'update:mode'` (debounced — calls `setActiveAction(infoMode.mode)` immediately on a mode change, or after a 200ms quiet period for repeated identical modes, using `this.lastTime`).
- `componentWillUnmount()` — removes the `window` `'resize'` listener and calls `helpScrollArrows()` once more.

### Render Structure
Branches entirely on `state.mobile`:
- **Mobile** (`.toolbar.with-scroll`): product-change button (`menuProduct`, gated by `query.item_id`/`lock_item`), "表裏袖" (sides) button, "カラー変更" (color) button with a live color swatch, a carpet-pulling button (`CARPET_PRODUCT` only), a laser "板をカット" button (`LASER_STICKER`/`LASER_NO_HOLE` only), a nail-product zoom-zone button (`DrawTool.is_nail` only), image-upload/text/layers/pointer/panning buttons (each further gated by a `taskbar.enable_*` flag map that is narrowed when `query.yaman` or `query.lock_item` are set), a left scroll-arrow, then — outside embroidery mode — sticker/user-stamp/love-stamp/brush buttons, an embroidery button (if `embroidery_enable`), a color-transparency button, an AI-image-gen button; then — inside embroidery mode — duplicated image-upload/brush/sticker/color-transparency buttons; a "テンプレ" (templates) button, a delete-all button, and a right scroll-arrow.
- **Desktop** (`.toolbar-container > .toolbar > Scrollbar`): zoom-in/out, nail zoom-zone button, undo/redo, a "ガイド" (grid/guide) button, a laser-cut button (conditional), a separator, carpet-pulling and carpet "image move" buttons (`CARPET_PRODUCT` only), then largely the same image/text/sticker/stamp/brush/layers/embroider/template/AI-gen button set as mobile (again duplicated for the embroidery-mode branch), and a delete-all button.

Both branches drive every button's `style` from `this.colorSelect.background`/`.textColor` (per-key highlight state) and their `disabled`/`active` props from `activeTool`, a local `disabled` flag (`activeTool === 'changeBorder'`), `disabledAll` (`DrawTool.checkModeLaser()`), and `is_embroidery`.

## src/containers/Options/Design3D.jsx

### Purpose
`Design3D` is an "Options" side-panel feature that lets the user turn a flat 2D image into an AI-generated 3D model (backed by a Tripo3D-style job/polling API), or alternatively upload an existing `.glb` 3D model file directly, and preview the result live via a `Preview3DTriple` viewer. It also owns a small "Upoint" (virtual currency) balance/cost display and a full login-gate dialog (mirroring `Header.checkLogin`) since 3D generation is a paid, login-required feature. It renders two parallel responsive layouts via `react-responsive`'s `MediaQuery`.

### Imports
`React, Component, Fragment`, `connect`, `DrawTool`, `postUpload3dImage, getResultModel, countUpointByUser, getFavoriteProductItem` (api/products), `uploadByString, uploadByStringWithProgress, uploadGLBWithProgress` (api/extras), `ProductActions`, `DrawToolActions` (+ `actions` alias), `query`, `swal`, `WEBHOST, HOST`, `Preview3DTriple` (components/3d-triple), `Swal` and `sww` (both aliases of sweetalert2), `MediaQuery` (react-responsive), `Message3dTripo` (components/message-3d-tripo), `persistSession`.

### State
- `previewImage3D` (string|null) — the uploaded flat image's URL, staged for 3D-ification.
- `show3dPlay` (bool) — whether to show the 3D player once a model is ready.
- `url3D` (string) — URL of the resulting (or directly-uploaded) `.glb` model.
- `fileData3D` (object|string|null) — metadata (`mime_type, content, extension, name`) about the uploaded file.
- `systemPoint` (number|null) — Upoint cost required to generate one 3D model.
- `systemLimit` (number|null) — free-usage threshold before Upoints are charged.
- `uploadType` (`'image'`|`'glb'`) — which of the two upload modes is active.
- `isLoading` (bool) — true while the 3D viewer is loading/rendering a model.
- (also `userCount`, set via `setState` in `updateInfoUpoint` though not present in the constructor's initial state object.)

### Props
From `mapStateToProps`: `colors, previewImage3D` (server-side value, distinct from the local `state.previewImage3D` of the same name), `url3D` (server-side value), `user`. Plus `dispatch`.
Used elsewhere in the file but **not** present in `mapStateToProps` (likely parent-supplied or otherwise unmapped): `tripo3D_mode` (read in `render()`), `capacity_uploading` (read in `fileUpload()`'s size-limit check).
Declared `propTypes`: `dispatch` only.

### Methods
- `updateInfoUpoint()` (arrow) — calls `countUpointByUser()` and stores `systemPoint`, `systemLimit`, `userCount` in state.
- `fileUpload(file)` (arrow) — handles a flat-image upload destined for 3D-ification: aborts with a `swal` if `DrawTool.sides.selected.checkItemCanAddValue()` returns a blocking message; validates MIME type (`jpeg/png/gif`, PostScript, PDF, Photoshop, or a `.psd` filename extension) and size (`<= 20MB`) and cumulative upload capacity (`props.capacity_uploading + file.size <= 30MB`), showing warning `swal`s and returning early on any violation; reads the file via `FileReader.readAsDataURL`, uploads it with progress via `uploadByStringWithProgress` (scaling the reported percent to 20% of the overall process bar via `actions.updateProcessBar`), then on success updates `DrawTool`'s tracked upload capacity and sets `state.previewImage3D`/`fileData3D`; shows an error `swal` on failure.
- `handleFileUploadGLB(file)` (arrow) — validates the file extension is `glb` (else warns and returns), reads it via `FileReader`, uploads via `uploadGLBWithProgress`, then on success sets `state.url3D`/`fileData3D` (clearing `previewImage3D`), triggers `DrawTool.trigger('option:model3d', {src: url})` to show it in the viewer, dispatches `ProductActions.setUrl3D(url)`, and shows a success `Swal` toast.
- `handleFileUpload3D(event)` (arrow) — the `<input type="file">` change handler; routes to `handleFileUploadGLB` or `fileUpload` based on `state.uploadType`.
- `handleCancel()` (arrow) — clears `previewImage3D`/`fileData3D`/`url3D` and the 3D viewer (`DrawTool.trigger('option:model3d', {src: null})`), and dispatches `ProductActions.setZipObjFile3d(null)` / `setImage3DCapture(null)`; if nothing was uploaded, shows an error `sww` instead of clearing.
- `login(content, api)` / `signup(content, api)` — raw XHR POST wrappers, duplicates of the pattern used in `Header`/`MobileNavigation`.
- `messageErr(message)` — SweetAlert2 error dialog helper (duplicate pattern).
- `checkLogin(type)` — the same large login-or-register `sww` dialog cascade as `Header.checkLogin`, gating the "AI 3D generation" feature behind authentication; on success dispatches `updateUser`/`updateUserAccess`, calls `persistSession`, calls `this.updateInfoUpoint()` to refresh the Upoint balance, and — if the design isn't empty — calls `this.handleSaveTemplate()`. **Note:** `Design3D` defines neither `handleSaveTemplate()` nor `ifLayersEmpty()` — these calls are dead/copy-pasted code from `Header.checkLogin` and would throw a `TypeError` if that branch were actually reached (i.e., if a user completes login/signup here while the design already has content).
- `handleAccept3D()` (arrow) — the "画像を3D化する" action handler. Destructures `{previewImage3D, dispatch, uploadType}` from `this.state` (note: `dispatch` is not a state field, so this destructured `dispatch` is `undefined`; the method actually uses `this.props.dispatch` elsewhere, so this has no functional effect but is dead/misleading code). If `uploadType === 'glb'`, dispatches `ProductActions.setPreviewImage3D(url3D)` — **`url3D` here is an undeclared free variable** (not destructured from `state` or `props` in this method), so this specific branch would throw a `ReferenceError` if actually executed. Otherwise, defines a local `create3DModel()` helper that opens a `Swal` loading modal, calls `postUpload3dImage(previewImage3D)`, and chains into `poll3DModelResult(jobId)`; the outer flow requires `checkLogin('upoint')` to pass and `previewImage3D` to be set, then calls `countUpointByUser()` to decide whether to show a "buy more Upoints" prompt (opens `WEBHOST/regist.php?type=pay_points` in a new tab), a "this will cost N Upoints, proceed?" confirmation, or to call `create3DModel()` immediately if usage is still under the free limit.
- `poll3DModelResult(jobId)` (arrow) — polls `getResultModel(jobId)` every 15 seconds (up to 15–16 attempts, ~4 minutes) until `status === 'success'` (then sets `state.url3D`/`show3dPlay`, triggers the viewer, dispatches `ProductActions.setUrl3D(url)`, shows a success `Swal`, and refreshes Upoint info) or times out with an error `Swal`.
- `handleUploadTypeChange(event)` (arrow) — switches `state.uploadType` between `'image'`/`'glb'` and resets `previewImage3D`/`fileData3D`/`url3D`, clearing the 3D viewer.

### Lifecycle
- `componentDidMount()` — calls `updateInfoUpoint()`; after 1 second, triggers `DrawTool.trigger('option:model3d', {src: null})` to reset the main 3D viewer.

### Render Structure
Two parallel `MediaQuery`-gated layouts:
- **Desktop** (`min-width: 1080px`): an upload-type radio toggle (image vs. glb, shown only before anything is uploaded); an upload placeholder area (shows the uploaded `.glb` filename or a placeholder graphic) with a hidden `<input type="file">` triggered via a ref; three action buttons ("画像をアップロード" / "画像を３D化する" (image mode only) / "キャンセル"), each `disabled` based on current state; a result panel with `Message3dTripo` (Upoint messaging, image mode) or a static "accepted format: .glb" note (glb mode), a "please upload" notice, a loading overlay while `isLoading`, and the `Preview3DTriple` viewer wired to `onLoadStart`/`onLoadComplete` (which toggle a `Swal` loading dialog and `state.isLoading`).
- **Mobile** (`max-width: 1080px`): a tab-style image/glb toggle (before upload); an upload button/area; once an image is staged, a preview image plus "画像を３D化する"/"キャンセル" action buttons; the same `Message3dTripo`/format-note block; and, once `url3D` is set, a second block showing the `Preview3DTriple` viewer plus a bottom "キャンセル" button.
