## src/containers/Options/FooterChat.jsx

### Purpose
`FooterChat` implements the real-time collaborative "design room" feature: a chat bubble fixed at the bottom of the screen (used in `room_chat` sessions where multiple users co-design a product together). Beyond plain chat, it maintains a Socket.IO connection that also relays and applies live canvas-editing commands (item add/update/delete/move, side resync, color/product switch) so every collaborator's `DrawTool` canvas stays in sync with the others'. This is the most networking/state-heavy file in this batch — the majority of its methods are outgoing-sync builders, incoming-command appliers, and debounce/retry bookkeeping rather than pure UI.

### Imports
`React`, `connect`, `DrawTool`, `sendMessageChat, getAllMessageChat, deleteMessageChat, getBeforeMessageChat, addItemDesign, deleteItemDesign, loadItemDesign, loadAllItemActionDesign, loadSideItemDesign, addMutiItemDesign, deleteSideDesign, checkUserInfo, loadAllItemDesign` (api/extras), `getProductWithColor, getProductByImagickModel` (api/products), `InputChat`, `MessageList` (components/chat-form), `ProductActions`, `DrawToolActions` (+ `actions` alias), `query`, `socketIOClient` (socket.io-client), `swal`, `WEBHOST`, `disableOnBeforeUnload`.

### State
- `inChatbox` (bool) — whether the chat panel is expanded/open.
- `lstMessage` (array) — chat message objects, each augmented with a `status_update` (read/unread) flag.
- `text` (string) — initialized but not directly manipulated elsewhere in this file (message composition is delegated to the `InputChat` child).
- `isLoadingMessge` (bool) — true while older messages are being fetched (infinite-scroll pagination indicator).

Instance fields (non-`state`): `chat_focus` (ref to the `InputChat` child, used to clear/flag the input box), `socketRef` (the live `socket.io-client` instance), `lstItemUpdate` (pending per-item outgoing-sync debounce entries), `intervalCheck`/`intervalCheckUpdate`/`intervalCheckCommand` (`setInterval` handles for the three polling loops described below), `lstCommand` (queue of incoming remote commands awaiting sequential processing), `counterNumberCommnand` (monotonic counter stamped onto incoming commands), `updateHightSpeed` (tracks items being rapidly/continuously edited, e.g. mid-drag, for coalescing).

### Props
From `mapStateToProps`: `infoChatSocket, infoRoomDesign` (room/session/socket-server metadata), `colorSelected, products, product, colors, list_preview_color, allFonts`. Plus `dispatch`.
Used but not present in `mapStateToProps`: `sidesPrice` (read in `calcPrice()` — a per-side price table, presumably a parent-supplied prop).
Declared `propTypes`: `dispatch` only.

### Methods
Grouped by purpose (all are class-field arrow functions):

**Chat UI**
- `onPopupDialogue()` — marks all messages read (`status_update = true`) and opens the chat window (`inChatbox: true`).
- `onPopupDialogueHidden()` — closes the chat window.
- `onSubmit(text)` — sends a message via `sendMessageChat`, prepends it to `lstMessage` on success and clears the input via `chat_focus`; on failure shows a connectivity-warning `swal` and flags the input as errored.
- `onDeleteMessage(mesage)` — if the message's author matches the current user, confirms via `swal`, then deletes it via `deleteMessageChat` and removes it from `lstMessage`.
- `finishLoadingInfo()` — after a 1s delay, nudges the message list's scroll position and clears `isLoadingMessge`.
- `onScrollMessage()` — infinite-scroll handler: fetches older messages via `getBeforeMessageChat` (paged from the oldest currently-loaded message's timestamp), merges them into `lstMessage` (deduplicated by `time`+`author`), then calls `finishLoadingInfo()`.
- `onLeaveRoom()` — posts a "have been leave!" system message, disables the `beforeunload` warning, and redirects to `WEBHOST` (both immediately and again after a 1s delay).

**Outgoing sync (local edit → other collaborators)**
- `checkItemImageUpdateIdBack(idBack)` — when an image finishes async background processing, finds the matching item(s) across all sides, builds a sync command via `DrawTool.createCommandChatDesign`, persists it (`addItemDesign`), and emits it over the socket.
- `checkItemUpdateLocation(item)` — debounced per-item change tracker: bumps the pending entry's timestamp if one exists; otherwise builds/persists/emits a command immediately and starts a 1s `intervalCheck` (running `checkInfoToRefesh`) if not already running.
- `updateItemInfoAgain(item)` — coalesces rapid repeated edits to the same item (e.g. mid-drag) via `updateHightSpeed` and a 500ms interval; once an item has been quiet for >1s, "restarts" its sync identity (deletes `id_item`, then re-adds via `checkItemUpdateLocation` after first emitting a `DELETE_ITEM` command).
- `checkInfoToRefesh()` — runs every 1s to flush `lstItemUpdate` entries to the server via `sendMessageItem()` based on elapsed-time thresholds (first-touch vs. quiet-for-1s logic), pruning completed entries.
- `sendMessageItem(item)` — builds and emits (persisting first for brand-new items) the sync command for a single item; wrapped in try/catch; stamps `DrawTool.lastTimeTosend`.
- `getComandStringifyTosend(infoProduct)` — wraps a payload in the `{fromId, toId, msg}` envelope the socket server expects and JSON-stringifies it.
- `commandToSocket(command)` — handles commands raised locally via the `DrawTool` `'online:update'` event: `DELETE_ITEM` (persist via `deleteItemDesign` then emit), `EMPTY_NOW` (persist via `deleteSideDesign` then emit), `UPDATE_SIDE` (delegates to `checkAllItemInSide` to resync the whole side), else emits the generic envelope directly.
- `reloadNewLayerSideDesign(reloadNewLayerSideDesign)` — if the given layer-order array has more than one entry, resyncs the whole side using that order (used after local layer reordering).
- `checkAllItemInDesign()` — resyncs every side (calls `checkAllItemInSide` per side with a shared `id_item` stamp).
- `checkAllItemInSide(side, id_item, indexSide, resetAll, sortData)` — rebuilds and persists+emits a full `UPDATE_SIDE` command for one side: optionally strips existing `id_item`s (`resetAll`), builds per-item JSON/command payloads (`DrawTool.getInfoItemJsonDesign`/`getInfoItemChatDesign`), reorders items to match a supplied `sortData` UUID order or falls back to Fabric canvas z-order, computes the side's border/area dimensions, persists via `addMutiItemDesign`, then emits.
- `reloadNewColorDesign()` — emits an `UPDATE_NEW_COLOR` command notifying collaborators that the base product color changed.

**Incoming sync (remote edit → apply locally)**
- `checkInfoTextFontToSocket(ItemJson, callback)` — before applying a remote item using custom fonts, ensures those fonts are loaded locally (dispatches `actions.updateFontsByName` for each, waits on `document.fonts.ready`, toggles a loading flag), then invokes `callback(ItemJson)`.
- `checkChangePropertiesItem()` — pulls the next command from `lstCommand` (a queue with a retry/staleness mechanism — up to ~5 "reback" retries via `numberReback` before force-finishing) and dispatches to `checkChangePropertiesItemInSide`, `checkChangePropertiesItemOtherSide`, or `checkChangeAnotherColor`, depending on whether the command targets the currently active product color and side. Driven by a 200ms interval set up inside the socket's `'receive-new-msg'` handler.
- `checkChangePropertiesItemInSide(ItemJson)` — applies a remote command targeting the side currently being viewed: `NEW_ITEM_ADD`/other item-level updates load the item (`loadItemDesign`) then apply it via `DrawTool.loadDirectJsonOnlineItem` (after font pre-loading); `DELETE_ITEM` removes it via `DrawTool.deleteOnlineItemCurrentSide`; `EMPTY_NOW` clears via `actions.emptyNow()`; `UPDATE_SIDE` clears and reloads the whole side (`loadSideItemDesign` + `loadDirectJsonOnlineItem`), and for laser-mode products re-runs border/layer setup after a delay; `UPDATE_COLOR_RIBON` applies a bulk color change via `items.imagechangeColorAll`.
- `checkChangePropertiesItemOtherSide(ItemJson)` — the same command set applied to a side other than the currently-visible one, via `DrawTool.loadOnlineItemOtherSide`/`deleteOnlineItemOtherSide`/`deleteAllOnlineItemOtherSide`.
- `checkChangeAnotherColor(ItemJson, reloadLastDesign=false)` — handles a remote collaborator switching the product's color/variant: fetches the new color's data (`getProductWithColor`), marks it as "main", loads its 3D preview (`loadItem3D`), and — depending on whether the product has locked stamp images, is a laser product, or is an embroidery product — filters out incompatible existing canvas items before dispatching `ProductActions.loadProduct(data)`; then polls (`setInterval`) `DrawTool.changeProductSelect(ItemJson)` until the swap completes, at which point it either reconciles via `DrawTool.checkLastDessignDataInfo` or applies the original command via `checkChangePropertiesItemInSide`.
- `checkConnectionInfo()` — runs on socket `'connection'` (initial connect and reconnect): marks the user "in room" (updates `infoRoomDesign.useinRoom`, dispatches `ProductActions.setInfoRoomDesign`, posts a "login back!" system message) if not already listed; and, if recovering from a detected disconnect (`DrawTool.disconnectSocket`), re-fetches room info (`checkUserInfo`), re-establishes the socket (`setupSocketInfo`), reloads full chat history, and reloads the full remote design-action history (`loadAllItemDesign` + `loadAllItemActionDesign`) to reconcile the latest known product/color state.

**Pricing / misc**
- `calcPrice()` — recomputes the running price by inspecting each side's Fabric object count / embroidery/print counts against the `sidesPrice` prop, then dispatches `ProductActions.updatePrice` (or uses a fixed `query.price` override) after a 500ms delay.
- `loadItem3D(id, colorSelectId, color)` — if the given color is eligible for 3D preview (`list_preview_color`), fetches `getProductByImagickModel` and dispatches `ProductActions.loadImagickModel(data)`; else clears it.
- `setupSocketInfo(data)` — establishes the `socket.io-client` connection (`socketIOClient(data.server, {query:{id: group_chat, pass: session}})`) and wires its handlers: `'connection'` → `checkConnectionInfo()`; `'channel'`/`'message'`/`'user-typing'`/`'stop-typing'` → no-ops; `'receive-new-msg'` → parses the envelope, ignores self-originated messages, routes chat-type commands to the `DrawTool` `'message:update'` event, otherwise stamps bookkeeping fields and pushes onto `lstCommand` (unless superseded by a newer queued command for the same item), starting the 200ms `checkChangePropertiesItem` interval if not already running, and scheduling a `calcPrice()` refresh 3s later; `'disconnect'` → sets `DrawTool.disconnectSocket = true` for later recovery.

### Lifecycle
- `componentDidMount()` — resets `DrawTool.lastTimeTosend`, calls `setupSocketInfo(infoRoomDesign.server)`, loads full chat history via `getAllMessageChat`, and subscribes to four `DrawTool` custom events: `'message:update'` (routes new-message/delete-message/room-update payloads, including re-fetching room membership via `checkUserInfo` on `UPDATE_ROOM`), `'draw:update'` (routes incoming remote canvas-edit notifications to the appropriate `check*`/`reload*` method based on a `model` field — `'all' | 'side' | 'item' | 'update_item' | 'image_item' | 'color' | 'layer'` — with an early-return bypass while in `EMBROIDER`/`EMBROIDER_EXTEND` special-draw mode), `'online:update'` (local edits needing broadcast, routed to `commandToSocket`), and `'online:finish_chat'` (auto-posts a "デザインが出来上がりました。ご退室ください!" completion message via `onSubmit`).

### Render Structure
A chat-bubble launcher icon (Font Awesome comment icon) with an unread-count badge (`numberUncount`, derived from messages with `status_update === false`), opening the chat window on click; a "非表示" (hide) button shown while open; the chat window itself (`chat-window-show`/`chat-window-hidden`) containing a header (room name from `infoRoomDesign.room_info.ChatRoomDesign.name_group`, a live participant count derived from `room_detail` entries where `chat_room_detail.deleted == "2"`, and the room's chat ID), an `InputChat` component (`onSubmit`, `onLeave` → `onLeaveRoom`, with a ref for focus/clear control), and a `MessageList` component (wired to `lstMessage`, `isLoadingMessge`, the current user id, the author list, `onScrollMessage`, and `onDeleteMessage`).

---

## src/containers/Options/GridChange.jsx

### Purpose
`GridChange` is a small stateless "Options" panel exposing a form to configure the canvas's alignment/snap grid (row and column counts) — presumably surfaced from the Toolbar's "ガイド" (guide/grid) button. It is the simplest file in this batch: a functional component with no lifecycle and a single DOM-reading event handler.

### Imports
`React`, `updateGridSize` (action creator, actions/draw-tool), `connect` (react-redux).

### State
None — `GridChange` is a stateless functional component; it has no `this.state` and reads current values purely from props (used as `defaultValue` on uncontrolled inputs) and writes changes by reading the DOM directly.

### Props
- `grid_rows` (number, from `mapStateToProps` → `state.drawTool.grid_rows`) — current grid row count, used as the row input's `defaultValue`.
- `grid_cols` (number, from `mapStateToProps` → `state.drawTool.grid_cols`) — current grid column count, used as the column input's `defaultValue`.
- `updateGridSize` (function, from `mapDispatchToProps`) — dispatches the `updateGridSize(data)` Redux action.

### Methods
- `handleChangeGrid(e)` — reads the raw DOM values of `#grid_row` and `#grid_col` via `document.getElementById(...).value` (the inputs are uncontrolled, not React-managed state), parses both as integers, and calls `props.updateGridSize({grid_rows, grid_cols})`.

### Lifecycle
None (functional component).

### Render Structure
A `.form-grid` div containing a `.list-field` with two `.number-field` blocks — "Rows" (`#grid_row`, `defaultValue={props.grid_rows}`) and "Cols" (`#grid_col`, `defaultValue={props.grid_cols}`) — separated by `.clearfix` spacer divs, followed by a "Change" `<button>` whose `onClick` calls `handleChangeGrid`.

# Part 6 — Components, Batch 1 (3D previews, color pickers, dropdowns, filters, fonts)

## UP-T Components — Batch 1 (17 files)

---

## src/components/3d-laser-preview/index.jsx

### Purpose
`Preview3DLaser` renders a Three.js-based interactive 3D preview used for laser/acrylic-cut products (keychains, acrylic stands, "Fuiger" figures). It converts the 2D fabric.js canvas artwork on the active `DrawTool` side into extruded 3D geometry (using SVG tracing + CSG boolean subtraction to cut holes and pedestal joints), textures the front face with the printed design, and renders a rotatable/zoomable acrylic model with an optional pedestal/stand. It also supports capturing a screenshot of the 3D view for download.

### Props
- `dispatch` — Redux dispatch function (used to update process-bar progress and loading flags via `DrawToolActions`/`ProductActions`).
- `product` — Redux product state slice, used for the downloaded file's title (`this.props.product.product.title`).

### State
This component keeps no `this.state`; all mutable data lives on instance fields set in the constructor:
- `scene`, `camera`, `renderer`, `controls` — Three.js core objects (`null` until `init()`).
- `width`, `height` — computed canvas render dimensions.
- `loading` — boolean loading flag (initially `true`, not read elsewhere in this file).
- `ratio` — camera aspect ratio, updated on resize.
- `zoomVal` — current camera zoom multiplier (starts at 1).
- `rotationRate` — cumulative Y rotation angle in radians (starts at 0).
- `is_mobile` — result of a `matchMedia('(max-width: 1079px)')` check.
- `background3D` — array of background image filenames used for the background picker UI.
- `imageProcessing` — instance of `MethodImageProcessing` used to compute laser/acrylic path data from the current `DrawTool` side.
- `checkInfoFuiger` — array (unused elsewhere, initialized empty).
- `groupPedestal` — Three.js `Group` holding the pedestal mesh (set during `addShapepedestal`), used by `setupAnglePedestal`.
- `anglePedestal` — current pedestal rotation angle (0 or Math.PI), toggled by `setupAnglePedestal`.
- `group` — top-level Three.js `Group` added to the scene, created in `init()`.
- `checkLoading` — `{ stepPedestal, stepStand }` booleans tracking async CSG completion, used by `checkFinisLoadingAcrylic`.

### Methods
- `resizeRendererToDisplaySize(renderer)` — compares renderer canvas pixel size to `window.innerWidth/innerHeight`; calls `this.renderer.setSize(width, height, false)` if they differ; returns whether a resize occurred.
- `componentDidMount()` — calls `this.init()` then `this.animate()`.
- `createCanvasFromDrawTool(index, setup=null)` — grabs `DrawTool.sides._collection[index]`, temporarily moves any items with `pathIndex > 0` off-canvas (`left/top = -1000`) so they're excluded from the exported preview, renders the canvas, calls `getImagePreviewTransparent` to get a base64 PNG, restores item positions, builds a `THREE.TextureLoader` texture (with `flipX/flipY`, `SRGBColorSpace`, tiling repeat computed from the side's cm border size), and returns a `THREE.MeshPhongMaterial` using that texture.
- `setupAnglePedestal` (arrow fn) — toggles `this.anglePedestal` between `0` and `Math.PI`, applies the rotation to `this.groupPedestal`, and re-renders the scene once.
- `findInfoData(path)` (arrow fn) — draws a filled `Path2D` region (built from a flat `[x,y,x,y,...]` point array) onto an offscreen canvas sized to the side's cm border, then runs `ImageTracer.imageToTracedataCanvas`/`getsvgstring` to produce an SVG path string representing that region in grayscale (used to build cut-hole/shape geometry via `SVGLoader`).
- `addStand(shape, extrudeSettings, color, s, hole=null)` (arrow fn) — builds the acrylic "stand" (the flat base piece) as extruded geometry with the printed texture on the front face and a plain colored back; if a `hole` shape is supplied it uses `CSG.fromMesh`/`.subtract()`/`CSG.toMesh` (staged with `setTimeout` delays to avoid blocking the UI) to boolean-subtract the hole from both textured and colored meshes, groups the results, and adds them to `this.group`; sets `checkLoading.stepStand = true` and calls `checkFinisLoadingAcrylic()` when done. Dispatches `DrawToolActions.updateProcessBar` at several progress fractions.
- `checkFinisLoadingAcrylic` (arrow fn) — once both `checkLoading.stepPedestal` and `checkLoading.stepStand` are true (checked immediately and again after a 500 ms `setTimeout`), dispatches `DrawToolActions.setLoadingProcess(false)` and `ProductActions.setLoadingData(false)` to hide the app's loading spinner.
- `shapeToPolygon(shape, divisions=32)` (arrow fn) — converts a `THREE.Shape` into a `polygon-clipping`-compatible ring array `[[x,y],...]`, closing the ring if needed.
- `polygonToShape(poly)` (arrow fn) — inverse of the above: builds a `THREE.Shape` (with holes for rings after the first) from a polygon-clipping style polygon.
- `addHolesToShapes(shapes, holes)` (arrow fn) — for each hole shape, converts to a polygon; for each input shape, computes `polygonClipping.intersection`/`difference` against every hole polygon to carve holes out, returning the resulting array of `THREE.Shape`s (used before extrusion so a real geometric hole exists, in addition to CSG-based holes elsewhere).
- `addShape(shape, extrudeSettings, color, x, y, z, rx, ry, rz, s, infoLocation, hole=null)` (arrow fn) — builds the main non-acrylic-pedestal laser shape ("Fuiger"/figure mode): applies `addHolesToShapes`, extrudes a textured front mesh and a colored back mesh, offsets them along Z by a computed `calculatorDistance`, positions/rotates/scales both, adds to `this.group`, then dispatches process-bar updates and finally `setLoadingProcess(false)`/`setLoadingData(false)`.
- `addCamera()` — creates a `THREE.PerspectiveCamera`, positions it based on `is_mobile` (fixed distance along Z, `lookAt(0,0,0)` only in `ACRYLIC_LASER` mode), and creates `OrbitControls` (damping enabled, panning disabled, no auto-rotate).
- `addLight()` — adds six `THREE.PointLight`s around the scene (front/back/left/right/top/bottom) for even acrylic lighting.
- `defaultBorder(enableArray=false)` — builds a rectangular `THREE.Shape` matching the current side's cm border size, used as a fallback shape when no drawn path exists; returns it wrapped in an array if `enableArray` is true.
- `smonthPathLaserClipInfo(pointsC)` — weighted 3-point smoothing pass over a flat point array based on inverse squared distance to neighbors (used by acrylic path smoothing, appears to be an earlier/alternate variant of `smonthPathLaserClipX`).
- `smonthPathLaserClip(pointsC)` — a more elaborate smoothing pass that computes local tangent angles to decide a variable smoothing window (`maxfillter`/`numberData`), then averages neighboring points within that window; used (3x in a row) inside `smonthPathLaser`.
- `smonthPathLaserClipXY(pointsC)` — duplicate of `smonthPathLaserClipInfo` (same distance-weighted smoothing), unused directly elsewhere in this file (legacy/dead code retained).
- `smonthPathLaserClipX(points)` — a cleaner rewrite of the weighted-neighbor smoothing (normalizes the three weights to sum to 1) — also not directly called elsewhere in this file (alternate/legacy implementation).
- `smonthPathLaser(points)` — rounds each coordinate, removes consecutive duplicate points, then runs `smonthPathLaserClip` three times in sequence to smooth the traced laser-cut path.
- `getPointExacty(data)` — offsets a flat point array by the side's `border.cm.left/top`, and mirrors the Y coordinate around the vertical center (`centery`) — used to convert fabric.js canvas coordinates into the mirrored coordinate space needed for the 3D/laser export.
- `getInfoHoleLaser()` — reads `DrawTool.sides.selected.enableHoleFuiger` (deep-cloned), converts each hole's point path via `getPointExacty`/`smonthPathLaser`, traces it to SVG via `findInfoData`, parses with `SVGLoader`, and collects the resulting `THREE.Shape`s; returns `null` if none found.
- `getPointAngleInfo(data)` — computes the turn angle at each point of a path (via `Math.atan2`) and flags points whose angle repeats the same value as neighbors as `remove: true` (candidate colinear-point pruning); returns the annotated point list (not directly consumed elsewhere in this file — utility/legacy).
- `getInfoAcrylicLaser()` — uses `this.imageProcessing.getInfoAcrylicLaser(...)` to get raw laser path data for acrylic mode, smooths/traces each path into a `THREE.Shape`, and wraps the results between two degenerate zero-area boundary shapes (start/end) that pin the overall bounding box to `(0,0)`–`(width,height)`.
- `getHoleAcrylicLaser()` — same pattern as above but sourced from `this.imageProcessing.getHoleAcrylicLaser(...)`, producing hole shapes for the acrylic body.
- `getHoleStandLaser()` — sourced from `this.imageProcessing.getloadPathBorder(DrawTool.sides.selected, true)`, producing hole shapes for the stand piece.
- `getInfoAcrylicStand()` — sourced from `this.imageProcessing.getloadPathBorder(DrawTool.sides.selected)` (no hole flag), with the same start/end boundary-shape padding as `getInfoAcrylicLaser`.
- `getInfoFuigerLaser()` — reads `DrawTool.sides.selected.enableFuiger` (deep-cloned), converts each entry's points (without extra smoothing) into `THREE.Shape`s via `SVGLoader`.
- `addShapepedestal(shape, extrudeSettings, color, s, hole=null, hole_position=null)` — builds the acrylic "pedestal" (the vertical display piece that slots into the stand): similar CSG subtract flow to `addStand`, but the resulting group is rotated to stand upright (`rotation.set(-Math.PI/2, 0, angle)`) and, if `hole_position` is given, positioned at that hole's `x/y`; stores the final group on `this.groupPedestal` (nested inside a new `THREE.Group` also assigned to `this.groupPedestal`) and adds it to `this.group`; sets `checkLoading.stepPedestal = true` and calls `checkFinisLoadingAcrylic()`.
- `init()` — main scene bootstrap: creates `THREE.Scene` with fog and a loaded background texture, sizes the renderer to the `#canvas_3d_preview` canvas (falling back to a 900px-tall virtual size on desktop), creates the `WebGLRenderer`, calls `addCamera()`/`addLight()`, creates the root `group` (Y-offset differs slightly for `ACRYLIC_LASER` mode and mobile), computes `DrawTool.mmLaser_Limmit` (bevel thickness derived from the side's `mmLaser`/`mmLaserEx`) if not already cached, then branches:
  - If `DrawTool.modeToolDraw === DrawTool.modeSetup.ACRYLIC_LASER`: resets `checkLoading`, computes hole/shape data via `getHoleAcrylicLaser`/`getInfoAcrylicLaser` (falling back to `defaultBorder()`), reads pedestal placement info from `DrawTool.sides.selected.pedestal` if present, calls `addShapepedestal(...)` and then `addStand(...)` for the stand piece (only if a real shape was found).
  - Otherwise (Fuiger/figure laser mode): computes hole/shape via `getInfoHoleLaser`/`getInfoFuigerLaser` (falling back to `defaultBorder(true)`), and calls `addShape(...)`.
- `disposeNode(node)` (arrow fn) — disposes a mesh's geometry and material (including `map`, `lightMap`, `bumpMap`, `normalMap`, `specularMap`, `envMap`), or detaches non-mesh nodes from their parent.
- `disposeHierarchy(node, callback)` (arrow fn) — recursively walks a node's children (depth-first, from the end), invoking `callback` (typically `disposeNode`) on each.
- `componentWillUnmount()` — tears down the whole Three.js scene: iteratively removes/disposes all `scene`, `camera`, `renderer`, `group`, and `controls` children via `disposeHierarchy`, disposes the renderer/controls, nulls out `scene/camera/renderer/controls/group`, clears any child DOM nodes under `#canvas_3d_preview`, and cancels the animation frame loop.
- `animate()` — the `requestAnimationFrame` render loop: updates `controls`, renders the scene, requests the next frame, and calls `resizeRendererToDisplaySize` to keep the camera aspect/projection matrix in sync with window size.
- `zoomIn()` / `zoomOut()` — adjust `this.zoomVal` by ±0.2 and apply it to `this.camera.zoom`, then `updateProjectionMatrix()`.
- `rotationObj()` — decrements `this.rotationRate` by 15° (in radians) and applies it to `this.group.rotation.y`, then renders once.
- `downloadImageToCrop` (arrow fn) — captures the current renderer canvas as a data URL, opens a SweetAlert2 (`Swal.fire`) modal containing a `<canvas id="chartCanvas">` and a download button; on open, builds a `CropFabricImage` instance (`MethodImageProcessing`-adjacent utility) to let the user crop/compose the screenshot, and on clicking `#canvas_to_url` exports the final data URL and triggers a browser download named after `this.props.product.product.title`.
- `render()` — see Render Structure.

### Render Structure
Renders a `.App` wrapper containing `#3d-rendering`:
- Two `MediaQuery` blocks (desktop `min-width:1080px` and mobile `max-width:1079px`), each rendering a near-identical control UI:
  - A "180° pedestal rotate" `Button` shown only when `DrawTool.modeToolDraw === ACRYLIC_LASER`, calling `setupAnglePedestal()`.
  - Drag/zoom hint buttons (non-interactive, `active` styling only).
  - Rotate/zoom-in/zoom-out `Button`s wired to `rotationObj`, `zoomIn`, `zoomOut`.
  - A download-image `Button` wired to `downloadImageToCrop`.
  - A `.background-list-3d` row mapping `this.background3D` to `Button`s that swap `this.scene.background` to the clicked image.
- A `<canvas id="canvas_3d_preview">` (min-height 800 on desktop) that Three.js renders into.

Connected via `connect(mapStateToProps)`, mapping only `product: state.product`.

---

## src/components/3d-preview/index.jsx

### Purpose
`Preview3D` is the general-purpose Three.js 3D product previewer (T-shirts, cups, phone cases, handbags, cards) used in the main product editor, as opposed to the laser/acrylic-specific previewer above. It loads a GLTF model (optionally from an in-memory buffer for offline/cached loads), applies per-side printed textures from `DrawTool`'s fabric canvases onto the model's mapped mesh regions, colors the model according to the selected product color (with special-casing for pure black/white), and provides camera controls (rotate/zoom) plus a screenshot/crop/download flow. Per-product-type camera and light rigs are defined for `T_SHIRT`, `CUP`, `PHONE`, `HANDBAG`, and `TYPE_CARD` (from `DRAW_3D_DEFFUNE`).

### Props
- `shapes3DSelect` — Redux `state.drawTool.shapes3DSelect` object describing the selected 3D model: `.url` (GLTF path), `.type_mesh`, `.info.Side3D` (array of `{childID, mtl}` texture-target descriptors and 3D→2D side index mapping, may include `.side`/`.overWriteImage` for overlay compositing), `.position` (`type_product`, `model_position{.x,.y,.z,.xs,.ys,.zs}`, `camera_position{...}`, optionally `children` for handbag texture-mapped submeshes, optionally `lock_color`), `.selected`.
- `dispatch` — Redux dispatch, used for `DrawToolActions.updateProcessBar`/`setLoadingProcess`.
- `product` — Redux `state.product`, used for the downloaded screenshot filename.
- `colorSelected` — Redux `state.product.colorSelected` (`{ value: '#rrggbb' }`), the active product color; mutated in place by `changeColorSelected`.
- `buffer3d` — optional pre-fetched GLTF binary buffer; when present, `loader.loadBuffer` is used instead of `loader.load`.

### State
No `this.state`; instance fields set in the constructor:
- `scene`, `camera`, `renderer`, `controls` — Three.js objects.
- `initial_MAP` — cached copy of `shapes3DSelect.info.Side3D` with an `.mtl` field attached to each entry (the initial placeholder material before textures load).
- `width`, `height` — render target size.
- `theModel` — the loaded GLTF scene root.
- `loading` — boolean flag (set false once the model finishes loading).
- `ratio` — camera aspect ratio.
- `zoomVal` — camera zoom multiplier.
- `rotationRate` — cumulative rotation (initialized to `Math.PI`).
- `is_mobile` — mobile media-query flag.
- `background3D` — background image filenames array.
- `INITIAL_MTL` — placeholder `THREE.MeshMatcapMaterial`/`MeshBasicMaterial` chosen based on `shapes3DSelect.type_mesh`.

### Methods
- `changeColorSelected` (arrow fn, called from constructor) — normalizes `this.props.colorSelected.value`: pure black (`#000000`) becomes `#202020`; pure white becomes `#C9C9C9`. Called for `CUP`, `PHONE`, `HANDBAG`, `TYPE_CARD` product types in the constructor; `T_SHIRT` only gets the black→`#202020` fix inline.
- `checkInfoVale(value)` (arrow fn) — converts a 0–255 numeric channel value into a `#RRGGBB`-style grayscale hex string using a hand-rolled hex digit lookup (utility, not called elsewhere in this file).
- `initColor(parent, type, mtl)` — traverses `parent`'s meshes and, for any mesh whose `.name` *includes* `type`, assigns `mtl` as its material and tags it with `nameID = type` (used for initial placeholder materials keyed by partial name match).
- `initColorMap(parent, type, mtl)` — traverses `parent`'s meshes and, for any mesh whose `.name` *equals* `type` exactly, builds a new `THREE.MeshBasicMaterial` using `mtl` as the map texture — colored by `colorSelected.value` unless `shapes3DSelect.position.lock_color` is set (used specifically for handbag texture-mapped submeshes).
- `setMaterial(parent, type, mtl)` — traverses `parent`'s meshes and, for any mesh already tagged with `nameID === type`, replaces its material with `mtl` (used to swap in the final printed-texture materials once loaded).
- `resizeRendererToDisplaySize(renderer)` — same pattern as the laser-preview version: resizes the renderer if the canvas's pixel size no longer matches `window.innerWidth/innerHeight`.
- `addImageProcess=(img)=>` — returns a Promise that resolves `true` once `img.onload` fires, after setting `img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous'`.
- `overwriteImage(currentBase64, overWriteImage, inputSetup=null, sideInfo=0)` (async) — draws `currentBase64` onto an offscreen canvas, then composites a second image (`overWriteImage`, cache-busted via `?not-from-cache-please` unless `inputSetup.overWriteImageCache` is provided) on top at 40% alpha, scaled/positioned per `inputSetup.map3D` metrics; returns the resulting composited data URL. Used when a 3D side has an explicit overlay image (e.g., product mockup lines) to blend with the printed design.
- `createCanvasFromDrawTool(index, side)` (async) — maps a 3D "side" index to the corresponding `DrawTool.sides._collection` index (with special-cased swap of indices 2/3 for `T_SHIRT`, or using `side.side` directly if provided), extracts a transparent preview PNG via `getImagePreviewTransparent`, optionally overlays it via `overwriteImage` when `side.side` is set, builds a flipped/repeating `THREE.TextureLoader` texture with anisotropic filtering, and returns a `THREE.MeshMatcapMaterial` using that texture.
- `componentDidMount()` — calls `this.init()` then `this.animate()`.
- `downloadImageToCrop=()=>` — same SweetAlert2 crop/download flow as the laser preview, using `CropFabricImage`, downloading a PNG named after `this.props.product.product.title`.
- `show3DModel=(gltf, dataInfoMTL)=>` — given a loaded `gltf` and an array of per-side printed materials (`dataInfoMTL`): sets `castShadow`/`receiveShadow` on every mesh, tints mesh `color`/`emissive` to `colorSelected.value` unless `lock_color` is set, dispatches process-bar progress, scales the model, rotates it 180° on Y, positions it per `model_position` (mobile vs desktop variants), for `HANDBAG` type maps texture data from named children via `initColorMap`, applies the placeholder `initial_MAP` materials via `initColor`, adds the model to the scene, then applies the real per-side printed materials via `setMaterial` for each `Side3D` entry, and finally clears `loading` and dispatches `updateProcessBar(0.99)`/`setLoadingProcess(false)`.
- `init()` (async) — chooses `INITIAL_MTL` based on `shapes3DSelect.type_mesh` (1 → `MeshMatcapMaterial`, else `MeshBasicMaterial`), builds `initial_MAP` from `shapes3DSelect.info.Side3D`, creates the `THREE.Scene` with a fog + background texture, sizes/creates the `WebGLRenderer` attached to `#canvas_3d_preview`, appends the renderer's canvas into `#3d-rendering`, calls `addCamera()`/`addLight()`, builds `dataInfoMTL` by awaiting `createCanvasFromDrawTool` for every `Side3D` entry, then loads the GLTF (`loader.loadBuffer` if `this.props.buffer3d` is set, else `loader.load`) and calls `show3DModel` on success, logging errors and clearing the loading state on failure.
- `disposeNode=(node)=>` — same geometry/material disposal logic as the laser preview.
- `disposeHierarchy=(node,callback)=>` — same recursive disposal walk.
- `componentWillUnmount()` — extensive teardown: disposes all scene/camera/renderer children, additionally disposes each `initial_MAP[i].mtl`'s children and resets it to `INITIAL_MTL`, disposes `theModel` and `controls` children, disposes `INITIAL_MTL` itself, clears the camera, disposes the renderer/controls, nulls all Three.js refs (including `theModel`), clears DOM children of `#canvas_3d_preview`, and cancels the animation frame.
- `animate()` — render loop identical in structure to the laser preview (updates controls, renders, requests next frame, handles resize/aspect updates).
- `addCamera()` — creates a `PerspectiveCamera` positioned from `shapes3DSelect.position.camera_position` (mobile `xs/ys/zs` vs desktop `x/y/z`), attaches `OrbitControls` with damping/no-pan/no-autorotate.
- `addLight()` — a `switch` on `shapes3DSelect.position.type_product` (`DRAW_3D_DEFFUNE.*`) building a distinct light rig per product type: `T_SHIRT` gets 6 point lights plus a directional light tinted with `colorSelected.value`; `CUP` gets a shadow-casting directional light plus a colored directional light; `PHONE` gets two shadow point lights plus a colored directional light; `HANDBAG` gets a single point light; `TYPE_CARD` gets two shadow point lights plus a colored directional light.
- `zoomIn()` / `zoomOut()` — adjust `zoomVal` by ±0.2 and update the camera projection.
- `rotationObj()` — rotate `theModel` by −15° increments around Y and re-render.
- `render()` — see Render Structure.

### Render Structure
`.App > #3d-rendering`, containing:
- Desktop (`MediaQuery min-width:1080px`) and mobile (`max-width:1079px`) control panels, each with drag/zoom hint buttons, rotate/zoom-in/zoom-out buttons wired to `rotationObj`/`zoomIn`/`zoomOut`, a download button wired to `downloadImageToCrop`, and a `.background-list-3d` row of background-swap buttons.
- `<canvas id="canvas_3d_preview">` for the renderer output.

Connected via `connect(mapStateToProps)` mapping `shapes3DSelect`, `colorSelected`, and `product`.

---

## src/components/3d-triple/index.jsx

### Purpose
`Preview3DTriple` is a standalone 3D model viewer/exporter (distinct from `Preview3D`/`Preview3DLaser`) used to display and re-export an already-generated GLTF/GLB model (`props.src`, arriving via a `DrawTool` custom event `option:model3d` carrying a JSON payload with `src`). Its main responsibilities beyond rendering are: capturing a PNG screenshot and uploading it (`captureScreenshot`), exporting the scene as a `.obj`+`.mtl`+textures bundle zipped client-side (`exportToZip`) and uploading it in chunks with retry logic, and triggering parent-supplied load-lifecycle callbacks (`onLoadStart`/`onLoadComplete`).

### Props
- `src` — required string (`PropTypes.string.isRequired`), though the component primarily reacts to the DrawTool event's `src` field rather than this prop directly.
- `color` — string (declared in `propTypes`, not directly referenced in the read logic).
- `dispatch` — Redux dispatch.
- `onLoadStart` — callback invoked before a model begins loading (new or replacement).
- `onLoadComplete` — callback invoked (via `setTimeout(...,3000)`) after the screenshot capture completes.
- `imageCapture` — Redux `state.product.image3DCapture` (mapped in, used to correlate a `filePair` entry when updating the zip URL).
- `filePair` — Redux `state.product.filePairs` (array of `{png, zip}` pairs).

### State
- `width`, `height` — initialized to `800`, not actively driven by resize logic shown (real client sizing lives in `width_client`/`height_client` instance fields).
- `isModelInitialized` — initialized `false`; assignment is commented out in `initialize3DModel` (dead/inactive flag).

### Instance fields (non-state)
`scene` (a live `THREE.Scene` created eagerly in the constructor), `loader` (`GLTFLoader`), `objExport` (`OBJExporter`, unused directly — export uses hand-rolled `exportToOBJ`), `model`, `rotationRate`, `background3D`, `zoomVal`, `width_client`/`height_client`, `props_src` (tracks the last-seen model URL from the DrawTool event), plus `renderer`/`camera`/`controls` created in `initialize3DModel`.

### Methods
- `initialize3DModel()` (async) — creates the `WebGLRenderer` bound to `#canvas_3d_preview_render` (alpha + antialias + `preserveDrawingBuffer`), a `PerspectiveCamera`, `OrbitControls` (damping, no pan, slow rotate), adds camera to scene, calls `setupScene`/`setupCamera`/`setupLights`, awaits `setupLoader()` then `updateDims()`, calls `updateCamera()`/`setupRenderer()`/`renderScene()`, awaits `captureScreenshot()`, schedules `this.props.onLoadComplete()` after 3s, awaits `exportToZip(this.scene)`, registers a `resize` listener (`handleResize`), and starts `animate()`.
- `checkLink3DChange=(link)=>` — toggles the CSS class on `#props_src_3d_download` between enabled/disabled (`...disable`) depending on whether `link` is a non-empty value, to gray out the "3D download" button until a zip/link is ready.
- `componentDidMount()` (async) — removes any existing `option:model3d` DrawTool listener, then re-registers one: on event, parses the JSON `data`, and if the `src` field changed from `this.props_src`, handles three cases — (1) first-ever load (`props_src` was null, new `src` non-null): calls `onLoadStart()` then `initialize3DModel()`; (2) replacing an existing model with a different non-null `src`: calls `removeModel()` if new src is null, otherwise `onLoadStart()` + `loadNewModel()`; (3) clearing to null when previously null: calls `removeModel()`. Always updates `this.props_src` and calls `checkLink3DChange`. If `this.props_src` is already set at mount time (rare/edge case), also eagerly calls `onLoadStart()` + `initialize3DModel()`.
- `shouldComponentUpdate()` — always returns `false`, so this component never re-renders from React prop/state changes; all visual updates happen imperatively via Three.js and the DrawTool event listener.
- `loadNewModel=async()=>` — removes the current `this.model` from the scene if present, calls `setupLoader()` to load the new model, re-renders, awaits `captureScreenshot()`, schedules `onLoadComplete()` after 3s, and awaits `exportToZip(this.scene)`.
- `removeModel=()=>` — traverses `this.model`'s meshes disposing geometry/material/map, removes it from the scene, nulls `this.model`, disposes the renderer's render lists, and re-renders.
- `resizeRendererToDisplaySize=()=>` — same pixel-size-mismatch resize check pattern as the other 3D components.
- `componentWillUnmount()` — calls `removeModel()` and removes the `resize` listener.
- `handleResize=async()=>` — re-runs `updateDims()`, `updateRenderer()`, `updateCamera()`, `renderScene()` on window resize.
- `renderScene=()=>` — `this.renderer.render(this.scene, this.camera)`.
- `setupCamera=()=>` — sets camera aspect from `width_client/height_client` and `position.z = 4`.
- `setupLights=()=>` — adds a `HemisphereLight`, a shadow-casting `DirectionalLight` (with configured shadow-camera frustum/bias), and a `PointLight`.
- `setupLoader=()=>` — returns a Promise; configures a `DRACOLoader` on `this.loader`, loads `this.props_src`; on success replaces `this.model`, adds it to the scene, forces every mesh's material color to white and flags `needsUpdate`, then resolves; on error, shows a SweetAlert2 error dialog ("3Dモデルの読み込みに失敗しました") and rejects.
- `setupModel=()=>` — adds a `THREE.SkeletonHelper` for `this.model` to the scene (debug/rigging visualization; not otherwise gated behind a flag in the read code).
- `setupRenderer=()=>` — sets clear color (transparent), size, gamma output/factor.
- `setupScene=()=>` — sets `scene.background` to a loaded `BG3D_Xam.jpg` texture.
- `updateCamera=()=>` — refreshes aspect ratio and calls `updateProjectionMatrix()`.
- `updateDims=()=>` — reads the renderer canvas's client size into `width_client`/`height_client`, then calls `updateCamera()`, `updateRenderer()`, `renderScene()`.
- `updateRenderer=()=>` — calls `this.renderer.setSize(width_client, height_client)`.
- `animate()` — standard render loop (controls update, render, next frame, resize check + aspect update).
- `downloadImageToCrop=()=>` — guarded by `if (this.props_src == null) return;`; otherwise renders once, captures a PNG data URL, and opens the same SweetAlert2 crop/download flow (via `CropFabricImage`) as the other previewers, downloading as `image.png`.
- `zoomIn()` / `zoomOut()` — clamp `zoomVal` between `0.1` and `10` in steps of `0.2`, apply to camera zoom + update projection.
- `rotationObjY()` / `rotationObjX()` — rotate `this.model` around Y or X by −15° increments and render once.
- `captureScreenshot=async()=>` — draws the `#canvas_3d_preview_render` canvas onto a 1024×1024 offscreen canvas, converts to a PNG data URL, uploads it via `uploadByString` (from `api/extras.js`), then dispatches `ProductActions.setImage3DCapture(url)` and `ProductActions.addFilePair({png: url, zip: ''})`. Logs errors to console on failure.
- `exportToOBJ(scene)` — hand-written Wavefront OBJ exporter: traverses meshes collecting UVs and per-material indices/names, computes vertex normals, writes `v`/`vn`/`vt`/`usemtl`/`f` lines (triangles only, using `geometry.index` if present) with running vertex/normal/UV offsets, and joins everything into a single OBJ text referencing `model.mtl`.
- `processTexture=async(material)=>` — if the material has a `map` with an image, draws it onto a canvas and resolves `{name, blob}` via `canvas.toBlob(...,'image/png')`; returns `null` if there's no texture.
- `splitBlob=(blobData, chunkSizeMB=18)=>` — slices a `Blob` into an array of chunks no larger than `chunkSizeMB` MB, for chunked upload.
- `uploadChunk=async(chunk, chunkIndex, totalChunks, fileName, sessionId, is_last, arrayFile)=>` — POSTs one chunk (plus metadata: index/total/filename/session/is_last/array_file JSON) via `exportZipFile` (from `api/extras.js`); returns `{data, return: true}` on success or `{data:"", return:false}` on falsy response; logs errors.
- `uploadLargeFile=async(blob, fileName, sessionId)=>` — splits the blob via `splitBlob(...,18)` and uploads each chunk sequentially via `uploadChunk`, accumulating responses into `arrayListFile`; returns `false` on any chunk failure, otherwise returns the final chunk's response data once the last chunk succeeds.
- `exportToZip(scene)` (async) — builds a `JSZip` containing `model.obj` (from `exportToOBJ`), `model.mtl` (from `generateMTL` over all unique materials found via `scene.traverse`), and each material's texture (`processTexture`); generates the zip as a `Blob` (`DEFLATE`, level 6) and — with up to 3 retry attempts — uploads it via `uploadLargeFile(content, 'models.zip', query.session)`; on success, finds the matching `filePair` entry by `png === imageCapture` and dispatches `ProductActions.setZipObjFile3d(response)` plus `ProductActions.updateFilePair({index, filePair: {zip: response}})`.
- `generateMTL(materials)` — builds a minimal Wavefront `.mtl` text block per material (`Ka/Kd/Ks/Ns/Ni/d/illum`, plus `map_Kd` if a texture map exists).
- `render()` — see Render Structure.

### Render Structure
`.viewer` (light-blue background) containing:
- `<canvas id="canvas_3d_preview_render">`.
- `.background-action > .background-list-3d-render` — buttons per `background3D` entry that swap `this.scene.background`.
- `.action-3d` — rotate-Y/rotate-X/zoom-in/zoom-out buttons (`rotationObjY`, `rotationObjX`, `zoomIn`, `zoomOut`), plus a `#props_src_3d_download` control (disabled until a link is set, per `checkLink3DChange`) that calls `downloadImageToCrop` and shows a "3D画像ダウンロード" label with a capture icon.

Connected via `connect(mapStateToProps)` mapping `imageCapture` and `filePair`.

---

## src/components/color-picker/index.jsx

### Purpose
`ColorPicker` is the main color-selection widget (a swatch button that opens a popover) used throughout the drawing UI for picking fill/stroke/etc. colors. It composites three sub-pickers — `PreinstalledPicker` (product-appropriate preset swatches), `LastUsagePicker` (recently used colors), and `PalettePicker` (a full RGBA `SketchPicker`) — behind a two-tab UI ("コレクション"/collection vs "カスタム"/custom), and tracks an "original" color so a Cancel action can revert. It also has mobile-Safari-specific handling that temporarily removes a CSS `transform` on `#block-options` while the picker is open (worked around iOS popover clipping issues) and restores it on accept/cancel.

### Props
- `color` — string, the initial/controlled color value (`propTypes.string`).
- `label` — string, text shown next to the swatch.
- `onChange` — function, called with the new color (and, in fill mode, also the previous color) whenever the color changes/commits.
- `is_fill` — (not in `propTypes` but read via `this.props.is_fill`) boolean flag altering `onChange` call signature and popover `id`.
- `transform` — (not in `propTypes`, read via `this.props.transform`) CSS transform string restored onto `#block-options` on accept/cancel for mobile.
- `active` — (read via `this.props.active`) when `"brush"`, adds a `color-picker-brush` class.
- `is_embroidery` — injected via `mapStateToProps` from `state.drawTool.is_embroidery`; hides the tab-links bar when true.

### State
- `displayColorPicker` — whether the popover is open.
- `displayThisTabOne` / `displayThisTabTwo` — which of the two tabs ("コレクション"/"カスタム") is active.
- `displayLastUsagePicker` — whether the "recently used" + preinstalled block has been revealed at least once (set true on first click and stays true).
- `showLastUsegeBlock` — whether the last-used-colors row has any colors to show (set true once a color is pushed).
- `color` — current live color (`rgba(...)` string), initialized from `props.color` or a default blue.
- `original_color` — the color to revert to on Cancel; updated to `color` on Accept.
- `lastColors` — array (max 6) of recently used `rgba(...)` strings, most-recent-first.
- `getLastcolor` — initialized to `''`, not read/used elsewhere in the file.
- `is_fill` — copied from `props.is_fill` (note: uses `|` not `||`, a likely bug, but functionally coerces truthy/falsy for the common cases used).
- `transform` — copied from `props.transform`.
- `colorCheck` — (not declared in the initial state object but assigned dynamically) holds the in-progress picked color object/string used by `handleColorUpdate`/`setColorByPick`.

### Methods
- `componentWillReceiveProps(props)` — syncs `state.color` whenever the `color` prop changes.
- `handleClick()` — toggles `displayColorPicker`; the first time it's opened also flips `displayLastUsagePicker` on; closes tab-two display if tab-one is active; on mobile devices (regex UA sniff) with `is_fill`, removes the `#block-options` CSS transform so the popover isn't clipped.
- `handleClose()` — sets `displayColorPicker: false` (bound to a click on the `.color-picker-substrate` overlay).
- `handleChangeComplete(color, is_cancel)` — normalizes `color` into an `rgba(...)` string (unless `is_cancel`, in which case `color` is already a string), updates `state.color`, and calls `props.onChange`: in fill mode, if `is_cancel` it reports the previous `colorCheck` value alongside `original_color`; otherwise it reports either the most recent `lastColors[0]` or `original_color` alongside the new `rgb`; in non-fill mode it simply calls `onChange(rgb)`.
- `handleAccept()` — closes the popover and commits `original_color = state.color`; restores the mobile `#block-options` transform from `props.transform` if applicable.
- `handleCancelPick()` — reverts `state.color` to `original_color`, closes the popover, clears `lastColors`, sets `colorCheck` back to `original_color`, calls `handleChangeComplete(original_color, true)` to notify the parent of the revert, and restores the mobile transform.
- `handleColorUpdate(colorValue)` — stores `colorValue.rgb || colorValue` into `state.colorCheck` (note: mutates `this.state` directly rather than via `setState`, a minor anti-pattern) and calls `setColorByPick()`. Passed down to all three sub-pickers as their color-change callback.
- `setColorByPick()` — toggles which tab is "active" bookkeeping (tab-one → shows tab-two next call, tab-two → keeps colorCheck), calls `handleChangeComplete(colorCheck, false)` to commit the pick, calls `pushLastUsageArr(colorCheck)` to record it in recents, and clears any `activeColor` CSS class via jQuery.
- `pushLastUsageArr(val)` — builds an `rgba(...)` string from `val`, and maintains `state.lastColors` as a most-recent-first, de-duplicated list capped at 6 entries (removes any existing occurrence of the same color before unshifting; drops the oldest entry once at capacity); sets `showLastUsegeBlock: true`.
- `handleChangeTabsOne()` — activates tab one, deactivates tab two, and (via `setTimeout` with a string argument — legacy `eval`-style API) re-runs `$('.colorpicker').wheelColorPicker('updateSelection')` (references a jQuery plugin not otherwise visible in this file, presumably dead/legacy code).
- `handleChangeTabsTwo()` — activates tab two (deactivating tab one) unless `DrawTool.modeToolDraw` is one of the ribbon-related modes (`TAP_RIBBON`, `TAP_RIBBON_2EDIT`, `RIBON_EXTEND_COLOR`), in which case the custom tab is disabled for those tools.
- `render()` — see Render Structure.

### Render Structure
- `.color-picker` (adds `.color-picker-brush` if `active === 'brush'`), containing:
  - `.color-picker-substrate` overlay (click → `handleClose`), shown when open.
  - `.swatch` (click → `handleClick`) showing the current color square and `label`.
  - `.popover` (shown when open; `id="fill"` if `is_fill`) containing `.jm-color-picker`:
    - `.tab-links-wrap` with "コレクション"/"カスタム" tab links (hidden entirely if `is_embroidery`).
    - `.header > .list-buttons` with "キャンセル" (Cancel → `handleCancelPick`) and "決定" (Accept → `handleAccept`) buttons.
    - Tab-one content (`.tab-content-custom`): `.last-usage-wrap` housing `<LastUsagePicker>` (shown/hidden via `showLastUsegeBlock`), and — once `displayLastUsagePicker` is true — a `.preinstalled-wrap` housing `<PreinstalledPicker>`.
    - Tab-two content (`.tab-content-colors`): `.left-place` housing `<PalettePicker>`.

Connected via `connect(mapStateToProps)` mapping `is_embroidery` from `state.drawTool.is_embroidery`.

## src/components/color-picker/last-usage-picker.jsx

### Purpose
`LastUsagePicker` renders the "recently used colors" row inside the main color picker, using `react-color`'s `CirclePicker` to display swatches for each color in `props.colors`.

### Props
- `colors` — array of color strings (each entry pushed into local `state.colors` via `componentWillReceiveProps`).
- `updateColor` — callback invoked with the picked color's `.rgb` object when a swatch is clicked.

### State
- `circleSize` — fixed at `36`.
- `circleSpacing` — fixed at `23`.
- `width` — fixed at `354`.
- `colors` — (set via `componentWillReceiveProps`, not in the constructor) local copy of `props.colors` used at render time.

### Methods
- `handleColorChange(e)` — calls `this.props.updateColor(e.rgb)`.
- `componentWillReceiveProps(nextProps)` — sets `state.colors = nextProps.colors`.
- `render()` — copies `state.colors` into a plain array (`Array.prototype.forEach.call`, guarding against array-like quirks, per an inline "stuppid bug" comment) and renders a `<CirclePicker>` with that `colors` array, sized per state, calling `handleColorChange` on selection.

### Render Structure
A single `react-color` `<CirclePicker>`.

- `color` — passed straight through as the `SketchPicker`'s controlled `color` value.

None.

None beyond the constructor (which just calls `super(props)`).

## src/components/color-picker/preinstalled-picker.jsx

### Purpose
`PreinstalledPicker` renders the preset/"collection" swatch palette shown in the color picker's first tab. The available color set is context-sensitive: it defaults to `DrawTool.colors` (the app's standard preset palette), but switches to hard-coded ribbon-specific palettes when the active `DrawTool` tool mode is one of the tap-ribbon modes, and to a reduced embroidery-safe palette when `is_embroidery` is true.

### Props
- `updateColor` — callback invoked with the picked color's `.rgb` object.
- `is_embroidery` — injected via `mapStateToProps` from `state.drawTool.is_embroidery`.

### State
- `colors` — initialized to `DrawTool.colors` (may be overridden by render-time logic below rather than by re-setting state).
- `circleSize` — `36`.
- `circleSpacing` — `23`.
- `width` — `354`.

### Methods
- `handleColorChange(e)` — calls `this.props.updateColor(e.rgb)`.
- `componentDidMount()` — attaches a jQuery click handler to `.preinstalled-wrap span div div` elements that clears any `.activeColor` class under `.tab-content-custom` and adds `.activeColor` to the clicked swatch (purely visual "selected swatch" highlighting, redundant with React state but implemented imperatively).
- `render()` — branches on `DrawTool.modeToolDraw`:
  - `TAP_RIBBON` → fixed 4-color palette (`red, blue, white, black`).
  - `TAP_RIBBON_2EDIT` → fixed 5-color palette (`red, blue, black, rgba(193,171,5,1), silver`).
  - `RIBON_EXTEND_COLOR` → uses `DrawTool.colorSetup` as the color list.
  - Otherwise, if not `is_embroidery` → uses `state.colors` (i.e. `DrawTool.colors`).
  - Otherwise (embroidery) → fixed 6-color palette (`red, green, blue, yellow, white, black`).
  Each branch renders a `<CirclePicker>` with the chosen colors and `handleColorChange` as `onChangeComplete`.

### Render Structure
A single `react-color` `<CirclePicker>`, with its `colors` prop chosen per the branching logic above.

Connected via `connect(mapStateToProps)` mapping `is_embroidery`.

## src/components/dieCut/dialog.jsx

### Purpose
`DialogDieCut` is the content component rendered inside a SweetAlert2 dialog (via `sweetalert2-react-content`) that lets the user multi-select design layers/items on the canvas and bulk-delete them — used for the "die cut" / cutting-line management flow. It also special-cases deletion of items that participate in an "acrylic laser" cutting relationship (`pathIndex === DrawTool.modeSetup.ACRYLIC_LASER`), cascading deletion to their linked `mode_design` father/child items.

### Props
- `dispatch` — Redux dispatch (destructured in `deleteItem` but not actually used to dispatch any action in the read code — likely vestigial).
- `layersAll` — array of layer indices to include when listing selectable items (filters `DrawTool.sides._collection[0].layers.list`).

### State
- `itemSelect` — array of selected item UUIDs (from `item.index`, toggled on checkbox click).

### Methods
- `selectOption = (index) =>` — toggles `index` in/out of `state.itemSelect` (adds if absent, removes if present via `splice`).
- `deleteItem = () =>` — for each selected UUID in `itemSelect`, looks up the matching fabric object in `DrawTool.sides.selected.items._collection`; if it has `pathIndex === DrawTool.modeSetup.ACRYLIC_LASER` and a `mode_design` property, records its `mode_design.id` (and `father_id` if non-zero) into `id_cutting_acrylic` for cascade deletion; removes the object from the Fabric canvas and splices it out of the items collection; re-renders the canvas after each removal. After the main loop, for every recorded `id_cutting_acrylic` id, finds any remaining item whose `mode_design.id` matches and removes/splices it too, then re-renders once more. Finally calls `SwalDialog.close()` to dismiss the dialog. Note: this method references the global `DrawTool` without importing it in this file (relies on it being a global, consistent with other files in this batch that do `import DrawTool from '../../draw-tool/drawtool'` — this file does not import it, so it depends on `DrawTool` being globally available, e.g. via `window`).
- `cancelSwalDialog = () =>` — calls `SwalDialog.close()`.
- `render()` — see Render Structure.

### Render Structure
`.dieCutBody` containing:
- `.item-show` — maps over `DrawTool.sides._collection[0].layers.list`, rendering a `.dieCutBodyHead` (checkbox wired to `selectOption(item.index)`, plus an `<img>` preview of `item.preview`) for each item whose `index` is included in `layersAll`.
- `.buttonFooter` — "キャンセル" (Cancel → `cancelSwalDialog`) and "削除" (Delete → `deleteItem`) buttons.

None declared.

### State
None.

### Methods

A single `<div className="draw-tool" ref={...}>` whose DOM node is captured into `this.node` for use by `DrawTool.initialize`.

## src/components/drop-down-colors/index.jsx

### Purpose
`DropDownColor` is a generic dropdown/list widget specialized for color selection lists — it wraps arbitrary `children` (expected to be selectable list items) in an expandable panel, tracks open/closed state, closes on outside click, and displays the currently `selected` color swatch + title at the bottom of the list.

### Props
- `label` — string or element, shown in the closed-state head.
- `style` — inline style object applied to the root.
- `className` — extra CSS class(es) merged via `classNames`.
- `onClick` — optional custom click handler for the root element; defaults to `this.openList` if not provided.
- `children` — array of list-item elements; each gets `onClick` injected via `React.cloneElement`.
- `onChange` — callback invoked with the selected item's `data-meta` (if present) or its text content.
- `selected` — object with `.value` (used as the swatch background color) and `.title` (label text) representing the currently selected color.

### State
- `active` — whether the dropdown list is open.
- `label` — initialized `null`; not actually reassigned by `select()` in a way that affects display (the visible label always comes from `props.label`), effectively unused for rendering.

### Methods
- `componentDidMount()` — adds a capturing `click` listener on `document` calling `handleClickOutside`.
- `componentWillUnmount()` — removes that listener.
- `handleClickOutside(e)` — if the click target is outside the root DOM node (`this.node`), sets `active: false`.
- `openList(e)` — if the click target has the `drop-down_head` class, toggles `active`.
- `select(val, meta)` — sets `state.label = val` and closes the list (`active: false`); calls `props.onChange(meta || val)` if provided.
- `close()` — sets `active: false` (wired to the list's close/×icon).
- `render()` — see Render Structure.

### Render Structure
Root `.drop-down-colors` (with `.active` class when open, plus any passed `className`), `label` attribute, `style`, `onClick` (custom or `openList`), containing:
- `.drop-down_head` — `.label` text plus an `#icon-list` SVG icon.
- `.list` — `.list-title` ("カラー" heading with a close `#icon-close` SVG icon wired to `close`), the cloned `children` (each wired to call `select` with its text/`data-meta` on click), and `.list-selected` showing "選択カラー: " plus a `.color` swatch (background = `selected.value`) and `selected.title`.

---

## src/components/drop-down-material/index.jsx

### Purpose
`DropDownMaterial` is a more feature-rich dropdown used for material/font/category/brush-size selectors throughout the editor. It renders differently depending on `className` (`'brushSize'`, `'fonts'`, `'categoriesFonts'`, generic) and screen size (native `<select>` on mobile for most variants; a custom styled dropdown with a `Scrollbars`-wrapped list on desktop). It supports "loading" placeholder options for fonts not yet loaded, and dispatches Redux actions to toggle a "load fonts category" container when the fonts/categoriesFonts dropdown head is clicked.

### Props
- `label` — string, optional label shown above/inside the head.
- `value` — string, the currently selected element's `val` (used to look up `valueNode` for display).
- `className` — string, drives which render branch is used (`'brushSize'`, `'fonts'`, `'categoriesFonts'`, `'icons'`, or default) and which state field mobile `<select>` changes update.
- `elements` — array of `{val, node, loaded?}` objects describing each option (`node` is a React element used as the option's visual content for non-native renders).
- `onChange` — callback invoked with the newly selected value.
- `style` — inline style for the root.
- `dispatch` — (not in `propTypes` but destructured from `this.props` in `openList`) Redux dispatch.

### State
- `active` — whether the desktop dropdown list is open.
- `value` — initialized `null`; set by `select()` to the chosen option's `node` (used only internally, display relies on `props.value`/`valueNode`).
- `selectValue` — mobile `<select>`'s current value for generic dropdowns, defaulting to `"日本語"`.
- `selectValueBrush` — mobile `<select>`'s current value for the brush-size dropdown, defaulting to `"20pt"`.
- `mobile` — result of `matchMedia('(max-width: 1079px)')`, determines which render branch is used.

### Methods
- `handleChange(event)` — for the mobile `<select>` variants: if `className === 'brushSize'`, updates `selectValueBrush` and calls `onChange` with the value minus its trailing `'pt'` (`.slice(0,-2)`); otherwise updates `selectValue` and calls `onChange` with the raw value.
- `getElementsOptions()` — builds the mobile `<option>` list from `props.elements`, formatted per `className`: `'brushSize'` → appends `"pt"` to each `val`; `'fonts'` → shows `element.node.props.children` as the label, and renders `disabled` "読み込み中" (loading) options for entries where `element.loaded` is falsy; default → shows `element.val` directly.
- `componentDidMount()` / `componentWillUnmount()` — add/remove a capturing outside-click listener (`handleClickOutside`).
- `handleClickOutside(e)` — closes the dropdown (`active: false`) if not mobile and the click was outside `this.node`.
- `openList(e)` — if `className` is `'categoriesFonts'` or `'fonts'`, dispatches `ProductActions.toggleLoadFontsCategoryContainer(true)` (opens a separate font-category modal) instead of toggling the inline list; otherwise, prevents default and — if the click target has the `drop-down_head` class — sets `active: true` (note: only ever opens, never toggles closed, from this handler; closing happens via outside-click or `select`).
- `select(val, node, el)` — no-ops if `el.loaded === false`; otherwise sets `state.value = node`, closes the list, and calls `props.onChange(val)`.
- `render()` — see Render Structure.

### Render Structure
Branches into one of five view variants based on `state.mobile` and `props.className`:
1. **Mobile + `brushSize`** — `.drop-down-material.brushSize` with an optional `label` and a native `<select>` bound to `selectValueBrush`/`handleChange`, options from `getElementsOptions()`.
2. **Mobile + other (not `icons`/`categoriesFonts`/`fonts`)** — same shape but bound to `selectValue`.
3. **Desktop + `fonts`** — `.drop-down-material.fonts` with a `.drop-down_head` (click → `openList`) showing `label` and the selected font preview (`valueNode.node` or a "通常フォント" fallback) plus an arrow, and a `.list.fonts-list` of cloned `el.node` elements (click → `select`).
4. **Desktop + `categoriesFonts`** — similar head/list structure but shows a right-arrow image instead of the plain arrow, and the value falls back to `elements[0].node` if nothing is selected.
5. **Default (desktop, any other className, including `icons`)** — `.drop-down_head` (click → `openList`) with `label`, selected value/fallback, and an arrow (a special "arrow2" variant with an image is used only if `className === 'categoriesFonts'`, which is unreachable here since that's handled by branch 4 — effectively dead code within this branch); `.list` wraps the cloned elements in a `<Scrollbars autoHide autoHeightMax={300}>` container.

Connected via `connect(mapStateToProps)` mapping `loadProductContainer`, `loadProductCatContainer`, `loadProductTypeContainer` from `state.product` (none of these three are actually referenced in the render/methods shown — likely used by a parent or leftover).

---

## src/components/drop-down/index.jsx

### Purpose
`DropDown` is the simpler, generic sibling of `DropDownColor` — a basic expandable list dropdown for arbitrary option children, with an optional "loading colors" spinner state used when populating color-dependent lists asynchronously.

### Props
- `label` — string or element shown in the head.
- `style` — inline style for the root.
- `className` — extra class(es).
- `hideLabel` — string, appended as an additional class name on the label `div` (e.g. to conditionally hide it via CSS).
- `onClick` — optional custom root click handler; defaults to `openList`.
- `children` — array of option elements; each gets a click handler injected.
- `onChange` — callback fired with the selected item's `data-meta` or text.
- `selected_color` — (not in `propTypes`, read via `this.props`) truthy value that, combined with `!loading_colors`, triggers the loading-spinner list view instead of real children.
- `loading_colors` — (not in `propTypes`) boolean flag paired with `selected_color` to decide whether to show the spinner or the real list.

### State
- `active` — whether the list is open.
- `label` — initialized `null`, set by `select()` but not used for display (same pattern as `DropDownColor`).

### Methods
- `componentDidMount()` / `componentWillUnmount()` — add/remove capturing outside-click listener.
- `handleClickOutside(e)` — closes the list if the click was outside `this.node`.
- `openList(e)` — toggles `active` if the click target has the `drop-down_head` class.
- `select(val, meta)` — sets `label` state, closes the list, calls `props.onChange(meta || val)`.
- `render()` — see Render Structure.

### Render Structure
Root `.drop-down` (`.active` when open, plus `className`), `label` attribute, `style`, `onClick` (custom or `openList`), containing:
- `.drop-down_head` — a `div` with class `"label " + hideLabel` showing `label`, plus an `#icon-list` SVG icon.
- Either:
  - `.list.loading-colors` with a single spinning `.square > .spin` placeholder item, shown when `selected_color && !loading_colors`; or
  - `.list` containing the cloned `children` (each wired to `select` on click), shown otherwise.

---

## src/components/drop-files/index.jsx

### Purpose
`DropFiles` renders a full-document drag-and-drop target (visible only on desktop-class devices per a `MediaQuery`) that lets users drag image/design files onto the app to upload them, using `react-file-drop`'s `FileDrop` bound to `document` as the drop frame, alongside a hidden `react-dropzone` `Dropzone` (present but styled `display:none`, likely retained for its file-type handling behavior rather than its own UI).

### Props
- `onUpload` — required function, called with the dropped `files` array.
- `bottom` — number, when present adds a `drop-zone_bottom` class (affects styling/positioning of the message).
- `showOptions` — bool; when falsy, adds a `drop-zone_no-options` class.

### State
Empty object (`this.state = {}`), unused.

### Methods
- `onDrop(files)` — calls `this.props.onUpload(files)`.
- `render()` — see Render Structure.

### Render Structure
`<MediaQuery query='(min-device-width: 1024px)'>` wrapping:
- A hidden `<Dropzone style={{display:'none'}}/>`.
- `<FileDrop frame={document} onDrop={onDrop} className={'drop-zone ' + conditional modifier classes}>` containing `.drop-zone__area` with the text "Drop your file here".
