## src/components/filter-box/index.jsx

### Purpose
`FilterBox` renders the (older/simpler) product-list filter sidebar UI — category, price, SP-case model, maker, bag style, size, color, and "こだわり条件" (condition/status) filters — driven almost entirely by Redux state and dispatching `ProductActions` update/toggle actions as the user interacts with each filter group. It computes "real" (actually-available) filter option subsets by cross-referencing the currently visible product list against each filter's full candidate list, so only relevant options are shown, and reports a live result count. (Compare with `FilterMenuBox`, a newer/alternate version of similar functionality with a tabbed "menu" layout.)

### Props (all via `mapStateToProps`, from `state.product` and `state.drawTool` unless noted)
- `availableFontsCategories` (from `state.drawTool`) — declared but unused in the render shown.
- `products`, `currentCategory`, `objPriceFilter`, `objKeyFilter`, `objSexFilter`, `objPrintFilter`, `objMaterialArrFilter`, `objModelSPCaseArrFilter`, `objSizeFilter`, `objColorFilter`, `objSideFilter`, `objMaterialFilter`, `objValueFilter`, `objCategoryFilter`, `arrStatusFilter`, `numberProductFilter`, `currentID`, `categories`, `objCategorySubFilter`, `priceFilters`, `keyCategoryFilters`, `keyMakerFilters`, `keySexFilters`, `keyMaterialFilters`, `keyPrintFilters`, `keySceneFilters`, `keySizeFilters`, `keyColorFilters`, `keyConditionFilters`, `typeOfProduct`, `keyTypeOfSPFilters`, `keyTypeOfBagFilters`, `allProductByMasterCategory`, `list_key_filter`, `objModelSPCaseArrFilterSelected`, `objSPCaseFilter` — the full set of current filter selections and their candidate option lists.
- `dispatch` — Redux dispatch.

### State
- `categoryFilter` — static array of two demo/legacy category entries (`レディース`, `キッズ・ベビー`) — not referenced elsewhere in the render shown (likely vestigial).
- `modelSPcases` — static two-entry array (`Iphone`/`iphone`, `Androids`/`android`) used to render the SP-case model filter buttons.
- `toggleCategory`, `toggleSex`, `toggleMaker`, `togglePrintMetod`, `toggleMaterial`, `toggleScene`, `toggleSize`, `toggleColor`, `toggleStatus`, `toggleSpCase` — booleans controlling collapse/expand of each filter section (most default `true`, `toggleCategory` defaults `false`).

### Methods
- `componentDidMount()` / `componentWillUnmount()` — empty (no-ops).
- `onCloseBox()` — calls `DrawTool.stopCurentAction()` (global `DrawTool`, not imported in this file), determines whether any filter is currently active (category/key/size/color/category/status/price checks), and dispatches either "reset to category browsing" actions (`toggleBoxFilter(false)`, `toggleLoadProductTypeContainer({state:false})`, `toggleLoadProductContainer(false)`, `toggleLoadProductCategoryContainer(true)`) when nothing is active, or "show filtered product list" actions (`toggleBoxFilter(false)`, `toggleLoadProductCategoryContainer(false)`, `toggleLoadProductTypeContainer({state:true, id})`, `toggleLoadProductContainer(true)`) when filters are active.
- `onUpdateKeyFilter(key, type)` — large `type`-dispatch function; for each filter `type` (`'maker'`, `'sp-case-filter'`, `'sex'`, `'print'`, `'materialArr'`, `'use'`, `'status'`, `'price'`, `'size'`, `'color'`, `'side'`, `'material'`, `'value'`, `'category'`, `'category_sub'`) it toggles `key` in/out of the corresponding `obj*Filter` array (add if absent, remove if present, using appropriate equality — string, `.id`, or `.code` comparisons per type) and dispatches the matching `ProductActions.update*Filter(...)` action. `'price'` and `'material'` read from a DOM `event.target.value` instead of a plain key.
- `onUpdateSPModel(model)` — toggles `model` in/out of `objModelSPCaseArrFilter`, recomputes the subset of `list_key_filter` whose `.type` matches any selected model, and dispatches `updateModelSPCaseArrFilter` + `updateModelSPCaseArrFilterSelected`.
- `handleCheckboxChange(event)` — toggles `event.target.id` in/out of `arrStatusFilter` and dispatches `updateArrStatusFilter` (deferred via `setTimeout(...,0)`).
- `filterRealKeys(arrProducts, arrKeys, key)` — generic helper: for a given product-object field name (`key`, e.g. `'print_methods'`), returns the subset of `arrKeys` whose `.name` appears in any product's `Product[key]` array.
- `onToggleBoxFilter(actionToggle)` — flips the corresponding `toggle*` boolean in state for each named section (`'category'`, `'sex'`, `'maker'`, `'print-method'`, `'material'`, `'scene'`, `'size'`, `'color'`, `'status'`, `'sp-case'`).
- `render()` — see Render Structure.

### Render Structure
`.filter-box-container`:
- Title bar "こだわり条件" with a close button (→ `onCloseBox`).
- `.filter-box-makeby` — item-category buttons (from `categoryFilterArr`, derived from `categories` filtered by `typeOfProduct`/`currentCategory`), each wired to `onUpdateKeyFilter(cate.Category, 'category_sub')`.
- `.filter-box-price` — a `<select>` of price ranges (`priceFilters`) wired to `onUpdateKeyFilter(e, 'price')`.
- Conditionally (when `typeOfProduct` is the SP-case type, `4`): an SP-case model filter block (`modelSPcases` buttons → `onUpdateSPModel`) plus dynamically computed `realKeySPCase` sub-filter buttons (→ `onUpdateKeyFilter(mo.id, 'sp-case-filter')`).
- Conditionally (when `typeOfProduct !== 4`): maker buttons (`realMaker` → `onUpdateKeyFilter(name,'maker')`).
- Conditionally (`typeOfProduct === 7`, bags): bag-style buttons (`realTypeOfProductBags` → `onUpdateKeyFilter(...,'typeOfBag')`).
- Conditionally (`typeOfProduct !== 4`): size buttons (`realKeySizeFilters` → `onUpdateKeyFilter(name,'size')`).
- Conditionally (`typeOfProduct === 1`, apparel): color swatches (`realKeyColorFilters`, including an "全て"/all option) → `onUpdateKeyFilter(colorFiler,'color')`.
- Conditionally (`typeOfProduct !== 4`): condition/status buttons (`keyConditionFilters` → `onUpdateKeyFilter(s,'status')`).
- `.filter-box-footer` — shows `numberProductFilter` result count and a "検索" (search) button (→ `onCloseBox`).

Connected via `connect(mapStateToProps)` with the extensive prop list above.

---

## src/components/filter-menu-box/index.jsx

### Purpose
`FilterMenuBox` is the newer/alternate tabbed filter UI (superseding or complementing `FilterBox`): a horizontal menu of filter category tabs (category, materials, print method, SP-case model, price, maker, size, color, sex, status, scene) with an expandable content panel per tab, plus a "selected filters" chip row at the bottom (each chip removable) and a live result count (`numberProduct`). It computes the same kind of "real" (currently-relevant) filter subsets as `FilterBox` but with added null-safety guards throughout, and adds product-type-specific visibility rules (via constants `T_SHIRT`, `BAG`, `SP_CASE`, `PAPER`, `KEY_CHAIN`, `CUP_KITCHEN`) plus a "hoodie" sub-category special case (`HOODIE_TYPE`).

### Props
- `children` — array or element (declared in `propTypes`; not referenced in the render shown).
- `close`, `title`, `back` — declared in `propTypes` but not used in the render shown (likely used by a related/parent component or leftover from a shared prop-types template).
- `dispatch` — Redux dispatch.
- All filter-state props are injected via `mapStateToProps` (same broad set as `FilterBox`, plus): `numberProduct`, `toogleFilter`, `typeFilter`, `objMaterialFilter`, `category_active`, `objModelPhone`.

### State
- `mobile` — `matchMedia('(max-width: 1079px)')` result.
- `toggleSex` — boolean, initialized `true` (not referenced in the render logic shown — likely vestigial from a copy of `FilterBox`).
- `activeFress` — boolean toggled (not read elsewhere) by `updateObjKeyFilter`; appears to be an unused "force refresh" trigger.
- `modelSPCase` — static two-entry array `{type:'iPhone', name:'iphone'}` / `{type:'Android', name:'android'}` used for the SP-case model filter UI.

### Methods
- `onCloseBox(type)` — same active/inactive-filter detection as `FilterBox.onCloseBox` (now also checking `objPrintFilter`), dispatching the same "browse categories" vs "show filtered list" Redux actions; the `type` parameter is accepted but not used in the body.
- `onUpdateKeyFilter(key, type)` — superset of `FilterBox.onUpdateKeyFilter`'s branches, adding a `'model-phone'` type (toggles `key` in `objModelPhone`, dispatches `updateModelPhone`, and additionally clears any `objSPCaseFilter` entries belonging to the *other* phone brand when switching between `iphone`/`android`) and a `'spcase'` type (toggles an object by `.id` in `objSPCaseFilter`, dispatching `updateSPCaseKeyFilter`). All other types (`maker`, `sex`, `print`, `materialArr`, `scene`, `status`, `price`, `size`, `color`, `side`, `material`, `value`, `category`, `category_sub`) mirror `FilterBox`'s logic. After dispatching, schedules `this.onCloseBox(type)` via `setTimeout(...,100)` — likely to trigger the result-count/visibility refresh shortly after the filter change (note: `FilterBox`'s version does not do this).
- `onUpdateSPModel(model)` — identical to `FilterBox`'s version.
- `handleCheckboxChange(event)` — identical to `FilterBox`'s version.
- `filterRealKeys(arrProducts, arrKeys, key)` — same purpose as `FilterBox`'s, but with added null-guards (`if(mapArr)`, `if(j)`) to avoid errors when a product's field is missing.
- `onToggleBoxFilter(actionToggle)` — unlike `FilterBox` (which flips local booleans), this dispatches `ProductActions.updateTypeFilter(actionToggle)` directly to Redux, driving the shared `typeFilter` state that controls which tab's content panel is expanded.
- `updateObjKeyFilter(type, key)` — the "remove a single filter chip" handler used by the bottom chip row: for each `type` (`'category'`, `'price'`, `'maker'`, `'size'`, `'color'`, `'status'`, `'print'`, `'material'`, `'sex'`, `'scene'`, `'spcase'`) it finds and splices out the matching entry from the corresponding filter array (or resets price to `'全て'`) and dispatches the update action; also toggles `state.activeFress` and schedules `onCloseBox(type)` after 1ms.
- `onSelectKeyAll(type)` — "select all / clear filter" handler for a tab's "全て" (all) option: dispatches the corresponding `update*Filter([])` action to clear that filter type (`category`→`updateCategorySubFilter`, `maker`→`updateKeyFilter`, `size`→`updateSizeFilter`, `status`→`updateArrStatusFilter`, `color`→`updateColorFilter`, `print`→`updatePrintFilter`, `materialArr`→`updateMaterialArrFilter`, `sex`→`updateSexFilter`, `scene`→`updateSceneFilter`, `spcase`→`updateSPCaseKeyFilter`).
- `render()` — see Render Structure. Computes (with null-safety) the same "real" filter subsets as `FilterBox` (`realKeyColorFilters`, `realKeySizeFilters`, `realMaker`, `realPrintMethods`, `realMatterials`, `realSexs`, `realScenes`, `realKeySPCases`/`realKeySPCasesByModel`, `realTypePhone`, `realHooDiType`), plus a `RealArrStatusFilters` list that starts as a full copy of `keyConditionFilters` and has entries spliced out based on live product-set checks (`checkSameDay`, `checkHiQuanlity`, `checkStylish`, `checkStock10`, `checkEmbroider` — matched by each filter's `.keyword`).

### Render Structure
- `.filter-box-n` — `.filter-box-n-menu`, a row of tab labels shown/hidden based on `category_active` (`T_SHIRT`/`BAG`/`SP_CASE`) and whether the corresponding "real" option list is non-empty: アイテムカテゴリ (category, hidden for SP_CASE), 生地・素材 (materials), プリント手法 (print), 機種 (SP-case model), 価格 (price, always shown), メーカー (maker), サイズ (size), 性別・タイプ (sex), カラー (color), こだわり条件 (status), 利用シーン (scene) — each tab click calls `onToggleBoxFilter(tabName)`, and gets an active class when `typeFilter` matches.
  - `.filter-box-n-wrap > .filter-box-n-body` — one `.filter-box-n-content` panel per tab (visibility toggled via an "-active" class matching `typeFilter`), each rendering an "全て" (select-all/clear) chip plus the tab's option chips (clicking toggles that option via `onUpdateKeyFilter`); the category panel also appends `realHooDiType` sub-options for `T_SHIRT`; the SP-case panel has a two-level structure (`key-master-sp` phone-brand row, then `key-sub-sp` model row filtered to the selected brand).
- `.filter-box-n-footer-wrap > .filter-box-n-footer` — a chip for every currently active filter selection across all types (category, price, maker, size, print, material, sex, scene, SP-case, color, status), each with a close icon calling `updateObjKeyFilter(type, value)` to remove it, plus `.filter-box-n-footer-result` showing `numberProduct`件 (result count).

Connected via `connect(mapStateToProps)` with the extensive prop list described above.

---

## src/components/font-card/index.jsx

### Purpose
`FontCard` is a small stateless functional component rendering a single selectable font preview card (used in font-selection grids), showing a font-family label, a rendered preview image, and a "選択" (select) action button.

### Props
- `title` — text label for the font, shown above the preview image.
- `image` — image URL for the rendered font preview.
- `applyFont` — function; called as `applyFont(name)` **at render time** (see note below) to produce the button's `onClick` handler — i.e. `applyFont` is expected to be a curried function `(name) => (event) => {...}`, not a direct click handler.
- `name` — the font's identifier/name, passed to `applyFont`.

### State
None (functional component).

### Methods
None (pure function).

### Render Structure
`.font-card` containing `.font` (title text), `.font-image-w > img.font-img` (preview image, `alt="フォント名"`), and `.action-font > button.btn-action-font` ("選択", `onClick={applyFont(name)}`).

---

## src/components/font-load/index.jsx

### Purpose
`FontsLoad` renders the full-screen "load a font" browser modal: a header with title/back/close controls, a category filter bar (desktop tab list or a horizontally-scrollable mobile variant with left/right scroll-arrow affordances), an optional maker filter bar (for product-load reuse of this same component), a loading spinner/progress bar, and a content area (`props.children`) showing the actual font/product grid. It is a fairly generic "browse and filter items in a modal" shell reused for both font loading and (per prop names like `screen_products`, `makers`) product loading contexts.

### Props
- `children` — array or element, the content grid to render inside `.content`.
- `close` — function, called on the close button and on `Escape` keyup.
- `title` — string, modal header title.
- `back` — function, called on the back button.
- `dispatch` — Redux dispatch (declared in `propTypes`; not directly used in the read code — actions are dispatched by the parent via passed-in callback props instead).
- `category_page` — (not in `propTypes`) boolean; when true, the back button is rendered `disable`d with a different icon (`back-m`).
- `fillterFontsByCategory` — function returning/being an event handler per category, invoked as `fillterFontsByCategory(categoryFont)` similar to the `FontCard.applyFont` pattern (curried).
- `cate_font_active` — currently active font category, used to highlight the matching tab.
- `loading_process` — boolean; shows a `<ProcessBar/>` when true.
- `category_styles`, `categories` — legacy/alternate category data sources (a `category_styles`-based tab bar exists in an `else` branch that is unreachable because the surrounding `if(true)` always takes the primary branch — effectively dead code).
- `template` — boolean; when true, the entire category/maker bar section is skipped.
- `screen_products` — boolean; when true, shows the maker filter bar.
- `makers` — array of maker name strings for the maker filter bar / mobile `<select>`.
- `maker_active` — currently active maker filter, for highlighting.
- `filterMaker` — curried handler `(makerName) => handler`, used both for the "全て" (all) option and each maker link.
- `screen_mobile` — boolean; selects mobile-specific rendering for the category and maker bars.
- `filter` — boolean; shows a `<Loader/>` overlay when true.
- `availableFontsCategories` — injected via `mapStateToProps` from `state.drawTool.availableFontsCategories`; the list of category tab labels.
- `is_embroidery` — injected via `mapStateToProps` from `state.drawTool.is_embroidery`; toggles an embroidery-specific disclaimer line and hides the "欧文" (Latin/Western) category tab on iOS.

### State
None declared (no constructor state; only bound methods).

### Methods
- `hide(e)` — if `e.keyCode === 27` (Escape), calls `this.props.close()`.
- `chooseMaker(event)` — reads `event.target.value` and calls `this.props.chooseMaker(event.target.value)` (note: `chooseMaker` is read from `this.props` here but is **not** declared in `propTypes` or passed via any visible caller in this file — presumably supplied by the parent when this component is reused for product/maker filtering).
- `componentDidMount()` — adds a capturing `keyup` document listener (`hide`) and adds the `fixed` class to `document.body` (locks page scroll while the modal is open).
- `componentWillUnmount()` — removes the `keyup` listener and removes the `fixed` class from `document.body`.
- `helpScrollArrowsLoad()` — reads the mobile category scroller's (`#ArrowsProductLoad`) `scrollLeft`/`scrollWidth`/`clientWidth` and recolors the left/right scroll-arrow elements (`#ArrowsProductLoadLeft`/`#ArrowsProductLoadRight`) between `white` and `#333` to indicate whether further scrolling is possible in each direction (called on the scroller's `onScroll`).
- `helpClickArrowsLoad(arrow)` — on clicking the left/right arrow affordance, sets `#ArrowsProductLoad`'s `scrollLeft` to either its max (`scrollWidth`, for `'right'`) or a small offset (`15`, for left), and updates the arrow elements' colors accordingly.
- `render()` — see Render Structure.

### Render Structure
`.product-load`:
- `<ProcessBar/>` if `loading_process`; `<Loader/>` if `filter`.
- `.head > .container` — `.title` (modal title), `.button-list` with a back button (`button_back`, disabled/alternate icon when `category_page`) and a close `Button` (→ `close`).
- `category_bar` (skipped entirely if `template` is true):
  - Primary path (the `category_styles` `else` branch is unreachable dead code) — `.list-categories` mapping `availableFontsCategories` to category tab links (→ `fillterFontsByCategory(categoryFont)`, active class via `cate_font_active`), plus a `.list-categories.font-description` disclaimer about unsupported characters in fonts (with an extra embroidery-specific line when `is_embroidery`).
  - If `screen_mobile` is true, `category_bar` is **overwritten** with a horizontally-scrollable variant: a left-arrow div (`helpClickArrowsLoad('left')`), a scrollable row of category links (`#ArrowsProductLoad`, `onScroll` → `helpScrollArrowsLoad`, skipping the "欧文" tab on iOS), and a right-arrow div (`helpClickArrowsLoad('right')`), plus a `.mobile-font-descriptions` disclaimer block.
- `maker_bar` (only if `screen_products`):
  - Mobile: a labeled native `<select>` (onChange → `chooseMaker`) with a "全て" option plus one per `makers` entry.
  - Desktop: `.list-makers` with a "全て" link (→ `filterMaker("全て")`) and one link per maker (→ `filterMaker(maker)`), active class via `maker_active`.
- `category_bar`, `maker_bar`, then `loading_category` — either a `<Loader/>` (if `categories.length === 0`) or `.content` wrapping `props.children`.

Connected via `connect(mapStateToProps)` mapping `availableFontsCategories` and `is_embroidery`.

---

# Part 7 — Components, Batch 2 (laser wizards, layers, product cards, text-to-image, misc.)

## UP-T Components Reference — Batch 2

Covers 30 presentational/feature React components (React 15 + Redux) used in the UP-T editor UI: text/laser tooling, layers panel, product cards/loaders, chat, text-to-image, shapes/stickers.

---

## src/components/add-text-form/index.jsx

### Purpose
Redux-connected form used to add or edit a text layer in the design tool. Renders a restricted text input (blocks a range of emoji characters) plus a submit `Button`. On every keystroke it also warms up font rendering by writing the typed text into hidden, per-font-family `<div>` elements so the browser loads/measures each available font ahead of use on the canvas.

### Props
- `onSubmit` — func, called with the current text value when the submit button is clicked.
- `onChange` — func, called with the current text value on input blur.
- `value` — string, initial/controlled text value.
- `selected` — bool, whether an existing text item is selected (changes button label to "edit" vs "add").
- `loadedFonts` — object, map of fonts already loaded (referenced in a commented-out `disabled` check).
- `loadedFont` — string, name of the font that just finished loading; used to avoid clobbering in-progress typing.
- `selectedFont` — string, currently selected font family (referenced in the commented-out `disabled` check).
- `fontsTS` (connected, from `state.drawTool.fontsTS`) — array of font descriptors used in `handleChange` to build the hidden font-warm-up divs.

### State
- `value` — string, current text in the input, initialized from `props.value`.
- `selected` — bool, initialized from `props.selected`.

### Methods
- `constructor(props)` — binds `handleChange`/`handleSubmit`/`handleBlur`; sets `this.loadedFont = null`.
- `componentWillReceiveProps(nextProps)` — if `props.loadedFont` differs from `this.loadedFont`, just updates `this.loadedFont`; otherwise syncs `state.value`/`state.selected` from `nextProps` (this guards against overwriting the user's in-progress typing while a font load is pending).
- `handleChange(event)` — reads `fontsTS` from props, clears the `#load-all-font` DOM container, then appends one hidden `<div>` per font (0px font-size, `style.fontFamily` set) containing the typed text, forcing the browser to load/measure every font for that text; updates `state.value`.
- `handleSubmit()` — calls `props.onSubmit(state.value)` if provided.
- `handleBlur()` — calls `props.onChange(state.value)` if provided.

### Render Structure
`div.add-text-form` containing a `RestrictedInput` (illegal regex `/[☀-⛿]/g` blocks a block of emoji/dingbat characters), wired to `handleChange`/`handleBlur`, and a `Button` whose label toggles between "テキストを編集" (edit text) and "テキストを追加" (add text) based on `state.selected`, calling `handleSubmit` on click.

---

## src/components/border-paths/index.jsx

### Purpose
Small Redux-connected control that renders a single "zoom to embroidery zone" button for the currently selected `DrawTool` side. A per-path selector list (numbered embroidery border segments) is fully implemented but commented out, leaving only the zoom-in action live.

### Props
- Component declares no `propTypes`; `mapStateToProps` injects `siteSelected` (`state.product.sideSelected`), which is unused in render.

### State
- `selectedSite` — the current `DrawTool.sides.selected` object, cached at construction.
- `selected_path_index` — `DrawTool.sides.selected.selected_path_index`, cached at construction.

### Methods
- `constructor(props)` — initializes state from `DrawTool.sides.selected`.
- `componentWillUpdate()` — if `DrawTool.sides.selected.id` differs from `state.selectedSite.id`, updates `state.selectedSite`; then compares `state.selected_path_index` (still the pre-update value due to closure) against `DrawTool.sides.selected.selected_path_index` and updates it if different.
- `componentDidMount()` — registers a `window` `resize` listener calling `getIsMobile`; calls it once immediately.
- `componentWillUnmount()` — removes the resize listener.
- `getIsMobile()` — sets `state.mobile` from `window.matchMedia('(max-width: 1080px)').matches`.
- `selectPath(index)` — if `index === 0`, calls `DrawTool.sides.selected.zoomEmbroideryZone()`; otherwise sets `DrawTool.sides.selected.selected_path_index = index` directly on the DrawTool side object and mirrors it into local state.

### Render Structure
`div.areas_selecter` > `div.item_selecter` with a single `Button` (`icon="zoom-in"`) bound to `selectPath(0)`. A commented-out block would have rendered one numbered `div.item_selecter` per `EmbroideryBorders` entry, each calling `selectPath(index+1)`.

---

## src/components/button/index.jsx

### Purpose
Generic, stateless "icon button" used throughout the toolbar UI. Supports an icon, an image, and/or a text label together, an ink ripple effect (`react-ink`), active/disabled styling, and special layout for "template category" labels and stamp-type images.

### Props
- `label` — string or element, button text/content.
- `icon` — string, icon name passed to `Icon`.
- `image` — string, image URL rendered before the icon/label.
- `onClick` — func, click handler.
- `disabled` — bool, adds the `disabled` CSS class.
- `disable` — bool, forwarded to the native `<button disabled>` attribute (distinct from `disabled`).
- `active` — bool, default `false`; adds the `active` CSS class.
- `type` — one of `'button' | 'reset' | 'submit'`, default `'button'`.
- `className` — string, extra class names.
- `style` — object, inline style forwarded to the `<button>` (and, unless `templateCategory`, to the label `<span>`).
- `ink` — bool, default `true`; toggles the `Ink` ripple effect.
- `styleImage` — object, custom style for the `<img>` (ignored when `typeActive === "stamps"`, which forces a fixed 22x22 size).
- `templateCategory` — bool/flag; switches the label to an absolutely-positioned style and adds a `label-template` class.
- `fontStyle` — bool; adds a `fomat-text-font` class to the rendered `Icon`.
- `hideLabel` — string, default `""`; prefixed onto the label's class name.
- `typeActive` — string; when `"stamps"`, forces the image to 22x22px regardless of `styleImage`.

### State
None (functional component).

### Render Structure
`<button className="button (active) (disabled) {className}">` containing: an optional `<img>` (image, styled per `styleImage`/stamps override), an optional `<Icon>` (with `fomat-text-font` class if `fontStyle`), an optional `<span>` label (class = `hideLabel + 'label'` or `'label label-template'`), and an `<Ink>` ripple if `ink` is true.

---

## src/components/chat-form/input-chat.jsx

### Purpose
Chat message composer bar for the in-app design-room chat. Provides a `contentEditable` text field, a send button (with a loading-spinner state while a submit is pending), and a "leave room" button gated by a SweetAlert2 confirmation dialog.

### Props
- `onSubmit` — func, required; called with the composed text.
- `onLeave` — func, required; called after the user confirms leaving.
- `html` — (not in `propTypes` but used) initial HTML rendered into the editable field via `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`.

### State
- `inputActive` — bool, whether the input is focused/being typed into (toggles an `active` class).
- `html` — string, mirrors the editable field's current text (via `outerText`).
- `loading` — bool, true while a submit is in flight; swaps the send button for a spinner.

### Methods
- `componentDidMount()` — empty (no-op).
- `handleKeyDown(event)` — if the Enter key (`keyCode 13`) is pressed, prevents default and calls `_submitText()`.
- `emitChange(e)` — on input/blur of the editable div, sets `state.html = e.target.outerText` and `state.inputActive = true`.
- `loadingChange(e)` — sets `inputActive:false, loading:true` (keeps current `html`); not wired to any DOM event in this file (available for external use).
- `_submitText()` — if `state.html` is non-empty: sets `inputActive:false, loading:true` and calls `props.onSubmit(text)`.
- `clearTextData()` — resets state to empty/loading:false and directly clears `#main_input_chat`'s `innerHTML` via `document.getElementById`.
- `errorTosend()` — sets `loading:false` (intended to be called by a parent after a failed submit).
- `onLeavePage()` — fires a SweetAlert2 confirm dialog ("退室したいですか?"); if confirmed, calls `props.onLeave()`.

### Render Structure
`div.sc-user-input` (adds `active` class when `inputActive`) containing: a `contentEditable` div `#main_input_chat` wired to `onKeyPress`/`onInput`/`onBlur` and seeded via `dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{__html: props.html}}`; a button row showing either a spinner (`loading`) or a "投稿" (post) button calling `_submitText`, plus a "退室" (leave) button calling `onLeavePage`.

---

## src/components/chat-form/message-detail.jsx

### Purpose
Renders a single chat message bubble. Handles three message shapes: a "user left" system message (`author === 0`), a "user joined" system message (`author === 1`), and a normal chat message, with hover/click-to-reveal delete affordance for messages authored by the current user.

### Props
- `message` — object, required; chat message record.
- `lstAuthor` — array, required; chat-room participant list used to resolve avatar/username via `chat_room_detail.user`.
- `onDeleteMessage` — func, required.
- `id_user` — string, required; current user's id, used for `sent`/`received` styling and to gate the delete UI.

### State
- `inputDelete` — bool, whether the delete ("削除") affordance is currently shown for this message.

### Methods
- `deleteMessage()` — if `message` has both `content` and `author` properties, calls `props.onDeleteMessage(props.message)`.
- `converTimestampeToDateString(time)` — formats a timestamp as `"H:M:S D/M/YYYY"`.

### Render Structure
Returns an empty `div.sc-message` if `message` lacks `content`/`author`. For `author == 0` (leave notice) or `author == 1` (join notice), looks up the matching participant in `lstAuthor`; if not found, renders empty; otherwise renders an avatar (falling back to a default S3 image URL if `profile_image` is missing/short) plus localized text ("...have been leave" / "...begin jon to room" — note the source typos) and a formatted timestamp. For a normal message: resolves the participant, computes `contentClassList` (`'sent'`/`'received'` by comparing `message.author` to `id_user`), and renders avatar, message text, username, and timestamp; a delete icon is shown when `state.inputDelete` is true, which is set on `mouseEnter`/`click` (only if the message belongs to `id_user`) and cleared on `mouseLeave`.

---

## src/components/chat-form/message-list.jsx

### Purpose
Scrollable container that renders a `MessageDetail` per chat message and requests more (older) messages when the user scrolls to the bottom of the list; shows a loading spinner while a fetch is pending.

### Props
- `messages` — array, required.
- `loadding` — bool, required (prop name misspelled throughout the codebase).
- `lstAuthor` — array, required.
- `onDeleteMessage` — func, required.
- `onScrollMessage` — func, required.
- `id_user` — string, required.

### State
None.

### Methods
- `componentDidUpdate(_prevProps, _prevState)` — no-op (a scroll-to-bottom line is commented out).
- `handleScroll(e)` — computes whether the container is scrolled to the bottom; also computes (but does not otherwise use beyond local computation) an `itemCheckRead` array marking which `.sc-message` elements are above/below the current viewport, based on cumulative element heights vs. scroll position. When at the bottom and `props.loadding` is false, nudges `scrollTop += 30` and calls `props.onScrollMessage()`.

### Render Structure
`div.sc-message-list#main_lst_message` (`onScroll={handleScroll}`) mapping `props.messages` to `MessageDetail` (passing `message`, `id_user`, `lstAuthor`, `onDeleteMessage`), followed by a trailing centered `div.sc-message` showing a spinner when `props.loadding` is true.

---

## src/components/icon/index.jsx

### Purpose
Minimal SVG icon component that references a sprite `<symbol>` by id, used throughout the app wherever a named icon glyph is needed.

### Props
- `icon` — string, required; suffix used to build the `#icon-{icon}` xlink reference.
- `className` — string, extra classes.
- `onClick` — func.

### Render Structure
`<svg className="icon {className}" onClick={onClick}>` containing a single `<use xlinkHref="#icon-{icon}" />`.

---

## src/components/laser-key-hold/index.jsx (LaserKeyHold)

### Purpose
Step-driven wizard (separate desktop and mobile layouts) guiding the user through creating a laser-cut keychain-with-hole product: (1) upload/position artwork, (2) add a chain hole, (3) cut the die-line around the design, then proceed to cart. Heavily integrates with the global `DrawTool` singleton (event bus + fabric.js canvas) and Redux for laser-specific actions. Uses `DrawTool.modeSetup.LASER` as the path-index marker for laser items.

### Props
- `dispatch` — func (declared).
- `product` (connected, `state.product.product`).
- `shapesLaser` (connected, `state.drawTool.shapesLaser`).

### State
- `step_key_hold` — number 1-3, current wizard step.
- `height_pedestal` — number, computed vertical offset reserved for dynamic option panels.
- `scale` — number, computed CSS scale factor so the fixed-height desktop panel fits the viewport.
- `height_element` — number, measured container height (`-1` until measured).
- `height_pedestal_upload` — number, computed top offset for the step panel.
- `mobile` — bool, `matchMedia('(max-width: 1079px)')` at construction.
- (instance, non-state) `current_product` — last-seen `product.id`, used to detect product changes.
- (instance, non-state) `time_event` — `{intervalResponsiveTime, time[]}` used to debounce layout recalculation on rapid `option:update` events.

### Methods
- `componentDidMount()` — clears any pre-existing `'option:update'` DrawTool listener; after 200ms calls `updatePedestalUpload()` (desktop only) and `addEvent()`.
- `getHeightMargin()` — sums child element heights under `.id-check-option` and `.ex-check-option` DOM nodes; returns `{maxHeight, maxHeight_ex}`.
- `updatePedestalUpload()` — measures `.app-container-inner` height and, if the space is tight, computes `scale`/`height_pedestal_upload` so a 690px-tall reference panel fits.
- `componentDidUpdate()` — re-registers `addEvent()`; on `product.id` change, resets a `.t-p` footer's `bottom` style, recalculates layout, and jumps to step 3 if `DrawTool.load_step_laser` is set.
- `addEvent()` — idempotently subscribes to DrawTool `'option:update'`: on `size_option` payloads, debounces (150ms trailing window via `setInterval` + timestamp filtering) then recomputes mobile/desktop panel heights and writes them into DOM element inline styles (`.pedestal-mobile-footer`, `.list-button-three-preview`); on `remove_all` payloads, recounts items, calls `clearDiecut`/`clearHoleDesign`, resets `step_key_hold` to 1, and (if in laser mode) triggers `DrawTool` `'item:option' {mode:'no_action'}`.
- `fileUploadBG()` — triggers `'item:option' {mode:'uploadbg'}` (background removal).
- `checkCreatePedestalLaser(enable)` — triggers `'item:option' {mode:'check_pedestal', data:enable}`.
- `createDieCutLaserDetail()` — no-op if `step_key_hold < 3`; else triggers `'item:option' {mode:'finish_diecut_info', data:false}`.
- `clearDiecut(finishPathOver=0)` — no-op if `0`; else triggers `'item:option' {mode:'clear_laser_cuting'}` and dispatches `actions.updateBorder()`.
- `addHoleToDesign()` — if `step_key_hold > 1`: ensures laser mode is active, finds a `"circle"` shape template in `props.shapesLaser`, dispatches `actions.insertLaser({...circle, enableHole:true})`, selects the new fabric object and calls `DrawTool.setLayerSetup(LASER)`; also (outside step 2) triggers a deferred `'no_action'` + `updateBorder()` dispatch.
- `clearHoleDesign(number_hole=0)` — no-op if `0`; else removes all non-print (`pathIndex != -1`) items directly from the fabric collection/canvas (detaching/reattaching event handlers around the loop), re-finalizes the brush, re-renders, then dispatches `setActiveTool('layer')` + `updateBorder()`.
- `gotoCart()` — triggers `'cart:item' {mode:true}`.
- `fileUpload(file)` — triggers `'update:mode' {mode:'uploadFile'}`, stashes `file` on `DrawTool.upload_file_data`, triggers `'item:option' {mode:'upload_file'}` after 200ms, sets `step_key_hold:3`.
- `createAlert(title, detail)` — SweetAlert2 informational dialog with a single "もどる" (back) button.
- `backInfoAction(number_print, number_hole, finishPathOver)` — step-back handler: from step 2 clears the hole design and returns to step 1; from step 3 clears the die-cut, returns to step 2, and re-enables laser mode if needed.
- `uploadInfoNext(number_print, number_hole, finishPathOver)` — step-forward handler: step 1→2 requires `number_print>0` (else alert); step 2→3 requires `number_hole>0` (else alert, re-enables laser mode); step 3 requires `finishPathOver>0` to call `gotoCart()` (else alert).
- `show3DInfo()` — triggers `'control:mode' {mode:'preview_3d', enable:true}`.
- `render()` — recomputes `number_print`/`number_hole`/`finishPathOver` from `DrawTool.sides.selected.items._collection` each render and auto-advances/rewinds `step_key_hold` accordingly (calling `clearDiecut`/`clearHoleDesign` as side effects inside render), then renders the mobile or desktop layout.

### Render Structure
Mobile: numbered step title + two description lines; footer with step-specific action(s) (Dropzone upload / "画像背景透過" / "穴を作る" / "画像をカット" or a 3D-preview prompt) plus 戻る/次へ navigation. Desktop: three stacked step cards (upload; add-hole with a delete-hole action; cut with a delete-cut action and a 3D-preview link) and a persistent "カートへ進む" footer button gated on `finishPathOver>0`.

---

## src/components/laser-key-no-hole/index.jsx (LaserKeyNoHold)

### Purpose
Sibling of `LaserKeyHold` for a keychain variant with no chain hole (2-step wizard: upload artwork, then cut the die-line). Uses `DrawTool.modeSetup.LASER_NO_HOLE` as the path-index marker.

### Props
Same as `LaserKeyHold`: `dispatch`, connected `product`, `shapesLaser`.

### State
Same shape as `LaserKeyHold` plus `enableClick` (bool) — debounces rapid double-clicks on action buttons (disabled for ~1s after each action).

### Methods
Mirrors `LaserKeyHold`'s full method set (`componentDidMount`, `getHeightMargin`, `updatePedestalUpload`, `componentDidUpdate`, `addEvent`, `fileUploadBG`, `checkCreatePedestalLaser`, `createDieCutLaserDetail`, `clearDiecut`, `addHoleToDesign`, `clearHoleDesign`, `gotoCart`, `fileUpload`, `createAlert`, `backInfoAction`, `uploadInfoNext`) with these differences:
- `fileUploadBG`, `checkCreatePedestalLaser`, `createDieCutLaserDetail` guard on/set `state.enableClick`, re-enabling after 1000ms.
- `createDieCutLaserDetail` additionally iterates all print items and calls `DrawTool.checkAcrylicStickerMoveDesign(side, element, false)` before triggering `'finish_diecut_info'`.
- `addHoleToDesign` does not call `DrawTool.setLayerSetup(LASER)` after inserting a hole (present in `LaserKeyHold`).
- `backInfoAction`/`uploadInfoNext` implement a simplified 2-effective-step flow (step "2" is largely bypassed since there is no hole step); `number_hole` is always 0 under `LASER_NO_HOLE`.
- `render()`'s auto step-transition logic jumps directly from step 1 to step 3 once artwork exists, never routing through step 2.

### Render Structure
Same mobile/desktop dual-layout pattern as `LaserKeyHold` but with only two active step cards (upload; cut); the mobile header's numbered icon maps step 3 to icon `"2"` since there is no user-facing step 2.

---

## src/components/laser-pedestal/index.jsx (LaserPedestal) — desktop

### Purpose
Desktop wizard for laser-cut acrylic "pedestal + stand" products: pick a pedestal (base) shape from a server list, optionally place/cut a design onto the pedestal, then upload/cut a separate "stand" design, before proceeding to cart. Two parallel step tracks (pedestal-design steps 1-4, stand-design steps 5-7) are shown/hidden via CSS (`pedestal-block-item`) rather than unmounted.

### Props
- `dispatch`.
- Connected: `step_acrylic` (`state.drawTool.step_acrylic`, effectively shadowed by local state), `shapesModelLaser` (`state.drawTool.shapesModelLaser`), `is_createPedestalLaser` (`state.drawTool.is_createPedestalLaser`), `product` (`state.product.product`), `loading_process` (`state.drawTool.loading_process`).

### State
- `step_acrylic` — number 1-7 (1-4 = pedestal-shape sub-steps, 5+ = stand sub-steps).
- `shapesModelLaser` — array of `{id, content_url, preview_url, title}` pedestal-shape options, filtered to the current side/product.
- `height_pedestal`, `scale`, `height_pedestal_upload` — layout metrics (same pattern as the laser-key components).
- `enable_set_pedestal_fake` — bool, checkbox UI mirror.
- `enable_set_pedestal` — bool, whether the user opted to place an image on the pedestal.
- `clear_die_cut` — bool, whether a stand die-cut currently exists (controls the "delete cut line" action's enabled state).

### Methods
- `componentDidMount()` — calls `getModelCuttingLaser()`, dispatches `updateModelShapesLaser(data)`, filters shapes to those matching the current side's existing `pedestal` (if any) or all `mode==="1"` shapes, stores them, then (500ms later) calls `updatePedestalUpload()`.
- `getHeightMargin()` — same DOM-measuring helper pattern as the laser-key components.
- `updatePedestalUpload()` — computes `scale`/offset to fit a 505px-tall reference panel; if `DrawTool.load_step_acrylic` is set, jumps to step 5 with `clear_die_cut`/`enable_set_pedestal` true.
- `componentDidUpdate()` — (re)subscribes to `'option:update'` on every update (no idempotency guard) to recompute `height_pedestal` via a debounced size-option handler, and resets `step_acrylic` to 1 on `'remove_all'`; on product-id change, resets footer position, recalculates `shapesModelLaser`/layout, and if `DrawTool.stand_save_acrylic` has pending object data, shows a SweetAlert2 loading dialog and polls (every 500ms) until the canvas item count matches, then triggers `'check_all_pedestal_save'` and resets to step 1; also syncs `DrawTool.check_step_acrylic` (`true` when `step_acrylic>4`) and sizes `#pedestalUploadStepAction`'s max-height to the shape list.
- `updateStatePedestal(number)` — sets `state.step_acrylic` and `DrawTool.check_step_acrylic`.
- `onSelectOptionLaserInfo(select)` — triggers `'item:option' {mode:'select_pedestal', data:select}`; after 500ms re-enables laser mode if needed and either advances to step 2 (placing an image) or triggers `check_pedestal(false)` + jumps to step 5 + `check_pedestal_set` (skipping pedestal placement).
- `onSelectOptionLaser(select)` — if pedestal-laser design items already exist, shows a confirm dialog warning the change clears them; on confirm clears the pedestal and calls `onSelectOptionLaserInfo`; otherwise (no existing items) triggers `update_print_pedestal` if not placing an image, then calls `onSelectOptionLaserInfo` directly.
- `uploadImage(mode)` / `uploadImageStep2(mode)` — trigger `'update:mode' {mode:'uploadFile'}` and set step; `uploadImageStep2` additionally (step>=2 and `enable_set_pedestal`) sets `DrawTool.drawModeIntervention` and dispatches `actions.setPedestalLaser({active:true, data:is_createPedestalLaser.data})`.
- `fileUploadBG()` — triggers `'item:option' {mode:'uploadbg'}`.
- `checkCreatePedestalLaser(enable)` — triggers `'item:option' {mode:'check_pedestal', data:enable}`; advances to step 4 if `enable_set_pedestal`.
- `createPedestalLaserDetail()` — if `enable_set_pedestal` and step>=2: triggers `update_print_pedestal` then (200ms) `check_pedestal_set`; advances to step 4.
- `createPedestalLaserHole()` — if `enable_set_pedestal`: at step 4 triggers `check_pedestal_mobile` and advances to step 5; else evaluates existing pedestal-laser design/print items to decide whether to trigger `update_print_pedestal`+`check_pedestal_set` before advancing to step 5, or advance directly.
- `laserPadFinish(info_check_draw)` — if no stand design present, shows a warning requiring a design; else triggers `'item:option' {mode:'finish_acrylic'}` and advances to step 7.
- `laserDieCutExisting()` — triggers `'item:option' {mode:'clear_laser_cuting'}` and sets `clear_die_cut:false`.
- `gotoCart()` — triggers `'cart:item' {mode:true}`.
- `eventCheckBoxPad()` — toggles `enable_set_pedestal`; turning it off resets step to 1 and (if a pedestal-laser is active) calls `checkCreatePedestalLaser(false)` after 200ms; turning it on jumps to step 2 if pedestal-laser design items already exist.
- `fileUpload(file, mode_info)` — triggers `'update:mode' {mode:'uploadFile'}`, stashes `upload_file_data`, triggers `'item:option' {mode:'upload_file'}` after 200ms; advances to step `mode_info` if unplaced print items exist.
- `backStatePedestal()` — chains `'clear_laser_cuting'` → `'clear_laser_border'` → `'update_print_pedestal'` (200ms apart) then sets step to 2.
- `render()` — computes `numberItem`/`numberCheck`/`check_finish_acrylic`/`check_design_Pedestal`/`check_laser_Pedestal`/`info_check_draw`/`numberCheckSpecial` from the canvas item collection; auto-resets to step 1 if no pedestal-laser items remain; derives `checkBlockInfoStep3` (footer next/back/hidden CSS state); toggles `clear_die_cut` based on `check_finish_acrylic`; renders a two-tab header, two parallel step-panel tracks, and a shared footer.

### Render Structure
Two-tab header (台座デザイン/スタンドデザイン); pedestal-shape step list (shape-picker grid + placement checkbox, upload step, cut/confirm step) shown when `step_acrylic<=4`; stand step list (upload, confirm/cut with delete-cut) shown when `step_acrylic>=5`; footer with a track-switch button ("スタンドデザインへ" / "台座デザインへ戻る") and, on the stand track, a "カートへ進む" button gated on `check_finish_acrylic`.

---

## src/components/laser-pedestal-mobile/index.jsx (LaserPedestalMobile)

### Purpose
Mobile counterpart to `LaserPedestal` implementing the same two-track acrylic laser wizard as a single linear 1-5 step sequence with a unified 戻る/次へ footer, plus additional confirmation dialogs suited to a touch flow (e.g. selecting a shape now requires an explicit "次へ" tap to apply it, rather than applying instantly).

### Props
Same shape as `LaserPedestal`: `dispatch`, connected `step_acrylic`, `shapesModelLaser`, `is_createPedestalLaser`, `product`, `loading_process`.

### State
- `step_acrylic` — number 1-5.
- `shapesModelLaser` — array, filtered pedestal-shape options.
- `height_pedestal` — layout metric.
- `enable_set_pedestal_fake`, `enable_set_pedestal` — bools (checkbox UI + effective flag).
- `id_pedestal_exist` — array, initialized empty, not read after construction.
- `selectedOption` — object|null; the pedestal shape the user has tapped (distinct from `LaserPedestal`, which applies the selection immediately).

### Methods
- `componentDidMount()` — loads shapes via `getModelCuttingLaser()`, dispatches `updateModelShapesLaser`, filters to the current side's shapes, then (1000ms later) calls `updatePedestalUpload()`.
- `updatePedestalUpload()` — if `DrawTool.load_step_acrylic` is set, jumps to step 5 with `clear_die_cut`/`enable_set_pedestal` true.
- `componentDidUpdate()` — subscribes to `'option:update'` (no idempotency guard) for the debounced size-option layout recompute (positions `.pedestal-mobile-footer`, `.three-pre-btn-3d`, `.list-button-three-preview`), resets step to 1 on `'remove_all'`; on product change, recalculates `shapesModelLaser` and handles `load_step_acrylic`/pending `stand_save_acrylic` data (loading dialog + polling), same pattern as the desktop version; syncs `DrawTool.check_step_acrylic` (`step_acrylic>3`) and sizes/positions various step-indicator elements.
- `componentWillUnmount()` — no-op.
- `updateStatePedestal(number)` — sets `step_acrylic` and `DrawTool.check_step_acrylic`.
- `backInfoActionPedestal()` — step-back handler: depending on step and `enable_set_pedestal`, triggers `clear_laser_cuting`/`clear_laser_border`/`update_print_pedestal` combinations before decrementing the step (or resetting to 1 when skipping the pedestal-design sub-track).
- `onSelectOptionLaser(select)` — only stores the tapped shape in `state.selectedOption` (applied later via `uploadInfoNext`/`onAllyOptionLaser`).
- `onAllyOptionLaser(select)` — shows a SweetAlert2 loading spinner, triggers `'item:option' {mode:'select_pedestal', data:select}`, then (500ms) re-enables laser mode and advances to step 2, or (skipping pedestal image placement) triggers `check_pedestal(false)` + advances to step 4 + `check_pedestal_set`; hides/closes the spinner after 1500ms.
- `uploadImage(mode)` — triggers `'update:mode' {mode:'uploadFile'}` and sets step.
- `fileUploadBG()` — triggers `'item:option' {mode:'uploadbg'}`.
- `checkCreatePedestalLaser(enable)` — triggers `'item:option' {mode:'check_pedestal', data:enable}`; advances to step 4 if `enable_set_pedestal`.
- `laserPadFinish(info_check_draw)` — if no stand design, shows a warning; else triggers `'item:option' {mode:'finish_acrylic'}` and advances to step 5.
- `gotoCart()` — triggers `'cart:item' {mode:true}`.
- `eventCheckBoxPad()` — toggles `enable_set_pedestal` (simpler than the desktop version — no "jump to step 2" branch on enable).
- `fileUpload(file, mode_info)` — triggers `'update:mode' {mode:'uploadFile'}`, stashes `upload_file_data`, triggers `'item:option' {mode:'upload_file'}` (step-advance code left commented out).
- `uploadInfoNext()` — the primary "次へ" handler; a large step-dependent state machine: step 1 applies the selected/default pedestal shape (with a confirm dialog if it would discard existing pedestal-laser items), then schedules `setPedestalLaser` dispatch if placing an image; step 2 warns (confirm to skip) if no print design exists on the pedestal before advancing to step 3; step 3 verifies all placed pedestal images have been die-cut (via `lastBorder`/`last_info_acrylic` markers) and shows a corrective alert if not, else advances to step 4 (triggering `check_pedestal_mobile`); step 4 warns (confirm to skip) if no stand design exists before advancing; step 5 requires a completed stand die-cut (`imageCuttingLaser`) to call `gotoCart()`, else shows a corrective alert.
- `createPedestalLaserDetail()` — if `enable_set_pedestal`, triggers `update_print_pedestal` then (200ms) `check_pedestal_set`.
- `render()` — computes `numberItem`/`numberCheck`/`check_finish_acrylic`/`lastID` (currently selected or first shape id) from the canvas collection; auto-resets to step 1 if no relevant items remain; derives the footer "next" button label (次へ / スタンドデザイン / カートへ進む) from the step; renders the single-column step sequence.

### Render Structure
Two-tab header; a series of conditionally-shown step-title blocks (pedestal shape picker with per-shape selection tick + placement checkbox; upload prompts; confirm/cut prompts for both pedestal and stand); footer with a contextual action (Dropzone upload / "画像をカット") plus 戻る/次へ navigation.

---

## src/components/laser-sticker/index.jsx (LaserSticker)

### Purpose
2-step wizard for laser-cut sticker products: upload artwork, then die-cut around it with a selectable offset distance (mm). Includes a substantial client-side image-processing pipeline (via `ImageCuttingLaser`/`MethodImageProcessing` utilities) that renders a transparent preview, detects hole/fringe boundaries via pixel analysis, and generates the laser-cutting SVG vector path — more elaborate than the sibling laser components. Uses `DrawTool.modeSetup.LASER_STICKER` as the path-index marker.

### Props
- `dispatch`; connected `product`, `shapesLaser`.

### State
- `step_key_hold` — number 1-2.
- `height_pedestal`, `scale`, `height_element`, `height_pedestal_upload`, `mobile` — layout metrics (same pattern as laser-key components).
- `mm_distance` — number, the selected cutting-offset distance in mm (can be negative for an inward "-1mm" offset).

### Methods
- `componentDidMount()` / `getHeightMargin()` / `updatePedestalUpload()` / `componentDidUpdate()` / `addEvent()` — same layout-measuring/event-subscription pattern as `LaserKeyHold`, keyed off `LASER_STICKER`; the `'remove_all'` handler in `addEvent` does not call a hole-clearing routine (stickers have no holes).
- `fileUploadBG()` — triggers `'item:option' {mode:'uploadbg'}`.
- `clearDiecut(finishPathOver=0)` — triggers `'item:option' {mode:'clear_laser_cuting'}` then dispatches `actions.updateBorder()`.
- `gotoCart()` — triggers `'cart:item' {mode:true}`.
- `fileUpload(file)` — triggers `'update:mode' {mode:'uploadFile'}`, stashes `upload_file_data`, triggers `'item:option' {mode:'upload_file'}` after 200ms, advances to step 2.
- `createAlert(title, detail)` — SweetAlert2 info dialog.
- `backInfoAction(number_print, finishPathOver)` — triggers `DrawTool` `'option:update' {size_option:10}` (forces a layout-resize event), then from step 2 returns to step 1; a dead step-3 branch (never reached in this component) would clear the die-cut and return to step 2.
- `uploadInfoNext(number_print, finishPathOver)` — triggers the same resize ping, then step 1 requires a design to advance to step 2 (else alert), step 2 requires a finished die-cut to `gotoCart()` (else alert).
- `checkImageCuttingLaser()` — returns true if any item across `DrawTool.sides._collection[0]` has `imageCuttingLaser` set.
- `addImageProcess(img)` — returns a Promise resolving `true` once `img.onload` fires; also sets `img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous'`.
- `resetModePrint()` — calls `DrawTool.setLayerSetup(-1)`, deselects/disables all non-print items, re-renders, and disables `side.drawingMode`.
- `async getImagePreviewData()` — core pixel-analysis routine: temporarily moves all laser/hole items off-canvas to render a "clean" transparent preview, then moves only print items off-canvas to render a "holes-only" preview, restoring original positions afterward; if no laser/hole items exist, returns the plain transparent preview directly. Otherwise loads both previews into off-DOM canvases, extracts alpha-channel pixel maps, uses a `MethodImageProcessing` instance (`detectAreaHoleAndFuirge`) to compute a fringe/hole boundary polygon per item center, fills that polygon (punch-out) on the base canvas, composites the images, and returns the result as a data URL.
- `async getImagePreviewToShow(mm_distance, disableDiecut)` — loads the composed preview, draws it onto a canvas sized to the image or to the side's real-world cm dimensions (`mmLaserEx`), extracts pixel data, and feeds it to `ImageCuttingLaser.getInfoDataVectorLaser` with a computed kerf/offset distance (`mmLaser`, derived from `side.mmLaser`/`side.mmLaserEx` scaled by `mm_distance` and zoom/scale factors) to produce vector cut paths; each result path is flagged `imageCuttingLaser:true` (and `sp_smoth:false` for `LASER_STICKER`) and added via `items.addLaserSVGDataInfo()`. Afterward calls `DrawTool.checkLaserColor()` and, if `disableDiecut`, resets print mode and dispatches `updateBorder()`/`setActiveTool('layers')`.
- `onClickDieCutOptionRight(mm_distance=3, disableDiecut=false)` — guarded to run only at step>=2; stores `mm_distance`; ensures laser mode/tool is active; if a die-cut already exists, for `LASER_STICKER` mode directly strips existing cut-line items from the fabric canvas/collection and regenerates via `getImagePreviewToShow`, else calls `showPopupImageCuttingLaser()` (referenced but not defined in this file — likely dead/inherited code); if no existing cut, shows a loading spinner and calls `getImagePreviewToShow` after a short delay.
- `render()` — computes `number_print`/`number_hole`/`finishPathOver` scoped to `LASER_STICKER`; auto-resets to step 1 (clearing any die-cut) if no print items remain, or auto-advances desktop straight to step 2 once a print item exists; renders mobile or desktop layout.

### Render Structure
Mobile: step title; footer with Dropzone upload+background-removal (step 1) or four offset radio buttons (-1mm/1mm/2mm/3mm, step 2), plus 戻る/次へ(カートへ進む). Desktop: two stacked step cards (upload; four offset radio options) and a "カートへ進む" footer button gated on `finishPathOver>0`.

---

## src/components/layers/index.jsx (Layers)

### Purpose
Renders the horizontally-scrollable, drag-reorderable "layers" strip (thumbnails of canvas objects) used to select/reorder items in the design tool. Wraps a `react-sortable-hoc` `SortableList` inside a custom scrollbar on desktop, or unwrapped on mobile.

### Props
- `items` — array of layer objects (`{index, active, preview, ...}`).
- `callbackNewOrder` — func, called with the reordered `items` array after a drag-sort completes.
- `onBlur` — func, called with a layer's `uuid` when it's deactivated via click.
- `onFocus` — func, called with a layer's `uuid` when it's activated via click.

### State
- `selected` — null (unused elsewhere).
- `selection` — array, always `[]` (unused elsewhere).
- `moving`, `movingstarted` — bools, unused elsewhere.
- `items` — array, local mirror of `props.items`.
- `mobile` — bool, `matchMedia('(max-width: 1080px)')`.

### Methods
- `componentDidMount()` — registers `window` resize → `getIsMobile`; calls it once.
- `componentWillReceiveProps(nextProps)` — syncs `state.items` from `nextProps.items`.
- `componentWillUnmount()` — removes the resize listener.
- `getIsMobile()` — updates `state.mobile`.
- `onClickCallback(uuid, event)` — finds the item by `uuid` (`item.index`), toggles its `active` flag, calls `props.onBlur(uuid)` on deactivate or `props.onFocus(uuid)` on activate, updates `state.items`, calls `event.preventDefault()`.
- `shouldCancelStart(e)` — returns `true` (cancel drag-start) unless the event target's `className` includes `'active'`, so only an already-selected thumbnail can start a drag.
- `onSortStart(event)` — if more than one item is active (multi-select drag), builds a custom `.helper` drag-preview by cloning each active layer's DOM node into it and hiding the originals, so multiple thumbnails appear to drag together.
- `onSortEnd({oldIndex, newIndex})` — restores visibility of all `.layer` DOM nodes, then reorders `state.items` by extracting all active items (preserving relative order) and reinserting them as a contiguous block at `newIndex`; updates state and calls `props.callbackNewOrder(items)`.
- `render()` — chooses a `<Scrollbars>`-wrapped `<SortableList>` (desktop, `distance={10}` drag threshold) or a bare `<SortableList>` (mobile); both use `axis="x"`, `useDragHandle={false}`, and the handlers above.

### Render Structure
Desktop: `<Scrollbars>` > `<SortableList>`. Mobile: bare `<SortableList>`. Both pass `uniqueIdToken="layers"`, `items`, `selection`, `onClickCallback`, `onSortStart`, `onSortEnd`, `shouldCancelStart`.

---

## src/components/layers/item.jsx (SortableItem)

### Purpose
A single draggable/sortable layer thumbnail `<li>`, wrapped with react-sortable-hoc's `SortableElement`. Renders a background-image thumbnail and dispatches selection differently on mobile (touch, `onMouseDown`) vs. desktop (`onClick`).

### Props
- `onClickCallback` — func.
- `checked` — bool, drives the `active` CSS class.
- `preview` — string, background-image URL.
- `uuid` — string, `data-uuid` attribute value.
- `uniqueIdToken`, `index` — used to build the `data-uniqueidtoken` attribute (passed from the parent list's map).

### State
- `mobile` — bool, `matchMedia('(max-width: 1080px)')`.

### Methods
- `constructor(props)` — binds `onClickCallback`/`getIsMobile`.
- `componentDidMount()` / `componentWillUnmount()` — register/unregister a `window` resize listener calling `getIsMobile`.
- `getIsMobile()` — updates `state.mobile`.
- `onClickCallback(event)` — delegates to `props.onClickCallback(props.uuid, event)`.

### Render Structure
Single `<li className="layer (active)">` styled via `backgroundImage: props.preview`; binds `onMouseDown` on mobile or `onClick` on desktop, both to the local `onClickCallback`.

---

## src/components/layers/list.jsx (SortableList)

### Purpose
The `SortableContainer`-wrapped `<ul>` laying out all layer thumbnails horizontally; a pure mapping/render component with no own logic (drag-state management is handled internally by the `SortableContainer` HOC).

### Props
- `items` — array, default `[]`.
- `selection` — array (unused within this file's body).
- `uniqueIdToken` — string.
- `onClickCallback` — func.

### Render Structure
`<ul className="layers">` mapping `items` to `<SortableItem>` (keyed `sortable-item-{index}`), passing `preview`, `checked` (=`item.active`), `uniqueIdToken`, `index`, `onClickCallback`, `uuid` (=`item.index`).

---

## src/components/loader/index.jsx (Loader)

### Purpose
Simple CSS-animated "blob" loading spinner shown throughout the app during async operations.

### Render Structure
`<div className="loader">` containing six `<div className="blob blob-N">` elements (N = 0..5), animated purely via CSS.

## src/components/menu-filter/index.jsx (MenuFilter)

### Purpose
Connected toolbar shown above a product category grid: displays the result count, a category-dependent sort-order `<select>`, a "絞り込み" (narrow down) filter-toggle button, and a price-notation info popup link.

### Props
- Declared: `children`, `close` (func), `title` (string), `back` (func), `dispatch` (func) — unused in this component's render (a leftover shared prop-type contract with sibling "load" components).
- Consumed via connect: `numberProduct`, `category_style_id`, `toogleFilterProp`, `category_active`, `current_Category_id` (plus several other connected fields fetched but unused in render, e.g. `keyFilters`, `objPriceFilter`, etc.).

### State
- `mobile` — bool, `matchMedia('(max-width: 1079px)')` captured once at construction (not resynced on resize).

### Methods
- `onChangeSort(event)` — dispatches `ProductActions.updateValueProductSort(event.target.value)`.
- `onToogleFilter()` — dispatches `ProductActions.updateToogleFilterBox(!toogleFilterProp)`.
- `onPopupPrice()` — fires a SweetAlert2 dialog explaining the two price notations (プリント込価格 / まとめて割価格).

### Render Structure
`.filter-menu-active` > result-count text (`検索結果：{numberProduct}件`); a sort `<select>` whose option set is chosen from three variants based on whether `current_Category_id` is in `CATEGORIES_ACC_STANDARD4=[34]`, `CATEGORIES_ACC_POPULARITY3=[55,21,27]`, or neither (general set, defaulting to popularity-first for `[T_SHIRT, BAG, PAPER, OTHER, CUP_KITCHEN, KEY_CHAIN]`); a "絞り込み" filter-toggle `div` (hidden when `category_style_id === '1986tn'`, the favorites pseudo-category); a footer legend row ("プ：ボディ料金 ま：まとめて割") and a "価格表記について" link opening the price popup. Defines a local `Number.prototype.format()` thousands-separator helper on every render.

## src/components/product-card-filter/index.jsx (ProductCardFilter)

### Purpose
Connected product tile used within the filtered product-listing grid: product image, maker logo, discount badge, thickness/size/color info, pricing (regular + bulk-discount), an "アイテム詳細" (item detail) popup trigger, and a favorite toggle with a timed "added to favorites" fade banner.

### Props
- `title`, `actionTitle`, `onClick` (func), `image`, `images`, `removeBtn` (bool, default `false`), `onRemove`, `product` (object, required shape `product.Product.*`), `is_product`, `id_product` (default `1`), `typeOfProduct`.
- Connected: `favoriteProducts`, `dispatch`, `user` (`state.product.user`), `availableFontsCategories` (fetched, unused).

### State
- `reloadState` — number (1/2/3/5), a status flag toggled through the favorite add/remove flow (not directly rendered).
- `fadeOutActive` — bool, controls the initial fade-in of the favorite-added banner.
- `opacityActive` — bool, controls the banner's delayed fade-out class.

### Module-level
- `count` — shared mutable counter across all instances of this component, used to run `adjustTitles()` only once per mount/update cycle (a fragile pattern since it's module-scoped rather than per-instance).

### Methods
- `componentDidUpdate()` — increments module-level `count`; the first time it equals `1`, calls `this.adjustTitles()`.
- `componentWillUnmount()` — resets `count` to `0`.
- `onProductDetail(product)` — fires a SweetAlert2 dialog rendering `product.Product.html_tool` (raw HTML) with confirm/cancel buttons; on confirm calls `props.onClick()`.
- `removeProductByID(id)` — dispatches `ProductActions.decreaseFavoriteProducts(id)`, calls the `removeFavoriteProductItem(id)` API, sets `state.reloadState = 3`.
- `addProductByID(product)` — if `user` is set: dispatches `ProductActions.increaseFavoriteProducts(product)`, shows the fade banner, calls `addFavoriteProductItem(product.Product.id)` API, and via two `setTimeout`s (3000ms, 5000ms) toggles `opacityActive` then resets the banner state; if not logged in, shows a SweetAlert2 "please log in" prompt and returns.
- `adjustTitles()` — DOM "equal row height" routine: resets all `.pro-sp-title-cont` elements to `height:auto`, groups them by their `getBoundingClientRect().top` (visual row), then sets every element in a row to that row's max `clientHeight`.

### Render Structure
`.product-c` > image wrap (`LazyLoadImage`, maker logo, discount badge chosen from `product.Product.discount_image` or a code-to-badge-URL map keyed by `product.Product.discount` `'1'`-`'6'`/`'7'`, optional thickness badge, size/color info, favorite-added fade banner) + detail body (title, item code/maker, price rows for 通常価格/まとめて割価格, "アイテム詳細" link, favorite add/remove toggle button) + a "選択" (select) call-to-action footer.

---

## src/components/product-card/index.jsx (ProductCard)

### Purpose
The primary connected product tile used across category browsing, template lists, and laser-item lists. Supports three rendering variants keyed off `id_product`/`is_product`: a plain "template" card (`id_product === -101`), a rich detailed card (`is_product && product`, normal `id_product`), and a simpler "category" card (otherwise) — each with maker logo, discount badges, pricing, print-method tags, favorites toggle, and a detail popup.

### Props
- `title`, `actionTitle`, `onClick` (func), `image`, `images`, `removeBtn` (default `false`), `onRemove`, `product`, `is_product`, `id_product` (default `1`), `category_active`, `discount`, `lazerItem` (bool, adds a `lazer-item` class).
- Connected: `favoriteProducts`, `dispatch`, `user` (`state.product.user`).

### State
- `reloadState` (number 1/2/3/5), `fadeOutActive` (bool), `opacityActive` (bool) — identical semantics to `ProductCardFilter`.

### Module-level
- Shared `count` (row-adjust-once guard), `X`/`Y` (module-scoped `setTimeout` handle placeholders for the favorite-banner timers), constants `FLYER = -102`, `BOOK_FILES = -103` (add a `cate-img-pages` class for those `id_product`s).

### Methods
- `componentDidUpdate()` — same "run `adjustTitles` once via module `count`" pattern as `ProductCardFilter`.
- `componentWillUnmount()` — resets `count`.
- `removeProductByID(id)` — same as `ProductCardFilter`.
- `addProductByID(product)` — same as `ProductCardFilter`, storing timer handles in module-scoped `X`/`Y`.
- `onProductDetail(product)` — same SweetAlert2 detail-popup pattern.
- `adjustTitles()` — same row-height-equalizing routine, targeting `.pro-title-cont` (vs. `.pro-sp-title-cont` in `ProductCardFilter`).
- `render()` — computes `class_name` (adds `'product'` when `is_product`), `mobile` (fresh `matchMedia` check per render), `responsi` (thumbnail height: 80 mobile / 140 desktop), `favoriteProduct`, and `imageDiscountProduct` (same discount-code mapping as `ProductCardFilter`); branches into the three JSX variants. A large block of alternate detailed-card markup is left commented out but unused.

### Render Structure
**Template variant** (`id_product===-101`): `.product-card` with optional remove icon, title header, maker/id text, preview image, product-info block (color/size/material + prices), action button. **Detailed variant** (`is_product && product`): `.product-c` (`+ .lazer-item` if `lazerItem`) with image wrap (`LazyLoadImage`, maker logo, discount badge, print-method tag list, thickness/size/color info, favorite-fade banner) and detail body (title/code/maker, print-method chip list, price rows, "アイテム詳細" + favorite toggle, "選択" CTA). **Category variant** (else): `.product-card` with optional remove icon, title header, category-level discount badge, preview image (FLYER/BOOK_FILES special class), action button.

---

### Purpose
Stateless product-tile variant used in laser-product color/side selection contexts. Supports a "colorMode" layout — a plain color-swatch card with a checkmark overlay when selected — in addition to the standard info-card layout.

## src/components/product-lazer-load/index.jsx (ProductLaserLoad)

### Purpose
Connected modal/overlay shell for browsing laser-product categories and items: header (title, back, close), a horizontally-scrollable category tab bar (with a "お気に入り" favorites entry and left/right scroll-arrow buttons on mobile), an optional maker filter bar, and a two-pane (categories | products) content area supplied via `children`. Manages Escape-to-close and locks background scroll while mounted.

### Props
- `children` (array|element), `close` (func), `title` (string), `back` (func), `dispatch` (func) — plus render-time-only props not declared via `propTypes`: `category_page`, `filterCategory`, `category_active`, `loading_process`, `category_styles`, `categories`, `template`, `screen_products`, `makers`, `maker_active`, `filterMaker`, `screen_mobile`, `filter`; connected `category_style_id`.

### State
None (only bound methods; no constructor state).

### Methods
- `hide(e)` — closes the overlay (`props.close()`) on Escape (`keyCode 27`); bound to `document` `'keyup'` in `componentDidMount`/removed in `componentWillUnmount`, which also toggle a `fixed` class on `document.body` to lock background scroll.
- `chooseMaker(event)` — calls `props.chooseMaker(event.target.value)` (not in `propTypes`, assumed supplied by the parent).
- `helpScrollArrowsLoad()` — inspects `#ArrowsProductLoad`'s scroll position to color the left/right scroll-arrow icons active/inactive.
- `helpClickArrowsLoad(arrow)` — on `'right'`, scrolls fully right and updates arrow colors; otherwise scrolls to a small fixed offset (15px) with inverted arrow coloring.
- `onProductFavoriteActive()` — dispatches `ProductActions.toggleLoadProductTypeContainer({state:true, id:'itemFavorite'})`, `toggleLoadProductCategoryContainer(false)`, and `updateStyleCategoryID('1986tn')` to switch into the favorites pseudo-category.

### Render Structure
`.product-load` > conditional `<ProcessBar/>`(`loading_process`)/`<Loader/>`(`filter`); header (title, back button — disabled variant on `category_page`, close button); `category_bar` (category tab links incl. favorites, mobile variant adds scroll-arrow buttons; hidden entirely when `template`); `maker_bar` (a `<select>` on mobile or a row of links on desktop, shown only when `screen_products`); and a responsive two-pane content area rendering `children[0]`/`children[1]` (horizontal-scroll on mobile, side-by-side columns on desktop), or a `<Loader/>` while `categories.length===0`.

---

## src/components/product-load/index.jsx (ProductLoad)

### Purpose
The general (non-laser) counterpart to `ProductLaserLoad` — a connected modal shell for the main "load a product" category browser, additionally embedding `MenuFilter` and `FilterMenuBox` for categories that support faceted filtering, and duplicating much of `MenuFilter`'s own favorites/sort/price-info dispatch logic directly.

### Props
- Declared: `children`, `close`, `title`, `back`, `dispatch`.
- Render-time (not all declared): `category_page`, `category_active`, `loading_process`, `category_styles`, `categories`, `template`, `screen_products`, `screen_mobile`, `filter`, `keyFilters`, `keyword_filter_id`, `numberProductFilter`, `toogleFilterProp`, `objPriceFilter`, `objKeyFilter`, `objSizeFilter`, `objColorFilter`, `objSideFilter`, `objMaterialFilter`, `objValueFilter`, `arrStatusFilter`, `objCategoryFilter`, `objCategorySubFilter`, `loadingKey`, `category_style_id`, `numberProduct` (many connected via `mapStateToProps`).

### State
- `mobile` — bool, `matchMedia('(max-width: 1079px)')` captured once at construction.

### Methods
- `hide(e)` — Escape-to-close, same pattern as `ProductLaserLoad`; wired via `componentDidMount`/`componentWillUnmount`, which also toggle `document.body`'s `fixed` class.
- `onProductFavoriteActive()` — same three-dispatch favorites-view switch.
- `chooseMaker(event)` — forwards to `props.chooseMaker(event.target.value)` (dead code in this file — no `maker_bar` is rendered here, unlike `ProductLaserLoad`).
- `helpScrollArrowsLoad()` / `helpClickArrowsLoad(arrow)` — identical mobile category-scroll-arrow logic to `ProductLaserLoad`.
- `onOpenBoxFilter()` — dispatches `ProductActions.toggleBoxFilter(true)`.
- `onChangeSort(event)` — dispatches `ProductActions.updateValueProductSort(event.target.value)`.
- `onPopupPrice()` — same SweetAlert2 price-notation popup as `MenuFilter`.

### Render Structure
`.product-load` > conditional `<Loader/>` (`loadingKey`/`filter`) and `<ProcessBar/>` (`loading_process`); header (title, custom-image back icon-button, custom-image close icon-button — image-based rather than `Icon`-based, unlike other "load" components); `category_bar` (category tab links + favorites tab; for categories in `[T_SHIRT, BAG, OTHER, FLAG, SP_CASE, PAPER, KEY_CHAIN, CUP_KITCHEN, UNIFORM]` when `screen_products` is true, embeds `<MenuFilter>` plus a `<FilterMenuBox>` panel toggled via `toogleFilterProp`/`actived-filter-box`; a parallel mobile variant adds the scroll-arrow wrapper); `loading_category` (a `<Loader/>` while `categories.length===0`, else `children` wrapped in `.content-product-filter` or `.content`). Defines a local `Number.prototype.format()` helper per render.
