## src/components/text-2-img/dialog.jsx (DialogAI)

### Purpose
Content component rendered inside a SweetAlert2 modal (opened from `Text2Image`) showing the grid of AI-generated image candidates as they stream in, letting the user multi-select candidates and insert the selected images onto the currently active `DrawTool` canvas side.

### Props
- `dispatch` — func.
- `dataLink` — object `{data, processing}`, passed imperatively as a prop into the SweetAlert2-rendered React content (not via normal parent-render prop flow).

### State
- `listImageCreateFromAI` — array of image URLs successfully generated so far.
- `image_select` — array of indices the user has toggled selected.
- `viewLoadingImagePreview` — array of 3 placeholder "loading" JSX tiles, shown until all expected images resolve, then set to `""` to hide them.

### Instance fields
- `dataLinkSave` — mirror of `props.dataLink`.
- `timeStartCallApi` — `Date` at construction (unused later).
- `dataProcessingToImage` — `{}` (unused).
- `checkSuccess` — array of `{key: bool}` per-item processed flags.
- `checkSuccessLoad` — counter of resolved items.

### Methods
- `async getBase64Image(url)` — fetches `url` (with a cache-busting query param) and resolves a base64 data URL via `FileReader`; defined but not invoked elsewhere in this file (utility/dead code).
- `selectImage(index)` — toggles `index` in/out of `state.image_select`.
- `checkEta(item)` — returns a Promise resolving after a delay derived from the server-reported `item.info.eta` relative to elapsed time since `item.timeStartRequest`; increments `checkSuccessLoad` and clears `viewLoadingImagePreview` once all expected items have resolved.
- `async loadImageAI()` — iterates `dataLinkSave.data`; for each not-yet-processed, non-`"processing"` item, awaits `checkEta(item)`, then merges its `file_url` into `state.listImageCreateFromAI` (deduped).
- `async componentDidMount()` — copies `props.dataLink` into `dataLinkSave`, initializes `checkSuccess` flags per item, calls `loadImageAI()` if any items exist.
- `checkUploadImage(link, location)` — returns a Promise calling `DrawTool.sides.selected.items.addImageWithSize(link + cache-bust)`; on success, if the current draw mode is a ribbon/tap-ribbon mode, selects the new fabric object and (200ms later) dispatches `actions.tapOrRibbonProcessingColorData()` before resolving; on failure, shows a `swal` error naming the failed image's position (`location`) and rejects.
- `async insertImageFromText()` — dispatches `DrawToolActions.setLoadingProcess(true)`, then for each selected index awaits `checkUploadImage(...)` and dispatches proportional progress (`DrawToolActions.updateProcessBar`, `1/N` per image), catching/reporting per-image failures via `swal`; finally dispatches `setLoadingProcess(false)` and `setIsText2IMG(false)`, and closes the dialog (`SwalDialog.close()`).

### Render Structure
`.AI-preview-list` grid mapping `listImageCreateFromAI` to clickable thumbnails (toggling an `image-select` highlight class via `selectImage`), followed by the `viewLoadingImagePreview` placeholder tiles; a button row with "キャンセル" (closes the dialog) and, only when at least one image is selected, "デザインに追加する" (calls `insertImageFromText`).

---

## src/components/text-2-img/index.jsx (Text2Image)

### Purpose
Connected panel (shown when `activeTool === "imageAI"`) letting the user type a text prompt, requiring login (with an inline SweetAlert2 login/signup/Yahoo-OAuth flow if not logged in), submitting the prompt to a text-to-image AI backend, polling for completion, and opening the `DialogAI` picker so the user can insert chosen results onto the canvas.

### Props
- `AIModel` — array, connected, unused in render.
- `activeTool` — string, connected; gates whether the panel renders.
- `dispatch` — func, connected.
- `user` — any, connected; truthy means logged in.

### State
- `content` — string, the current text prompt.
- `url_preview` — string, default `"na"`; a preview image URL, though nothing in this file sets it away from `"na"` (an apparently vestigial/incomplete feature).
- `image_select` — array, declared but unused within this file (the real selection state lives in `DialogAI`).

### Instance fields
- `checkKeyImageAI` — `0` (unused).
- `dataLinkSave` — `{data:[], processing:[]}`, accumulates AI results across the generate+poll flow.

### Methods
- `onCloseBox()` — dispatches `DrawToolActions.setIsText2IMG(false)`.
- `handleAddText(event)` — updates `state.content`.
- `waitForNextEvent(element, type)` — generic helper returning a Promise resolving on the next occurrence of a DOM event (defined but not directly invoked in this file).
- `login(content, api)` — raw `XMLHttpRequest` POST to `api` with email/password form-encoded body; resolves/rejects a Promise based on HTTP 200.
- `signup(content, api)` — same XHR pattern for nickname/email/password/`regist_from_site`.
- `makeid(length)` — generates a random alphanumeric string (used as a request correlation key and as a CSRF-like `state` param for Yahoo OAuth).
- `gup(name, url)` — extracts a query-string parameter value from a URL via regex.
- `saveDesign()` — builds a Yahoo! JAPAN OAuth authorization URL (using `KEY_YAHOO`/`HOST` constants and a random `state` token embedding the current page URL) and redirects the browser to it (`window.location.replace`) — a "continue with Yahoo login, then resume this design" flow.
- `messageErr(message)` — shows a simple SweetAlert2 warning dialog.
- `alertLogin()` — full login/signup modal flow: an initial "UP-T会員限定機能です" (members-only) prompt with ログイン/会員登録 choices; confirming opens a login form (email/password, with a Yahoo-login cancel action and a 会員登録 close action) — a successful `login()` call dispatches `ProductActions.updateUser(true)`/`updateUserAccess(...)`, persists the session (`persistSession`), reloads favorites (`getFavoriteProductItem`), and re-invokes `handleSubmit()`; the registration branch validates nickname/email/password via regex (non-empty, valid email, 8+ char alphanumeric password), calls `signup()`, and on success performs the same login-success side effects; dismissing via the Yahoo option calls `saveDesign()` to redirect to Yahoo OAuth.
- `checkLogin()` — if `props.user` is falsy, dispatches `setLoading(false)` and (after 500ms) calls `alertLogin()`, returning `false`; otherwise returns `true`.
- `viewListPreview()` — opens `DialogAI` inside a SweetAlert2-react-content modal (`SwalDialog.fire`), passing `dataLink={dataLinkSave}` and `dispatch`.
- `checkDataToAdd(key)` — if `key.file_url` is non-empty, pushes `{info:key, repeat:0, timeStartRequest: Date.now()}` onto `dataLinkSave.data`.
- `checkUpploadTextToImage(key_ramdom, keyModel)` — orchestrates 3 parallel generation attempts via a 500ms-interval state machine, calling the `textToImage` API for each slot, retrying once on failure before marking it errored, resolving with the count of successes once all 3 slots finish or a 60-second timeout elapses.
- `async handleSubmit()` — requires login (`checkLogin`) and a non-empty prompt, generates a random key, shows an "AI画像を生成中です" loading dialog, calls `registerProcessAi(content, key_ramdom)` up to 3 times (retrying on transient errors but breaking out immediately on a detected rate-limit `"Limit"` or `"login"`-required error), and on success calls `checkUpploadTextToImage()` — showing an error dialog if none succeeded, or closing the loading dialog and opening `viewListPreview()` if at least one succeeded; on failure shows a rate-limit dialog (with the server-reported daily limit), re-prompts login (`alertLogin`) if login is required, or a generic failure dialog otherwise.

### Render Structure
Only renders when `props.activeTool === "imageAI"` (otherwise a hidden empty `div`). `.options.options-image-AI-body` containing an instructional line, a text input (`handleAddText`), a "生成" (generate) button (`handleSubmit`), and (mobile `MediaQuery`) an inline preview image; a desktop `MediaQuery` variant shows the preview image in a separate block outside `.image-AI-body`.

---

## src/components/text-options/index.jsx (TextOptions)

### Purpose
Connected control panel for text-layer effects: lets the user pick among straight/curved/smallToLarge/largeToSmallBottom/bulge text effects (icon list) and, when "curved" is active, adjust curve radius and character spacing via range sliders, plus flip direction. Operates directly on the currently-selected fabric.js text object via the global `DrawTool`.

### Props
- `text_effect` — string, connected (`state.drawTool.text_effect`) — current effect name.
- `text_spacing` — number, connected (`state.drawTool.text_spacing`).
- `dispatch` — connected.

### State
- `is_straight` — bool, initialized from `props.text_effect != 'curved'`.
- `radius` — number, default `200` (curve-radius slider value).
- `text_effect_options` — bool, default `false`; toggled by `textEffect()` but not read in render (vestigial).
- `toogleEffect` — bool, default `false`; drives an unused `icon`/`icon caret-rotate` class computation.
- `flip` — bool, default `false`; tracks the text's reverse/flip state.

### Methods
- `changeTextEffect(type, text_effect)` — main dispatcher, branching on `type`:
  - `'effect'` — reads the target effect from the `text_effect` arg or a `#effect` select's value; gets the selected fabric text item (`DrawTool.sides.selected.items.selected.item`) and builds a shared `options` object (font props, position/scale, spacing/charSpacing). If switching to `"straight"`: recreates the item via `items.addText`, removes the old item from the internal collection and fabric canvas, and broadcasts an online-sync delete (`DrawTool.triggerDrawUpdateChat('online:update', {command: DELETE_ITEM, id_item})`). Otherwise: normalizes whitespace in the text (strips newlines, removes internal spaces, pads a leading/trailing space) and — if the item has no existing `effect` — recreates it via `items.addCurvedText` (removing/broadcasting deletion of the old item; for `"curved"`, rotates +90° once via a `rotate_curved` flag and sets `is_straight:false`), or — if it already has an effect — calls `item.setEffect(value)` in place, rotating ±90° as effects toggle in/out of curved/arc and updating `is_straight` accordingly. Always re-renders the canvas, dispatches `DrawToolActions.updateTextEffect(value)`, calls `this.textEffect()`, re-renders again, and calls `DrawTool.checkItemCarpetSide(...)`.
  - `'radius'` — calls `item.setRadius(value)`, updates `state.radius`, triggers `DrawTool.triggerDrawUpdate('update_item')`.
  - `'spacing'` — calls `item.setSpacing(value/5)` and dispatches `DrawToolActions.updateTextSpacing(value)`.
  - `'flip'` — calls `item.setReverse(!state.flip)` and toggles `state.flip`.
  - After the switch, always calls `this.textEffect()`, re-renders the canvas, and calls `DrawTool.checkItemCarpetSide(...)`.
- `textEffect()` — toggles `state.text_effect_options`.
- `toogleEffect()` — toggles `state.toogleEffect`.

### Render Structure
`.text-options` > `.text-effect` > `<ul>` of effect-icon `<li>`s (`straight, curved, smallToLarge, largeToSmallBottom, bulge`, each calling `changeTextEffect('effect', effect)`); when `props.text_effect === 'curved'`, additionally a `.text-radius` 曲率 (curvature) slider (0-300, `changeTextEffect('radius', false)`) and a `.text-spacing` スペーシング slider (0-500, `changeTextEffect('spacing', false)`).

---

## src/components/upload/index.jsx (IconButton — upload variant)

### Purpose
A drag-and-drop / click-to-upload button (distinct component from `../button`'s `IconButton` despite the identical export name) — wraps `react-dropzone` so clicking or dropping a file over the button triggers a file-upload callback; used for image-upload affordances.

### Props
- `label` — string.
- `icon` — string.
- `image` — string.
- `type` — one of `'button' | 'reset' | 'submit'`, default `'button'`.
- `className` — string.
- `style` — object.
- `disabled` — bool.
- `onUpload` — func, required; called with the dropped/selected `files` array.

### State
`{}` (empty, unused).

### Methods
- `constructor(props)` — binds `onDrop`.
- `onDrop(files)` — calls `props.onUpload(files)`.

### Render Structure
`<div className="button (disabled) {className}">` wrapping a `<Dropzone multiple={false} onDrop={onDrop}>` (ref stored as `this.dropzone`) that renders the optional `image`, `Icon`, and `label` passed via props; an `Ink` ripple import is present but commented out/unused.

---

# Part 8 — Draw Engine Public API & Build/Config Files

## src/draw-tool/drawtool.js (vendored library)

### Purpose

`src/draw-tool/drawtool.js` is a **vendored, pre-built webpack bundle** (56,529 lines, header comment `/*! Draw-Tool library version: "1.1.733" */`), not hand-written application source. It is wrapped in a standard `webpackUniversalModuleDefinition` UMD shim (`module.exports = factory()` / `root["DrawTool"] = factory()`) and internally bundles dozens of its own webpack modules (module `0`, `1`, `2`, … up to the hundreds), including what appears to be a forked/embedded copy of **fabric.js** (canvas object model: `FabricCanvas`, `FabricBorder`, `aCoords`, `scaleX/scaleY`, `toObject()`, `deactivateAll()`, brushes, etc.) plus large amounts of custom business logic for embroidery, laser cutting, acrylic/pedestal products, and "carpet" (rug) products layered on top.

The library exposes a single global **singleton object**, not a constructor class in the classic sense — the app does `import DrawTool from '../draw-tool/drawtool'` then `window.DrawTool = DrawTool;`, and calls `DrawTool.initialize(target, size)` once to bootstrap it (see Constructor Options below). There is no `new DrawTool(...)` anywhere in the app; every consumer imports the same singleton instance and mutates/reads it directly (e.g. `DrawTool.sides.selected`, `DrawTool.modeToolDraw`).

Because this is a compiled third-party-style dependency, this section documents only the **surface actually consumed by the rest of the app** (`src/containers`, `src/components`, `src/middleware`, `src/utils`) — not the thousands of internal bundled helper functions/modules inside the file. Signatures below were cross-checked by grepping the bundle for the matching `methodName: function methodName(...)` definitions (the bundle retains original parameter names and, in places, JSDoc comments such as `@memberOf DrawTool`, `@param`, `@method`, which confirms this really is a documented library that was bundled/minified-with-names-kept rather than obfuscated).

### Constructor Options

The app initializes the singleton once, in `src/components/draw-tool/index.jsx` (out of scope for detailed docs per instructions, but this is the sole init call site):

```js
componentDidMount() {
  DrawTool.initialize(this.node, {});
}
```

`initialize: function initialize(target, size)` (drawtool.js line ~321), per its JSDoc:
- `target` **{HTMLElement}** — required. The DOM container the canvas/editor will be mounted into (throws `HTMLElementUndefined` error if not an `HTMLElement`; also unwraps a jQuery-wrapped element if `window.jQuery` exists). The app passes the React ref `this.node` (an empty `<div className="draw-tool">`).
- `size` **{Object}** — optional sizing config, documented fields:
  - `size.width` **{Number}** — canvas width.
  - `size.height` **{Number}** — canvas height.
  - In this app's only call site it is passed as `{}` (empty), so the library falls back to its own internal defaults/auto-sizing rather than an explicit pixel size.

Internally `initialize()` clears `target.textContent`, sets `target.style.position = 'relative'`, and constructs the two top-level sub-objects the rest of the app relies on:
- `this.sides = new Sides()` — the collection of print/design "sides" (front, back, sleeve, etc.).
- `this.history = new DrawHistory()` — per-side undo/redo stack manager.

No other constructor-style options object is passed anywhere else in the app; all further configuration happens by setting properties on the singleton after init (see "State / Enum Properties" below) or via later method calls such as `setEditable(true)`.

### Public API (as consumed by the app)

The app talks to four layers of the singleton, all reached off the one global `DrawTool` object:
1. **`DrawTool.*`** — top-level singleton methods (business logic, mode/state management, import/export, laser/embroidery/carpet/acrylic helpers, online-chat sync).
2. **`DrawTool.sides`** — the `Sides` collection (`_collection`, `selected`, `getSide(id)`, `addSide(id)`, `empty()`).
3. **`DrawTool.sides.selected`** — the currently active `Side` instance (a wrapper around one Fabric.js canvas / print area).
4. **`DrawTool.sides.selected.items`** — the `Items` collection for that side (add/remove drawable objects, brush selection).
5. **`DrawTool.history`** — the `DrawHistory` instance (`undo(id)`, `redo(id)`, `pushState(id)`).

Grep across `src/` (excluding `drawtool.js` itself) found **125 distinct top-level `DrawTool.<method>()` call patterns**, **51 distinct `DrawTool.sides.selected.<method>()` call patterns**, and **26 distinct `DrawTool.sides.selected.items.<method>()` call patterns** — this is a large, business-domain-specific API surface, not a small generic drawing-library wrapper. They are grouped below by function; every method observed in application code is listed.

#### Event system (`DrawTool.on` / `.trigger`)

The singleton implements a minimal pub/sub system (`_callbacks: {}` object keyed by event name):

- `DrawTool.on(event, handler)` — registers `handler` in `this._callbacks[event]` (array push, no de-dupe). Used ~30+ times across containers/components to subscribe to canvas/UI events (see event list below). Verified definition: `on: function on(event, handler) { this._callbacks[event] = this._callbacks[event] || []; this._callbacks[event].push(handler); }`.
- `DrawTool.off(event, handler)` — intended to remove a handler; verified implementation is `this._callbacks[event].delete(handler)`, which is a bug (plain arrays have no `.delete`) — not observed being called from app code (app uses `offAll` instead).
- `DrawTool.offAll(event)` — clears all handlers for an event (`this._callbacks[event] = []`). Used in `src/containers/Options/index.jsx:481` before re-registering a handler, to avoid stacking duplicate listeners.
- `DrawTool.checkOnEventExist(event)` — returns `true` if at least one handler is registered for `event`. Used in `src/containers/Options/index.jsx:480` as a guard before calling `offAll`/`on` again.
- `DrawTool.trigger(event, data)` — fires all registered handlers for `event`, calling each with `JSON.stringify(data)` (so handlers receive a **JSON string**, not the raw object — several `.on()` callbacks in app code do `JSON.parse(...)` internally). Special-cased: when `event === 'history:update'`, it also pushes an undo/redo snapshot via `this.history.pushState(data.side.id)` and refreshes `this.sides.getSide(data.side.id).layers.update()` — i.e. **firing `'history:update'` is the app's primary way of committing a state change to the undo stack.**
- `DrawTool.triggerDrawUpdate(typeUpdate, extend = {})` — only fires (after a 500 ms `setTimeout`) if `DrawTool.hasOwnProperty("serverSocketData")` (i.e. only when a live collaborative/online-design session socket is active); internally calls `DrawTool.trigger('draw:update', Object.assign(extend, { model: typeUpdate }))`. Called with `typeUpdate` values `'all'`, `'color'`, `'image_item'`, `'item'`, `'layer'`, `'side'`, `'update_item'` throughout `Options/index.jsx`, `App/index.jsx`, `text-options/index.jsx`.
- `DrawTool.triggerDrawUpdateChat(typeUpdate, data)` — same online-session guard, forwards to `DrawTool.trigger(typeUpdate, data)` after 500 ms. Used to broadcast `'message:update'`, `'online:finish_chat'`, `'online:update'` for the live chat/collaboration feature (`FooterChat.jsx`, `middleware/draw-tool.js`).

**Event names observed** (fired via `.trigger()`/`triggerDrawUpdate*` and consumed via `.on()`):

| Event | Emitted from (representative) | Listened to in |
|---|---|---|
| `'history:update'` | `middleware/draw-tool.js`, `middleware/product.js`, `containers/App`, `containers/Options` — after almost every canvas mutation, with payload `{ side: { id }, isWorkSpaceBorder }` | `containers/App/index.jsx:252` (recomputes layers) |
| `'layers:update'` | `middleware/draw-tool.js` (`UPDATE_LAYERS` action), `containers/Options/index.jsx` | `containers/App/index.jsx:503` (`calcPriceUpdate()`) |
| `'item:unselect'` | `middleware/draw-tool.js` (`UNSELECT_ITEM` action) | `containers/Toolbar/index.jsx:71`, `containers/Options/index.jsx:473` |
| `'item:option'` | `components/laser-key-hold`, `laser-key-no-hole`, `laser-pedestal(-mobile)`, `laser-sticker` — with `{mode: "..."}` payloads driving die-cut/pedestal UI state machine | `containers/Options/index.jsx:484` |
| `'update:activeTool'` | `middleware/draw-tool.js` (`SET_ACTIVE_TOOL` action) | `containers/Toolbar/index.jsx:67` |
| `'update:mode'` | laser/pedestal/sticker components (`{mode: "laser"}`, `{mode: "uploadFile"}`) | `containers/Toolbar/index.jsx:105`, `containers/Options/index.jsx:408` |
| `'update:mouse'` | `middleware/product.js` | `containers/Toolbar/index.jsx:75` |
| `'update:pointer'` | `middleware/product.js` | `containers/Toolbar/index.jsx:101` |
| `'object:selected'` | fabric.js canvas selection internals | `containers/App/index.jsx:301` |
| `'object:rotating'` / `'object:scaling'` | fabric.js canvas transform internals | `containers/App/index.jsx:458`, `:483` |
| `'object:delete_item'` | internal item-deletion path | `containers/App/index.jsx:476` |
| `'selection:cleared'` | `containers/App/index.jsx:481` (self-fired on deselect) | `containers/App/index.jsx:416` |
| `'colorpicker:update'` | internal color-picker integration | `containers/App/index.jsx:500` |
| `'figure3d:update'` | `middleware/product.js` (3D preview data) | `containers/App/index.jsx:504` |
| `'control:mode'` | `components/laser-key-hold` (`{mode:"preview_3d"}`) | `containers/App/index.jsx:509` |
| `'view3d:update'` | `middleware/product.js` | `containers/App/index.jsx:527` |
| `'carpet:update'` | `middleware/product.js` (carpet/rug size overload/limit flags) | `containers/App/index.jsx:266` |
| `'cart:item'` | laser/pedestal components (add-to-cart signal, `{mode:true}`) | `containers/Header/index.jsx:212`, `containers/HeaderMobile/index.jsx:74` |
| `'option:update'` | pedestal/sticker components, `middleware/draw-tool.js` (`{size_option:10}`, `{remove_all:true}`) | `components/laser-*` (multiple), `Options/index.jsx` |
| `'option:model3d'` | `containers/Options/Design3D.jsx` (`{src: url\|null}`) | `components/3d-triple/index.jsx:103` |
| `'draw:update'` | `triggerDrawUpdate()` internal | `containers/Options/FooterChat.jsx:98` |
| `'message:update'` | `triggerDrawUpdateChat()` (chat) | `containers/Options/FooterChat.jsx:59` |
| `'online:update'` | `triggerDrawUpdateChat()`, `middleware/draw-tool.js` (collaborative sync commands) | `containers/Options/FooterChat.jsx:116` |
| `'online:finish_chat'` | `triggerDrawUpdateChat()` | `containers/Options/FooterChat.jsx:117` |
| `'product:load'` | `middleware/product.js` | (product/loading flow) |
| `'Laser:finish'` | internal laser flow (grep hit; consumer not directly located) | — |
| `'mouse:move'` | internal pointer tracking | — |

#### Lifecycle / import-export (top-level)

- `DrawTool.initialize(target, size)` — see Constructor Options.
- `DrawTool.setEditable(val = true)` — toggles `this.editable` and, for every side with a `FabricBorder`, sets it `selectable`/re-renders. Not observed called from app code directly (only 1 raw property read of `DrawTool.editable` was found), but present in the public API.
- `DrawTool.exportJSON()` — maps every side to `side.toObject()` and returns the array; used for producing the serialized design payload.
- `DrawTool.importJSON(json, initialZoom, proportions = defaultParams.proportions)` — returns a **Promise**. Flushes any in-progress brush (`items.finalizeBrush()`), empties `this.sides`, resets `this.history.history = {}`, then rebuilds every side via `this.sides.addSide(side.id)` and loads each side's image/objects. Called throughout `src/middleware/product.js` as `DrawTool.importJSON(JSON.stringify(data)).then(() => {...})` whenever a saved design / product template is loaded.
- `DrawTool.stopCurentAction()` — cancels the current interactive action; concretely, when `modeToolDraw === modeSetup.CARPET_PRODUCT` it turns off drawing mode and clears `carpet_pulling`. Called before switching tools (`middleware/draw-tool.js`, `containers/App`, `containers/Header`).
- `DrawTool.setLayerSetup(val = 0)` — sets `this.modeSpecialDrawEnable = val` and syncs `sides.selected._selected_path_index` / `is_embroidery` accordingly, then re-renders. This is the primary "which special drawing layer/mode is active" switch (laser, embroider-extend, etc.), called with `DrawTool.modeSetup.*` enum values.
- `DrawTool.setEmbroidery(val = false)` — sets `this.is_embroidery`, updates `sides.selected._selected_path_index`/`modeSpecialDrawEnable`, calls `side.setSelectableForDesign()` on every side, re-renders. Called from `middleware/draw-tool.js` and `containers/Options/index.jsx` when entering/leaving embroidery editing.
- `DrawTool.checkModeLaser(item = null, remove_mode = -1)` — returns boolean; with no `item`, checks whether the current `modeSpecialDrawEnable` is one of `MODE_LASER_DEFFINE`; with an `item`, checks the item's own `pathIndex` against the same list (excluding `remove_mode`). Extremely widely used as a guard (dozens of call sites in `Options/index.jsx`, `MethodImageProcessing.js`).
- `DrawTool.checkProductLaser()` — `MODE_LASER_DEFFINE.includes(DrawTool.modeToolDraw)`; "is the whole product a laser-type product".
- `DrawTool.getCurrentTypeProductLaser()` — returns `modeToolDraw` if `checkProductLaser()` is true, else `-1`.
- `DrawTool.checkModeEmbroidery()` / `DrawTool.checkInModeEmbroidery(item)` / `DrawTool.inModeEmbroidery()` — variants of "is current mode/selected item in embroidery mode".
- `DrawTool.checkEmbroideryItem(item)` / `DrawTool.checkEmbroideryProductContain()` / `DrawTool.checkSpecialTypeEmbroidery(item)` — embroidery-related item/product predicate checks.
- `DrawTool.getEmbroiderData()` — collects embroidery-specific export data, used in `containers/Header/index.jsx:560` (checkout/export flow).
- `DrawTool.rasterizeText(apiUrl, session)` — returns a Promise; walks every side's canvas objects and (per side) rasterizes text layers via a server round-trip (`apiUrl`) tied to a `session`. Used before checkout/preview generation in `Header/index.jsx`, `Options/index.jsx`.
- `DrawTool.rasterizeImage(apiUrl, session, typeRasterizeImage)` — same pattern for raster/vector image layers.
- `DrawTool.fontLoader(...)` — loads/embeds custom fonts for canvas text rendering.
- `DrawTool.getRoundItem(value)` — small numeric rounding helper.
- `DrawTool.parseFloatToFixed(...)` — numeric formatting helper.
- `DrawTool.getEndProducts()` — aggregates each side's `getEndProduct()` to build a short code string (`'p'`=print, `'e'`=embroidery, `'l'`=laser/other) describing the overall product composition; used by `containers/Header/index.jsx:733`.

#### Layer / product-data / image-upload management

- `DrawTool.countNumberDataUseInSide(...)` / `DrawTool.getNumberDataSide(...)` — counts/enumerates design items on a side (used for price/limits checks in `Toolbar`, `Header`, `App`).
- `DrawTool.countStickerImage(...)` — counts sticker-type image items (`Header/index.jsx:620`, `App/index.jsx:1564`).
- `DrawTool.checkItemEmptyInSideData(...)` — whether a side has no design content.
- `DrawTool.checkImageUploadEmpty(...)` / `DrawTool.checkImageToUpload(...)` — validates pending image uploads.
- `DrawTool.checkItemOnlineInSide(...)` / `DrawTool.checkItemCarpetSide(...)` — item-type predicate helpers for online/carpet items.
- `DrawTool.getJsonDessignPadam(...)` — (sic, "design param") builds the JSON payload describing current design parameters, used before persisting/sending design state (`App/index.jsx`, `Options/index.jsx`).
- `DrawTool.getInfoItemJsonDesign(...)` / `DrawTool.getInfoItemChatDesign(...)` — per-item JSON info builders, one for design persistence and one for the chat/collab protocol.
- `DrawTool.createCommandChatDesign(...)` — builds an online/chat command payload (paired with `CommandStatus` enum).
- `DrawTool.createCanvasImage(...)` / `DrawTool.createLockBackImage(...)` — generate raster preview/backing images from the canvas.
- `DrawTool.createNewUUid()` — generates a UUID for new design items; verified as a zero-arg helper.
- `DrawTool.addImageCanvasToDesign(...)` — adds a rasterized canvas image into the design (used after carpet-border edits and pedestal loads to "bake" one representation into another).
- `DrawTool.addRuleCanvasToDesign(...)` — adds a ruler/guide canvas overlay.
- `DrawTool.addInputImageUpload(...)` / `DrawTool.upDateImageUpload(...)` — register/update a user-uploaded image within the tool's internal upload tracking.
- `DrawTool.removeSpecialDesignData(...)` / `DrawTool.removeRuleInCarretInfo(...)` / `DrawTool.removeItemAcrylicLigtStyle(...)` — targeted cleanup of special design metadata (special layers, caret/cursor info, acrylic "light style" markers).
- `DrawTool.fillerByPathIndex(...)` — fills/colors items grouped by `pathIndex` (the numeric layer-purpose tag also seen in `modeSetup`).
- `DrawTool.checkTextValue(...)` / `DrawTool.checkTextItemValue(...)` / `DrawTool.checkTextRuleConvert(...)` — text-layer validation/conversion helpers (`components/text-options/index.jsx`).
- `DrawTool.refeshSideAndLayer(side)` — (sic, "refresh") forces a side + its layer list to recompute/redraw.
- `DrawTool.checkTimeOfAction(...)` — debouncing/rate-limit helper for actions.

#### Laser / acrylic / pedestal products

- `DrawTool.checkLaserColor()` — validates laser-item color constraints (used heavily around laser color pickers in `Options/index.jsx`).
- `DrawTool.getBorderInfoLaser(...)` / `DrawTool.getBorderCurrentSideLaser(...)` / `DrawTool.getOptionBorderLaser(...)` — retrieve laser cut-border geometry/options for the current side.
- `DrawTool.changePathDrawBorder(...)` — recomputes the drawable border path for laser mode.
- `DrawTool.getLimitLeftPointLaser(...)` / `DrawTool.getLimitTopPointLaser(...)` — clamps for laser item placement (`utils/MethodImageProcessing.js`).
- `DrawTool.checkStandLaserDesign()` — validates a "stand" (freestanding) laser design; called after undo (`history.js` internal) and pedestal-add flows.
- `DrawTool.checkZoomBorderInfo()` / `DrawTool.zoomBorderInfo(side)` — border-zoom state check/apply for laser/acrylic zoomed print areas.
- `DrawTool.checkAcrylicMoveDesign(side, target)` / `DrawTool.checkAcrylicStickerMoveDesign(side, target)` / `DrawTool.checkcheckAcrylicMoveDesignTarget(...)` — validates whether an acrylic (or acrylic-sticker) design item can be moved to `target`.
- `DrawTool.checkAcrylicAddToDesignTarget(...)` / `DrawTool.checkSickerAcrylicAddToDesignTarget(...)` (sic) / `DrawTool.checkStickerAcrylicAddToDesign(...)` — validates adding new acrylic/sticker items.
- `DrawTool.checkAllItemInAcrylicDesign()` / `DrawTool.checkAllItemInAcrylicStickerDesign()` — zero-arg validators scanning the whole design for acrylic/sticker constraint violations.
- `DrawTool.checkLocationBorderAcrylic(...)` — location/border validation for acrylic pieces.
- `DrawTool.setBorderForPedestal()` / `DrawTool.setBorderForPedestalOwner(itemSelectItem, ij, width_mm_add, setupBorder)` — sets up the pedestal (stand) border geometry, the latter for a specific sub-item/index with a millimeter width adjustment.
- `DrawTool.loadNewBorderPedestal()` — reloads pedestal border after edits; called throughout `App/index.jsx` and `Options/index.jsx` after acrylic/laser border changes.
- `DrawTool.moveLocationBorderInfo(side, margin_diff)` / `DrawTool.moveLocationKeyHoleInfo(...)` — repositions the print border / keyhole cut based on a margin/UI change.

#### Carpet (rug) product mode

- `DrawTool.getDefaultCarpet(side)` — default carpet size/shape for a side.
- `DrawTool.getSizeCarpet()` / `DrawTool.getJsonSizeOfCarpet(...)` — current carpet dimensions, plain and JSON forms.
- `DrawTool.changeBorderVirtualCarpet(value, limit)` — adjusts the "virtual" (preview) carpet border by `value`, clamped by `limit`.
- `DrawTool.changeCarpetPullingBorder()` / `DrawTool.changeCarpetPullingBorderPreview(...)` — updates the interactive "pulling" (resize-by-drag) border, live and preview variants.
- `DrawTool.checkCarpetPulling()` / `DrawTool.checkCarpetPullingPreview(...)` — state predicates for the pulling interaction.
- `DrawTool.checkCarpetRevertSide()` — whether the current side's carpet edit can be reverted.
- `DrawTool.checkCarpetLockBackAll()` — checks lock-state across all carpet sides.
- `DrawTool.revertCarpetPulling()` — undoes an in-progress carpet resize.
- `DrawTool.saveOldBorderCarperPulling(...)` (sic) — snapshots the pre-pulling border for revert.
- `DrawTool.resetLocationCarpet()` — resets carpet position/offset.
- `DrawTool.editBorderCarpet(...)` / `DrawTool.editInfoScaleCarpet(...)` — mutate carpet border / scale metadata.
- `DrawTool.loadOldDesignCarpet(side, overlayImages, data, index)` — restores a previously-saved carpet design onto a side.
- `DrawTool.loadSetupBorderCarpet(...)` — applies initial carpet border setup.
- `DrawTool.loadSideBorderCarpetProduct(data)` — bulk-restores per-side carpet border/pedestal data (verified: iterates `data[i]`, applies `border_special`/`pixel_to_cm`, then calls `addImageCanvasToDesign()`).
- `DrawTool.saveInfoResponsiveCarpet(...)` — persists responsive-layout carpet info.
- `DrawTool.drawRuleToSideLeft(...)` / `DrawTool.drawRuleToSideTop(...)` — draws ruler guide lines on a side.

#### Online / chat / collaborative editing

- `DrawTool.loadOnlineItem(ItemJson)` / `DrawTool.loadOnlineItemOtherSide(...)` — applies an item received from another collaborator (current vs. other side).
- `DrawTool.loadDirectJsonOnlineItem(...)` / `DrawTool.loadDirectJsonOnlineArrayItem(...)` — bulk item load variants for online sync.
- `DrawTool.deleteOnlineItemCurrentSide(ItemJson)` / `DrawTool.deleteOnlineItemOtherSide(...)` / `DrawTool.deleteAllOnlineItemOtherSide(...)` — remove item(s) pushed by a remote delete event.
- `DrawTool.checkAndReloadChatInfo()` — re-syncs chat/collab session info; called repeatedly through `App/index.jsx` around design-load points.
- `DrawTool.informDataRecived(Json, data)` (sic, "received") / `DrawTool.informToSend(...)` — inbound/outbound message shaping for the socket.io chat channel (`socket.io-client` dependency).
- `DrawTool.saveCarretInfo(...)` (sic, "caret") — persists cursor/selection info for collaborative awareness.
- `DrawTool.checkTimeOfAction(...)` — (see above) also used to throttle collab actions.

#### Product / lock / misc.

- `DrawTool.rebackLockProduct()` (sic, "revert-back") — resets a product from a "locked" (view-only/finalized) state; called in `Toolbar/index.jsx`, `Header/index.jsx`, `Options/index.jsx`.
- `DrawTool.checkLockRevertSide(size_setup)` — validates whether a side's lock state can be reverted for a given size setup.
- `DrawTool.checkLastDessignDataInfo(...)` (sic, "design") / `DrawTool.checkDefaultProductInval(...)` (sic, "invalid") — start-up validation of the last-saved design vs. the default product, in `App/index.jsx` on mount.
- `DrawTool.checkProductToInfoDetail(...)` / `DrawTool.checkSpeedupData(...)` — product-detail / performance("speedup") data checks used before checkout (`Header/index.jsx`).
- `DrawTool.checkActionDeleteItem(...)` — guards a delete action.
- `DrawTool.changeProductSelect(...)` / `DrawTool.changeProductSelectAll(arrayJson)` — swap the active product (single or bulk, e.g. when switching color/variant), reloading side data from `arrayJson`.
- `DrawTool.loadFistSide(dataSide)` (sic, "first") — loads the initial side to display on product open.
- `DrawTool.coppyAllDataCompoment()` (sic, "copy...component") — deep-copies all side/component data, used in carpet preview flow.
- `DrawTool.checkItemInMode` / other `checkXxx` predicates not detailed above follow the same naming convention (return booleans gating UI actions on current mode/enum state).

#### `DrawTool.sides` / `DrawTool.sides.selected` (the active `Side`)

`DrawTool.sides` is the `Sides` collection: `_collection` (array of `Side` objects), `selected` (the active one), plus `getSide(id)` / `addSide(id)` / `empty()` used internally and from `middleware/product.js`. The 51 distinct methods called on `DrawTool.sides.selected` (a single `Side`) include, grouped:

- **Border / print-area**: `getBorder()`, `setBorder(item, triggerFlag)`, `setBorderCutting(...)`, `setBorderInfo(...)`, `setBorderLaser(...)`, `setOverlayBorder(...)`, `setPathBorderItem(...)`, `saveBoder(...)` (sic), `saveNameSide(...)`.
- **Zoom / grid**: `zoomIn()`, `zoomOut()`, `zoomInEmbroierAre(...)` (sic "embroider area"), `zoomOutEmbroierAre(...)`, `zoomEmbroideryZone(...)`, `addGrid(rows, cols)`, `removeGrid()`, `addCenterPoint()`, `removeCenterPoint()`.
- **Drawing mode / brushes**: `drawingMode(bool)` — toggles Fabric.js free-drawing mode on the side's canvas; `.panning`/`.draw`/`.changeBorder` are plain boolean properties set alongside it (not methods).
- **Serialization**: `toJSON()`, `fromJSON(...)`, `fromJSONLayer(...)`, `toSVG()`.
- **Item/state queries**: `checkEmpty()`, `checkItemCanAddValue(...)`, `checkItemEmptyInSide(...)`, `checkItemInMode(...)`, `checkModeExistingBefore(mode)`, `checkOtherModeExistingBefore(mode)`, `checkBorderAcess(mode)` (sic "access"), `checkBorderEmbroierExisting()`, `checkItemEmbroierInPrintSideExistingBefore()`, `checkItemPrintInEmbroierSideExistingBefore()`, `checkAcessOnmoveInAreaSpecial(...)` (sic), `getCountColors()`, `getOccupiedAreaSize()`, `getValuefromHash(...)` (sic), `doPolygonsIntersect(...)`.
- **Item mutation**: `addItemLayer(...)`, `copyItem(...)`, `checkAndRemoveElementImage(...)`, `changeToCropPathProcessing(...)`, `emptyItemHoldLaser()`, `setSelectableForDesign()`, `triggerCreated()`.
- **Preview / export images**: `getImagePreview(...)`, `getImagePreviewTransparent(...)`, `getImagePreviewTransparentAdujst(...)` (sic "adjust"), `getPreviewForLayers(...)`, `getFullImagePreview(apiUrl, session)` (defined at top level too, per-side version called internally).
- **Embroidery-area geometry**: `getLocationForAreaEmbroier(...)` (sic).

`DrawTool.sides.selected.items` is the `Items` collection for that side, with 26 distinct call patterns observed:
- **Adding content**: `addText(options, text)`, `addImage(url)`, `addImageWithSize(...)`, `addImageWithSizeID(...)`, `addImageLaser(...)`, `addCurvedText(...)`, `addSVG(...)`, `addObject(...)`, `addLaserSVG(...)`, `addLaserSVGDataInfo(...)`, `addLaserSvgAndPoint(...)`, `addLaserFromOldString(...)`.
- **Brush selection** (called dynamically as `items[activeBrush](brushOptions)`): brush names used include `'laserBrush'`, `'laserLineVectorBrush'`, `'laserCurveVectorBrush'`, `'changeToCropPathBrush'`, plus whatever `activeBrush` holds from Redux state (e.g. a pencil/eraser brush) — the exact key is looked up dynamically, so the method name equals the brush's name.
- **Bulk / lifecycle**: `empty()`, `emptyLaser()`, `finalizeBrush()` (commits an in-progress brush stroke — called before almost every non-drawing action to avoid losing an unfinished stroke), `forEach(...)`, `map(...)`, `push(...)`, `splice(...)` (native-array-like methods — `items._collection` is a plain array wrapped by this object).
- **Color / cut processing**: `imagechangeColorAll(...)`, `rimImageProcessing(...)` (sic "trim"?), `setClipOtherBorder(...)`, `checkItemExistingToAddEmbroier(...)`, `checkLaser()`, `triggerCheckSpecialItemsBorder(borderSpecial)`, `triggerCreated()`.

#### `DrawTool.history` (undo/redo)

A small `DrawHistory` class, one instance per app (`this.history`), keyed **per side id** (`this.history[id] = { collection: [...], currentIndex }`):

- `DrawTool.history.pushState(id)` — snapshots the given side's `FabricCanvas.toObject([...whitelisted custom props])` as a JSON string, truncates any "future" (redo) states past `currentIndex`, and pushes the new state. Debounced: skipped if called again within 200 ms (`time_state_history`) or while `DrawTool.lock_history` is truthy. This is invoked automatically whenever the app fires the `'history:update'` event via `DrawTool.trigger(...)` (see Event system above) — app code essentially never calls `pushState` directly, it fires the event instead.
- `DrawTool.history.undo(id)` — steps `currentIndex` back one, reloads that side via `side.loadFromJSON(state, side)`, calls `DrawTool.checkStandLaserDesign()`, fires `DrawTool.trigger('history:undo', {...})`, and re-applies selectability. Called directly as `DrawTool.history.undo(DrawTool.sides.selected.id)` from `Options/index.jsx` and via the `undo` action creator from `Toolbar/index.jsx` / `HeaderMobile/index.jsx` (`dispatch(actions.undo(DrawTool.history.history[DrawTool.sides.selected.id]))`).
- `DrawTool.history.redo(id)` — mirror of `undo`, steps `currentIndex` forward.
- `DrawTool.history.resetHistory()` — clears all sides' history (`this.history = {}`); used together with `importJSON`.
- `DrawTool.history.history` — the raw per-side state object, read directly by `Toolbar`/`HeaderMobile`/`Options` to compute whether undo/redo buttons should be enabled (e.g. checking `currentIndex`/`collection.length`).

### State / Enum Properties (read/written directly, not via methods)

These are plain properties on the singleton that the app reads or sets directly rather than through accessor methods — usage counts from a repo-wide grep are shown to indicate how central each is:

| Property | Uses in app | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `DrawTool.sides` | 2370+ | The `Sides` collection / `.selected` side — the single most-referenced property in the whole app. |
| `DrawTool.modeSetup` | 408 | Enum object of numeric mode constants: `NOMAL: -1, EMBROIDER: 1, LASER: 3, MASK_LASER: 4, TAP_RIBBON: 5, TAP_RIBBON_2EDIT: 6, RIBON_EXTEND_COLOR: 8, EMBROIDER_EXTEND: 9, LOCK_PRODUCT: 10, CARPET_PRODUCT: 11, LASER_STICKER: 12, ACRYLIC_LASER: 13, ZOOM_PRINT: 14, LASER_NO_HOLE: 15, DRAW3D: 20`. |
| `DrawTool.modeToolDraw` | 355 | The currently active mode, set to one of the `modeSetup` values; drives which tool UI/behavior is enabled. |
| `DrawTool.is_embroidery` | 53 | Boolean flag: is embroidery editing currently active (also settable via `setEmbroidery()`). |
| `DrawTool.CommandStatus` | 38 | Enum of online/chat command codes: `NEW_ITEM_ADD, TYPE_UPDATE_ITEM, DELETE_ITEM, EMPTY_NOW, UPDATE_SIDE, UPDATE_NEW_COLOR, UPDATE_COLOR_RIBON, TYPE_MESSAGE, UPDATE_MESSAGE, DELETE_MESSAGE, UPDATE_ROOM, START_LIVE, STOP_LIVE, KILL_BEFORE_LIVE` — used with `triggerDrawUpdateChat` / `CommandStatus.UPDATE_SIDE` etc. |
| `DrawTool.history` | 19 | The `DrawHistory` instance (see above). |
| `DrawTool.TypeAcess` | 7 | Enum: `FREE_STYLE: 1, ONLY_EMBROIDERY: 2, ONLY_PRINT_OR_EMBROIDERY: 3, ONLY_PRINT: 4` — governs which content types a bordered area accepts. |
| `DrawTool.is_sp_product` | 6 | Boolean: "special product" flag altering embroidery zoom behavior. |
| `DrawTool.editable` | 1 | Global editable flag set by `setEditable()`. |

Other enums defined in the bundle and referenced by name in app code (via `DrawTool.<enum>`) include `ModeDrawBush` (`NOMAL: -1, LASER: 1, CUTTING_IMAGE: 2`), `DrawBrushType` (`NOT_DRAW, START, CIRCLE_CIRVE, LINE_CIRVE, LINE_ORDER`), and array constants `LAYER_LASER_DEFFINE`, `MODE_LASER_DEFFINE`, `MODE_LASER_UI` (all sic — "DEFFINE"/"CIRVE" typos are in the original library source, preserved here for accuracy) used internally by `checkModeLaser`/`checkProductLaser`.

## webpack.config.js

**Purpose**: Webpack 1.x build configuration. Builds a multi-environment bundle (`local` / `development` / `production`, selected via `NODE_ENV`) from `src/index.jsx`, producing `app-*.js` and a separate `vendor-*.js` chunk, plus an `index.html` and copied `assets/`.

- `config.context` — set to `<root>/src`; all entry/loader paths are relative to `src/`.
- `config.entry` — two entry points:
  - `app`: `./index.jsx` (the app itself).
  - `vendor`: an explicit list of libraries bundled into a separate chunk for caching — `babel-polyfill`, `react`, `react-dom`, `react-redux`, `redux`, `react-responsive`, `react-custom-scrollbars`, `react-smooth-scrollbar`, `react-restricted-input`, `react-ink`, `react-draggable`, **`./draw-tool/drawtool.js`** (the vendored canvas engine — bundled as a vendor dependency, confirming it's treated as third-party), `three`, `./assets/js/jm-color-picker.js`.
- `config.resolve.extensions` — `['', '.js', '.jsx']` (old webpack 1 syntax allowing extensionless imports).
- `config.module.loaders` — one rule: any `src/**/*.js(x)` file (excluding `node_modules`, `drawtool.js`, `jm-color-picker.js`) is run through `babel-loader`. The vendored/legacy files are explicitly excluded from Babel transpilation.
- `config.postcss` — `[autoprefixer]` (CSS vendor-prefixing).
- `config.plugins` (common to all envs):
  - `HtmlWebpackPlugin({ filename: 'index.html', template: 'index.html' })` — generates the output HTML from `src/index.html`.
  - `webpack.DefinePlugin` — injects `NODE_ENV` and `process.env.NODE_ENV` as string literals.
  - `ExtractTextPlugin('[name]-[hash].css')` — extracts SCSS/CSS into a separate hashed file.
- **`local`** build (`NODE_ENV=local`): outputs to `build/[name].min.js`; adds SCSS/CSS extraction with minification, a `CommonsChunkPlugin('vendor', 'vendor.min.js')`, `UglifyJsPlugin` (dead-code elimination, IE8-unsafe optimizations, mangling), `CopyWebpackPlugin` for `assets/`, and a `DefinePlugin` injecting hardcoded `process.env.*` endpoint URLs (API, web, Yahoo API key, background-removal service, chat server) sourced from `config.json`'s `local` block plus several hardcoded literals (`API_NODE`, `KEY_YAHOO`, `URL_REMOVE_BG`, `URL_SERVER_CHAT`).
- **`development`** build: outputs to `build/[name]-[hash].js` with `libraryTarget: "umd"`; SCSS/CSS extraction without minification but with sourcemaps; `CommonsChunkPlugin('vendor', 'vendor-[hash].js')`; `CopyWebpackPlugin` for `assets/` (no unmodified-file copy); `DefinePlugin` with beta/dev endpoint URLs (including an extra `URL_ORDERMADE` endpoint not present in the `local` block).
- **`production`** build: outputs to `build/[name]-[hash].min.js`; minified SCSS/CSS; `CommonsChunkPlugin('vendor', 'vendor-[hash].min.js')`; `UglifyJsPlugin` (webpack 1 `uglifyjs-webpack-plugin`, `keep_fnames: true`, sourcemaps on); `CopyWebpackPlugin` for `assets/` **and** `../.github` (copies the GitHub Actions workflow folder into the build output — likely incidental/unintended given `copyUnmodified: true`); `DefinePlugin` with production endpoint URLs; a `CompressionPlugin` using `@gfx/zopfli`'s `gzip` to pre-compress `.js/.jsx/.css/.scss` output as `.gz`. A commented-out `BrotliGzipPlugin` block shows Brotli compression was considered but disabled.
- All three `process.env.API_ENDPOINT` / `WEB_ENDPOINT` values are read from **`config.json`** (`envConfig[NODE_ENV].api_endpoint` / `.web_endpoint`) — this is why `config.json` must exist locally (see below) for any build to succeed.

## .babelrc

**Purpose**: Babel 6 transpilation config for the `babel-loader` webpack rule.

- `presets: ["es2015", "react", "stage-1"]` — ES2015 syntax, JSX/React transform, and Stage-1 (experimental, pre-ES2016) proposal syntax (object spread, etc.), applied in all environments.
- `env.development` — empty override (no extra presets).
- `env.production` — adds `"react-optimize"` preset (production React optimizations: strips `propTypes`, inlines elements, constant-folds, etc.), applied only when `BABEL_ENV`/`NODE_ENV=production`.

## .eslintrc.js

**Purpose**: ESLint 3.x linting configuration.

- `parser: "babel-eslint"` — lets ESLint parse the Babel/Stage-1/JSX syntax used in the codebase.
- `extends: "airbnb"` — inherits the (pre-split, ESLint-3-era) Airbnb style guide, which itself bundles React/JSX/a11y/import rules.
- `plugins: ["react", "jsx-a11y", "import"]` — explicit plugin declarations backing the Airbnb config's rules.
- `env: { browser: true }` — enables browser global variables (`window`, `document`, etc.).
- No custom `rules` overrides are defined — the project relies entirely on the Airbnb defaults.

## config.json

**Purpose**: Non-committed-looking (but present) runtime environment map consumed by `webpack.config.js` (`require('./config.json')`) to inject `API_ENDPOINT`/`WEB_ENDPOINT` per build environment. Contains three keys, `local`, `development`, `production`, each an object with:
- `api_endpoint` — backend API base URL, as a **quoted string with embedded quotes** (e.g. `"'http://api-v2-dev.up-t.jp'"`) — the extra single-quotes are intentional, since the value is spliced directly into `webpack.DefinePlugin` as a raw JS expression (needs to become a string literal in the bundled code, i.e. `process.env.API_ENDPOINT` literally equals `'http://...'` including the quotes).
- `web_endpoint` — frontend/web base URL, same quoting convention.

Values in this copy of `config.json`:
- `local`: `api_endpoint: 'http://api-v2-dev.up-t.jp'`, `web_endpoint: 'http://dev.up-t.jp'`.
- `development`: `api_endpoint: 'http://api-beta.up-t.jp'`, `web_endpoint: 'http://beta.up-t.jp'`.
- `production`: `api_endpoint: 'https://api.up-t.jp'`, `web_endpoint: 'https://up-t.jp'`.

## config.json.example

Template/example version of `config.json` for new developers to copy. Identical structure (`local`/`development`/`production`, `api_endpoint`/`web_endpoint`), and in this repo's snapshot the **only difference** from the real `config.json` is the `local.api_endpoint` value:
- `config.json.example` → `local.api_endpoint: 'http://api-dev.up-t.jp'`
- `config.json` (actual) → `local.api_endpoint: 'http://api-v2-dev.up-t.jp'`

All other values (`local.web_endpoint`, and the full `development`/`production` blocks) are identical between the two files, suggesting `config.json` was hand-edited locally to point at a newer (`v2`) local/dev API host while the example template was left pointing at the older host.

## deploy.production.js

**Purpose**: Standalone Node script (run via `npm run deploy-production`) that publishes the built `build/` directory to the `deploy-production` branch of the repo's GitHub Pages (or equivalent git-based static host) using the `gh-pages` npm package.

- Calls `ghpages.publish("build", options, callback)` with:
  - `branch: "deploy-production"` — target branch to push the static build to.
  - `dotfiles: true` — includes dotfiles (e.g. `.github/`, `.nojekyll`-style files) in the published output.
  - `message: " Deploy production"` — the git commit message used for the publish commit.
  - `silent: false` — verbose logging.
- Callback: logs `"✅ Deploy success!"` on success, or `console.error("❌ Deploy failed:", err)` and `process.exit(1)` on failure (non-zero exit code for CI to detect failure).

## Jenkinsfile

**Purpose**: Jenkins declarative pipeline that deploys the already-built app to production servers by SSH-ing in and running `git pull`. It is designed to be triggered **only** via a Generic Webhook Trigger (not Jenkins' default SCM polling/push trigger), specifically from the companion GitHub Actions workflow (`.github/workflows/jenkins-trigger.yml`).

High-level stages/behavior:
- `options { skipDefaultCheckout true }` — the job does not auto-checkout source; it's a pure deploy/orchestration job.
- `triggers { GenericTrigger(...) }` — listens for an inbound webhook carrying `X_DEPLOY_TOKEN`, `ACTION`, `ACTOR`, `REPO`, `BRANCH` fields (matching what the GitHub Action sends), gated by a shared `token` and a `regexpFilterExpression` that requires the webhook's `BRANCH` to match the Jenkins job's own configured branch (prevents cross-branch trigger contamination in a multibranch setup).
- **Stage: `Validation`** — checks that `X_DEPLOY_TOKEN` matches the hardcoded `DEPLOY_TOKEN` env var, `ACTION == 'deploy'`, `REPO` matches the hardcoded `TARGET_REPO` (`MaruiorimonoTeam/up-t-web-draw-1`), and `ACTOR` matches an allow-list regex of four usernames (`longtvnws|le-minh-thang|Nguyenkain|truongtvnws`). Fails the build (`error(...)`) if any check fails.
- **Stage: `Deploy to Servers`** — runs two `parallel` sub-stages, each using the `sshagent` step with a named Jenkins SSH credential:
  - `Deploy Server Old` (`upt-web-prod` credential): SSHes into two EC2 hosts (`52.198.22.205`, `35.75.183.202`) and runs `cd /var/www/html/drawer && git pull`.
  - `Deploy Server PHP8` (`upt-web-php8` credential): same `git pull` deploy against two other EC2 hosts (`54.64.189.100`, `52.199.58.123`) — presumably a PHP8-upgraded server pool running in parallel with the legacy pool.
- **`post { always { ... } }`** — calls `sendTeamsNotification(status, "Servers (Old & PHP8)")`, a helper function defined at the top of the file that builds a JSON payload (repo, branch, actor, timestamp in `Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh` timezone, build URL, commit SHA, success/failure) and POSTs it via `curl` to a hardcoded Power Automate webhook URL (`NOTI_URL_SUCCESS`) to notify a Teams channel of the deploy outcome, regardless of success or failure.
- Secrets/config are hardcoded in the `environment {}` block: `DEPLOY_TOKEN`, `TARGET_REPO`, `NOTI_URL_SUCCESS` (a long Power Automate flow URL with embedded SAS signature).
- Comments throughout are in Vietnamese, explaining the webhook-trigger rationale and the Power Automate payload shape.

## .github/workflows/jenkins-trigger.yml

**Purpose**: GitHub Actions workflow (`Trigger Jenkins Deployment`) that acts as the front door for deploys — it validates the pusher's identity on GitHub's side, then fires the Generic Webhook Trigger that the Jenkinsfile above listens for.

- **Trigger**: `on.push.branches: [main, master, deploy-production]` — runs on every push to any of these three branches (a commented-out `pull_request` trigger block shows PR-based deploys were considered but not enabled).
- **Job `deploy`** (`ubuntu-latest`), two steps:
  1. **`Validation Check`** — shell step that checks `github.actor` against the same allow-list regex used in the Jenkinsfile (`longtvnws|le-minh-thang|Nguyenkain|truongtvnws`); exits `1` (failing the workflow, so the second step never runs) if the pusher isn't on the list. This is a redundant, defense-in-depth copy of the Jenkinsfile's own actor check.
  2. **`Trigger Jenkins Pipeline`** — `curl -X POST` to `${{ secrets.JENKINS_URL }}/generic-webhook-trigger/invoke?token=${{ secrets.DEPLOY_TOKEN }}` with a JSON body containing `X_DEPLOY_TOKEN`, `ACTION: "deploy"`, `ACTOR`, `REPO` (`github.repository`), `BRANCH` (`github.ref_name`) — i.e. exactly the fields the Jenkinsfile's `GenericTrigger` expects to receive and re-validate.
- Both `JENKINS_URL` and `DEPLOY_TOKEN` are GitHub Actions **secrets**, not hardcoded here (unlike the Jenkinsfile, which does hardcode its copy of the token/repo for its own-side validation).

## src/index.jsx

**Purpose**: The application's JavaScript entry point (referenced as `app: './index.jsx'` in `webpack.config.js`). Responsible for polyfilling, global style/legacy-script imports, Redux store creation, and mounting the root React component.

Execution order:
1. `import 'whatwg-fetch'` — polyfills `window.fetch` for older browsers.
2. `import React from 'react'`, `import ReactDOM from 'react-dom'`, `import { Provider } from 'react-redux'` — core React/Redux.
3. `import './styles/styles.scss'` — global SCSS entry (pulled in via the webpack SCSS loader chain).
4. `import '../src/assets/js/jquery-1.7.1.min.js'` — loads legacy jQuery 1.7.1 as a side-effecting global script (not treated as an ES module).
5. `import '../src/components/color-picker/jquery.wheelcolorpicker.js'` — a jQuery-plugin-based wheel color picker, also loaded for its side effects (registers itself on the jQuery global).
6. `import '../src/utils/onbeforeunload'` — installs a `window.onbeforeunload` handler (presumably to warn users about losing unsaved design work).
7. `import App from './containers/App'` — the root application container/component.
8. `import '../node_modules/intro.js/introjs.css'` — styles for the `intro.js` / `intro.js-react` guided-tour library.
9. `import configure from './store'` — the Redux store factory (`src/store/index.js`).
10. `import "react-responsive-carousel/lib/styles/carousel.min.css"` — carousel component styles.
11. `const store = configure();` — instantiates the Redux store.
12. `ReactDOM.render(<Provider store={store}><App /></Provider>, document.getElementById('root'));` — mounts the whole app under the Redux `Provider` into the `#root` DOM node defined in `src/index.html`.

## src/index.html

**Purpose**: The `HtmlWebpackPlugin` template (`template: 'index.html'` in `webpack.config.js`) used to generate the build's output `index.html`. Not itself a React/JS file — plain HTML with third-party tracking/analytics scripts and the app's mount point.

Key contents:
- `<meta name="robots" content="noindex">` — tells search engines not to index this page (it's an embedded design tool, not a marketing page).
- `<meta name="facebook-domain-verification" ...>` — Facebook Business domain-verification tag.
- `<title>UP-T</title>` and a mobile-friendly `viewport` meta tag that disables user zoom (`user-scalable=0`, fixed `minimum-scale`/`maximum-scale` — typical for a canvas-editing tool where pinch-zoom would conflict with in-app zoom controls).
- FontAwesome 5.2.0 loaded from a CDN (`use.fontawesome.com`) with SRI (`integrity`) hash.
- **Google Tag Manager** snippet (container `GTM-TKSCFQJ`) in both `<head>` (script) and `<body>` (`<noscript>` iframe fallback) — standard GTM install pattern.
- `<%= require('html!./assets/icons.svg') %>` — an `html-loader` webpack directive that inlines the contents of `src/assets/icons.svg` directly into the page (an SVG spritesheet for icon `<use>` references elsewhere in the app).
- `<div id="root"></div>` — the mount point targeted by `ReactDOM.render` in `src/index.jsx`.
- A **Mouseflow** session-recording/analytics snippet (`cdn.mouseflow.com`, project id `6bf71c32-...`), injected by dynamically creating a `<script>` tag.
- A `window.onpageshow` handler that force-reloads the page if it was restored from the browser's back-forward cache (`event.persisted`) — likely to avoid showing a stale canvas state after back-navigation.
- A **DoubleClick Floodlight** conversion-tracking tag (`9250690.fls.doubleclick.net`, activity `LPG_Original lab_2019-03`) with both a dynamically-written tracking `<iframe>` and a `<noscript>` fallback iframe, plus a MathTag pixel script (`pixel.mathtag.com`) — both are advertising/marketing conversion-tracking pixels, unrelated to the drawing tool itself.

## package.json

**Purpose**: Project manifest for `up-t` (v1.0.0, author "Alexey Korzun", ISC license). Defines the npm scripts driving the webpack 1.x build/deploy pipeline and the full dependency list (React 15-era stack, `three.js`, image-processing/canvas utilities, and the vendored `drawtool.js`'s runtime peer libraries).

### Scripts

| Script | Command | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| `start` | `webpack-dev-server -d --inline --progress --colors --port 8080 --host 0.0.0.0 --public 192.168.13.109` | Runs the webpack-dev-server with debug/inline mode on port 8080, bound to all interfaces, advertising a specific LAN IP (`--public`) — likely for testing from other devices on the same network. |
| `watch` | `NODE_ENV=development webpack` | One-off (non-watching, despite the name) development build. |
| `build` | `NODE_ENV=production webpack` | Production build via webpack. |
| `local` | `NODE_ENV=local webpack` | Local-environment build (uses `config.json`'s `local` endpoints). |
| `production` | `rm -rf build/; npm run build; cp _config.yml build/; cp drawtool.min.js build/; cp Jenkinsfile build/;` | Clean + production build, then copies Jekyll config, a separately-committed minified drawtool build, and the Jenkinsfile into `build/` (for GitHub Pages-style hosting that also serves these files). |
| `stagging` | `rm -rf build/; npm run watch; cp _config.yml build/; cp drawtool.min.js build/;` | (sic "staging") Clean + dev-mode build with the same file copies (minus Jenkinsfile). |
| `deploy-test` | `gh-pages -d build` | Publishes `build/` to the default `gh-pages` branch. |
| `deploy-w1` | `gh-pages -d build` | Identical to `deploy-test` (alternate named target, likely for a second GitHub Pages remote/workflow). |
| `deploy-production` | `node deploy.production.js` | Runs the custom script documented above (publishes to `deploy-production` branch). |
| `deploy-core-production` | `gh-pages -d build --branch deploy-production` | Equivalent one-liner alternative to `deploy-production` using the `gh-pages` CLI directly instead of the custom script. |
| `develop` | `rm -rf build/; npm run local; cp _config.yml build/; cp drawtool.min.js build/;` | Clean + local-environment build with the same file copies. |

### Dependencies (grouped)

**Core framework**: `react` / `react-dom` **^15.3.2** (pre-Fiber, pre-hooks React), `react-redux` **^4.4.5**, `redux` **^3.6.0**, `redux-actions` **^0.12.0** — an early React/Redux stack consistent with the webpack 1 / Babel 6 tooling.

**Canvas / design-tool runtime**: the vendored `src/draw-tool/drawtool.js` itself has no separate npm entry (it's committed source, not an npm dependency), but its likely fabric.js-based canvas engine pulls in adjacent runtime deps used directly by app code: `three` **^0.124.0** (3D product preview, paired with the hand-written `src/utils/OrbitControls.js`, `GLTFLoader.js`, `DRACOLoader.js`, `OBJExporter.js`, `SVGLoader.js`, `three-csg.js`/`csg-lib.js`/`csg-worker.js` for 3D boolean ops), `polygon-clipping` **^0.15.7** (vector path boolean operations, used by the laser-cutting path logic), `jszip` **^3.1.5** (packaging exported design assets), `@gfx/zopfli` **^1.0.11** (production build-time gzip compression via `webpack.config.js`'s `CompressionPlugin`), `exif-js` **^2.1.1** (reading EXIF orientation from uploaded photos before placing them on canvas).

**UI component libraries**: `react-color` (color pickers), `react-draggable` / `react-restricted-input` / `react-sortable-hoc` (drag/reorder interactions for canvas items and layer lists), `react-custom-scrollbars` / `react-smooth-scrollbar` (custom scrollbar styling), `react-responsive` / `react-responsive-carousel` (breakpoints + image carousels), `react-dropzone` / `react-file-drop` (image upload UX), `react-tabs`, `react-ink` (material-style ripple effect), `react-lazy-load-image-component`, `react-progressbar.js` / `progressbar.js` / `rc-progress` (upload/progress indicators), `intro.js` / `intro.js-react` / `introjs` (guided product tours), `sweetalert` / `sweetalert2` / `sweetalert2-react-content` (modal dialogs, used e.g. by `middleware/draw-tool.js`'s die-cut deletion dialog), `classnames` (conditional CSS class helper).

**Connectivity / integration**: `socket.io-client` **^4.4.1** (the real-time collaborative chat/design-sync channel driving `DrawTool.triggerDrawUpdateChat`/`'online:update'` events), `agora-rtc-sdk` **^3.6.11** (real-time audio/video, likely for a live chat-with-designer feature alongside the socket-based text chat), `react-ga` (Google Analytics), `whatwg-fetch` / `promise-polyfill` / `babel-polyfill` / `querystring` (polyfills for older-browser support, matching the ES2015 Babel target).

**Build-only / deploy**: `gh-pages` **^2.2.0** and `ghpages` **0.0.10** (two separate GitHub Pages publishing packages — the former used by `deploy.production.js` and the `deploy-*` npm scripts, the latter appears to be an unused/legacy duplicate), `compression-webpack-plugin` / `uglifyjs-webpack-plugin` (referenced from `webpack.config.js`'s production pipeline).

### devDependencies

The webpack 1.x toolchain itself: `webpack` **^1.15.0**, `webpack-dev-server` **^1.16.1**, `babel`/`babel-core`/`babel-loader`/`babel-eslint` **^6.x**, Babel presets `babel-preset-es2015` / `babel-preset-react` / `babel-preset-stage-1` / `babel-preset-react-optimize` (matching `.babelrc`), CSS pipeline (`node-sass`, `sass-loader`, `css-loader`, `style-loader`, `extract-text-webpack-plugin`, `postcss`, `postcss-loader`, `autoprefixer`), `html-loader` / `html-webpack-plugin` (for `src/index.html`'s templating and the inlined `icons.svg`), `copy-webpack-plugin` (static `assets/` copying), and the ESLint 3.x + Airbnb config stack (`eslint`, `eslint-config-airbnb`, `eslint-plugin-import`, `eslint-plugin-jsx-a11y`, `eslint-plugin-react`) matching `.eslintrc.js`.
